佛說海龍王經
fó shuō hǎi lóng wáng jīng
The Sutra Spoken by the Buddha on the Sea Dragon King — Complete, Volumes 1-4
西晉月氏國三藏竺法護譯
xī jìn yuè zhī guó sān zàng zhú fǎ hù yì
Translated in the Western Jin by the Tripitaka Master Dharmaraksa of the Yuezhi.

Volume 4, Chapter Twenty: Entrusting and Upholding
行品第一
xíng pǐn dì yī
Chapter One: Practice
聞如是：
wén rú shì：
Thus have I heard:
一時佛遊王舍城靈鷲山，
yī shí fó yóu wáng shě chéng líng jiù shān，
At one time the Buddha was dwelling on Vulture Peak at Rajagrha,
與大比丘眾俱，比丘八千，
yǔ dà bǐ qiū zhòng jù， bǐ qiū bā qiān，
together with a great assembly of bhiksus — eight thousand bhiksus —
菩薩萬二千，
pú sà wàn èr qiān，
and twelve thousand bodhisattvas,
一切大聖十方來會——
yī qiè dà shèng shí fāng lái huì——
all of them great sages come from the ten directions to the gathering.
眾德具足，得諸總持；
zhòng dé jù zú， dé zhū zǒng chí；
Complete in every virtue, they had attained the dharanis;
無所不博，辯才至真；
wú suǒ bù bó， biàn cái zhì zhēn；
there was nothing they had not mastered, and their eloquence was utterly true.
決一切疑，入大神通；
jué yī qiè yí， rù dà shén tōng；
They resolved every doubt and entered the great spiritual penetrations;
分剖慧義諸度無極，
fēn pōu huì yì zhū dù wú jí，
they dissected the meanings of wisdom and the perfections without limit,
濟於彼岸究暢開士，
jì yú bǐ àn jiū chàng kāi shì，
ferrying beings to the far shore, open-minded ones who fathomed all things.
定意正受諸佛諮嗟；
dìng yì zhèng shòu zhū fó zī jiē；
In concentration and right absorption they were praised by all the buddhas;
普遊殊域，神足飛行，降化眾魔；
pǔ yóu shū yù， shén zú fēi xíng， jiàng huà zhòng mó；
they roamed to distant realms, flew by spiritual power, and subdued the hosts of Mara.
分別諸法，知如本諦，
fēn bié zhū fǎ， zhī rú běn dì，
They discerned all dharmas and knew them according to the original truth,
覩見一切眾生之原；
dǔ jiàn yī qiè zhòng shēng zhī yuán；
and beheld the source of every living being.
積累道品，
jī lèi dào pǐn，
They accumulated the limbs of the Way,
於世八法而無所著，
yú shì bā fǎ ér wú suǒ zhuó，
and amid the eight worldly conditions were without attachment,
以大慈哀嚴身口意；
yǐ dà cí āi yán shēn kǒu yì；
adorning body, speech and mind with great loving-kindness and compassion.
被無極鎧過大精進，
bèi wú jí kǎi guò dà jīng jìn，
Clad in boundless armor, surpassing in great vigor,
於無數劫而不厭惓；
yú wú shù jié ér bù yàn quán；
through countless kalpas they never grew weary.
為師子吼開化外道，
wéi shī zǐ hǒu kāi huà wài dào，
They gave the lion's roar and converted those of other paths,
以不退轉印如印之；
yǐ bù tuì zhuǎn yìn rú yìn zhī；
sealing them as with the seal of non-regression;
曉了諸佛深要法藏——
xiǎo liǎo zhū fó shēn yào fǎ zàng——
and they understood the profound and essential Dharma-treasury of all buddhas.
其名曰：山光菩薩、惠山菩薩、
qí míng yuē： shān guāng pú sà、 huì shān pú sà、
Their names were: the Bodhisattvas Mountain Light, Mountain of Kindness,
大明菩薩、總持山剛菩薩、
dà míng pú sà、 zǒng chí shān gāng pú sà、
Great Brightness, Dharani Adamantine Mountain,
山鎧王菩薩、山頂菩薩、
shān kǎi wáng pú sà、 shān dǐng pú sà、
King of Mountain Armor, Mountain Summit,
山幢菩薩、山王菩薩、
shān chuáng pú sà、 shān wáng pú sà、
Mountain Banner, Mountain King,
石磨王菩薩、雷音菩薩、
shí mò wáng pú sà、 léi yīn pú sà、
King of the Grindstone, Thunder Voice,
雨王菩薩、寶雨菩薩、
yǔ wáng pú sà、 bǎo yǔ pú sà、
Rain King, Jewel Rain,
寶英菩薩、寶首菩薩、
bǎo yīng pú sà、 bǎo shǒu pú sà、
Jewel Blossom, Jewel Crest,
寶藏菩薩、寶明菩薩、
bǎo zàng pú sà、 bǎo míng pú sà、
Jewel Treasury, Jewel Brightness,
寶幢菩薩、寶頂菩薩、
bǎo chuáng pú sà、 bǎo dǐng pú sà、
Jewel Banner, Jewel Summit,
寶印手菩薩、寶暢菩薩、
bǎo yìn shǒu pú sà、 bǎo chàng pú sà、
Jewel Seal in Hand, Jewel Fluency,
寶嚴菩薩、寶水菩薩、
bǎo yán pú sà、 bǎo shuǐ pú sà、
Jewel Adornment, Jewel Water,
寶光菩薩、寶鎧菩薩、
bǎo guāng pú sà、 bǎo kǎi pú sà、
Jewel Radiance, Jewel Armor,
寶現菩薩、寶造菩薩、
bǎo xiàn pú sà、 bǎo zào pú sà、
Jewel Manifestation, Jewel Making,
樂嚴法菩薩、淨王菩薩、
lè yán fǎ pú sà、 jìng wáng pú sà、
Delighting in Adorning the Dharma, King of Purity,
嚴頂相菩薩、金光飾菩薩、
yán dǐng xiāng pú sà、 jīn guāng shì pú sà、
Adorned Crown-Mark, Golden Light Ornament,
寶髻菩薩、天冠菩薩、
bǎo jì pú sà、 tiān guān pú sà、
Jewel Topknot, Celestial Diadem,
千光菩薩、原嶮菩薩、
qiān guāng pú sà、 yuán xiǎn pú sà、
Thousand Lights, Plain and Precipice,
照昧菩薩、月辯菩薩、
zhào mèi pú sà、 yuè biàn pú sà、
Illuminer of Darkness, Moon Eloquence,
發意轉法輪菩薩、金光淨菩薩、
fā yì zhuǎn fǎ lún pú sà、 jīn guāng jìng pú sà、
Turning the Dharma Wheel at the Arousing of the Mind, Golden Light Purity,
常施無畏菩薩。
cháng shī wú wèi pú sà。
and Ever Bestowing Fearlessness.
萬二千菩薩，德皆如是。
wàn èr qiān pú sà， dé jiē rú shì。
All twelve thousand bodhisattvas were of such virtue.
是賢劫中大士彌勒、軟首等六十大聖，
shì xián jié zhōng dà shì mí lè、 ruǎn shǒu děng liù shí dà shèng，
There were also the sixty great sages of this Worthy Kalpa, the great beings Maitreya, Manjusri and the rest;
不可思議、解縛等十六正士，
bù kě sī yì、 jiě fù děng shí liù zhèng shì，
the sixteen right-minded beings, Inconceivable, Loosener of Bonds and the rest;
帝釋、四天王與忉利天人俱；
dì shì、 sì tiān wáng yǔ dāo lì tiān rén jù；
Sakra and the Four Heavenly Kings together with the devas of Trayastrimsa;
焰天、兜術天、不憍樂天、化自在天、
yàn tiān、 dōu shù tiān、 bù jiāo lè tiān、 huà zì zài tiān、
the Yama devas, the Tusita devas, the Nirmanarati devas, the Paranirmitavasavartin devas;
魔子道師、梵天王、梵淨天王、
mó zi dào shī、 fàn tiān wáng、 fàn jìng tiān wáng、
Daoshi the son of Mara; the Brahma King, the King of Pure Brahma,
善梵天王、梵具足天王、
shàn fàn tiān wáng、 fàn jù zú tiān wáng、
the King of Good Brahma, the King of Perfect Brahma,
大神妙天、淨居天、離垢光天，
dà shén miào tiān、 jìng jū tiān、 lí gòu guāng tiān，
the Great Divine Wondrous devas, the Suddhavasa devas, the devas of Undefiled Light,
乃至一善天、燕居無善神王，
nǎi zhì yī shàn tiān、 yān jū wú shàn shén wáng，
and even the devas of Single Goodness; the asura king Yanju,
各與眷屬六萬，
gè yǔ juàn shǔ liù wàn，
each with sixty thousand of his retinue;
山樹神王四萬二千，
shān shù shén wáng sì wàn èr qiān，
forty-two thousand kings of mountain and tree spirits;
力士神王，一名持華，三萬二千，
lì shì shén wáng， yī míng chí huā， sān wàn èr qiān，
thirty-two thousand mighty spirit kings, one of them named Flower Bearer,
與香音神俱；
yǔ xiāng yīn shén jù；
together with the gandharvas;
無焚龍王與七萬二千諸龍俱；
wú fén lóng wáng yǔ qī wàn èr qiān zhū lóng jù；
the Dragon King Anavatapta with seventy-two thousand dragons;
四方金翅鳥王，
sì fāng jīn chì niǎo wáng，
the garuda kings of the four quarters,
及餘一切諸大尊神、天龍鬼神、
jí yú yī qiè zhū dà zūn shén、 tiān lóng guǐ shén、
and all the other great and honored spirits — devas, dragons, ghosts and spirits,
無善神、鳳凰神王、山樹神王、甜柔神等，
wú shàn shén、 fèng huáng shén wáng、 shān shù shén wáng、 tián róu shén děng，
asuras, garuda kings, mountain-and-tree spirit kings, kinnaras and the rest —
各與眷屬來詣佛所。
gè yǔ juàn shǔ lái yì fó suǒ。
each with his retinue came to where the Buddha was.
稽首畢，一面住。
qǐ shǒu bì， yí miàn zhù。
Having bowed their heads, they stood to one side.
比丘、比丘尼、清信士、清信女前為佛作禮，
bǐ qiū、 bǐ qiū ní、 qīng xìn shì、 qīng xìn nǚ qián wèi fó zuò lǐ，
Bhiksus, bhiksunis, laymen and laywomen came forward and made obeisance to the Buddha,
各坐一面。
gè zuò yí miàn。
and each sat down to one side.
彼時佛與若干百千之眾營從圍遶，
bǐ shí fó yǔ ruò gān bǎi qiān zhī zhòng yíng cóng wéi rào，
At that time the Buddha was encircled by a following of many hundreds of thousands.
佛處諸天嚴淨師子高廣之座，
fó chù zhū tiān yán jìng shī zǐ gāo guǎng zhī zuò，
He was seated upon a lion throne, high and broad, adorned and purified by the devas,
為四部眾而普說法。
wéi sì bù zhòng ér pǔ shuō fǎ。
and preached the Dharma universally to the fourfold assembly.
佛在眾中如安明山王現于大海，
fó zài zhòng zhōng rú ān míng shān wáng xiàn yú dà hǎi，
Amid the assembly the Buddha was like the royal Mount Sumeru appearing above the great ocean:
德超諸天，世無雙比；
dé chāo zhū tiān， shì wú shuāng bǐ；
his virtue surpassed all the devas, and in the world he had no equal;
光明巍巍，靡所不照。
guāng míng wēi wēi， mí suǒ bù zhào。
his radiance was lofty, and there was nothing it did not illumine.
如來威變，應時空中化有寶蓋，
rú lái wēi biàn， yìng shí kōng zhōng huà yǒu bǎo gài，
By the Tathagata's majestic transformation there appeared at once in the sky a jeweled canopy,
眾珍雜珓，遍覆四方。
zhòng zhēn zá jiào， biàn fù sì fāng。
wrought of many treasures intermingled, covering all four quarters.
無數百千垂珠瓔珞，
wú shù bǎi qiān chuí zhū yīng luò，
Countless hundreds of thousands of pearl garlands hung down,
青黃赤白無垢寶珠，
qīng huáng chì bái wú gòu bǎo zhū，
with undefiled gems of blue, yellow, red and white,
照虛空珠，光從珠出，
zhào xū kōng zhū， guāng cóng zhū chū，
and pearls that lit up the empty sky, light streaming forth from them,
其色無量不可稱計。
qí sè wú liàng bù kě chēng jì。
in colors immeasurable and beyond all reckoning.
雨諸香華，華至于膝，
yǔ zhū xiāng huā， huā zhì yú xī，
Fragrant flowers rained down until they reached the knee,
虛空之中出大雷音，雨眾名香。
xū kōng zhī zhōng chū dà léi yīn， yǔ zhòng míng xiāng。
and from the sky came a great sound of thunder, raining down every famous incense.
於是賢者大目犍連，承佛聖旨，
yú shì xián zhě dà mù jiān lián， chéng fó shèng zhǐ，
Thereupon the venerable Mahamaudgalyayana, receiving the Buddha's holy intent,
前問佛言：「今所感動，未曾見聞，
qián wèn fó yán： 「jīn suǒ gǎn dòng， wèi céng jiàn wén，
came forward and asked: “What has been stirred forth today has never been seen or heard before.
此何瑞應？」
cǐ hé ruì yīng？」
What auspicious sign is this?”
佛告目連：「今海龍王欲來見佛，
fó gào mù lián： 「jīn hǎi lóng wáng yù lái jiàn fó，
The Buddha told Maudgalyayana: “The Sea Dragon King is about to come and behold the Buddha;
故先現瑞。」
gù xiān xiàn ruì。」
therefore these portents appear beforehand.”
佛語未竟，尋時龍王與七十二億婇女、
fó yǔ wèi jìng， xún shí lóng wáng yǔ qī shí èr yì cǎi nǚ、
Before the Buddha had finished speaking, the Dragon King arrived with seven billion two hundred million palace women
八十四億眷屬，
bā shí sì yì juàn shǔ，
and eight billion four hundred million of his retinue,
皆齎香華、幢幡、寶蓋、百千伎樂，
jiē jī xiāng huā、 chuáng fān、 bǎo gài、 bǎi qiān jì yuè，
all bearing incense and flowers, banners and pennants, jeweled canopies, and a hundred thousand kinds of music,
往詣佛所。
wǎng yì fó suǒ。
and came to where the Buddha was.
前稽首畢，遶佛七匝，
qián qǐ shǒu bì， rào fó qī zā，
Having bowed their heads before him, they circled the Buddha seven times,
各以所持用散佛上，伎樂供養，
gè yǐ suǒ chí yòng sàn fó shàng， jì yuè gòng yǎng，
scattered what they carried over the Buddha, and made offering with music.
與中宮眷屬俱住佛前，以偈讚曰：
yǔ zhōng gōng juàn shǔ jù zhù fó qián， yǐ jì zàn yuē：
Then, with the retinue of his inner palace, he stood before the Buddha and praised him in verse:
「慈施愍傷俗，示現與世眼，
「cí shī mǐn shāng sú， shì xiàn yǔ shì yǎn，
“In kindness you give, pitying the world; you reveal yourself as the eye of the world.
雖生於世俗，無著如蓮華。
suī shēng yú shì sú， wú zhuó rú lián huā。
Though born into the world, you are unstained, like the lotus flower.
施俗之安隱，在世照三世，
shī sú zhī ān yǐn， zài shì zhào sān shì，
You bestow peace and ease upon the world; dwelling here, you illumine the three times.
解法如日光，稽首世最上。
xiè fǎ rú rì guāng， qǐ shǒu shì zuì shàng。
You expound the Dharma like sunlight — we bow to the Supreme One of the world.
十力超施戒，自調成眷屬，
shí lì chāo shī jiè， zì diào chéng juàn shǔ，
In the ten powers you surpass all in giving and precepts; self-tamed, you form your retinue;
燒除塵勞冥，御眾如調馬。
shāo chú chén láo míng， yù zhòng rú diào mǎ。
you burn away the gloom of defilement and guide the multitude as one tames a horse.
施與七大財，恩慈加眾生，
shī yǔ qī dà cái， ēn cí jiā zhòng shēng，
You bestow the seven great riches and extend kindness to living beings;
為一切父母，稽首最福田。
wéi yī qiè fù mǔ， qǐ shǒu zuì fú tián。
you are father and mother to all — we bow to the supreme field of merit.
眉間相光曜，如日白雪光，
méi jiān xiāng guāng yào， rú rì bái xuě guāng，
The mark between your brows blazes with light, like the sun upon white snow;
梵天人在上，無能見其頂。
fàn tiān rén zài shàng， wú néng jiàn qí dǐng。
though Brahma gods stand above, none can see your crown.
佛面出大光，蒙光獲安隱，
fó miàn chū dà guāng， méng guāng huò ān yǐn，
From the Buddha's face streams a great light, and those it touches gain peace and ease;
普照百千國，至于無擇獄。
pǔ zhào bǎi qiān guó， zhì yú wú zé yù。
it shines through a hundred thousand lands, reaching even the Avici hell.
柔軟言無極，解決眾疑結，
róu ruǎn yán wú jí， jiě jué zhòng yí jié，
Your gentle speech is without limit and unties the knots of every doubt;
音遍天世間，清淨無垢穢。
yīn biàn tiān shì jiān， qīng jìng wú gòu huì。
your voice pervades the heavens and the world, pure and free of stain.
除婬怒癡冥，照以智慧光，
chú yín nù chī míng， zhào yǐ zhì huì guāng，
You dispel the darkness of lust, anger and delusion, shining with the light of wisdom;
施安令歡喜，為示現解脫。
shī ān lìng huān xǐ， wéi shì xiàn jiě tuō。
you bestow peace and give joy, revealing liberation to all.
無礙達三世，求比無等倫，
wú ài dá sān shì， qiú bǐ wú děng lún，
Unobstructed, you penetrate the three times; seek his equal and there is none;
知人群萌行，了善惡所趣。
zhī rén qún méng xíng， le shàn è suǒ qù。
you know the conduct of the multitudes and understand where good and evil lead.
觀察人根原，一時咸能覩，
guān chá rén gēn yuán， yī shí xián néng dǔ，
You examine the root and origin of every being and behold them all at once;
開心令解脫，稽首諸慧上。
kāi xīn lìng jiě tuō， qǐ shǒu zhū huì shàng。
you open their minds and set them free — we bow to the highest of all wisdom.
百千億諸魔，詣樹求佛便，
bǎi qiān yì zhū mó， yì shù qiú fó biàn，
A hundred thousand million maras came to the tree seeking advantage over the Buddha;
至德願威神，降化伏邪心。
zhì dé yuàn wēi shén， jiàng huà fú xié xīn。
by your utmost virtue and majestic power you subdued and converted their crooked minds.
不瞋不厭惓，導以慈哀力，
bù chēn bù yàn quán， dǎo yǐ cí āi lì，
Without anger, without weariness, you lead by the power of kindness and compassion.
供養世之尊，孰敢懈慢者。
gòng yǎng shì zhī zūn， shú gǎn xiè màn zhě。
Making offering to the Honored One of the world, who would dare be idle or remiss?
觀法無所有，譬之如虛空，
guān fǎ wú suǒ yǒu， pì zhī rú xū kōng，
You contemplate dharmas as having nothing at all, likened to empty space,
猶電霧泡沫，幻化及野馬。
yóu diàn wù pào mò， huàn huà jí yě mǎ。
like lightning, mist and foam, like conjuring and a shimmering mirage.
本空緣相與，計之無吾我，
běn kōng yuán xiāng yǔ， jì zhī wú wú wǒ，
Originally empty, they arise together through conditions; examined, there is no self in them.
照示生死法，是故莫不供。
zhào shì shēng sǐ fǎ， shì gù mò bù gōng。
You illumine and reveal the dharma of birth and death — therefore all make offering.
所以無數劫，勤行億那術，
suǒ yǐ wú shù jié， qín xíng yì nà shù，
Thus for countless kalpas you practiced diligently through millions of nayutas,
供養億萬姟，不可計諸佛。
gòng yǎng yì wàn gāi， bù kě jì zhū fó。
making offerings to buddhas beyond all counting, in millions upon millions.
布施及戒忍，精進禪智慧，
bù shī jí jiè rěn， jīng jìn chán zhì huì，
Giving, precepts and patience, vigor, dhyana and wisdom —
尊願已具足，稽首大聖雄。」
zūn yuàn yǐ jù zú， qǐ shǒu dà shèng xióng。」
Honored One, your vows are fulfilled; we bow to the great heroic Sage.”
於是海龍王說此偈讚佛已，前白佛言：
yú shì hǎi lóng wáng shuō cǐ jì zàn fó yǐ， qián bái fó yán：
When the Sea Dragon King had praised the Buddha with this verse, he came forward and said:
「願欲有所問，儻肯聽者乃敢宣陳！」
「yuàn yù yǒu suǒ wèn， tǎng kěn tīng zhě nǎi gǎn xuān chén！」
“There is something I wish to ask; if you would consent to hear it, I shall venture to set it forth.”
佛言：「在汝所問。
fó yán： 「zài rǔ suǒ wèn。
The Buddha said: “Ask what you will.
若有疑者，如來當為具發遣之！」
ruò yǒu yí zhě， rú lái dāng wéi jù fā qiǎn zhī！」
If you have any doubt, the Tathagata will fully resolve it for you.”
龍王見聽，喜踊問曰：
lóng wáng jiàn tīng， xǐ yǒng wèn yuē：
Permitted to speak, the Dragon King leapt with joy and asked:
「何謂菩薩除諸惡趣？
「hé wèi pú sà chú zhū è qù？
“How does a bodhisattva remove the evil destinies?
何謂菩薩超出諸難？
hé wèi pú sà chāo chū zhū nàn？
How does a bodhisattva rise above all hardships?
何謂菩薩生天上人間？
hé wèi pú sà shēng tiān shàng rén jiān？
How is a bodhisattva born among devas and men?
何謂菩薩不離諸佛？
hé wèi pú sà bù lí zhū fó？
How is a bodhisattva never parted from the buddhas?
何謂菩薩得值善友？
hé wèi pú sà dé zhí shàn yǒu？
How does a bodhisattva meet with good friends?
何謂菩薩常在安隱？
hé wèi pú sà cháng zài ān yǐn？
How does a bodhisattva always dwell in peace and ease?
何謂菩薩常懷篤信？
hé wèi pú sà cháng huái dǔ xìn？
How does a bodhisattva always hold firm faith?
何謂菩薩多所悅護？
hé wèi pú sà duō suǒ yuè hù？
How does a bodhisattva gladden and protect many?
何謂菩薩濟眾因緣？
hé wèi pú sà jì zhòng yīn yuán？
How does a bodhisattva aid beings through causes and conditions?
何謂菩薩長益善法？
hé wèi pú sà cháng yì shàn fǎ？
How does a bodhisattva increase wholesome dharmas?
何謂菩薩喜造德本？
hé wèi pú sà xǐ zào dé běn？
How does a bodhisattva delight in making roots of virtue?
何謂菩薩常樂於義？
hé wèi pú sà cháng lè yú yì？
How does a bodhisattva always delight in the meaning?
何謂菩薩不著五陰？
hé wèi pú sà bù zhuó wǔ yīn？
How is a bodhisattva unattached to the five aggregates?
何謂菩薩常好於法？
hé wèi pú sà cháng hǎo yú fǎ？
How does a bodhisattva always love the Dharma?
何謂菩薩樂於法樂？
hé wèi pú sà lè yú fǎ lè？
How does a bodhisattva delight in the joy of Dharma?
何謂菩薩所聞無厭？
hé wèi pú sà suǒ wén wú yàn？
How does a bodhisattva never tire of what he hears?
何謂菩薩請益觀義？
hé wèi pú sà qǐng yì guān yì？
How does a bodhisattva seek further teaching and contemplate the meaning?
何謂菩薩聞能奉行？
hé wèi pú sà wén néng fèng xíng？
How does a bodhisattva put into practice what he hears?
何謂菩薩具出家德？
hé wèi pú sà jù chū jiā dé？
How does a bodhisattva perfect the virtue of one gone forth?
何謂菩薩離居順戒？
hé wèi pú sà lí jū shùn jiè？
How does a bodhisattva leave the household and accord with the precepts?
何謂菩薩棄於重擔？
hé wèi pú sà qì yú zhòng dàn？
How does a bodhisattva lay down the heavy burden?
何謂菩薩常處樹下？
hé wèi pú sà cháng chù shù xià？
How does a bodhisattva always dwell beneath a tree?
何謂菩薩樂處閑居？
hé wèi pú sà lè chù xián jū？
How does a bodhisattva delight in secluded dwelling?
何謂菩薩而獨燕處？
hé wèi pú sà ér dú yān chù？
How does a bodhisattva abide alone in quietude?
何謂菩薩離諸諛諂？
hé wèi pú sà lí zhū yú chǎn？
How does a bodhisattva leave behind all flattery?
何謂菩薩具出家慧？
hé wèi pú sà jù chū jiā huì？
How does a bodhisattva perfect the wisdom of one gone forth?
何謂菩薩入深要法？
hé wèi pú sà rù shēn yào fǎ？
How does a bodhisattva enter the profound and essential Dharma?
何謂菩薩觀法如幻？
hé wèi pú sà guān fǎ rú huàn？
How does a bodhisattva contemplate dharmas as illusions?
何謂菩薩不墮滅見？
hé wèi pú sà bù duò miè jiàn？
How does a bodhisattva avoid falling into the view of annihilation?
何謂菩薩不墮常見？
hé wèi pú sà bù duò cháng jiàn？
How does a bodhisattva avoid falling into the view of permanence?
何謂菩薩超因緣法？
hé wèi pú sà chāo yīn yuán fǎ？
How does a bodhisattva transcend the dharma of causes and conditions?
何謂菩薩離諸邪見？
hé wèi pú sà lí zhū xié jiàn？
How does a bodhisattva leave behind all wrong views?
何謂菩薩神通自樂？
hé wèi pú sà shén tōng zì lè？
How does a bodhisattva take joy in the spiritual penetrations?
何謂菩薩而得六通？
hé wèi pú sà ér dé liù tōng？
How does a bodhisattva gain the six penetrations?
何謂菩薩而得慧通？
hé wèi pú sà ér dé huì tōng？
How does a bodhisattva gain the penetration of wisdom?
何謂菩薩漏盡神通？
hé wèi pú sà lòu jǐn shén tōng？
How does a bodhisattva gain the penetration of the exhaustion of the outflows?
何謂菩薩現無蓋慈？
hé wèi pú sà xiàn wú gài cí？
How does a bodhisattva manifest unveiled loving-kindness?
何謂菩薩所見無礙？
hé wèi pú sà suǒ jiàn wú ài？
How is a bodhisattva's vision unobstructed?
何謂菩薩曉了眾生心之所行？
hé wèi pú sà xiǎo liǎo zhòng shēng xīn zhī suǒ xíng？
How does a bodhisattva understand the workings of living beings' minds?
何謂菩薩行無厭足？
hé wèi pú sà xíng wú yàn zú？
How does a bodhisattva practice without ever being satisfied?
何謂菩薩分別所受教化之言？
hé wèi pú sà fēn bié suǒ shòu jiào huà zhī yán？
How does a bodhisattva discern the words of teaching he has received?
何謂菩薩降伏魔怨？
hé wèi pú sà xiáng fú mó yuàn？
How does a bodhisattva subdue Mara and his foes?
何謂菩薩離諸恐懼？
hé wèi pú sà lí zhū kǒng jù？
How does a bodhisattva leave behind all fear?
何謂菩薩御退轉者？
hé wèi pú sà yù tuì zhuǎn zhě？
How does a bodhisattva guide those who have fallen back?
何謂菩薩得不退轉？
hé wèi pú sà dé bù tuì zhuǎn？
How does a bodhisattva attain non-regression?
何謂菩薩逮不起忍？
hé wèi pú sà dǎi bù qǐ rěn？
How does a bodhisattva attain the forbearance of non-arising?
何謂菩薩過於諸淨？
hé wèi pú sà guò yú zhū jìng？
How does a bodhisattva pass beyond all purities?
何謂菩薩諸行清淨？
hé wèi pú sà zhū xíng qīng jìng？
How are all a bodhisattva's practices pure?
何謂菩薩世尊授決？」
hé wèi pú sà shì zūn shòu jué？」
How does a bodhisattva receive the World-Honored One's prediction?”
佛言：「善哉，善哉！海龍王！
fó yán： 「shàn zāi， shàn zāi！ hǎi lóng wáng！
The Buddha said: “Excellent, excellent, Sea Dragon King!
乃問如來如此之義。
nǎi wèn rú lái rú cǐ zhī yì。
That you should ask the Tathagata about such matters!
諦聽，諦聽！善思念之！」
dì tīng， dì tīng！ shàn sī niàn zhī！」
Listen well, listen well, and consider it carefully.”
龍王曰：「唯然，世尊！願樂欲聞！」
lóng wáng yuē： 「wéi rán， shì zūn！ yuàn lè yù wén！」
The Dragon King said: “So be it, World-Honored One. We gladly wish to hear.”
佛言：「菩薩有四事棄諸惡趣。何等為四？
fó yán： 「pú sà yǒu sì shì qì zhū è qù。 hé děng wéi sì？
The Buddha said: “A bodhisattva has four things by which he casts off the evil destinies. Which four?
菩薩無害心於眾生，常護十德，
pú sà wú hài xīn yú zhòng shēng， cháng hù shí dé，
He bears no harmful thought toward living beings; he always guards the ten virtues;
不說人短亦不輕慢，
bù shuō rén duǎn yì bù qīng màn，
he does not speak of others' faults, nor does he slight them;
自省己過不訟彼穢；是為四。
zì xǐng jǐ guò bù sòng bǐ huì； shì wéi sì。
he examines his own errors and does not arraign the impurities of others. These are the four.
復有四事超出諸難。何等為四？
fù yǒu sì shì chāo chū zhū nàn。 hé děng wéi sì？
There are four more by which he rises above all hardships. Which four?
常歎三寶佛、法、聖眾，
cháng tàn sān bǎo fó、 fǎ、 shèng zhòng，
He always praises the Three Jewels — Buddha, Dharma, and the holy assembly;
有樂法者而不嬈亂，不造人疑，
yǒu lè fǎ zhě ér bù ráo luàn， bù zào rén yí，
he does not disturb those who delight in the Dharma, nor sow doubt in others;
有猶豫者悉開導之；是為四。
yǒu yóu yù zhě xī kāi dǎo zhī； shì wéi sì。
and he guides all who waver. These are the four.
復有四事生天上人間。何等四？
fù yǒu sì shì shēng tiān shàng rén jiān。 hé děng sì？
There are four more by which he is born among devas and men. Which four?
不捨道心，又教他人亦不毀戒，
bù shě dào xīn， yòu jiào tā rén yì bù huǐ jiè，
He does not abandon the mind of the Way; he teaches others and does not break the precepts;
心願清淨，為人說經而發大哀；是為四事。
xīn yuàn qīng jìng， wèi rén shuō jīng ér fā dà āi； shì wéi sì shì。
his aspiration is pure; and he expounds the sutras for others out of great compassion. These are the four.
復有八事不離諸佛。何等八？
fù yǒu bā shì bù lí zhū fó。 hé děng bā？
There are eight more by which he is never parted from the buddhas. Which eight?
常念諸佛，供養如來，嗟歎世尊，
cháng niàn zhū fó， gòng yǎng rú lái， jiē tàn shì zūn，
He is ever mindful of the buddhas; he makes offerings to the Tathagata; he extols the World-Honored One;
作佛像形，勸化眾生使見如來，
zuò fó xiàng xíng， quàn huà zhòng shēng shǐ jiàn rú lái，
he has images of the Buddha made; he exhorts beings so that they may behold the Tathagata;
其所向方聞佛之名，
qí suǒ xiàng fāng wén fó zhī míng，
in whatever direction he turns he hears the name of a buddha;
願生彼國志不怯弱，
yuàn shēng bǐ guó zhì bù qiè ruò，
he vows to be born in that land, and his resolve does not falter;
常樂微妙佛之正慧；是為八事。
cháng lè wēi miào fó zhī zhèng huì； shì wéi bā shì。
and he always delights in the subtle right wisdom of the Buddha. These are the eight.
復有四事得值善友。何謂四？
fù yǒu sì shì dé zhí shàn yǒu。 hé wèi sì？
There are four more by which he meets good friends. Which four?
不慢無諂，常加恭敬，
bù màn wú chǎn， cháng jiā gōng jìng，
He is neither arrogant nor fawning; he is always respectful;
柔和順言而不自大，常受言教；是為四。
róu hé shùn yán ér bù zì dà， cháng shòu yán jiào； shì wéi sì。
his speech is gentle and agreeable and he does not exalt himself; and he always accepts instruction. These are the four.
復有三事常在隱處。何等三？
fù yǒu sān shì cháng zài yǐn chù。 hé děng sān？
There are three more by which he always dwells in peace. Which three?
不剛不䩕而不諛諂，除諸貪嫉，
bù gāng bù áng ér bù yú chǎn， chú zhū tān jí，
He is neither harsh nor stiff, and does not flatter; he removes greed and envy;
見人得供代其歡喜；是為三。
jiàn rén dé gōng dài qí huān xǐ； shì wéi sān。
and when he sees another receive offerings he rejoices on his behalf. These are the three.
復有五事常懷篤信。何等五？
fù yǒu wǔ shì cháng huái dǔ xìn。 hé děng wǔ？
There are five more by which he always holds firm faith. Which five?
曉樂脫力，積功德力，入報應力，
xiǎo lè tuō lì， jī gōng dé lì， rù bào yìng lì，
The power of understanding and delighting in liberation; the power of accumulated merit; the power of entering into recompense;
遵道心力，將御法力；是為五。
zūn dào xīn lì， jiāng yù fǎ lì； shì wéi wǔ。
the power of upholding the mind of the Way; and the power of guiding by the Dharma. These are the five.
復有二事多所悅護。何等二？
fù yǒu èr shì duō suǒ yuè hù。 hé děng èr？
There are two more by which he gladdens and protects many. Which two?
不捨歡喜，不在瞋恨；是為二。
bù shě huān xǐ， bù zài chēn hèn； shì wéi èr。
He does not abandon joy, and he does not abide in hatred. These are the two.
復有二十事護眾因緣。何等二十？
fù yǒu èr shí shì hù zhòng yīn yuán。 hé děng èr shí？
There are twenty more by which he sustains beings through causes and conditions. Which twenty?
常信佛教，不著他緣，
cháng xìn fó jiào， bù zhuó tā yuán，
He always trusts the Buddha's teaching; he does not cling to other conditions;
所作自護，他作他受，
suǒ zuò zì hù， tā zuò tā shòu，
what he does he answers for himself, and what others do others receive;
法法相應，法法相照，
fǎ fǎ xiàng yīng， fǎ fǎ xiàng zhào，
dharma answers to dharma, and dharma illumines dharma;
善惡報應，無亂不順，
shàn è bào yìng， wú luàn bù shùn，
good and evil have their recompense, without confusion or discord;
心無想念，無我無人，
xīn wú xiǎng niàn， wú wǒ wú rén，
the mind is free of conceiving; there is no self and no person;
都無所有，亦無往來，
dōu wú suǒ yǒu， yì wú wǎng lái，
there is nothing whatever, and no coming or going;
無所歸趣，除因緣報，
wú suǒ guī qù， chú yīn yuán bào，
there is nowhere to resort to; he removes the recompense of causes and conditions;
由罪福安危，將護諸緣，
yóu zuì fú ān wēi， jiāng hù zhū yuán，
safety and peril follow from sin and merit; he guards all conditions;
諸佛、世尊皆由清淨而成道德，
zhū fó、 shì zūn jiē yóu qīng jìng ér chéng dào dé，
the buddhas, the World-Honored Ones, all accomplished the virtue of the Way through purity;
除眾惡事，以故吾等修行善本；是為二十。
chú zhòng è shì， yǐ gù wú děng xiū xíng shàn běn； shì wéi èr shí。
he removes all evil deeds — therefore we cultivate the roots of good. These are the twenty.
復有二事長益善法。何等二？
fù yǒu èr shì cháng yì shàn fǎ。 hé děng èr？
There are two more by which he increases wholesome dharmas. Which two?
知於三品，行無放逸；是為二。
zhī yú sān pǐn， xíng wú fàng yì； shì wéi èr。
He knows the three grades, and he practices without laxity. These are the two.
復有二事。何等二？
fù yǒu èr shì。 hé děng èr？
There are two more. Which two?
喜造德本，亦不想報；是為二。
xǐ zào dé běn， yì bù xiǎng bào； shì wéi èr。
He delights in making roots of virtue, yet does not conceive of reward. These are the two.
復有五事常樂於義。何等五？
fù yǒu wǔ shì cháng lè yú yì。 hé děng wǔ？
There are five more by which he always delights in the meaning. Which five?
不著色、聲、香、味、識；是為五。
bù zhuó sè、 shēng、 xiāng、 wèi、 shí； shì wéi wǔ。
He does not cling to form, sound, scent, taste, or consciousness. These are the five.
復有五事常好於法。何等為五？
fù yǒu wǔ shì cháng hǎo yú fǎ。 hé děng wéi wǔ？
There are five more by which he always loves the Dharma. Which five?
不貪色、痛，志得智、慧，擁護一切；是為五。
bù tān sè、 tòng， zhì dé zhì、 huì， yōng hù yī qiè； shì wéi wǔ。
He does not crave form or sensation; he sets his mind on knowledge and wisdom; and he protects all. These are the five.
復有六事常樂於法。何等六？
fù yǒu liù shì cháng lè yú fǎ。 hé děng liù？
There are six more by which he always delights in the Dharma. Which six?
樂於五根，不樂五欲；
lè yú wǔ gēn， bù lè wǔ yù；
He delights in the five faculties and not in the five desires;
常樂法會，不樂世談；
cháng lè fǎ huì， bù lè shì tán；
he delights in Dharma gatherings and not in worldly talk;
樂講說經，不樂衣食；
lè jiǎng shuō jīng， bù lè yī shí；
he delights in expounding the sutras and not in clothing and food;
常樂觀法，不樂不淨；
cháng lè guān fǎ， bù lè bù jìng；
he delights in contemplating the Dharma and not in what is impure;
樂遵修法，不樂文字；
lè zūn xiū fǎ， bù lè wén zì；
he delights in cultivating the Dharma and not in mere letters;
樂於佛法，不樂聲聞、緣覺法；是為六。
lè yú fó fǎ， bù lè shēng wén、 yuán jué fǎ； shì wéi liù。
he delights in the Buddha's Dharma and not in that of the sravaka or pratyekabuddha. These are the six.
復有八事樂於法樂。何等八？
fù yǒu bā shì lè yú fǎ lè。 hé děng bā？
There are eight more by which he delights in the joy of Dharma. Which eight?
樂講佛道，不樂卑賤；
lè jiǎng fó dào， bù lè bēi jiàn；
He delights in expounding the buddha path and not in what is base;
樂度無極，不樂聲聞、緣覺道；
lè dù wú jí， bù lè shēng wén、 yuán jué dào；
he delights in the perfections without limit and not in the sravaka or pratyekabuddha path;
樂讚四恩，不樂非法；
lè zàn sì ēn， bù lè fēi fǎ；
he delights in praising the four kindnesses and not in what is contrary to Dharma;
樂大慈哀，不樂世事；
lè dà cí āi， bù lè shì shì；
he delights in great loving-kindness and compassion and not in worldly affairs;
樂說大道，不樂終始；
lè shuō dà dào， bù lè zhōng shǐ；
he delights in preaching the great Way and not in birth and death;
樂講深法緣起之本，
lè jiǎng shēn fǎ yuán qǐ zhī běn，
he delights in expounding the profound Dharma, the root of dependent arising,
不樂常、無常、我、人、壽命；
bù lè cháng、 wú cháng、 wǒ、 rén、 shòu mìng；
and not in permanence, impermanence, self, person, or lifespan;
樂空、無想、無願真諦之法，
lè kōng、 wú xiǎng、 wú yuàn zhēn dì zhī fǎ，
he delights in the true dharma of emptiness, signlessness and wishlessness,
不樂調戲，離放逸想；
bù lè tiáo xì， lí fàng yì xiǎng；
and not in frivolity, departing from thoughts of laxity;
樂嚴佛國，不樂觀滅；是為八。
lè yán fó guó， bù lè guān miè； shì wéi bā。
he delights in adorning a buddha-land and not in contemplating extinction. These are the eight.
「復有五事所聞無厭。何等五？
「fù yǒu wǔ shì suǒ wén wú yàn。 hé děng wǔ？
“There are five more by which he never tires of what he hears. Which five?
博聞智慧，利於明達，聞無厭足；
bó wén zhì huì， lì yú míng dá， wén wú yàn zú；
Wide learning and wisdom sharpen his understanding, so he never tires of hearing;
普聽不懈，決諸狐疑，聞無厭足；
pǔ tīng bù xiè， jué zhū hú yí， wén wú yàn zú；
listening universally without slackening, he resolves every doubt, so he never tires of hearing;
因聞覺了塵勞恚恨，故無厭足；
yīn wén jué le chén láo huì hèn， gù wú yàn zú；
through hearing he awakens to defilement, toil, hatred and resentment, so he never tires;
因聞斷欲而除一切眾生垢著，故無厭足；
yīn wén duàn yù ér chú yī qiè zhòng shēng gòu zhuó， gù wú yàn zú；
through hearing he severs desire and removes the stains and attachments of all beings, so he never tires;
因聞勇猛決一切疑，故無厭足；是為五。
yīn wén yǒng měng jué yī qiè yí， gù wú yàn zú； shì wéi wǔ。
through hearing he grows valiant and resolves every doubt, so he never tires. These are the five.
復有二事觀義求聞不惓。何等二？
fù yǒu èr shì guān yì qiú wén bù quán。 hé děng èr？
There are two more by which he contemplates the meaning and seeks to hear without weariness. Which two?
興於賢聖正見之行，
xìng yú xián shèng zhèng jiàn zhī xíng，
He rouses the practice of right view belonging to the holy sages,
得無礙辯總持之要；是為二。
dé wú ài biàn zǒng chí zhī yào； shì wéi èr。
and gains unobstructed eloquence and the essentials of dharani. These are the two.
復有十事聞能奉行。何等十？
fù yǒu shí shì wén néng fèng xíng。 hé děng shí？
There are ten more by which he puts into practice what he hears. Which ten?
利知厭足，在於閑居；
lì zhī yàn zú， zài yú xián jū；
He knows keenly when he has enough, and dwells in seclusion;
身口心寂，進止安詳；
shēn kǒu xīn jì， jìn zhǐ ān xiáng；
body, speech and mind are still, and his going and staying are tranquil;
所聞觀淨；
suǒ wén guān jìng；
what he hears he contemplates purely;
獨處少事，不樂眾閙；
dú chù shǎo shì， bù lè zhòng nào；
he dwells alone with few affairs and takes no delight in crowds and clamor;
初夜後夜常觀精進；
chū yè hòu yè cháng guān jīng jìn；
in the first and last watches of the night he is ever mindful and vigorous;
敬重善友；志懷羞恥；
jìng zhòng shàn yǒu； zhì huái xiū chǐ；
he honors good friends; he keeps a sense of shame;
常以大哀護於一切；等賢聖禪；
cháng yǐ dà āi hù yú yī qiè； děng xián shèng chán；
he always protects all beings with great compassion; he equals the sages in dhyana;
至德具足，以惠救護天上世間；是為十。
zhì dé jù zú， yǐ huì jiù hù tiān shàng shì jiān； shì wéi shí。
his utmost virtue is complete, and with kindness he rescues and shelters the heavens and the world. These are the ten.
復有五事具出家德。何等五？
fù yǒu wǔ shì jù chū jiā dé。 hé děng wǔ？
There are five more by which he perfects the virtue of one gone forth. Which five?
所作已成，不復忘失；滅除塵結；
suǒ zuò yǐ chéng， bù fù wàng shī； miè chú chén jié；
What he set out to do is accomplished and he no longer forgets it; he extinguishes the knots of defilement;
其心當捨一切諸著；
qí xīn dāng shě yī qiè zhū zhuó；
his mind lets go of every attachment;
諸佛、世尊不訟其短；
zhū fó、 shì zūn bù sòng qí duǎn；
the buddhas, the World-Honored Ones, do not arraign his faults;
已得解脫，見諸縛者為說脫法；是為五。
yǐ dé jiě tuō， jiàn zhū fù zhě wèi shuō tuō fǎ； shì wéi wǔ。
and having gained liberation, when he sees those in bondage he preaches the dharma of release. These are the five.
復有五出家順戒。何等五？
fù yǒu wǔ chū jiā shùn jiè。 hé děng wǔ？
There are five more by which one gone forth accords with the precepts. Which five?
救順禁法救濟毀戒，
jiù shùn jìn fǎ jiù jì huǐ jiè，
By according with the restraining dharma he rescues those who break the precepts;
順諸所聞救濟尠聞，
shùn zhū suǒ wén jiù jì xiǎn wén，
by according with what he has heard he rescues those of little learning;
順所定意救濟亂心，
shùn suǒ dìng yì jiù jì luàn xīn，
by according with concentration he rescues those of disordered mind;
順所智慧救濟惡智，
shùn suǒ zhì huì jiù jì è zhì，
by according with wisdom he rescues those of evil understanding;
順所度知立於眾人安隱無為；是為五。
shùn suǒ dù zhī lì yú zhòng rén ān yǐn wú wéi； shì wéi wǔ。
and by according with the knowledge of deliverance he establishes all in peace, ease and the unconditioned. These are the five.
復有五棄。何等五？
fù yǒu wǔ qì。 hé děng wǔ？
There are five relinquishments. Which five?
棄於重擔而除五陰，
qì yú zhòng dàn ér chú wǔ yīn，
He lays down the heavy burden and removes the five aggregates;
斷恩愛結及諸所習，
duàn ēn ài jié jí zhū suǒ xí，
he severs the bonds of affection and craving and all his habits;
常以寂定捨於寂滅，
cháng yǐ jì dìng shě yú jì miè，
with constant stillness and concentration he relinquishes even quiescence;
入于道德奉行八道，
rù yú dào dé fèng xíng bā dào，
he enters the virtue of the Way and practices the eightfold path;
入於聖諦立一切人於正諦法；是為五。
rù yú shèng dì lì yī qiè rén yú zhèng dì fǎ； shì wéi wǔ。
and entering the holy truths he establishes all people in the dharma of right truth. These are the five.
復有四法常處樹下。何等四？
fù yǒu sì fǎ cháng chù shù xià。 hé děng sì？
There are four more by which he always dwells beneath a tree. Which four?
不惜身命，奉行一切諸德善法，
bù xī shēn mìng， fèng xíng yī qiè zhū dé shàn fǎ，
He does not spare his own life; he practices every virtuous and wholesome dharma;
發神通慧，
fā shén tōng huì，
he brings forth the wisdom of the spiritual penetrations;
行寂然事天人欣悅；是為四。
xíng jì rán shì tiān rén xīn yuè； shì wéi sì。
and by his practice of stillness devas and men are gladdened. These are the four.
復有四事樂處閑居。何等四？
fù yǒu sì shì lè chù xián jū。 hé děng sì？
There are four more by which he delights in secluded dwelling. Which four?
發大哀以處閑居，諸佛所歎；
fā dà āi yǐ chù xián jū， zhū fó suǒ tàn；
He dwells in seclusion having brought forth great compassion, which the buddhas praise;
欲救眾生，無偏邪行；
yù jiù zhòng shēng， wú piān xié xíng；
wishing to rescue beings, he has no partial or crooked conduct;
坐成佛時，莊嚴自由，不為塵勞；
zuò chéng fó shí， zhuāng yán zì yóu， bù wéi chén láo；
when he sits and becomes a buddha his adornment is free and unbound by defilement;
學餘菩薩究竟之行，積閑居德，
xué yú pú sà jiū jìng zhī xíng， jī xián jū dé，
he studies the ultimate practice of other bodhisattvas and amasses the virtue of seclusion,
入於郡國、縣邑、聚落為眾說法；是為四。
rù yú jùn guó、 xiàn yì、 jù luò wèi zhòng shuō fǎ； shì wéi sì。
then enters commanderies, districts and villages to preach the Dharma to the multitude. These are the four.
復有三事而習燕坐，
fù yǒu sān shì ér xí yān zuò，
There are three more by which he practices sitting in quietude,
最尊無上功德微妙為賢聖行。何等三？
zuì zūn wú shàng gōng dé wēi miào wéi xián shèng xíng。 hé děng sān？
the most honored and unsurpassed subtle merit, the conduct of the holy sages. Which three?
不習憎愛亦無所慕；
bù xí zēng ài yì wú suǒ mù；
He cultivates neither aversion nor attachment, and longs for nothing;
自在離欲，心無縛著；
zì zài lí yù， xīn wú fù zhuó；
free and apart from desire, his mind is unbound and unattached;
行步自由，等心一切，疾得定意；是為三。
xíng bù zì yóu， děng xīn yī qiè， jí dé dìng yì； shì wéi sān。
his movements are unhindered, his mind impartial toward all, and he swiftly attains concentration. These are the three.
復有三事離諸諛諂：
fù yǒu sān shì lí zhū yú chǎn：
There are three more by which he leaves behind all flattery:
其心質直而無恚恨，
qí xīn zhì zhí ér wú huì hèn，
his mind is upright and without hatred,
已住於行，眾結便斷；是為三。
yǐ zhù yú xíng， zhòng jié biàn duàn； shì wéi sān。
and abiding in practice, the knots are severed. These are the three.
復有八事具出家德。何等八？
fù yǒu bā shì jù chū jiā dé。 hé děng bā？
There are eight more by which he perfects the virtue of one gone forth. Which eight?
賢聖知足在於獨處，得知限節，
xián shèng zhī zú zài yú dú chù， dé zhī xiàn jié，
Like the sages he is content and dwells alone; he knows his limits and measure;
逮諸博聞，棄恨忍辱，
dǎi zhū bó wén， qì hèn rěn rǔ，
he attains wide learning; he casts off resentment and is patient;
不捨道心，行四意止，
bù shě dào xīn， xíng sì yì zhǐ，
he does not abandon the mind of the Way; he practices the four foundations of mindfulness;
專精定意而應智慧，
zhuān jīng dìng yì ér yìng zhì huì，
he is single and intent in concentration, answering to wisdom;
一切所興以行為要；是為八。
yī qiè suǒ xìng yǐ xíng wéi yào； shì wéi bā。
and in all he undertakes, practice is his essential. These are the eight.
復有十事入深要法。何等十？
fù yǒu shí shì rù shēn yào fǎ。 hé děng shí？
There are ten more by which he enters the profound and essential Dharma. Which ten?
見身自然諸法自然，
jiàn shēn zì rán zhū fǎ zì rán，
Seeing the body to be as it is of itself, he sees all dharmas so;
身入於淨一切法淨，
shēn rù yú jìng yī qiè fǎ jìng，
the body entering purity, all dharmas are pure;
見已無吾諸法無我，
jiàn yǐ wú wú zhū fǎ wú wǒ，
seeing that there is no 'I', all dharmas are without self;
自觀身空不疑諸法空，
zì guān shēn kōng bù yí zhū fǎ kōng，
contemplating the body as empty, he does not doubt that all dharmas are empty;
己身無聲諸法如響，
jǐ shēn wú shēng zhū fǎ rú xiǎng，
his own body being soundless, all dharmas are like an echo;
察身寂寞諸法靜默，
chá shēn jì mò zhū fǎ jìng mò，
examining the body as still, all dharmas are silent;
我者審諦觀諸法諦，
wǒ zhě shěn dì guān zhū fǎ dì，
discerning the truth of self, he contemplates the truth of all dharmas;
我志深妙見諸法奧，
wǒ zhì shēn miào jiàn zhū fǎ ào，
his own aim being deep and subtle, he sees the depth of all dharmas;
己身無聞諸法如聾，
jǐ shēn wú wén zhū fǎ rú lóng，
his own body hearing nothing, all dharmas are as if deaf;
吾無所受見一切法無可取者；是為十。
wú wú suǒ shòu jiàn yī qiè fǎ wú kě qǔ zhě； shì wéi shí。
and receiving nothing himself, he sees that in no dharma is there anything to grasp. These are the ten.
復有十事：
fù yǒu shí shì：
There are ten more:
諸法如幻，興誑詐相；
zhū fǎ rú huàn， xìng kuáng zhà xiāng；
dharmas are like a conjuring trick, raising deceptive marks;
諸法如夢，所見無實；
zhū fǎ rú mèng， suǒ jiàn wú shí；
dharmas are like a dream, what is seen is not real;
諸法如野馬，起顛倒想，見不諦故；
zhū fǎ rú yě mǎ， qǐ diān dào xiǎng， jiàn bù dì gù；
dharmas are like a shimmering mirage, rousing inverted notions because what is seen is untrue;
諸法如影，所作因緣無以為樂；
zhū fǎ rú yǐng， suǒ zuò yīn yuán wú yǐ wéi lè；
dharmas are like a shadow, made of causes and conditions and offering no delight;
諸法如水月，捉不可得，其相離行；
zhū fǎ rú shuǐ yuè， zhuō bù kě dé， qí xiāng lí xíng；
dharmas are like the moon in water, ungraspable, their marks apart from action;
諸法如響，本末悉空；
zhū fǎ rú xiǎng， běn mò xī kōng；
dharmas are like an echo, empty from beginning to end;
諸法如電，晃現隨滅；
zhū fǎ rú diàn， huǎng xiàn suí miè；
dharmas are like lightning, flashing forth and vanishing at once;
諸法如畫，離婬怒癡；
zhū fǎ rú huà， lí yín nù chī；
dharmas are like a painting, apart from lust, anger and delusion;
諸法本淨，不為客垢之所沾污；
zhū fǎ běn jìng， bù wéi kè gòu zhī suǒ zhān wū；
dharmas are originally pure, unstained by adventitious defilement;
諸法如虛空，適起尋滅，無有處所；是為十。
zhū fǎ rú xū kōng， shì qǐ xún miè， wú yǒu chù suǒ； shì wéi shí。
dharmas are like empty space, arising and immediately ceasing, with no place at all. These are the ten.
復有二事不墮滅見。何等為二？
fù yǒu èr shì bù duò miè jiàn。 hé děng wéi èr？
There are two more by which he does not fall into the view of annihilation. Which two?
隨時之慧入於罪福，
suí shí zhī huì rù yú zuì fú，
The wisdom that accords with the times enters into sin and merit,
了別諸佛聖智之明；是為二。
liǎo bié zhū fó shèng zhì zhī míng； shì wéi èr。
and he discerns the brightness of the holy wisdom of the buddhas. These are the two.
復有二事不墮常見。何等為二？
fù yǒu èr shì bù duò cháng jiàn。 hé děng wéi èr？
There are two more by which he does not fall into the view of permanence. Which two?
一切說無常慧，適起便滅意無永存；是為二。
yī qiè shuō wú cháng huì， shì qǐ biàn miè yì wú yǒng cún； shì wéi èr。
The wisdom that declares all things impermanent: what arises at once ceases, and no thought endures. These are the two.
復有四事超因緣法。何等為四？
fù yǒu sì shì chāo yīn yuán fǎ。 hé děng wéi sì？
There are four more by which he transcends the dharma of causes and conditions. Which four?
無黠之習入生死習，
wú xiá zhī xí rù shēng sǐ xí，
The habit of ignorance enters the habit of birth and death;
無黠已滅生死便除，
wú xiá yǐ miè shēng sǐ biàn chú，
ignorance being extinguished, birth and death are removed;
不墮滅見，不住常觀；是為四。
bù duò miè jiàn， bú zhù cháng guān； shì wéi sì。
he does not fall into the view of annihilation, nor abide in the view of permanence. These are the four.
復有四事離諸邪見。何等四？
fù yǒu sì shì lí zhū xié jiàn。 hé děng sì？
There are four more by which he leaves behind all wrong views. Which four?
曉空慧不見彼我，解無相不見壽命，
xiǎo kōng huì bù jiàn bǐ wǒ， jiě wú xiàng bù jiàn shòu mìng，
Understanding the wisdom of emptiness he sees no other and no self; understanding signlessness he sees no lifespan;
了無願不見三處，
le wú yuàn bù jiàn sān chù，
understanding wishlessness he sees no three abodes;
分別緣起離常無常；是為四。
fēn bié yuán qǐ lí cháng wú cháng； shì wéi sì。
and discerning dependent arising he departs from both permanence and impermanence. These are the four.
復有六事以諸神通而自娛樂。何等六？
fù yǒu liù shì yǐ zhū shén tōng ér zì yú lè。 hé děng liù？
There are six more by which he takes joy in the spiritual penetrations. Which six?
不以惡眼視於眾生，得天眼淨；
bù yǐ è yǎn shì yú zhòng shēng， dé tiān yǎn jìng；
Not looking upon beings with a malicious eye, he gains the purity of the deva eye;
聞惡聲音則能忍辱，逮天耳淨；
wén è shēng yīn zé néng rěn rǔ， dǎi tiān ěr jìng；
hearing evil sounds he is able to be patient, and attains the purity of the deva ear;
其心不亂，覩他心淨；
qí xīn bù luàn， dǔ tā xīn jìng；
his mind undisturbed, he beholds the purity of others' minds;
殖眾德本則識過事；
zhí zhòng dé běn zé shí guò shì；
planting manifold roots of virtue, he knows past events;
如口所語身行亦爾，獲神足淨；
rú kǒu suǒ yǔ shēn xíng yì ěr， huò shén zú jìng；
his bodily conduct matching his speech, he obtains the purity of the bases of spiritual power;
修行諸敬，不欺法師，盡諸漏淨；是為六。
xiū xíng zhū jìng， bù qī fǎ shī， jǐn zhū lòu jìng； shì wéi liù。
practicing every reverence and never deceiving his Dharma master, he exhausts the outflows in purity. These are the six.
復有六事得六通。何等六？
fù yǒu liù shì dé liù tōng。 hé děng liù？
There are six more by which he gains the six penetrations. Which six?
以然燈故，得天眼淨；
yǐ rán dēng gù， dé tiān yǎn jìng；
By lighting lamps he gains the purity of the deva eye;
施諸音樂，得天耳淨；
shī zhū yīn yuè， dé tiān ěr jìng；
by offering music he gains the purity of the deva ear;
施無悕望，了眾生心；
shī wú xī wàng， le zhòng shēng xīn；
by giving without expectation he understands the minds of beings;
殖眾德本，知過去事；
zhí zhòng dé běn， zhī guò qù shì；
by planting roots of virtue he knows past events;
却諸陰蓋，決眾狐疑，逮神足淨；
què zhū yīn gài， jué zhòng hú yí， dǎi shén zú jìng；
by dispelling the hindrances and resolving doubts he attains the purity of spiritual power;
以法布施，盡諸漏淨；是為六。
yǐ fǎ bù shī， jǐn zhū lòu jìng； shì wéi liù。
and by the gift of Dharma he exhausts the outflows in purity. These are the six.
復有六事而得通慧。何等六？
fù yǒu liù shì ér dé tōng huì。 hé děng liù？
There are six more by which he gains penetrating wisdom. Which six?
適見如來，得天眼淨；
shì jiàn rú lái， dé tiān yǎn jìng；
On beholding the Tathagata he gains the purity of the deva eye;
合會說法，得天耳淨；
hé huì shuō fǎ， dé tiān ěr jìng；
by gathering others to preach the Dharma he gains the purity of the deva ear;
制伏其心，見眾生意；
zhì fú qí xīn， jiàn zhòng shēng yì；
by mastering his own mind he sees the minds of beings;
常習六念，得識宿命；
cháng xí liù niàn， dé shí sù mìng；
by constantly practicing the six recollections he comes to know former lives;
棄諸貪濁，逮成神足，輕舉能飛；
qì zhū tān zhuó， dǎi chéng shén zú， qīng jǔ néng fēi；
by casting off greed and turbidity he perfects the bases of spiritual power and rises lightly into flight;
遵修諸法，得盡諸漏；是為六。」
zūn xiū zhū fǎ， dé jǐn zhū lòu； shì wéi liù。」
and by cultivating all dharmas he exhausts the outflows. These are the six.”
佛復告龍王：「以有神通而自娛樂：
fó fù gào lóng wáng： 「yǐ yǒu shén tōng ér zì yú lè：
The Buddha further told the Dragon King: “As to taking joy in the spiritual penetrations:
聲聞、緣覺及外道、神仙、
shēng wén、 yuán jué jí wài dào、 shén xiān、
of all the deva eyes possessed by sravakas, pratyekabuddhas, followers of other paths, divine immortals,
天、龍、鬼、神、無善神、鳳凰神王、
tiān、 lóng、 guǐ、 shén、 wú shàn shén、 fèng huáng shén wáng、
devas, dragons, ghosts and spirits, asuras, garuda kings,
山神王、甜柔神、人與非人，所有天眼，
shān shén wáng、 tián róu shén、 rén yǔ fēi rén， suǒ yǒu tiān yǎn，
mountain-spirit kings, kinnaras, humans and non-humans,
計菩薩眼，最上無極清淨明徹，
jì pú sà yǎn， zuì shàng wú jí qīng jìng míng chè，
the bodhisattva's eye is reckoned supreme, boundless, pure and clear;
除如來眼，菩薩之眼無所不見，
chú rú lái yǎn， pú sà zhī yǎn wú suǒ bù jiàn，
excepting the Tathagata's eye, there is nothing the bodhisattva's eye does not see —
天人光色諸法之本，無所罣礙。
tiān rén guāng sè zhū fǎ zhī běn， wú suǒ guà ài。
the radiance and forms of devas and men and the root of all dharmas — without obstruction.
又聲聞、緣覺及天、龍、神、人與非人，
yòu shēng wén、 yuán jué jí tiān、 lóng、 shén、 rén yǔ fēi rén，
Again, of sravakas, pratyekabuddhas, devas, dragons, spirits, humans and non-humans,
計菩薩耳，最上無極清淨明徹，
jì pú sà ěr， zuì shàng wú jí qīng jìng míng chè，
the bodhisattva's ear is reckoned supreme, boundless, pure and clear;
除如來耳，菩薩之耳無所不聞，
chú rú lái ěr， pú sà zhī ěr wú suǒ bù wén，
excepting the Tathagata's ear, there is nothing the bodhisattva's ear does not hear —
天、人音聲諸法之講，無所罣礙。
tiān、 rén yīn shēng zhū fǎ zhī jiǎng， wú suǒ guà ài。
the voices of devas and men and the expounding of all dharmas — without obstruction.
聞諸音聲知三達事，
wén zhū yīn shēng zhī sān dá shì，
Hearing these sounds he knows the matters of the three insights,
皆了一切眾生之心，
jiē le yī qiè zhòng shēng zhī xīn，
and understands the minds of all living beings:
所行造念因緣報應往來之想，
suǒ xíng zào niàn yīn yuán bào yìng wǎng lái zhī xiǎng，
their conduct, their conceiving, the notions of cause, condition, recompense, coming and going;
淨不淨，著不著，若干種心：
jìng bù jìng， zhuó bù zhuó， ruò gān zhǒng xīn：
pure and impure, attached and unattached, minds of many kinds:
若逆心，若順心、縛心、解心、
ruò nì xīn， ruò shùn xīn、 fù xīn、 jiě xīn、
a contrary mind or a compliant mind, a bound mind, a freed mind,
依心、不依心、惑心、定心、
yī xīn、 bù yī xīn、 huò xīn、 dìng xīn、
a dependent mind, an independent mind, a deluded mind, a settled mind,
有處心、無處心，若興衰心，已曉了之，
yǒu chù xīn、 wú chù xīn， ruò xīng shuāi xīn， yǐ xiǎo liǎo zhī，
a mind with a place, a mind without a place, a mind of rise or decline — all these he understands.
悉見人根如應說法，
xī jiàn rén gēn rú yìng shuō fǎ，
He sees the faculties of all and preaches the Dharma as befits them;
以識宿命知彼我本，
yǐ shí sù mìng zhī bǐ wǒ běn，
by knowledge of former lives he knows the origins of self and other,
終始所起無所不達。
zhōng shǐ suǒ qǐ wú suǒ bù dá。
and there is nothing in the whole course of birth and death he does not penetrate.
至誠不虛，神足無猗，無所不現，
zhì chéng bù xū， shén zú wú yī， wú suǒ bù xiàn，
Utterly sincere and never false, his spiritual power rests on nothing, and there is nothing he does not manifest.
是為菩薩五神通。
shì wéi pú sà wǔ shén tōng。
These are the bodhisattva's five spiritual penetrations.
又心自在所作具足，是為娛樂。
yòu xīn zì zài suǒ zuò jù zú， shì wéi yú lè。
Moreover his mind is free and his works complete — this is his joy.
示現佛身而般泥洹，不永滅度。
shì xiàn fó shēn ér bān ní huán， bù yǒng miè dù。
He manifests a buddha's body and enters parinirvana, yet is not extinguished forever.
何謂菩薩漏盡神通？
hé wèi pú sà lòu jǐn shén tōng？
What is the bodhisattva's penetration of the exhaustion of the outflows?
菩薩超越聲聞、緣覺，
pú sà chāo yuè shēng wén、 yuán jué，
The bodhisattva surpasses the sravaka and the pratyekabuddha;
所得解脫猗於佛慧，
suǒ de jiě tuō yī yú fó huì，
the liberation he gains rests upon the Buddha's wisdom;
曉了眾生一切本淨，
xiǎo liǎo zhòng shēng yī qiè běn jìng，
he understands that all beings are originally pure;
不盡諸漏而不取證，
bù jǐn zhū lòu ér bù qǔ zhèng，
he does not exhaust the outflows nor seize upon realization,
為一切人讚諸漏盡。是為六神通。
wèi yī qiè rén zàn zhū lòu jǐn。 shì wéi liù shén tōng。
yet for the sake of all he praises the exhaustion of the outflows. These are the six penetrations.
復有四事見無蓋慧。何等四？
fù yǒu sì shì jiàn wú gài huì。 hé děng sì？
There are four more by which he sees unveiled wisdom. Which four?
遵修慧德，致此五通；
zūn xiū huì dé， zhì cǐ wǔ tōng；
By cultivating the virtue of wisdom he attains these five penetrations;
行大慈大哀，知四解行；
xíng dà cí dà āi， zhī sì jiě xíng；
by practicing great loving-kindness and great compassion he knows the four modes of understanding;
奉善權慧，逮四無礙；
fèng shàn quán huì， dǎi sì wú ài；
by upholding the wisdom of skillful means he attains the four unobstructed knowledges;
定意正受空、無相、無願，
dìng yì zhèng shòu kōng、 wú xiàng、 wú yuàn，
and by concentration and right absorption in emptiness, signlessness and wishlessness,
致三十七道品之法；是為四。」
zhì sān shí qī dào pǐn zhī fǎ； shì wéi sì。」
he attains the thirty-seven limbs of the Way. These are the four.”
佛復告龍王：「何謂所見無蓋？
fó fù gào lóng wáng： 「hé wèi suǒ jiàn wú gài？
The Buddha further told the Dragon King: “What is unveiled vision?
諸有塵勞除一切垢；
zhū yǒu chén láo chú yī qiè gòu；
Wherever there is defilement and toil, he removes every stain;
所有罣礙現生死本，導御泥洹；
suǒ yǒu guà ài xiàn shēng sǐ běn， dǎo yù ní huán；
wherever there is obstruction, he reveals the root of birth and death and guides toward nirvana;
現聲聞、緣覺乘，化至道場；
xiàn shēng wén、 yuán jué chéng， huà zhì dào chǎng；
he manifests the sravaka and pratyekabuddha vehicles and leads beings to the bodhimanda (place of practice);
隨勸習俗，示人行寂。是謂無蓋。
suí quàn xí sú， shì rén xíng jì。 shì wèi wú gài。
he exhorts in accordance with custom and shows people the practice of stillness. This is called unveiled.
復有無蓋：現一切數至無所有，
fù yǒu wú gài： xiàn yī qiè shù zhì wú suǒ yǒu，
There is a further unveiling: he manifests all that is countable and arrives at nothing at all;
現滅諸數逮無所著，
xiàn miè zhū shù dǎi wú suǒ zhuó，
he manifests the ceasing of all number and attains non-attachment;
雖在有數諸行之事，於無數法無所罣礙，
suī zài yǒu shù zhū xíng zhī shì， yú wú shù fǎ wú suǒ guà ài，
though amid the countable affairs of conditioned action, he is unobstructed in the dharma of the countless;
彼無陰蓋得至無為，
bǐ wú yīn gài dé zhì wú wéi，
free of the aggregate-hindrances he reaches the unconditioned,
於有為法亦無罣礙，是謂菩薩現無礙慧。
yú yǒu wéi fǎ yì wú guà ài， shì wèi pú sà xiàn wú ài huì。
and in conditioned dharmas too he is unobstructed. This is the bodhisattva manifesting unobstructed wisdom.
復有四事曉了眾生心之所行：
fù yǒu sì shì xiǎo liǎo zhòng shēng xīn zhī suǒ xíng：
There are four more by which he understands the workings of beings' minds:
隨習俗慧，正受明了識意所為，
suí xí sú huì， zhèng shòu míng liǎo shí yì suǒ wéi，
wisdom that accords with custom; right absorption that clearly knows what the mind intends;
善權方便，於諸法自在；是為四。
shàn quán fāng biàn， yú zhū fǎ zì zài； shì wéi sì。
skillful means; and sovereign freedom in all dharmas. These are the four.
復有五事行無厭足。何等為五？
fù yǒu wǔ shì xíng wú yàn zú。 hé děng wéi wǔ？
There are five more by which he practices without ever being satisfied. Which five?
已獲大安令眾生安，
yǐ huò dà ān lìng zhòng shēng ān，
Having gained great peace, he brings beings to peace;
大哀堅強，視一切人如己骨髓，
dà āi jiān qiáng， shì yī qiè rén rú jǐ gǔ suǐ，
his great compassion is firm, and he regards every person as his own flesh and bone;
隨人所行而示現行，
suí rén suǒ xíng ér shì xiàn xíng，
he manifests conduct according to the conduct of others;
立於極上奇特之德；是為五。
lì yú jí shàng qí tè zhī dé； shì wéi wǔ。
and he is established in the highest and most extraordinary virtue. These are the five.
復有六事分別所受教化之言。何等六？
fù yǒu liù shì fēn bié suǒ shòu jiào huà zhī yán。 hé děng liù？
There are six more by which he discerns the words of teaching he has received. Which six?
逮得總持，心立寂然，
dǎi dé zǒng chí， xīn lì jì rán，
He attains dharani; his mind is established in stillness;
入審諦淨，心入諸慧，
rù shěn dì jìng， xīn rù zhū huì，
he enters the purity of careful discernment; his mind enters every wisdom;
辯才無著無止，
biàn cái wú zhuó wú zhǐ，
his eloquence is unattached and unhindered;
方便之慧次第解脫；是為六。
fāng biàn zhī huì cì dì jiě tuō； shì wéi liù。
and the wisdom of skillful means brings liberation in due order. These are the six.
復有八事降伏魔怨。何等八？
fù yǒu bā shì xiáng fú mó yuàn。 hé děng bā？
There are eight more by which he subdues Mara and his foes. Which eight?
曉了五陰，譬若如幻，
xiǎo liǎo wǔ yīn， pì ruò rú huàn，
He understands the five aggregates to be like a conjuring trick,
離貪見塵而行空事；
lí tān jiàn chén ér xíng kōng shì；
and free of greed he sees defilement and practices emptiness;
知一切法皆無所生，
zhī yī qiè fǎ jiē wú suǒ shēng，
he knows that all dharmas are unarisen,
隨其所生如開導之；
suí qí suǒ shēng rú kāi dǎo zhī；
and guides beings according to how they arise;
不捨道意，堅強精進；
bù shě dào yì， jiān qiáng jīng jìn；
he does not abandon the aspiration to enlightenment and is firm in vigor;
不捨佛道，不畏三界；
bù shě fó dào， bù wèi sān jiè；
he does not abandon the buddha path and does not fear the three realms;
離於所有，觀於人物；
lí yú suǒ yǒu， guān yú rén wù；
apart from possessions, he contemplates beings;
求審諦慧，觀無常相；
qiú shěn dì huì， guān wú cháng xiāng；
he seeks the wisdom of careful discernment and contemplates the mark of impermanence;
積德不厭；
jī dé bù yàn；
he amasses virtue without weariness;
合集智慧，不樂聲聞、緣覺之智；是為八。
hé jí zhì huì， bù lè shēng wén、 yuán jué zhī zhì； shì wéi bā。
and gathering wisdom, he takes no delight in the knowledge of sravakas or pratyekabuddhas. These are the eight.
復有十事離諸恐懼，行菩薩事。何等十？
fù yǒu shí shì lí zhū kǒng jù， xíng pú sà shì。 hé děng shí？
There are ten more by which he leaves all fear behind and does a bodhisattva's work. Which ten?
行於布施，以莊嚴想；
xíng yú bù shī， yǐ zhuāng yán xiǎng；
He practices giving with thoughts of adornment;
立于禁戒，斷諸惡趣；
lì yú jìn jiè， duàn zhū è qù；
established in the precepts, he cuts off the evil destinies;
遊於忍辱，諸根不亂；
yóu yú rěn rǔ， zhū gēn bù luàn；
dwelling in patience, his faculties are undisturbed;
堅強精進，種善不惓；
jiān qiáng jīng jìn， zhòng shàn bù quán；
firm in vigor, he plants good without weariness;
修行禪定，其心不荒；
xiū xíng chán dìng， qí xīn bù huāng；
cultivating dhyana, his mind does not run wild;
成于智慧而離塵勞；
chéng yú zhì huì ér lí chén láo；
perfecting wisdom, he departs from defilement and toil;
善權方便曉了無邊聖智之願，得分別事；
shàn quán fāng biàn xiǎo liǎo wú biān shèng zhì zhī yuàn， dé fēn bié shì；
by skillful means he understands the boundless vow of holy wisdom and gains discernment of all matters;
解知法義，辯才隨順；
jiě zhī fǎ yì， biàn cái suí shùn；
he understands the meaning of the Dharma, and his eloquence accords with it;
逮得總持，決除眾生諸所狐疑；
dǎi dé zǒng chí， jué chú zhòng shēng zhū suǒ hú yí；
he attains dharani and resolves the doubts of living beings;
得住佛住，護一切法；是為十。
dé zhù fó zhù， hù yī qiè fǎ； shì wéi shí。
and he comes to abide in the abiding of a buddha and guards all dharmas. These are the ten.
復有八事御退轉者。何等八？
fù yǒu bā shì yù tuì zhuǎn zhě。 hé děng bā？
There are eight more by which he guides those who have fallen back. Which eight?
言行相應；
yán xíng xiāng yìng；
His words and deeds correspond;
自省己過，不說彼闕；
zì xǐng jǐ guò， bù shuō bǐ quē；
he examines his own faults and does not speak of others' shortcomings;
寧失身命不造輕重；
níng shī shēn mìng bù zào qīng zhòng；
he would sooner lose his life than commit an offense, light or grave;
獲利不喜，無利不慼；
huò lì bù xǐ， wú lì bù qī；
gaining, he is not elated; lacking, he is not downcast;
心不懷害，誘導一切興眾祐意；
xīn bù huái hài， yòu dǎo yī qiè xìng zhòng yòu yì；
he harbors no thought of harm, and leads all to rouse the aspiration to blessedness;
等敷禁戒，不捨師法；
děng fū jìn jiè， bù shě shī fǎ；
he sets forth the precepts impartially and does not abandon his teacher's dharma;
安悅眾人，不自求安；
ān yuè zhòng rén， bù zì qiú ān；
he brings ease and gladness to others and does not seek ease for himself;
一切所愛施而不悔；是為八。
yī qiè suǒ ài shī ér bù huǐ； shì wéi bā。
and he gives away all he loves without regret. These are the eight.
復有五事得不退轉於無上正真道。何等五？
fù yǒu wǔ shì dé bù tuì zhuǎn yú wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào。 hé děng wǔ？
There are five more by which he attains non-regression from unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment. Which five?
善權方便成諸度無疑；
shàn quán fāng biàn chéng zhū dù wú yí；
By skillful means he perfects the perfections without doubt;
入深妙法，了審諦義；
rù shēn miào fǎ， le shěn dì yì；
he enters the profound and subtle Dharma and understands the meaning of careful discernment;
神通無礙，見眾生根分別諸慧；
shén tōng wú ài， jiàn zhòng shēng gēn fēn bié zhū huì；
his spiritual penetrations are unobstructed, and seeing beings' faculties he discerns every wisdom;
行無所著，行不可盡；
xíng wú suǒ zhuó， xíng bù kě jǐn；
his practice is unattached and inexhaustible;
遊於緣起，不盡一切諸漏之證；是為五。
yóu yú yuán qǐ， bù jǐn yī qiè zhū lòu zhī zhèng； shì wéi wǔ。
and dwelling in dependent arising, he does not exhaust the realization of the outflows. These are the five.
復有三事逮不起忍。何等三？
fù yǒu sān shì dǎi bù qǐ rěn。 hé děng sān？
There are three more by which he attains the forbearance of non-arising. Which three?
察人清淨而無吾我，
chá rén qīng jìng ér wú wú wǒ，
He examines beings as pure and without self;
法淨寂寞，慧淨無著；是為三。
fǎ jìng jì mò， huì jìng wú zhuó； shì wéi sān。
the Dharma is pure and still; wisdom is pure and unattached. These are the three.
復有三事過諸清淨：
fù yǒu sān shì guò zhū qīng jìng：
There are three more by which he passes beyond all purities:
過去清淨諸法常盡，
guò qù qīng jìng zhū fǎ cháng jǐn，
past purity — all dharmas are ever ended;
當來清淨法無所起，
dāng lái qīng jìng fǎ wú suǒ qǐ，
future purity — dharmas do not arise;
今現清淨法無所住；是為三。
jīn xiàn qīng jìng fǎ wú suǒ zhù； shì wéi sān。
present purity — dharmas do not abide. These are the three.
復有三事：
fù yǒu sān shì：
There are three more:
身行清淨功德熾盛，
shēn xíng qīng jìng gōng dé chì shèng，
bodily conduct pure, his merit blazes;
口言清淨智慧巍巍，
kǒu yán qīng jìng zhì huì wēi wēi，
speech pure, his wisdom is lofty;
意念清淨定而不轉；是為三。
yì niàn qīng jìng dìng ér bù zhuǎn； shì wéi sān。
thought pure, he is settled and does not turn back. These are the three.
復有四事為佛世尊所見授決。何等四？
fù yǒu sì shì wèi fó shì zūn suǒ jiàn shòu jué。 hé děng sì？
There are four more by which he receives the prediction of the Buddha, the World-Honored One. Which four?
情性和順，奉遵于法；
qíng xìng hé shùn， fèng zūn yú fǎ；
His disposition is harmonious and compliant, and he honors and upholds the Dharma;
具足諸行，觀清白行；
jù zú zhū xíng， guān qīng bái xíng；
he perfects all practices and contemplates pure conduct;
逮得慧力，解一切心；
dǎi dé huì lì， jiě yī qiè xīn；
he attains the power of wisdom and understands every mind;
了諸法本淨，不起不滅，所由不亂。」
le zhū fǎ běn jìng， bù qǐ bù miè， suǒ yóu bù luàn。」
and he understands that all dharmas are originally pure, neither arising nor ceasing, their course undisturbed.”
佛告龍王：「是為四法菩薩所行，
fó gào lóng wáng： 「shì wéi sì fǎ pú sà suǒ xíng，
The Buddha told the Dragon King: “These are the four dharmas of a bodhisattva's practice
為佛、世尊所見授決。」
wèi fó、 shì zūn suǒ jiàn shòu jué。」
by which he receives the prediction of the Buddha, the World-Honored One.”
分別品第二
fēn bié pǐn dì èr
Chapter Two: Discernment
佛說是已，
fó shuō shì yǐ，
When the Buddha had spoken thus,
十二億百千諸天、龍、神、香音神、人與非人，
shí èr yì bǎi qiān zhū tiān、 lóng、 shén、 xiāng yīn shén、 rén yǔ fēi rén，
one billion two hundred million devas, dragons, spirits, gandharvas, humans and non-humans
皆發無上正真道意；
jiē fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì；
all aroused the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment;
七萬二千菩薩得不起法忍；
qī wàn èr qiān pú sà dé bù qǐ fǎ rěn；
seventy-two thousand bodhisattvas attained the forbearance of the non-arising of dharmas;
百四十萬眾得法眼淨，遠塵離垢；
bǎi sì shí wàn zhòng dé fǎ yǎn jìng， yuǎn chén lí gòu；
one million four hundred thousand of the assembly gained the pure Dharma eye, far from dust and free of defilement;
八千比丘漏盡意解；
bā qiān bǐ qiū lòu jǐn yì jiě；
eight thousand bhiksus exhausted the outflows and their minds were released;
五千天子得離愛欲。
wǔ qiān tiān zǐ dé lí ài yù。
and five thousand devaputras were freed from craving and desire.
三千大千世界六返震動，
sān qiān dà qiān shì jiè liù fǎn zhèn dòng，
The three-thousandfold great-thousand world quaked in six ways,
其大光明普照世界，
qí dà guāng míng pǔ zhào shì jiè，
a great radiance illumined the world everywhere,
空中自然而雨天華。
kōng zhōng zì rán ér yǔ tiān huā。
and celestial flowers rained down of themselves from the sky.
諸天在上鼓百千伎樂，共歎頌曰：
zhū tiān zài shàng gǔ bǎi qiān jì yuè， gòng tàn sòng yuē：
The devas above struck up a hundred thousand kinds of music and together praised him, saying:
「今者如來所說經法，為再轉法輪；
「jīn zhě rú lái suǒ shuō jīng fǎ， wéi zài zhuǎn fǎ lún；
“The Dharma of the sutra the Tathagata has now preached is a second turning of the Dharma wheel.
在波羅奈所轉法輪，
zài bō luó nài suǒ zhuǎn fǎ lún，
The wheel he turned at Varanasi
今說斯經復加增倍。
jīn shuō sī jīng fù jiā zēng bèi。
is doubled and surpassed by the preaching of this sutra now.
所以者何？
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？
Why is this so?
於此經者，為無央數不可計人開導利義。
yú cǐ jīng zhě， wéi wú yāng shù bù kě jì rén kāi dǎo lì yì。
Through this sutra, countless and incalculable beings are guided to what benefits them.
若人聞此，德本不忘，
ruò rén wén cǐ， dé běn bù wàng，
If a person hears it, his roots of virtue will not be lost —
何況受持能奉行者！
hé kuàng shòu chí néng fèng xíng zhě！
how much more one who upholds it and is able to practice it!
善得人身，快見如來，諦聞此法。
shàn dé rén shēn， kuài jiàn rú lái， dì wén cǐ fǎ。
Well has he gained a human body, gladly beheld the Tathagata, and truly heard this Dharma.
聞此法已，便發無上正真道者，
wén cǐ fǎ yǐ， biàn fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào zhě，
One who, having heard this Dharma, arouses the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment
閉塞惡趣，開天人迹。
bì sāi è qù， kāi tiān rén jì。
shuts off the evil destinies and opens the path of devas and men.
當觀此比，如獲滅度！」
dāng guān cǐ bǐ， rú huò miè dù！」
Consider it thus: it is as though he had already attained final quiescence!”
於是世尊讚諸天子曰：
yú shì shì zūn zàn zhū tiān zǐ yuē：
Thereupon the World-Honored One praised the devaputras, saying:
「善哉，善哉！快說此言！
「shàn zāi， shàn zāi！ kuài shuō cǐ yán！
“Excellent, excellent! Well have you spoken these words!
聞斯經法歡喜信者，佛所建立，
wén sī jīng fǎ huān xǐ xìn zhě， fó suǒ jiàn lì，
Those who hear this Dharma with joy and faith are established by the Buddha
開化大乘，是等之類逮如來慧，
kāi huà dà chéng， shì děng zhī lèi dǎi rú lái huì，
and led into the Great Vehicle; such as these attain the Tathagata's wisdom,
不退轉印而以印之，
bù tuì zhuǎn yìn ér yǐ yìn zhī，
and are sealed with the seal of non-regression.
終不餘趣，順至佛道，超諸苦難。」
zhōng bù yú qù， shùn zhì fó dào， chāo zhū kǔ nán。」
They will never go to any other destiny, but proceed in accord to the buddha path and rise above all suffering and hardship.”
於是龍王聞說斯經，欣然喜踊，善心生焉。
yú shì lóng wáng wén shuō sī jīng， xīn rán xǐ yǒng， shàn xīn shēng yān。
Then the Dragon King, hearing this sutra preached, was delighted and leapt for joy, and a wholesome mind arose in him.
有摩尼珠，名曰立海清淨寶嚴普明，
yǒu mó ní zhū， míng yuē lì hǎi qīng jìng bǎo yán pǔ míng，
He had a mani jewel named Established in the Ocean, Pure, Jewel-Adorned, Universally Bright,
價直三千大千世界，以奉世尊。
jià zhí sān qiān dà qiān shì jiè， yǐ fèng shì zūn。
worth the whole three-thousandfold great-thousand world, and he offered it to the World-Honored One.
其珠之光覆蔽日月之明，
qí zhū zhī guāng fù bì rì yuè zhī míng，
The light of that jewel eclipsed the brightness of sun and moon,
一切眾會得未曾有，禮佛而住，同音而歎：
yī qiè zhòng huì dé wèi céng yǒu， lǐ fó ér zhù， tóng yīn ér tàn：
and the whole assembly, marveling as never before, bowed to the Buddha, stood, and exclaimed with one voice:
「佛興難值既興於世，
「fó xìng nán zhí jì xìng yú shì，
“A buddha's appearance is hard to encounter, yet he has appeared in the world
乃現若茲未曾有法！」
nǎi xiàn ruò zī wèi céng yǒu fǎ！」
and revealed such an unprecedented Dharma as this!”
時海龍王獻寶珠已，而白佛言：
shí hǎi lóng wáng xiàn bǎo zhū yǐ， ér bái fó yán：
When the Sea Dragon King had presented the precious jewel, he said to the Buddha:
「以是德本逮得無礙佛身光明，
「yǐ shì dé běn dǎi dé wú ài fó shēn guāng míng，
“By this root of virtue may I attain the unobstructed radiance of a buddha's body,
令其光明普照十方諸佛國土。
lìng qí guāng míng pǔ zhào shí fāng zhū fó guó tǔ。
and may that radiance shine throughout the buddha-lands of the ten directions.
若人蒙光，除諸塵勞。
ruò rén méng guāng， chú zhū chén láo。
May whoever is touched by that light be rid of defilement and toil.
如今如來眉間光明，
rú jīn rú lái méi jiān guāng míng，
As the Tathagata's light now shines from between his brows,
令我如是蠲却眾冥，逮平等覺，
lìng wǒ rú shì juān què zhòng míng， dǎi píng děng jué，
so may I sweep away all darkness and attain the enlightenment of equality,
其入邪者令立正道。」
qí rù xié zhě lìng lì zhèng dào。」
and may those who have entered wrong paths be established in the right Way.”
六度品第三
liù dù pǐn dì sān
Chapter Three: The Six Perfections
海龍王白佛：「何謂菩薩蠲却眾冥？」
hǎi lóng wáng bái fó： 「hé wèi pú sà juān què zhòng míng？」
The Sea Dragon King said to the Buddha: “How does a bodhisattva sweep away all darkness?”
佛語龍王：「菩薩智慧殊異，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「pú sà zhì huì shū yì，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “The bodhisattva's wisdom is extraordinary.
手執慧燈，分明智慧，智慧最勝。
shǒu zhí huì dēng， fēn míng zhì huì， zhì huì zuì shèng。
In his hand he holds the lamp of wisdom; his wisdom is clear and distinct, and supreme.
持智慧劍，有所興造，
chí zhì huì jiàn， yǒu suǒ xìng zào，
Grasping the sword of wisdom, whatever he undertakes
皆以智慧建立智慧，
jiē yǐ zhì huì jiàn lì zhì huì，
he establishes wisdom by means of wisdom;
而以布施、持戒、忍辱、精進、
ér yǐ bù shī、 chí jiè、 rěn rǔ、 jīng jìn、
and by giving, upholding the precepts, patience, vigor,
禪思、智慧建立智慧。
chán sī、 zhì huì jiàn lì zhì huì。
meditative thought and wisdom, he establishes wisdom.
修行於戒、忍辱、精進、一心，
xiū xíng yú jiè、 rěn rǔ、 jīng jìn、 yī xīn，
Cultivating precepts, patience, vigor and single-mindedness,
普觀諸法，建立智慧，開化眾生。
pǔ guān zhū fǎ， jiàn lì zhì huì， kāi huà zhòng shēng。
he contemplates all dharmas universally, establishes wisdom, and transforms living beings.
菩薩何謂建立智慧而行布施？
pú sà hé wèi jiàn lì zhì huì ér xíng bù shī？
How does a bodhisattva establish wisdom and practice giving?
等於布施。布施已等，等於吾我；
děng yú bù shī。 bù shī yǐ děng， děng yú wú wǒ；
He is impartial in giving. Giving being made equal, he is equal toward self;
吾我已等，便等於人；
wú wǒ yǐ děng， biàn děng yú rén；
self being made equal, he is equal toward others;
已等於人，諸法得等；
yǐ děng yú rén， zhū fǎ dé děng；
others being made equal, all dharmas are made equal;
諸法已等，得諸佛等。
zhū fǎ yǐ děng， dé zhū fó děng。
all dharmas being equal, he attains equality with the buddhas.
雖有所施，不捨是等。
suī yǒu suǒ shī， bù shě shì děng。
Though he gives, he does not forsake this equality.
既所施者，不隨塵勞，
jì suǒ shī zhě， bù suí chén láo，
What he gives does not follow after defilement and toil,
而以施時捨一切塵，亦復如是。
ér yǐ shī shí shě yī qiè chén， yì fù rú shì。
and in the moment of giving he relinquishes every stain — just so.
捨一切生則一切施。
shě yī qiè shēng zé yī qiè shī。
To relinquish all arising is to give all.
離諸住見，棄諸所有，
lí zhū zhù jiàn， qì zhū suǒ yǒu，
Departing from all fixed views and casting off all possessions —
是為菩薩建立智慧而以施彼。
shì wéi pú sà jiàn lì zhì huì ér yǐ shī bǐ。
this is the bodhisattva establishing wisdom and giving to others.
「何謂菩薩建立智慧而奉禁戒？
「hé wèi pú sà jiàn lì zhì huì ér fèng jìn jiè？
“How does a bodhisattva establish wisdom and uphold the precepts?
見身意寂，斯護禁戒。
jiàn shēn yì jì， sī hù jìn jiè。
Seeing body and mind to be still — this is guarding the precepts.
不倚身口意，不倚今世後世，
bù yǐ shēn kǒu yì， bù yǐ jīn shì hòu shì，
He does not lean upon body, speech or mind, nor upon this world or the next,
亦無內外，
yì wú nèi wài，
nor upon inner or outer;
不倚陰蓋四大諸入，
bù yǐ yīn gài sì dà zhū rù，
he does not lean upon the hindrances, the four elements or the sense-fields,
不倚覺意，不倚滅度，
bù yǐ jué yì， bù yǐ miè dù，
nor upon the factors of awakening, nor upon final quiescence;
於一切法亦無所倚，則為護戒。
yú yī qiè fǎ yì wú suǒ yǐ， zé wéi hù jiè。
in no dharma whatever does he lean — this is guarding the precepts.
不以戒戲，亦不放逸，
bù yǐ jiè xì， yì bù fàng yì，
He does not make sport of the precepts, nor is he lax.
是為菩薩建立慧戒。
shì wéi pú sà jiàn lì huì jiè。
This is the bodhisattva establishing the precepts through wisdom.
「彼行忍辱亦不得我，亦不得人，
「bǐ xíng rěn rǔ yì bù dé wǒ， yì bù dé rén，
“In practicing patience he apprehends no self and no other,
亦不得我人；
yì bù dé wǒ rén；
nor self-and-other;
不住吾所，不住我所我，
bú zhù wú suǒ， bú zhù wǒ suǒ wǒ，
he does not abide in what is mine, nor in the self of what is mine;
淨人淨，我淨人淨；
jìng rén jìng， wǒ jìng rén jìng；
others being pure he is pure, and being pure himself others are pure;
見一切法淨，是為行忍。
jiàn yī qiè fǎ jìng， shì wéi xíng rěn。
he sees all dharmas as pure — this is the practice of patience.
彼雖行忍於法無作，
bǐ suī xíng rěn yú fǎ wú zuò，
Though he practices patience, he makes nothing of dharmas;
於法不起不滅。
yú fǎ bù qǐ bù miè。
in dharmas there is neither arising nor ceasing.
彼雖行忍，於諸法無寂不寂。
bǐ suī xíng rěn， yú zhū fǎ wú jì bù jì。
Though he practices patience, in dharmas there is neither stillness nor non-stillness.
彼雖行忍，見人空寂而無吾我，亦不恐怖。
bǐ suī xíng rěn， jiàn rén kōng jì ér wú wú wǒ， yì bù kǒng bù。
Though he practices patience, he sees beings as empty and still and without self, and is not afraid.
彼雖行忍，亦不得身口意。
bǐ suī xíng rěn， yì bù dé shēn kǒu yì。
Though he practices patience, he apprehends no body, speech or mind.
彼雖壞身段節解之，
bǐ suī huài shēn duàn jié jiě zhī，
Though his body be broken and his limbs severed joint from joint,
自觀其身如草木牆壁，則為忍辱。
zì guān qí shēn rú cǎo mù qiáng bì， zé wéi rěn rǔ。
he regards his body as grass, wood or a wall — this is patience.
彼聞惡言、罵言、自在言、
bǐ wén è yán、 mà yán、 zì zài yán、
Though he hear evil words, abusive words, presumptuous words,
不可取言、清淨言，
bù kě qǔ yán、 qīng jìng yán，
words not worth taking up, or pure words,
無處所曉了所言，則為忍辱。
wú chù suǒ xiǎo liǎo suǒ yán， zé wéi rěn rǔ。
he understands that what is said has no place at all — this is patience.
彼雖亂心，心無所結。
bǐ suī luàn xīn， xīn wú suǒ jié。
Though his mind be disturbed, it forms no knot.
本無之心各各無實，須臾滅盡，
běn wú zhī xīn gè gè wú shí， xū yú miè jǐn，
The mind, being originally nonexistent, is in each instance unreal and vanishes in a moment;
以觀如此則為忍辱。
yǐ guān rú cǐ zé wéi rěn rǔ。
to contemplate it thus is patience.
是為菩薩建立慧忍。
shì wéi pú sà jiàn lì huì rěn。
This is the bodhisattva establishing patience through wisdom.
「彼修精進，長諸善法，
「bǐ xiū jīng jìn， zhǎng zhū shàn fǎ，
“In cultivating vigor he increases wholesome dharmas,
觀其法界不增不減，
guān qí fǎ jiè bù zēng bù jiǎn，
and contemplates the dharma-realm as neither increasing nor decreasing.
等御法界，察一切法，不見諸法，
děng yù fǎ jiè， chá yī qiè fǎ， bù jiàn zhū fǎ，
Guiding the dharma-realm impartially, he examines all dharmas and yet sees no dharmas;
立成就者，觀猗世者，
lì chéng jiù zhě， guān yī shì zhě，
he considers those who are established and accomplished, and those who lean upon the world,
由從不實顛倒而興。
yóu cóng bù shí diān dào ér xìng。
and how they arise from what is unreal and inverted.
彼以清淨智慧之明，
bǐ yǐ qīng jìng zhì huì zhī míng，
With the brightness of pure wisdom
觀一切法不隨諸法，不捨諸法，
guān yī qiè fǎ bù suí zhū fǎ， bù shě zhū fǎ，
he contemplates all dharmas without following them and without forsaking them;
不覩諸法之所積聚，
bù dǔ zhū fǎ zhī suǒ jī jù，
he does not behold any accumulation of dharmas,
不見去來，何所從來，何所從去。
bù jiàn qù lái， hé suǒ cóng lái， hé suǒ cóng qù。
nor sees any going or coming — whence they come, whither they go.
曉了諸法，遵法如是，
xiǎo liǎo zhū fǎ， zūn fǎ rú shì，
He understands dharmas and follows the Dharma in this way,
分別苦諦顛倒之事，
fēn bié kǔ dì diān dào zhī shì，
discerning the truth of suffering and the matter of inversion,
為人說法修行精進。
wèi rén shuō fǎ xiū xíng jīng jìn。
and preaches the Dharma to others while cultivating vigor.
彼諸眾生無實無諦，
bǐ zhū zhòng shēng wú shí wú dì，
Those living beings are without substance and without truth;
若人無得，一切諸法亦不可得。
ruò rén wú dé， yī qiè zhū fǎ yì bù kě dé。
if the person cannot be apprehended, neither can any dharma.
所以者何？人不離法，法不離人。
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ rén bù lí fǎ， fǎ bù lí rén。
Why? The person is not apart from dharmas, and dharmas are not apart from the person.
如人自然，吾我自然；
rú rén zì rán， wú wǒ zì rán；
As the person is so of itself, so is the self;
吾我自然，諸法自然；
wú wǒ zì rán， zhū fǎ zì rán；
as the self is so of itself, so are all dharmas;
諸法自然，佛法自然。
zhū fǎ zì rán， fó fǎ zì rán。
as all dharmas are so of themselves, so is the Buddha's Dharma.
其以如是求諸佛法，
qí yǐ rú shì qiú zhū fó fǎ，
One who seeks the dharmas of the buddhas in this way,
如自然者解自然已，便逮佛法。
rú zì rán zhě jiě zì rán yǐ， biàn dǎi fó fǎ。
understanding what is so of itself as it is, thereby attains the Buddha's Dharma.
其有求者，若已求者，甫當求者，
qí yǒu qiú zhě， ruò yǐ qiú zhě， fǔ dāng qiú zhě，
Whoever seeks, has sought, or is about to seek,
彼求此已求無所得，
bǐ qiú cǐ yǐ qiú wú suǒ de，
in that seeking finds nothing to be gained.
是為菩薩建立智慧精進之行。
shì wéi pú sà jiàn lì zhì huì jīng jìn zhī xíng。
This is the bodhisattva establishing the practice of vigor through wisdom.
「彼於禪定而以正受，
「bǐ yú chán dìng ér yǐ zhèng shòu，
“In dhyana he abides in right absorption,
不壞平等，亦不成就。
bù huài píng děng， yì bù chéng jiù。
neither breaking equality nor bringing anything to completion.
彼於禪定而以正受，諸法無思，
bǐ yú chán dìng ér yǐ zhèng shòu， zhū fǎ wú sī，
In dhyana and right absorption, dharmas are beyond thought;
亦無所捨，亦不合會。
yì wú suǒ shě， yì bù hé huì。
there is nothing relinquished and nothing joined together.
於諸境界行無著禪，
yú zhū jìng jiè xíng wú zhuó chán，
Amid all objects he practices unattached dhyana
立諸禪法，於諸法等亦無錯亂，
lì zhū chán fǎ， yú zhū fǎ děng yì wú cuò luàn，
and establishes the dharmas of dhyana; in the equality of dharmas there is no confusion.
非身非心，思惟禪定志性，
fēi shēn fēi xīn， sī wéi chán dìng zhì xìng，
Neither body nor mind, he ponders the nature and intent of dhyana,
無所應行，不以禪行。
wú suǒ yìng xíng， bù yǐ chán xíng。
with nothing that must be done, not practicing by way of dhyana.
等於本無，而以正受，
děng yú běn wú， ér yǐ zhèng shòu，
Equal to original nonbeing, he abides in right absorption;
於本淨法而致平等，
yú běn jìng fǎ ér zhì píng děng，
in the originally pure Dharma he attains equality,
等一切人則致平等。
děng yī qiè rén zé zhì píng děng。
and being equal toward all people he attains equality.
諸法本淨，等無有色，
zhū fǎ běn jìng， děng wú yǒu sè，
Dharmas are originally pure, equal and without form;
不以三昧，所行如應。
bù yǐ sān mèi， suǒ xíng rú yìng。
not by samadhi, yet his practice accords.
心而不住內，亦不起遊外，
xīn ér bú zhù nèi， yì bù qǐ yóu wài，
The mind does not abide within, nor does it rise and roam without;
識無所住，
shí wú suǒ zhù，
consciousness abides nowhere.
度於一切墮顛倒者，
dù yú yī qiè duò diān dào zhě，
He delivers all who have fallen into inversion,
超外五通、聲聞、緣覺禪定正受。
chāo wài wǔ tōng、 shēng wén、 yuán jué chán dìng zhèng shòu。
surpassing the five penetrations of outsiders and the absorptions of sravakas and pratyekabuddhas.
彼以禪定出智慧上，除塵勞見；
bǐ yǐ chán dìng chū zhì huì shàng， chú chén láo jiàn；
By dhyana he rises above into wisdom and removes the view of defilement;
彼以禪定志願于道，開化眾生。
bǐ yǐ chán dìng zhì yuàn yú dào， kāi huà zhòng shēng。
by dhyana he aspires to the Way and transforms living beings.
是則如來常一禪定至于滅度，
shì zé rú lái cháng yī chán dìng zhì yú miè dù，
This is the Tathagata's constant, single dhyana, on to final quiescence.
是為菩薩建立慧定。
shì wéi pú sà jiàn lì huì dìng。
This is the bodhisattva establishing concentration through wisdom.
「彼觀諸法以慧眼察，
「bǐ guān zhū fǎ yǐ huì yǎn chá，
“He contemplates all dharmas and examines them with the eye of wisdom,
亦非肉眼，亦不天眼，
yì fēi ròu yǎn， yì bù tiān yǎn，
not with the fleshly eye nor with the deva eye.
觀諸法已，見諸法寂，觀諸法默。
guān zhū fǎ yǐ， jiàn zhū fǎ jì， guān zhū fǎ mò。
Contemplating dharmas, he sees them as still and beholds them as silent.
諸法寂寞，無行無處；
zhū fǎ jì mò， wú xíng wú chù；
Dharmas are quiescent, without action and without place;
諸法澹然，無所成就。
zhū fǎ dàn rán， wú suǒ chéng jiù。
dharmas are tranquil, with nothing accomplished.
普觀諸法皆已如是。
pǔ guān zhū fǎ jiē yǐ rú shì。
Contemplating all dharmas universally, he finds them all to be thus.
如是觀者，是為法觀。
rú shì guān zhě， shì wéi fǎ guān。
One who contemplates in this way — this is contemplation of the Dharma.
法觀如是，不見諸法之所歸趣。
fǎ guān rú shì， bù jiàn zhū fǎ zhī suǒ guī qù。
Contemplating Dharma thus, he sees no destination to which dharmas resort.
其有見法而不觀者，
qí yǒu jiàn fǎ ér bù guān zhě，
Whoever sees dharmas but does not contemplate them
不以見法而成觀也。
bù yǐ jiàn fǎ ér chéng guān yě。
does not accomplish contemplation merely by seeing them.
無求無曉，不知不見，是為見法。
wú qiú wú xiǎo， bù zhī bù jiàn， shì wéi jiàn fǎ。
Without seeking and without knowing, neither cognizing nor seeing — this is seeing the Dharma.
無我、無人、無壽、無命是為見法。
wú wǒ、 wú rén、 wú shòu、 wú mìng shì wéi jiàn fǎ。
No self, no person, no lifespan, no living being — this is seeing the Dharma.
假使菩薩觀法如此，
jiǎ shǐ pú sà guān fǎ rú cǐ，
If a bodhisattva contemplates dharmas in this way,
見人顛倒益於眾生，而發大哀，
jiàn rén diān dào yì yú zhòng shēng， ér fā dà āi，
seeing beings inverted he works for their benefit and brings forth great compassion.
法淨如是，眾人猗著。
fǎ jìng rú shì， zhòng rén yī zhuó。
The Dharma is pure in this way, yet people lean upon it and cling.
於是菩薩發弘大志，欲度群萌。
yú shì pú sà fā hóng dà zhì， yù dù qún méng。
Therefore the bodhisattva rouses a vast resolve, wishing to deliver the multitudes.
是群萌者，常無萌類，
shì qún méng zhě， cháng wú méng lèi，
Yet these multitudes are, in truth, never any multitude at all.
是為菩薩建立慧法。」
shì wéi pú sà jiàn lì huì fǎ。」
This is the bodhisattva establishing the Dharma through wisdom.”
無盡藏品第四
wú jìn zàng pǐn dì sì
Chapter Four: The Inexhaustible Treasury
佛告龍王：「何謂菩薩建立智慧，
fó gào lóng wáng： 「hé wèi pú sà jiàn lì zhì huì，
The Buddha told the Dragon King: “How does a bodhisattva establish wisdom,
為人說法，不見有人？
wèi rén shuō fǎ， bù jiàn yǒu rén？
preach the Dharma to people, and yet see no person at all?
人者無我，無人非身，
rén zhě wú wǒ， wú rén fēi shēn，
A person has no self; there is no person and no body;
人者寂寞，人無所有，
rén zhě jì mò， rén wú suǒ yǒu，
a person is quiescent; a person has nothing whatever;
人者本淨，人者音聲，人者名耳，
rén zhě běn jìng， rén zhě yīn shēng， rén zhě míng ěr，
a person is originally pure; a person is but a sound; a person is only a name;
人空、無相、無願，
rén kōng、 wú xiàng、 wú yuàn，
a person is empty, signless, wishless;
人非有數，人而審諦，
rén fēi yǒu shù， rén ér shěn dì，
a person is not among the countable; a person examined truly,
人無所生，人不有起。
rén wú suǒ shēng， rén bù yǒu qǐ。
a person is unborn; a person does not arise.
為人說法，講人清淨，不懷吾我，
wèi rén shuō fǎ， jiǎng rén qīng jìng， bù huái wú wǒ，
He preaches the Dharma to people, expounds their purity, harbors no self,
無壽無命，不滅自然，不滅所有，
wú shòu wú mìng， bù miè zì rán， bù miè suǒ yǒu，
no lifespan, no living being; he does not extinguish what is so of itself, nor extinguish what there is;
隨人本行而為說法。
suí rén běn xíng ér wèi shuō fǎ。
and he preaches the Dharma according to each person's original conduct.
何況眾生本淨自然，
hé kuàng zhòng shēng běn jìng zì rán，
How much more, since beings are originally pure and so of themselves,
無我自然，無形自然，則人自然。
wú wǒ zì rán， wú xíng zì rán， zé rén zì rán。
selfless of themselves, formless of themselves — so the person is so of itself.
設人自然，以此自然諸法自然；
shè rén zì rán， yǐ cǐ zì rán zhū fǎ zì rán；
If the person is so of itself, then by this all dharmas are so of themselves;
設諸法自然，一切佛法亦復自然。
shè zhū fǎ zì rán， yī qiè fó fǎ yì fù zì rán。
and if all dharmas are so of themselves, so too is the whole Dharma of the buddhas.
是謂一切諸法悉為佛法。
shì wèi yī qiè zhū fǎ xī wèi fó fǎ。
This is why all dharmas whatsoever are the Dharma of the buddhas.
一切諸法但假名耳，因號有名，
yī qiè zhū fǎ dàn jiǎ míng ěr， yīn hào yǒu míng，
All dharmas are but provisional designations; through labeling they have names.
設說諸法則講非法。
shè shuō zhū fǎ zé jiǎng fēi fǎ。
To speak of dharmas is to expound what is not dharma.
所以者何？如法所言，非法亦然；
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ rú fǎ suǒ yán， fēi fǎ yì rán；
Why? As it is said of dharma, so is it of non-dharma;
如呼法音，則非法音。
rú hū fǎ yīn， zé fēi fǎ yīn。
and as one calls out the sound of dharma, so it is not the sound of dharma.
所以者何？諸法法界及與本淨不可言說，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ zhū fǎ fǎ jiè jí yǔ běn jìng bù kě yán shuō，
Why? The dharma-realm of all dharmas, together with original purity, cannot be spoken,
亦無所得。
yì wú suǒ de。
and cannot be apprehended.
法界本淨，亦無所持。
fǎ jiè běn jìng， yì wú suǒ chí。
The dharma-realm is originally pure, and there is nothing to hold.
一切法界諸法本淨，
yī qiè fǎ jiè zhū fǎ běn jìng，
All the dharma-realm and all dharmas are originally pure;
壞一切法何所成就？
huài yī qiè fǎ hé suǒ chéng jiù？
were one to demolish every dharma, what would there be to accomplish?
是為諸佛法、為說經法。
shì wéi zhū fó fǎ、 wèi shuō jīng fǎ。
