This is the Dharma of the buddhas, and this is the preaching of the sutra's Dharma.
以是因緣寂寞如是，
yǐ shì yīn yuán jì mò rú shì，
By this cause and condition it is quiescent in just this way,
不有諸佛法聲之化識也！
bù yǒu zhū fó fǎ shēng zhī huà shí yě！
and there is no consciousness transformed by the sound of the buddhas' Dharma.
佛法無教而不可處有為、無為。
fó fǎ wú jiào ér bù kě chù yǒu wéi、 wú wéi。
The Buddha's Dharma is without teaching and cannot be placed in the conditioned or the unconditioned.
所以者何？不離有為、無為而以解脫。
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ bù lí yǒu wéi、 wú wéi ér yǐ jiě tuō。
Why? Liberation is not apart from the conditioned or the unconditioned.
寧有異法，可計數乎？」
níng yǒu yì fǎ， kě jì shù hū？」
Is there then any separate dharma that could be counted?”
龍王答曰：「不也。世尊！
lóng wáng dá yuē： 「bù yě。 shì zūn！
The Dragon King answered: “There is not, World-Honored One.
諸法無數，如來無數。」
zhū fǎ wú shù， rú lái wú shù。」
Dharmas are beyond number, and the Tathagata is beyond number.”
佛言：「如是，如是！如仁所言，
fó yán： 「rú shì， rú shì！ rú rén suǒ yán，
The Buddha said: “Just so, just so! As you have said,
諸法無數，如來無數，則無有二。
zhū fǎ wú shù， rú lái wú shù， zé wú yǒu èr。
dharmas are beyond number and the Tathagata is beyond number — thus there are not two.
於龍王意云何，無數之言有處所乎？」
yú lóng wáng yì yún hé， wú shù zhī yán yǒu chù suǒ hū？」
What do you think, Dragon King: does the word 'numberless' have any place?”
答曰：「不也。世尊！」
dá yuē： 「bù yě。 shì zūn！」
He answered: “It does not, World-Honored One.”
佛言：「何以故？當知當作斯觀，
fó yán： 「hé yǐ gù？ dāng zhī dāng zuò sī guān，
The Buddha said: “Why? You should know and contemplate it thus:
佛法無處無言。
fó fǎ wú chù wú yán。
the Buddha's Dharma has no place and no speech.
如佛法無言無處，
rú fó fǎ wú yán wú chù，
As the Buddha's Dharma is without speech and without place,
一切諸法無處無言亦復如是！
yī qiè zhū fǎ wú chù wú yán yì fù rú shì！
so likewise are all dharmas without place and without speech.
龍王觀視如來大哀巍巍，
lóng wáng guān shì rú lái dà āi wēi wēi，
Dragon King, behold the Tathagata's great compassion, lofty as it is,
若茲開化眾生，令立堅固。
ruò zī kāi huà zhòng shēng， lìng lì jiān gù。
transforming living beings in this way and establishing them firmly.
又一切法無處無教，說因緣教：
yòu yī qiè fǎ wú chù wú jiào， shuō yīn yuán jiào：
Again, all dharmas are without place and without teaching, yet the teaching of causes and conditions is preached:
是法有漏，是法無漏，
shì fǎ yǒu lòu， shì fǎ wú lòu，
this dharma has outflows, this dharma is without outflows;
是有世，是度世事，
shì yǒu shì， shì dù shì shì，
this belongs to the world, this is what carries beyond the world;
有著無著，有數無數，有為無為，
yǒu zhuó wú zhuó， yǒu shù wú shù， yǒu wéi wú wéi，
attached and unattached, countable and countless, conditioned and unconditioned;
塵勞瞋恨，習是捨是，
chén láo chēn hèn， xí shì shě shì，
defilement and hatred, this to be cultivated and this to be relinquished;
凡法、聖法、學法、不學法，
fán fǎ、 shèng fǎ、 xué fǎ、 bù xué fǎ，
the dharma of the ordinary and of the holy, of the learner and of one beyond learning,
聲聞法、緣覺法、菩薩法、佛法。」
shēng wén fǎ、 yuán jué fǎ、 pú sà fǎ、 fó fǎ。」
the dharma of the sravaka, of the pratyekabuddha, of the bodhisattva, and of the Buddha.”
佛言：「龍王！如來如是為人說法，
fó yán： 「lóng wáng！ rú lái rú shì wèi rén shuō fǎ，
The Buddha said: “Dragon King, thus does the Tathagata preach the Dharma to people,
講其處所，亦不見法，無諸法想。
jiǎng qí chù suǒ， yì bù jiàn fǎ， wú zhū fǎ xiǎng。
expounding their place, yet seeing no dharma and holding no notion of dharmas.
譬如有人虛空無色無見，
pì rú yǒu rén xū kōng wú sè wú jiàn，
Suppose a man, where space is formless and invisible,
欲以諸色畫於虛空，
yù yǐ zhū sè huà yú xū kōng，
wished to paint colors upon the empty sky
而作天像及諸人像、象馬、步乘，
ér zuò tiān xiàng jí zhū rén xiàng、 xiàng mǎ、 bù chéng，
and to make images of devas and of men, of elephants, horses and chariots:
彼人畫是，寧難不乎？」
bǐ rén huà shì， níng nán bù hū？」
would that man's painting not be difficult?”
答曰：「甚難，甚難！至未曾有。天中之天！」
dá yuē： 「shèn nán， shèn nán！ zhì wèi céng yǒu。 tiān zhōng zhī tiān！」
He answered: “Most difficult, most difficult! Unprecedented indeed, Deva among devas!”
佛言：「龍王！如來所為甚難，
fó yán： 「lóng wáng！ rú lái suǒ wéi shèn nán，
The Buddha said: “Dragon King, what the Tathagata does is more difficult still.
於彼諸法無色無取而不可見，
yú bǐ zhū fǎ wú sè wú qǔ ér bù kě jiàn，
Those dharmas are formless, ungraspable and invisible;
亦無文字，亦無所得，
yì wú wén zì， yì wú suǒ de，
they have no letters and cannot be apprehended;
而為一切講說言教，示現文字，
ér wéi yī qiè jiǎng shuō yán jiào， shì xiàn wén zì，
yet for all beings he expounds a spoken teaching and displays letters.
設以方便此乃甚難。
shè yǐ fāng biàn cǐ nǎi shèn nán。
To do this by skillful means is exceedingly difficult.
其有信入如是像法，
qí yǒu xìn rù rú shì xiàng fǎ，
Whoever enters in faith into a Dharma of such a kind,
是等諸人多所成辦。
shì děng zhū rén duō suǒ chéng bàn。
such people accomplish a great deal.
若有受此深妙義者，
ruò yǒu shòu cǐ shēn miào yì zhě，
Whoever receives this profound and subtle meaning
不為諸魔之所得便。
bù wéi zhū mó zhī suǒ de biàn。
gives no advantage to the hosts of Mara.
憶念我過世，龍王！
yì niàn wǒ guò shì， lóng wáng！
I recall my past lives, Dragon King.
更見供事無央數佛，
gèng jiàn gòng shì wú yāng shù fó，
Moreover I beheld and served countless buddhas,
恒輒捨家，淨修梵行。
héng zhé shě jiā， jìng xiū fàn xíng。
ever leaving the household and purely cultivating the holy life.
彼如來等未曾為吾說深妙法，
bǐ rú lái děng wèi céng wéi wú shuō shēn miào fǎ，
Yet those Tathagatas never preached the profound and subtle Dharma to me;
應病如講布施、持戒學道之法，
yìng bìng rú jiǎng bù shī、 chí jiè xué dào zhī fǎ，
as medicine suits the illness they expounded giving, precepts and the study of the Way,
聽聞忍辱仁和之教，
tīng wén rěn rǔ rén hé zhī jiào，
and I heard the teaching of patience, kindness and concord,
燕居靜處止足功德。
yān jū jìng chù zhǐ zú gōng dé。
of dwelling in quietude and stillness, and the merit of contentment.
所以者何？行德未了。
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ xíng dé wèi liǎo。
Why? Because my practice of virtue was not yet complete.
了行德已，從大殊曜，
le xíng dé yǐ， cóng dà shū yào，
Once the practice of virtue was complete, from the Greatly Radiant One
如來即得聞斯深妙之法，
rú lái jí dé wén sī shēn miào zhī fǎ，
I was able to hear this profound and subtle Dharma,
應時逮成柔順法忍。
yìng shí dǎi chéng róu shùn fǎ rěn。
and there and then attained the pliant forbearance of dharmas.
以是之故，當知此義，當作是觀，
yǐ shì zhī gù， dāng zhī cǐ yì， dāng zuò shì guān，
For this reason you should know this meaning and contemplate it thus:
聞是深法功德具足。
wén shì shēn fǎ gōng dé jù zú。
hearing this profound Dharma, one's merit is complete.
從過去正覺受此深經，
cóng guò qù zhèng jué shòu cǐ shēn jīng，
To receive this profound sutra from the enlightened ones of the past —
無想無名眾穢因緣，
wú xiǎng wú míng zhòng huì yīn yuán，
free of notions, free of names, free of the causes of defilement,
無我、無人、無壽、無命，
wú wǒ、 wú rén、 wú shòu、 wú mìng，
without self, person, lifespan or living being —
信樂受持諷誦，為他人說，其福甚多。
xìn lè shòu chí fěng sòng， wèi tā rén shuō， qí fú shèn duō。
and to receive, uphold, recite it in faith and joy and preach it to others: such merit is very great.
若有菩薩愍傷一切，欲令安隱，
ruò yǒu pú sà mǐn shāng yī qiè， yù lìng ān yǐn，
Suppose a bodhisattva, pitying all beings and wishing them peace,
使三千大千世界一一眾生皆得所安，
shǐ sān qiān dà qiān shì jiè yī yī zhòng shēng jiē dé suǒ ān，
brought every single being of the three-thousandfold great-thousand world to peace,
諸天人民合集此德，施與一人，
zhū tiān rén mín hé jí cǐ dé， shī yǔ yī rén，
and all devas and men gathered this merit together and gave it to one person.
於龍王意云何，
yú lóng wáng yì yún hé，
What do you think, Dragon King:
菩薩寧為眾生加無極安不乎？」
pú sà níng wéi zhòng shēng jiā wú jí ān bù hū？」
would that bodhisattva not have added boundless peace for living beings?”
答曰：「甚多，甚多！天中天！」
dá yuē： 「shèn duō， shèn duō！ tiān zhōng tiān！」
He answered: “Very great indeed, Deva among devas!”
佛言：「其有菩薩施諸眾生若干安隱，
fó yán： 「qí yǒu pú sà shī zhū zhòng shēng ruò gān ān yǐn，
The Buddha said: “Yet if a bodhisattva bestows manifold peace upon beings,
若為人說一句無常、苦、空、非身之義，
ruò wèi rén shuō yī jù wú cháng、 kǔ、 kōng、 fēi shēn zhī yì，
and if he preaches to a person a single line on impermanence, suffering, emptiness and non-self,
空、無相、無願，
kōng、 wú xiàng、 wú yuàn，
on emptiness, signlessness and wishlessness,
無我、無人、無壽、無命、不生不起之事，
wú wǒ、 wú rén、 wú shòu、 wú mìng、 bù shēng bù qǐ zhī shì，
on the absence of self, person, lifespan and living being, on non-birth and non-arising,
則是施安福難稱量。
zé shì shī ān fú nán chēng liàng。
then the merit of that gift of peace is beyond measuring.
所以者何？有為之安眾生皆更，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ yǒu wéi zhī ān zhòng shēng jiē gēng，
Why? Conditioned peace all beings have already experienced;
無為之安未曾歷也。
wú wéi zhī ān wèi céng lì yě。
unconditioned peace they have never passed through.
彼其以此深妙之法而暢音聲，
bǐ qí yǐ cǐ shēn miào zhī fǎ ér chàng yīn shēng，
When he sets forth this profound and subtle Dharma in speech,
於無為安以為服食。
yú wú wéi ān yǐ wéi fú shí。
the unconditioned peace becomes their clothing and their food.
是故菩薩欲自立義具世人願，
shì gù pú sà yù zì lì yì jù shì rén yuàn，
Therefore a bodhisattva who wishes to establish the meaning for himself and fulfill the wishes of the world
當學深妙之法。
dāng xué shēn miào zhī fǎ。
should study the profound and subtle Dharma.
若有菩薩所在會坐，捨深妙法，
ruò yǒu pú sà suǒ zài huì zuò， shě shēn miào fǎ，
If a bodhisattva, seated in any assembly, forsakes the profound and subtle Dharma
說雜句飾，則為斷絕正法之化。
shuō zá jù shì， zé wéi duàn jué zhèng fǎ zhī huà。
and speaks in mixed and ornamented phrases, he cuts off the transforming power of the true Dharma.
所以者何？是深妙法布閻浮利而不沒盡，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ shì shēn miào fǎ bù yán fú lì ér bù mò jìn，
Why? This profound Dharma is spread through Jambudvipa and is not lost;
人所聽受，樂於法者，不足言耳，
rén suǒ tīng shòu， lè yú fǎ zhě， bù zú yán ěr，
but of those who listen and receive it, those who delight in the Dharma are too few to speak of,
非人最多。
fēi rén zuì duō。
while those who do not are very many.
假使法師藏深妙法，
jiǎ shǐ fǎ shī cáng shēn miào fǎ，
If a Dharma master conceals the profound and subtle Dharma
讀雜句者不樂深法，天則不歡悅：
dú zá jù zhě bù lè shēn fǎ， tiān zé bù huān yuè：
and those who recite mixed phrases take no delight in the profound Dharma, the devas are not glad:
『是族姓子！隨世所樂而說俗事。
『shì zú xìng zǐ！ suí shì suǒ lè ér shuō sú shì。
‘This son of good family follows what the world enjoys and speaks of worldly affairs.
嗚呼痛哉！此眾會中無說法者。』
wū hū tòng zāi！ cǐ zhòng huì zhōng wú shuō fǎ zhě。』
Alas, how grievous! In this assembly there is no one who preaches the Dharma.’
心懷悁慼，而退捨去。」
xīn huái yuān qī， ér tuì shě qù。」
Grieved at heart, they withdraw and depart.”
時海龍王白世尊曰：
shí hǎi lóng wáng bái shì zūn yuē：
Then the Sea Dragon King said to the World-Honored One:
「布施、持戒學道之法是俗事耶？
「bù shī、 chí jiè xué dào zhī fǎ shì sú shì yé？
“Are giving, upholding precepts and studying the Way then worldly affairs?
棄家出學、淨修梵行非佛法耶？」
qì jiā chū xué、 jìng xiū fàn xíng fēi fó fǎ yé？」
Is leaving home to study and purely cultivating the holy life not the Buddha's Dharma?”
世尊答曰：「諸佛興已，起無起法，
shì zūn dá yuē： 「zhū fó xìng yǐ， qǐ wú qǐ fǎ，
The World-Honored One answered: “When the buddhas appear, they raise up the dharma of non-arising.
於三界行有所救護，皆是俗事，非是佛語。
yú sān jiè xíng yǒu suǒ jiù hù， jiē shì sú shì， fēi shì fó yǔ。
Whatever rescues and shelters within the three realms is a worldly matter, not the Buddha's word.
彼則何謂？四禪、四等心、
bǐ zé hé wèi？ sì chán、 sì děng xīn、
What are these? The four dhyanas, the four immeasurable minds,
四無色定、五通、十善之行，
sì wú sè dìng、 wǔ tōng、 shí shàn zhī xíng，
the four formless absorptions, the five penetrations, the practice of the ten wholesome deeds,
布施、持戒、忍辱、精進、一心、智慧、
bù shī、 chí jiè、 rěn rǔ、 jīng jìn、 yī xīn、 zhì huì、
giving, precepts, patience, vigor, single-mindedness and wisdom,
書疏校計、經卷體醫、
shū shū jiào jì、 jīng juǎn tǐ yī、
writing and reckoning, texts and the arts of medicine,
方藥巧工技術、
fāng yào qiǎo gōng jì shù、
prescriptions, crafts and skills,
身想衣食財物、所愛禪定，
shēn xiǎng yī shí cái wù、 suǒ ài chán dìng，
notions of the body, clothing, food and goods, and the absorptions one cherishes —
在三界行皆是俗事，非為佛言。」
zài sān jiè xíng jiē shì sú shì， fēi wèi fó yán。」
all conduct within the three realms is a worldly matter, not the Buddha's word.”
佛告龍王：「佛興於世間，
fó gào lóng wáng： 「fó xìng yú shì jiān，
The Buddha told the Dragon King: “When a buddha appears in the world,
未曾所聞非常之苦、非我之寂。
wèi céng suǒ wén fēi cháng zhī kǔ、 fēi wǒ zhī jì。
there is heard what was never heard: the suffering of impermanence, the stillness of non-self.
除於苦義，斷乎習義，
chú yú kǔ yì， duàn hū xí yì，
The meaning of removing suffering, of severing its origin,
證於盡義，遵修道義，
zhèng yú jǐn yì， zūn xiū dào yì，
of realizing cessation, of cultivating the path;
入乎空義，度於無想，導御無願，
rù hū kōng yì， dù yú wú xiǎng， dǎo yù wú yuàn，
of entering emptiness, passing through signlessness, being guided by wishlessness;
於諸眾行不生不起，
yú zhū zhòng xíng bù shēng bù qǐ，
of non-birth and non-arising in all conditioned actions;
義意止、意斷、根、力、
yì yì zhǐ、 yì duàn、 gēn、 lì、
the meaning of the foundations of mindfulness, the right exertions, the faculties, the powers,
神足、覺意觀、八寂路，
shén zú、 jué yì guān、 bā jì lù，
the bases of spiritual power, the contemplation of the factors of awakening, and the eightfold path of peace;
求真諦本淨，
qiú zhēn dì běn jìng，
seeking the original purity of the true truth;
如無所起陰種諸入，為空寂義。
rú wú suǒ qǐ yīn zhǒng zhū rù， wéi kōng jì yì。
the aggregates and sense-fields as unarisen — this is the meaning of empty quiescence.
所由諸義，不壞諸法，不壞非法，
suǒ yóu zhū yì， bù huài zhū fǎ， bù huài fēi fǎ，
By these meanings he demolishes neither dharmas nor non-dharmas,
解一切法不生不長，皆無所起。
jiě yī qiè fǎ bù shēng bù zhǎng， jiē wú suǒ qǐ。
and understands that all dharmas neither arise nor grow, and nothing at all comes into being.
不計有常無常，
bù jì yǒu cháng wú cháng，
He does not reckon permanence or impermanence;
由因緣起得無所生，
yóu yīn yuán qǐ dé wú suǒ shēng，
through dependent arising he attains non-birth,
還於本淨而離色欲，
hái yú běn jìng ér lí sè yù，
returns to original purity and departs from desire for form,
現無數法入於道法，
xiàn wú shù fǎ rù yú dào fǎ，
manifests countless dharmas and enters the dharma of the Way.
在於道法，無想不想，無應不應，
zài yú dào fǎ， wú xiǎng bù xiǎng， wú yìng bù yìng，
In the dharma of the Way there is neither notion nor non-notion, neither response nor non-response;
捨於一切念、淨不淨想，
shě yú yī qiè niàn、 jìng bù jìng xiǎng，
he relinquishes every thought and every notion of pure and impure,
無舉無下，
wú jǔ wú xià，
neither raising up nor putting down.
陰幽冥門自然如空，得平等行。
yīn yōu míng mén zì rán rú kōng， dé píng děng xíng。
The dark gate of the aggregates is of itself like empty space, and he attains the practice of equality.
於想等想、無想，於想離想，
yú xiǎng děng xiǎng、 wú xiǎng， yú xiǎng lí xiǎng，
Amid notions he is equal toward notion and non-notion; amid notions he departs from notion;
均於一想離一切想，
jūn yú yī xiǎng lí yī qiè xiǎng，
level in a single notion, he departs from all notions;
無所觀見，寂然諸所見，
wú suǒ guān jiàn， jì rán zhū suǒ jiàn，
with nothing contemplated or seen, all that is seen is stilled,
現諸顛倒一切平等。
xiàn zhū diān dào yī qiè píng děng。
and all inversions are shown to be wholly equal.
謂得果跡皆音聲耳，
wèi dé guǒ jì jiē yīn shēng ěr，
To speak of attaining the traces of the fruits is mere sound;
彼無所得，亦無不得，不受不捨。」
bǐ wú suǒ de， yì wú bù dé， bù shòu bù shě。」
therein nothing is gained and nothing not gained, nothing received and nothing relinquished.”
佛語龍王：「是所先說諸法之御，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「shì suǒ xiān shuō zhū fǎ zhī yù，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “The guidance of dharmas spoken of above,
所得不可稱說！
suǒ de bù kě chēng shuō！
what is attained by it cannot be told.
至於聲聞，獲聲聞乘；
zhì yú shēng wén， huò shēng wén chéng；
For the sravaka, he obtains the sravaka vehicle;
至於緣覺，獲緣覺乘。
zhì yú yuán jué， huò yuán jué chéng。
for the pratyekabuddha, the pratyekabuddha vehicle.
菩薩逮得不起法忍，
pú sà dǎi dé bù qǐ fǎ rěn，
The bodhisattva attains the forbearance of the non-arising of dharmas
成於如來無上正真道，為最正覺。
chéng yú rú lái wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào， wéi zuì zhèng jué。
and accomplishes the Tathagata's unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment, becoming perfectly enlightened.
斯謂佛言。
sī wèi fó yán。
This is called the Buddha's word.
是所言者，隨習俗教，
shì suǒ yán zhě， suí xí sú jiào，
What is spoken here accords with conventional teaching,
皆是佛法教於真諦。
jiē shì fó fǎ jiào yú zhēn dì。
and all of it is the Buddha's Dharma teaching the true truth.
佛道無文，佛言無言；
fó dào wú wén， fó yán wú yán；
The buddha path is without letters; the Buddha's word is without speech;
佛教無跡，佛教無想；
fó jiào wú jī， fó jiào wú xiǎng；
the Buddha's teaching is without trace; the Buddha's teaching is without notion;
佛教無歎，佛教無化；
fó jiào wú tàn， fó jiào wú huà；
the Buddha's teaching is without praise; the Buddha's teaching is without transforming;
佛教無正，佛教無名，佛教無思，
fó jiào wú zhèng， fó jiào wú míng， fó jiào wú sī，
the Buddha's teaching is without correcting, without name, without thought;
佛無心意識，亦無所念，
fó wú xīn yì shí， yì wú suǒ niàn，
the Buddha has no mind, thought or consciousness, and conceives nothing.
是謂佛教，而不可說，
shì wèi fó jiào， ér bù kě shuō，
This is called the Buddha's teaching, and it cannot be spoken,
亦無言教，不可指現。」
yì wú yán jiào， bù kě zhǐ xiàn。」
has no spoken teaching, and cannot be pointed out.”
佛語龍王：「如是比教乃為佛言。
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「rú shì bǐ jiào nǎi wèi fó yán。
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “A teaching of this kind is what the Buddha's word is.
如來不以文義說法，
rú lái bù yǐ wén yì shuō fǎ，
The Tathagata does not preach the Dharma by the sense of letters;
無文字教而為說法，
wú wén zì jiào ér wèi shuō fǎ，
he preaches by a teaching without letters,
是故無文則為佛教。
shì gù wú wén zé wèi fó jiào。
and therefore it is the absence of letters that is the Buddha's teaching.
佛所說法不有所逮，
fó suǒ shuō fǎ bù yǒu suǒ dǎi，
The Dharma the Buddha preaches attains nothing;
滅一切得佛所說法，
miè yī qiè dé fó suǒ shuō fǎ，
it extinguishes all gaining — such is the Dharma the Buddha preaches,
是故無得為佛所言。
shì gù wú dé wèi fó suǒ yán。
and therefore it is non-attainment that is the Buddha's word.
佛所說經曾無言教，寂滅言教，
fó suǒ shuō jīng céng wú yán jiào， jì miè yán jiào，
The sutras the Buddha preaches have never had a spoken teaching; the spoken teaching is quiescent.
以故言曰無教佛言。
yǐ gù yán yuē wú jiào fó yán。
For this reason it is said: the Buddha's word is without teaching.
說法無取，法無猗法、無放逸法、
shuō fǎ wú qǔ， fǎ wú yī fǎ、 wú fàng yì fǎ、
The Dharma preached grasps nothing: a dharma that leans on nothing, a dharma without laxity,
不想法、無起法、無壞法、
bù xiǎng fǎ、 wú qǐ fǎ、 wú huài fǎ、
a dharma without notion, without arising, without destruction,
無究竟法、無所得法、無所志法、
wú jiū jìng fǎ、 wú suǒ de fǎ、 wú suǒ zhì fǎ、
without finality, without attainment, without aim,
無所念法、無所行法、無分別法、
wú suǒ niàn fǎ、 wú suǒ xíng fǎ、 wú fēn bié fǎ、
without thought, without practice, without discrimination,
無有想法、無所至法、無所推法。」
wú yǒu xiǎng fǎ、 wú suǒ zhì fǎ、 wú suǒ tuī fǎ。」
without conception, without destination, and without inference.”
佛語龍王：「如來為人說法，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「rú lái wèi rén shuō fǎ，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “When the Tathagata preaches the Dharma to people,
未曾有行、有所證也！
wèi céng yǒu xíng、 yǒu suǒ zhèng yě！
there has never been any practice nor anything realized.
吾之所言一切本淨，法無形色。
wú zhī suǒ yán yī qiè běn jìng， fǎ wú xíng sè。
All that I say is originally pure; dharmas have no shape or form.
是故龍王！諸法無像，是名佛言。
shì gù lóng wáng！ zhū fǎ wú xiàng， shì míng fó yán。
Therefore, Dragon King, dharmas have no image — this is called the Buddha's word.
又復何謂號為佛言？
yòu fù hé wèi hào wèi fó yán？
And again, what is designated the Buddha's word?
解一切音，無所不達，故曰佛言。
jiě yī qiè yīn， wú suǒ bù dá， gù yuē fó yán。
It understands every sound and penetrates all — therefore it is called the Buddha's word.
察去來今，無所罣礙，故曰佛言。
chá qù lái jīn， wú suǒ guà ài， gù yuē fó yán。
It examines past, future and present without obstruction — therefore the Buddha's word.
覺了眾言，故曰佛言。
jué le zhòng yán， gù yuē fó yán。
It comprehends all speech — therefore the Buddha's word.
報答諸問，故曰佛言。
bào dá zhū wèn， gù yuē fó yán。
It answers every question — therefore the Buddha's word.
一切所說因緣有言無所不博，故曰佛言。
yī qiè suǒ shuō yīn yuán yǒu yán wú suǒ bù bó， gù yuē fó yán。
In all it says by way of causes and conditions there is nothing it does not master — therefore the Buddha's word.
覺諸所說如呼聲響，故曰佛言。
jué zhū suǒ shuō rú hū shēng xiǎng， gù yuē fó yán。
It knows all that is said to be like an echo answering a call — therefore the Buddha's word.
無字無說，故曰佛言。
wú zì wú shuō， gù yuē fó yán。
It is without letters and without speech — therefore the Buddha's word.
諸所字說亦皆佛言。
zhū suǒ zì shuō yì jiē fó yán。
And yet all that is said in letters is likewise the Buddha's word.
所以者何？是諸文字，去、來、今佛所說。
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ shì zhū wén zì， qù、 lái、 jīn fó suǒ shuō。
Why? These letters are what the buddhas of past, future and present have spoken.
今佛所說者、已說之者、當來說者，
jīn fó suǒ shuō zhě、 yǐ shuō zhī zhě、 dāng lái shuō zhě，
What the present buddha speaks, what has been spoken, what will be spoken —
以是之故，一切文字諸所言教皆名佛言。
yǐ shì zhī gù， yī qiè wén zì zhū suǒ yán jiào jiē míng fó yán。
for this reason all letters and all spoken teachings are called the Buddha's word.
入如此比，曉了眾慧，
rù rú cǐ bǐ， xiǎo liǎo zhòng huì，
Entering into this kind of understanding and comprehending all wisdom
是謂菩薩分別道義，
shì wèi pú sà fēn bié dào yì，
is called the bodhisattva's discernment of the meaning of the Way;
故曰文字言說皆號佛言。
gù yuē wén zì yán shuō jiē hào fó yán。
therefore letters and speech are all designated the Buddha's word.
不壞法界，志一味慧，
bù huài fǎ jiè， zhì yī wèi huì，
Not demolishing the dharma-realm, aiming at the wisdom of a single savor,
是謂菩薩分別經本，
shì wèi pú sà fēn bié jīng běn，
is called the bodhisattva's discernment of the root of the sutras;
故曰文字言說皆號佛言。
gù yuē wén zì yán shuō jiē hào fó yán。
therefore letters and speech are all designated the Buddha's word.
其有如應順于法慧，
qí yǒu rú yìng shùn yú fǎ huì，
To accord fittingly with the wisdom of the Dharma
是謂菩薩分別順寂，
shì wèi pú sà fēn bié shùn jì，
is called the bodhisattva's discernment of concordant quiescence;
故曰文字言說皆號佛言。
gù yuē wén zì yán shuō jiē hào fó yán。
therefore letters and speech are all designated the Buddha's word.
其有說慧無處無著，
qí yǒu shuō huì wú chù wú zhuó，
To speak of a wisdom that has no place and no attachment
是謂菩薩分別曉了。
shì wèi pú sà fēn bié xiǎo liǎo。
is called the bodhisattva's discernment of full understanding.
是故，龍王！一切諸法莫不歸此，分別四義。
shì gù， lóng wáng！ yī qiè zhū fǎ mò bù guī cǐ， fēn bié sì yì。
Therefore, Dragon King, all dharmas resort to this: the discernment of these four meanings.
菩薩解四義者，文字言說諸所歸趣，
pú sà jiě sì yì zhě， wén zì yán shuō zhū suǒ guī qù，
For a bodhisattva who understands the four meanings, wherever letters and speech resort,
身有所在莫不誘進皆入佛教，
shēn yǒu suǒ zài mò bù yòu jìn jiē rù fó jiào，
wherever he is present he draws all forward into the Buddha's teaching.
是故無著、本無所住，
shì gù wú zhuó、 běn wú suǒ zhù，
Therefore, unattached and originally abiding nowhere,
於百千劫有所言說，無能制者。
yú bǎi qiān jié yǒu suǒ yán shuō， wú néng zhì zhě。
though he speak for a hundred thousand kalpas, none can restrain him.
所以者何？是名曰無盡之藏總持門也！
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ shì míng yuē wú jǐn zhī zàng zǒng chí mén yě！
Why? This is what is called the dharani gate of the inexhaustible treasury.
「假使菩薩逮斯持者，說無盡句，
「jiǎ shǐ pú sà dǎi sī chí zhě， shuō wú jǐn jù，
“If a bodhisattva attains this dharani, he speaks inexhaustible phrases,
善順於教，棄去來瑕，如應無猗，
shàn shùn yú jiào， qì qù lái xiá， rú yìng wú yī，
accords well with the teaching, casts off the flaws of past and future, and rests on nothing as is fitting;
莊嚴百千真妙之句，
zhuāng yán bǎi qiān zhēn miào zhī jù，
he adorns a hundred thousand true and wondrous phrases,
忍於本淨，將護不亂，
rěn yú běn jìng， jiāng hù bù luàn，
is patient in original purity, guards without confusion,
尊卑之義曉了平等，
zūn bēi zhī yì xiǎo liǎo píng děng，
and understands high and low alike in their equality;
光曜所有塵勞、瞋恚，
guāng yào suǒ yǒu chén láo、 chēn huì，
he illumines all defilement, toil and anger,
入一切行而順解脫，
rù yī qiè xíng ér shùn jiě tuō，
enters every practice and accords with liberation;
八萬四千諸根尋如所應，
bā wàn sì qiān zhū gēn xún rú suǒ yìng，
the eighty-four thousand faculties he meets at once as is fitting,
善講本性而為說法。
shàn jiǎng běn xìng ér wèi shuō fǎ。
and he expounds original nature well in preaching the Dharma.
不盡八難音聲諸法，亦無有盡，
bù jǐn bā nàn yīn shēng zhū fǎ， yì wú yǒu jǐn，
The dharmas of sound amid the eight difficulties are not exhausted, nor is there any exhausting;
及譬喻慧三世無盡，
jí pì yù huì sān shì wú jǐn，
the wisdom of parables through the three times is inexhaustible.
及報應果願可盡耶？
jí bào yìng guǒ yuàn kě jǐn yé？
Can the fruits of recompense and the vows be exhausted?
經典順普可盡耶？
jīng diǎn shùn pǔ kě jǐn yé？
Can the universal accord of the scriptures be exhausted?
心之所入可盡耶？
xīn zhī suǒ rù kě jǐn yé？
Can what the mind enters be exhausted?
因緣愚跡可盡耶？
yīn yuán yú jī kě jǐn yé？
Can the traces of folly in causes and conditions be exhausted?
順在愛欲可盡耶？
shùn zài ài yù kě jǐn yé？
Can what accords with craving and desire be exhausted?
發于所持可盡耶？
fā yú suǒ chí kě jǐn yé？
Can what is roused from the dharani be exhausted?
說乘所處可盡耶？
shuō chéng suǒ chù kě jǐn yé？
Can the ground of the vehicles preached be exhausted?
分別法處可盡耶？
fēn bié fǎ chù kě jǐn yé？
Can the discernment of the places of dharmas be exhausted?
深妙雜句可盡耶？
shēn miào zá jù kě jǐn yé？
Can the profound and subtle varied phrases be exhausted?
至於究竟可盡耶？
zhì yú jiū jìng kě jǐn yé？
Can what reaches the ultimate be exhausted?
逆順之言可盡耶？
nì shùn zhī yán kě jǐn yé？
Can words of contradiction and accord be exhausted?
名字之訓可盡耶？
míng zì zhī xùn kě jǐn yé？
Can the glossing of names be exhausted?
歎佛法眾可盡耶？
tàn fó fǎ zhòng kě jǐn yé？
Can praise of the Buddha, Dharma and Sangha be exhausted?
說正諦可盡耶？
shuō zhèng dì kě jǐn yé？
Can the preaching of the right truth be exhausted?
佛道法品可盡耶？
fó dào fǎ pǐn kě jǐn yé？
Can the chapters of the Dharma of the buddha path be exhausted?
罪福所應可盡耶？
zuì fú suǒ yìng kě jǐn yé？
Can the recompense of sin and merit be exhausted?
講度無極可盡耶？」
jiǎng dù wú jí kě jǐn yé？」
Can the expounding of the perfections without limit be exhausted?”
佛語龍王：「是名曰所說無盡故，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「shì míng yuē suǒ shuō wú jǐn gù，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “Because what is spoken is thus inexhaustible,
號無盡法藏為總持門也！」
hào wú jǐn fǎ zàng wéi zǒng chí mén yě！」
it is named the inexhaustible Dharma treasury, the gate of dharani.”
佛說海龍王經卷第一
fó shuō hǎi lóng wáng jīng juǎn dì yī
Here ends Volume One of the Sutra Spoken by the Buddha on the Sea Dragon King.
總持品第五
zǒng chí pǐn dì wǔ
Chapter Five: Dharani
佛告龍王：「有四事法無盡之教、無盡之藏，
fó gào lóng wáng： 「yǒu sì shì fǎ wú jǐn zhī jiào、 wú jǐn zhī zàng，
The Buddha told the Dragon King: “There are four dharmas that are the inexhaustible teaching and the inexhaustible treasury,
為總持也。何等四？
wéi zǒng chí yě。 hé děng sì？
and they are dharani. Which four?
分別無盡，慧無盡，
fēn bié wú jǐn， huì wú jǐn，
Inexhaustible discernment; inexhaustible wisdom;
明智無盡，總持辯才無盡；是為四。
míng zhì wú jǐn， zǒng chí biàn cái wú jǐn； shì wéi sì。
inexhaustible clear knowing; inexhaustible eloquence of dharani. These are the four.
復有四事難攝無盡之藏，為總持也。何等四？
fù yǒu sì shì nán shè wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí yě。 hé děng sì？
There are four more, hard to grasp, that are the inexhaustible treasury and dharani. Which four?
其性難攝，道心難攝，
qí xìng nán shè， dào xīn nán shè，
Its nature is hard to grasp; the mind of the Way is hard to grasp;
入法難攝，入眾生行難攝；是為四。
rù fǎ nán shè， rù zhòng shēng xíng nán shè； shì wéi sì。
entry into the Dharma is hard to grasp; entry into beings' conduct is hard to grasp. These are the four.
復有四堅固要無盡之藏，為總持也。何等四？
fù yǒu sì jiān gù yào wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí yě。 hé děng sì？
There are four more firm essentials that are the inexhaustible treasury and dharani. Which four?
所願堅固，奉行堅固，
suǒ yuàn jiān gù， fèng xíng jiān gù，
Firmness of vow; firmness of practice;
立忍堅固，度於因緣所造堅固；是為四。
lì rěn jiān gù， dù yú yīn yuán suǒ zào jiān gù； shì wéi sì。
firmness in establishing patience; firmness in passing beyond what causes and conditions have made. These are the four.
復有四所說無盡之藏，為總持也。何等四？
fù yǒu sì suǒ shuō wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí yě。 hé děng sì？
There are four more things preached that are the inexhaustible treasury and dharani. Which four?
講諸至誠，講諸緣起，
jiǎng zhū zhì chéng， jiǎng zhū yuán qǐ，
Expounding utter sincerity; expounding dependent arising;
講眾生行，講諸乘本無慧；是為四。
jiǎng zhòng shēng xíng， jiǎng zhū chéng běn wú huì； shì wéi sì。
expounding beings' conduct; expounding the wisdom of original nonbeing in all the vehicles. These are the four.
復有四光無盡之藏，為總持也。何等四？
fù yǒu sì guāng wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí yě。 hé děng sì？
There are four more lights that are the inexhaustible treasury and dharani. Which four?
照于法界，照于智慧，
zhào yú fǎ jiè， zhào yú zhì huì，
Illumining the dharma-realm; illumining wisdom;
照于慧明，照于如應之所說法；是為四。
zhào yú huì míng， zhào yú rú yìng zhī suǒ shuō fǎ； shì wéi sì。
illumining the brightness of wisdom; illumining the Dharma preached as befits. These are the four.
復有四上曜無盡之藏，為總持也。何等四？
fù yǒu sì shàng yào wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí yě。 hé děng sì？
There are four more supreme radiances that are the inexhaustible treasury and dharani. Which four?
精進為上，禁戒修行勤力為上，
jīng jìn wéi shàng， jìn jiè xiū xíng qín lì wéi shàng，
Vigor is supreme; diligent effort in precepts and practice is supreme;
求積功德為上，合集求慧為上；是為四。
qiú jī gōng dé wéi shàng， hé jí qiú huì wéi shàng； shì wéi sì。
seeking to amass merit is supreme; gathering and seeking wisdom is supreme. These are the four.
「復有四無窮無盡之藏，為總持也。何等四？
「fù yǒu sì wú qióng wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí yě。 hé děng sì？
“There are four more without end that are the inexhaustible treasury and dharani. Which four?
求諸度無極而無窮極，
qiú zhū dù wú jí ér wú qióng jí，
Seeking the perfections without limit, and that without end;
不厭生死而無窮極，
bù yàn shēng sǐ ér wú qióng jí，
not wearying of birth and death, and that without end;
開化度人而無窮極，
kāi huà dù rén ér wú qióng jí，
transforming and delivering people, and that without end;
求諸通慧而無窮極；是為四。
qiú zhū tōng huì ér wú qióng jí； shì wéi sì。
seeking penetrating wisdom, and that without end. These are the four.
復有四無厭無盡之藏，為總持也。何等四？
fù yǒu sì wú yàn wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí yě。 hé děng sì？
There are four more without weariness that are the inexhaustible treasury and dharani. Which four?
佛前聽經而無厭足，
fó qián tīng jīng ér wú yàn zú，
Hearing the sutras before the Buddha without ever having enough;
為人說經而無厭足，
wèi rén shuō jīng ér wú yàn zú，
preaching the sutras to others without ever having enough;
求諸德本而無厭足，
qiú zhū dé běn ér wú yàn zú，
seeking roots of virtue without ever having enough;
供養如來而無厭足；是為四。
gòng yǎng rú lái ér wú yàn zú； shì wéi sì。
making offerings to the Tathagata without ever having enough. These are the four.
復有四無能勝者無盡之藏，為總持也。何等四？
fù yǒu sì wú néng shèng zhě wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí yě。 hé děng sì？
There are four more that none can overcome, the inexhaustible treasury and dharani. Which four?
一切塵勞亦無能勝，
yī qiè chén láo yì wú néng shèng，
No defilement or toil can overcome it;
一切諸魔亦不能勝，
yī qiè zhū mó yì bù néng shèng，
no host of maras can overcome it;
諸外異道亦不能勝，
zhū wài yì dào yì bù néng shèng，
no outsider or heterodox path can overcome it;
一切怨敵亦不能勝；是為四。
yī qiè yuàn dí yì bù néng shèng； shì wéi sì。
no foe or adversary can overcome it. These are the four.
復有四無習無盡之藏，為總持也。何等四？
fù yǒu sì wú xí wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí yě。 hé děng sì？
There are four more not cultivated that are the inexhaustible treasury and dharani. Which four?
不習聲聞、緣覺之乘，
bù xí shēng wén、 yuán jué zhī chéng，
He does not cultivate the vehicles of the sravaka and pratyekabuddha;
不習一切供養之利，
bù xí yī qiè gòng yǎng zhī lì，
he does not cultivate the gain that comes from offerings;
不習一切諸所著求，
bù xí yī qiè zhū suǒ zhuó qiú，
he does not cultivate any attached seeking;
不習一切諸凡夫行；是為四。
bù xí yī qiè zhū fán fū xíng； shì wéi sì。
he does not cultivate the conduct of ordinary beings. These are the four.
復有四無得無盡之藏，為總持也。何等四？
fù yǒu sì wú dé wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí yě。 hé děng sì？
There are four more without gaining that are the inexhaustible treasury and dharani. Which four?
不得所生，
bù dé suǒ shēng，
He does not apprehend what is born;
不得開化惡戒之人，
bù dé kāi huà è jiè zhī rén，
he does not apprehend the converting of those of evil precepts;
不得說經在於有為為上大乘，
bù dé shuō jīng zài yú yǒu wéi wéi shàng dà chéng，
he does not apprehend preaching the sutras, in the conditioned, as the supreme Great Vehicle;
不得乞求；是為四。
bù dé qǐ qiú； shì wéi sì。
he does not apprehend begging. These are the four.
復有四力無盡之藏，為總持。何等四？
fù yǒu sì lì wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí。 hé děng sì？
There are four more powers that are the inexhaustible treasury and dharani. Which four?
忍力——忍於一切所作，眾惡
rěn lì—— rěn yú yī qiè suǒ zuò， zhòng è
The power of patience — bearing all that is done and every evil;
慧力——蠲除一切眾生疑結，
huì lì—— juān chú yī qiè zhòng shēng yí jié，
the power of wisdom — clearing away the knots of doubt in all beings;
神通力——見一切眾生心之所念，
shén tōng lì—— jiàn yī qiè zhòng shēng xīn zhī suǒ niàn，
the power of spiritual penetration — seeing what all beings hold in mind;
善權力——為一切人如應說法；是為四。
shàn quán lì—— wèi yī qiè rén rú yìng shuō fǎ； shì wéi sì。
the power of skillful means — preaching the Dharma to all as befits them. These are the four.
復有四大藏無盡之藏，為總持。何等四？
fù yǒu sì dà zàng wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí。 hé děng sì？
There are four more great treasuries that are the inexhaustible treasury and dharani. Which four?
不自侵欺而斷三寶，是則大藏；
bù zì qīn qī ér duàn sān bǎo， shì zé dà zàng；
Not violating or deceiving himself so as to cut off the Three Jewels — this is a great treasury;
入於無量之法，是則大藏；
rù yú wú liàng zhī fǎ， shì zé dà zàng；
entering the immeasurable Dharma — this is a great treasury;
得一切心，隨其所志，是則大藏；
dé yī qiè xīn， suí qí suǒ zhì， shì zé dà zàng；
knowing every mind and following its aim — this is a great treasury;
慧等如空，是則大藏；是為四。
huì děng rú kōng， shì zé dà zàng； shì wéi sì。
wisdom equal to space — this is a great treasury. These are the four.
復有四無極無盡之藏，為總持。何等四？
fù yǒu sì wú jí wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí。 hé děng sì？
There are four more without limit that are the inexhaustible treasury and dharani. Which four?
博聞無極，智慧無極，
bó wén wú jí， zhì huì wú jí，
Wide learning without limit; wisdom without limit;
所願無極，順眾生說法無極；是為四。
suǒ yuàn wú jí， shùn zhòng shēng shuō fǎ wú jí； shì wéi sì。
vows without limit; preaching the Dharma in accord with beings without limit. These are the four.
菩薩復有四事不自侵至無盡之藏，
pú sà fù yǒu sì shì bù zì qīn zhì wú jǐn zhī zàng，
A bodhisattva has four more ways of not violating himself, reaching the inexhaustible treasury,
為總持。何等四？
wéi zǒng chí。 hé děng sì？
which is dharani. Which four?
說法不自侵，說至誠不自侵，
shuō fǎ bù zì qīn， shuō zhì chéng bù zì qīn，
In preaching the Dharma he does not violate himself; in speaking sincerely he does not violate himself;
順法行不自侵，得至道極不自侵；是謂四。
shùn fǎ xíng bù zì qīn， dé zhì dào jí bù zì qīn； shì wèi sì。
in practicing according to the Dharma he does not violate himself; in reaching the utmost of the Way he does not violate himself. These are the four.
復有四事得無所畏無盡之藏，為總持。何等四？
fù yǒu sì shì dé wú suǒ wèi wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí。 hé děng sì？
There are four more that gain fearlessness, the inexhaustible treasury and dharani. Which four?
不畏惡趣，不畏眾會，
bù wèi è qù， bù wèi zhòng huì，
He does not fear the evil destinies; he does not fear assemblies;
不畏決疑，不畏失佛道；
bù wèi jué yí， bù wèi shī fó dào；
he does not fear resolving doubts; he does not fear losing the buddha path.
是為四無盡之藏為總持也。」
shì wéi sì wú jǐn zhī zàng wéi zǒng chí yě。」
These are the four inexhaustible treasuries that are dharani.”
佛告龍王：「是無盡藏總持，說德無量，
fó gào lóng wáng： 「shì wú jìn zàng zǒng chí， shuō dé wú liàng，
The Buddha told the Dragon King: “This dharani of the inexhaustible treasury — its virtue is immeasurable to tell.
入無極慧，集菩薩行，
rù wú jí huì， jí pú sà xíng，
It enters boundless wisdom and gathers the bodhisattva's conduct;
所可由慧，光曜莊嚴菩薩所求，
suǒ kě yóu huì， guāng yào zhuāng yán pú sà suǒ qiú，
by the wisdom it proceeds from, it illumines and adorns what the bodhisattva seeks.
菩薩財寶所入法藏，
pú sà cái bǎo suǒ rù fǎ zàng，
It is the bodhisattva's wealth, the Dharma treasury he enters;
入總持門分別言教，
rù zǒng chí mén fēn bié yán jiào，
entering the gate of dharani he discerns the spoken teaching,
嚴身口意，得淨諸國，
yán shēn kǒu yì， dé jìng zhū guó，
adorns body, speech and mind, and purifies the buddha-lands;
合集自在，護念正道，
hé jí zì zài， hù niàn zhèng dào，
he gathers sovereign freedom and guards the right Way in mind;
入眾生敷慧化導正法，
rù zhòng shēng fū huì huà dǎo zhèng fǎ，
entering beings, he spreads wisdom and leads them by the true Dharma;
力精進具諸度無極，
lì jīng jìn jù zhū dù wú jí，
with vigorous strength he perfects the perfections without limit,
嚴淨道場，逮諸佛法，
yán jìng dào chǎng， dǎi zhū fó fǎ，
adorns and purifies the bodhimanda (place of practice), and attains the dharmas of the buddhas.
是謂無盡之藏總持。
shì wèi wú jǐn zhī zàng zǒng chí。
This is called the dharani of the inexhaustible treasury.
其有文名字號之數，及法諸數，
qí yǒu wén míng zì hào zhī shù， jí fǎ zhū shù，
Whatever there is of letters, names, designations and their number, and of the number of dharmas,
遊于正法，皆來歸斯無盡之藏，為總持也。
yóu yú zhèng fǎ， jiē lái guī sī wú jǐn zhī zàng， wéi zǒng chí yě。
moving within the true Dharma, all comes home to this inexhaustible treasury, which is dharani.
菩薩入斯，於諸文字無所分別。
pú sà rù sī， yú zhū wén zì wú suǒ fēn bié。
A bodhisattva who enters it makes no discrimination among letters.
諸法清白，不壞本淨故。
zhū fǎ qīng bái， bù huài běn jìng gù。
Dharmas are pure and white, because original purity is not destroyed.
樂一切法，不侵樂法故。
lè yī qiè fǎ， bù qīn lè fǎ gù。
He delights in all dharmas, because he does not violate the dharma of delight.
究竟諸法，所志諸法亦無侵欺故。
jiū jìng zhū fǎ， suǒ zhì zhū fǎ yì wú qīn qī gù。
He fathoms all dharmas, because in the dharmas he aims at there is no violation or deceit.
一切眼法，不侵諸法明故。
yī qiè yǎn fǎ， bù qīn zhū fǎ míng gù。
All dharmas of the eye — because he does not violate the brightness of dharmas.
諸法假號，不侵會法故。
zhū fǎ jiǎ hào， bù qīn huì fǎ gù。
Dharmas are provisional labels, because he does not violate the dharma of assembling.
以逮諸法，不侵行精進故。
yǐ dǎi zhū fǎ， bù qīn xíng jīng jìn gù。
He attains all dharmas, because he does not violate the practice of vigor.
諸法調定，於柔順法無所侵故。
zhū fǎ diào dìng， yú róu shùn fǎ wú suǒ qīn gù。
Dharmas are tamed and settled, because he violates nothing in the pliant dharma.
諸法說之，無侵光曜焚燒諸法，
zhū fǎ shuō zhī， wú qīn guāng yào fén shāo zhū fǎ，
Dharmas are spoken, without violating the radiance that burns away dharmas,
於無起法而無侵故。
yú wú qǐ fǎ ér wú qīn gù。
because in the dharma of non-arising there is no violation.
信一切法，於所好法亦無侵故。
xìn yī qiè fǎ， yú suǒ hào fǎ yì wú qīn gù。
He has faith in all dharmas, because in the dharma he loves there is no violation.
說一切法，於諸言教無所侵故。
shuō yī qiè fǎ， yú zhū yán jiào wú suǒ qīn gù。
He preaches all dharmas, because in the spoken teachings there is no violation.
諸法本無，於無趣法而無侵故。
zhū fǎ běn wú， yú wú qù fǎ ér wú qīn gù。
Dharmas are originally nonexistent, because in the dharma without destination there is no violation.
諸法審諦，等三世法亦無侵故。
zhū fǎ shěn dì， děng sān shì fǎ yì wú qīn gù。
Dharmas are truly discerned, because in the equality of the three times there is no violation.
諸法常住，於不動法亦不侵故。
zhū fǎ cháng zhù， yú bù dòng fǎ yì bù qīn gù。
Dharmas abide constantly, because in the unmoving dharma there is no violation.
諸法有哀，隨本所樂而為現法故。
zhū fǎ yǒu āi， suí běn suǒ lè ér wèi xiàn fǎ gù。
Dharmas hold compassion, because he manifests the Dharma according to what each originally delights in.
諸法悉等，說無差特故。
zhū fǎ xī děng， shuō wú chā tè gù。
Dharmas are wholly equal, because he preaches without distinction.
諸法求跡，示現諸法至平等故。
zhū fǎ qiú jī， shì xiàn zhū fǎ zhì píng děng gù。
Dharmas are sought by their traces, because he shows that dharmas arrive at equality.
諸法所至，示現入深道門故。
zhū fǎ suǒ zhì， shì xiàn rù shēn dào mén gù。
Dharmas have their destination, because he shows the entry into the profound gate of the Way.
諸法至力，現諸上法故。
zhū fǎ zhì lì， xiàn zhū shàng fǎ gù。
Dharmas reach their power, because he manifests the supreme dharmas.
諸法愚冥，為現智明故。
zhū fǎ yú míng， wèi xiàn zhì míng gù。
Dharmas are dark with folly, because he manifests the brightness of knowing.
懷來諸法，示現諸法無所亡失故。
huái lái zhū fǎ， shì xiàn zhū fǎ wú suǒ wáng shī gù。
He embraces all dharmas, because he shows that no dharma is ever lost.
總持諸法，示現諸法為無盡故。
zǒng chí zhū fǎ， shì xiàn zhū fǎ wéi wú jǐn gù。
He holds all dharmas, because he shows that dharmas are inexhaustible.
諸法寂然，現憺怕故。
zhū fǎ jì rán， xiàn dàn pò gù。
Dharmas are still, because he manifests tranquillity.
諸法虛空，而為示現廣普之法故。
zhū fǎ xū kōng， ér wèi shì xiàn guǎng pǔ zhī fǎ gù。
Dharmas are empty space, because he manifests the vast and universal Dharma.
諸法無明，現癡本故。
zhū fǎ wú míng， xiàn chī běn gù。
Dharmas are ignorance, because he manifests the root of delusion.
諸法悉住，現所立處，
zhū fǎ xī zhù， xiàn suǒ lì chù，
Dharmas all abide, because he manifests where they are established;
故諸法入慧現離癡法故。
gù zhū fǎ rù huì xiàn lí chī fǎ gù。
and dharmas enter wisdom, because he manifests the dharma free of delusion.
諸法人也，而為示現分別諸法故。
zhū fǎ rén yě， ér wèi shì xiàn fēn bié zhū fǎ gù。
Dharmas are the person, because he manifests the discernment of dharmas.
諸法離有，而為示現離所有法故。
zhū fǎ lí yǒu， ér wèi shì xiàn lí suǒ yǒu fǎ gù。
Dharmas are apart from existence, because he manifests the dharma apart from all possession.
諸法有難，而為示現諸法瑕穢故。
zhū fǎ yǒu nán， ér wèi shì xiàn zhū fǎ xiá huì gù。
Dharmas have hardship, because he manifests the flaws and stains of dharmas.
諸法常念，而為示現宿命事故。
zhū fǎ cháng niàn， ér wèi shì xiàn sù mìng shì gù。
Dharmas are constantly recollected, because he manifests the affairs of former lives.
諸法有緣，示現諸法而有侵故。
zhū fǎ yǒu yuán， shì xiàn zhū fǎ ér yǒu qīn gù。
Dharmas have conditions, because he shows that in dharmas there is violation.
諸法入志，而為示現寂諸亂故。
zhū fǎ rù zhì， ér wèi shì xiàn jì zhū luàn gù。
Dharmas enter the aim, because he manifests the stilling of all disturbance.
諸法極重，而為示現無所動法故。
zhū fǎ jí zhòng， ér wèi shì xiàn wú suǒ dòng fǎ gù。
Dharmas are utterly weighty, because he manifests the dharma that nothing moves.
諸法住處，而為示現眾法界處故。
zhū fǎ zhù chù， ér wèi shì xiàn zhòng fǎ jiè chù gù。
Dharmas have their dwelling, because he manifests the ground of the whole dharma-realm.
諸法導師，而為示現審諦之法故。
zhū fǎ dǎo shī， ér wèi shì xiàn shěn dì zhī fǎ gù。
Dharmas are the guiding teacher, because he manifests the dharma of true discernment.
諸法致果，而為示現志無所念故。
zhū fǎ zhì guǒ， ér wèi shì xiàn zhì wú suǒ niàn gù。
Dharmas bring forth fruit, because he manifests an aim that conceives nothing.
諸法唯陰，而為示現蠲除五陰諸法苦患故。
zhū fǎ wéi yīn， ér wèi shì xiàn juān chú wǔ yīn zhū fǎ kǔ huàn gù。
Dharmas are but the aggregates, because he manifests the clearing away of the suffering of the five aggregates.
諸法生死，示現諸法無塵故。
zhū fǎ shēng sǐ， shì xiàn zhū fǎ wú chén gù。
Dharmas are birth and death, because he shows that dharmas are without stain.
諸法寂空，示現諸法無所猗故。
zhū fǎ jì kōng， shì xiàn zhū fǎ wú suǒ yī gù。
Dharmas are still and empty, because he shows that dharmas lean on nothing.
諸法如固，示現諸法斷眾固故。
zhū fǎ rú gù， shì xiàn zhū fǎ duàn zhòng gù gù。
Dharmas are as if solid, because he shows that dharmas sever all solidity.
諸法寂滅，示現諸法斷因緣故。」
zhū fǎ jì miè， shì xiàn zhū fǎ duàn yīn yuán gù。」
Dharmas are quiescent, because he shows that dharmas sever causes and conditions.”
佛語龍王：「是名曰文字緣會無盡藏總持。
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「shì míng yuē wén zì yuán huì wú jìn zàng zǒng chí。
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “This is called the dharani of the inexhaustible treasury of the conjunction of letters and conditions.
菩薩得是，分別一切文字所興。
pú sà dé shì， fēn bié yī qiè wén zì suǒ xìng。
A bodhisattva who gains it discerns the arising of every letter.
譬如文字而不可盡，
pì rú wén zì ér bù kě jǐn，
Just as letters cannot be exhausted,
諸法所說不可盡，亦復如是！
zhū fǎ suǒ shuō bù kě jǐn， yì fù rú shì！
so what is preached of dharmas cannot be exhausted.
譬如文字亦不從身出、不從心出，
pì rú wén zì yì bù cóng shēn chū、 bù cóng xīn chū，
Just as letters come forth neither from the body nor from the mind,
諸法如是，不可知處，
zhū fǎ rú shì， bù kě zhī chù，
so are dharmas: their place cannot be known,
不住在身，不住在心。
bú zhù zài shēn， bú zhù zài xīn。
they abide neither in the body nor in the mind.
譬如文字無所依倚，
pì rú wén zì wú suǒ yī yǐ，
Just as letters lean upon nothing,
而求解說塵勞之事，亦無所淨。
ér qiú jiě shuō chén láo zhī shì， yì wú suǒ jìng。
and though one seeks to explain the matter of defilement, there is nothing purified.
菩薩已得無盡藏總持，
pú sà yǐ dé wú jìn zàng zǒng chí，
A bodhisattva who has gained the dharani of the inexhaustible treasury,
雖說塵勞，不著塵垢，究竟本淨。
suī shuō chén láo， bù zhuó chén gòu， jiū jìng běn jìng。
though he speaks of defilement, is not stained by it, and fathoms original purity.
譬如文字不合在身，然為他人有所解說；
pì rú wén zì bù hé zài shēn， rán wèi tā rén yǒu suǒ jiě shuō；
Just as letters are not joined to the body, yet by them one explains things to others,
諸法如是，有所發起教心清淨。
zhū fǎ rú shì， yǒu suǒ fā qǐ jiào xīn qīng jìng。
so are dharmas: what they rouse is a teaching whose mind is pure.
如文字有所說時無所至湊，
rú wén zì yǒu suǒ shuō shí wú suǒ zhì còu，
As letters, when something is said, go nowhere and gather nowhere,
無所言時不處在內；
wú suǒ yán shí bù chù zài nèi；
and when nothing is said do not reside within,
諸法如是，假使說時無所至到，
zhū fǎ rú shì， jiǎ shǐ shuō shí wú suǒ zhì dào，
so are dharmas: when spoken they arrive nowhere,
設不說時不積在內。
shè bù shuō shí bù jī zài nèi。
and when unspoken they do not accumulate within.
如文字無色無見而現在外；
rú wén zì wú sè wú jiàn ér xiàn zài wài；
As letters are formless and invisible yet appear outwardly,
諸色如是，無色無見，
zhū sè rú shì， wú sè wú jiàn，
so are forms: formless and invisible,
由心因緣而有退轉。
yóu xīn yīn yuán ér yǒu tuì zhuǎn。
and by the causes and conditions of mind they turn and change.
如文字虛自在寂寞，悉以怳惚無作字者；
rú wén zì xū zì zài jì mò， xī yǐ huǎng hū wú zuò zì zhě；
As letters are void, free and still, wholly indistinct, with no one making them,
諸法如是，虛靜寂寞，莫有造作。
zhū fǎ rú shì， xū jìng jì mò， mò yǒu zào zuò。
so are dharmas: void, quiet and still, with nothing fabricating them.
如文字不出染污、瞋恚、愚癡，
rú wén zì bù chū rǎn wū、 chēn huì、 yú chī，
As letters do not give rise to defilement, anger or folly,
又因文字而有音教；
yòu yīn wén zì ér yǒu yīn jiào；
and yet through letters there is a spoken teaching,
諸法如是，不出染污、貪騃之惑，
zhū fǎ rú shì， bù chū rǎn wū、 tān ái zhī huò，
so are dharmas: they do not give rise to defilement or the confusion of greed and stupidity,
由從想念起婬、怒、癡。
yóu cóng xiǎng niàn qǐ yín、 nù、 chī。
yet from conceiving and recollecting arise lust, anger and delusion.
如文字因諸貪緣說得果證，
rú wén zì yīn zhū tān yuán shuō dé guǒ zhèng，
As letters, by reason of grasping at conditions, are said to attain fruit and realization,
文字無得亦無有證；
wén zì wú dé yì wú yǒu zhèng；
while letters gain nothing and realize nothing,
諸法如是，因其緣對說有果證，
zhū fǎ rú shì， yīn qí yuán duì shuō yǒu guǒ zhèng，
so are dharmas: through the pairing of conditions they are said to have fruit and realization,
計於本法無果無證。
jì yú běn fǎ wú guǒ wú zhèng。
yet reckoned in the original dharma there is neither fruit nor realization.
譬如諸法無不因字；
pì rú zhū fǎ wú bù yīn zì；
Just as there is no dharma that does not depend on letters,
諸行如是，所有諸法皆由佛道。」
zhū xíng rú shì， suǒ yǒu zhū fǎ jiē yóu fó dào。」
so it is with all practices: every dharma there is proceeds from the buddha path.”
總持身品第六
zǒng chí shēn pǐn dì liù
Chapter Six: The Body of Dharani
佛語龍王：「菩薩已住無盡藏，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「pú sà yǐ zhù wú jìn zàng，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “A bodhisattva who abides in the inexhaustible treasury
而以文字求於佛道。
ér yǐ wén zì qiú yú fó dào。
seeks the buddha path by means of letters.
總持文字，力也；
zǒng chí wén zì， lì yě；
Holding letters is its strength;
歸趣文字，身也；
guī qù wén zì， shēn yě；
resorting to letters is its body;
滅盡像，色也；
miè jǐn xiàng， sè yě；
the extinction of images is its form;
入法門，頂也；
rù fǎ mén， dǐng yě；
entering the Dharma gate is its crown;
觀瞻，額也；
guān zhān， é yě；
gazing is its brow;
慧眼，眼也；
huì yǎn， yǎn yě；
the eye of wisdom is its eyes;
天耳，耳也；
tiān ěr， ěr yě；
the deva ear is its ears;
說名字，鼻也；
shuō míng zì， bí yě；
the speaking of names is its nose;
制亂意眉間，闕庭也；
zhì luàn yì méi jiān， quē tíng yě；
restraining the disturbed mind between the brows is the space above the nose;
攝一切心，面也；
shè yī qiè xīn， miàn yě；
gathering in every mind is its face;
解喻一切可眾生心，舌根也；
jiě yù yī qiè kě zhòng shēng xīn， shé gēn yě；
explaining so as to reach the minds of all beings is its tongue;
調定其心，齒也；
diào dìng qí xīn， chǐ yě；
taming and settling the mind is its teeth;
師子觀奮迅，髭也；
shī zǐ guān fèn xùn， zī yě；
the lion's rousing gaze is its whiskers;
藏匿空語，脣也；
cáng nì kōng yǔ， chún yě；
concealing empty talk is its lips;
觀一切法，咽也；
guān yī qiè fǎ， yàn yě；
contemplating all dharmas is its throat;
勉出眾生令其歡悅，肩也；
miǎn chū zhòng shēng lìng qí huān yuè， jiān yě；
drawing beings forth and gladdening them is its shoulders;
端正所謂，脾也；
duān zhèng suǒ wèi， pí yě；
rectitude in what is said is its spleen;
察諸法等，腹也；
chá zhū fǎ děng， fù yě；
examining the equality of dharmas is its belly;
入於深門，臍也；
rù yú shēn mén， qí yě；
entering the profound gate is its navel;
入左右路，掌也；
rù zuǒ yòu lù， zhǎng yě；
entering the left and right roads is its palms;
合會諸法，臂也；
hé huì zhū fǎ， bì yě；
joining dharmas together is its arms;
十善之句為善救護，指也；
shí shàn zhī jù wéi shàn jiù hù， zhǐ yě；
the phrases of the ten wholesome deeds as good protection are its fingers;
清淨法銅，爪也；
qīng jìng fǎ tóng， zhǎo yě；
the pure metal of the Dharma is its nails;
來致虛無之念，脇也；
lái zhì xū wú zhī niàn， xié yě；
bringing forth thought of the void is its flanks;
次第講法，脊也；
cì dì jiǎng fǎ， jí yě；
expounding the Dharma in order is its spine;
說不侵時無所為度，尻也；
shuō bù qīn shí wú suǒ wéi dù， kāo yě；
speaking without violation, when there is nothing to be delivered, is its haunches;
具足寂觀，髕也；
jù zú jì guān， bìn yě；
perfecting still contemplation is its kneecaps;
趣審諦法，膝也；
qù shěn dì fǎ， xī yě；
inclining toward the dharma of true discernment is its knees;
曉知一切，踹也；
xiǎo zhī yī qiè， chuài yě；
understanding everything is its calves;
心意寂然，足趺也；
xīn yì jì rán， zú fū yě；
stillness of mind is its insteps;
遊到十方，足心也；
yóu dào shí fāng， zú xīn yě；
roaming to the ten directions is the soles of its feet;
次第說諦，步也；
cì dì shuō dì， bù yě；
preaching the truths in order is its stride;
知羞慚恥，衣也；
zhī xiū cán chǐ， yī yě；
A sense of shame is its robe;
法鬘莊嚴，傅飾也；
fǎ mán zhuāng yán， fù shì yě；
the adornment of the Dharma garland is its ornament;
法華若干，臥具也；
fǎ huā ruò gān， wò jù yě；
the manifold flowers of Dharma are its bedding;
說種種法，枕也；
shuō zhǒng zhǒng fǎ， zhěn yě；
preaching dharmas of every kind is its pillow;
不瞋不諍，塗香也；
bù chēn bù zhēng， tú xiāng yě；
freedom from anger and strife is its anointing perfume;
所行如應無所不了，雜香也；
suǒ xíng rú yìng wú suǒ bù liǎo， zá xiāng yě；
conduct that befits and understands all is its blended incense;
入深戒，說香也；
rù shēn jiè， shuō xiāng yě；
entering the profound precepts is its fragrance of speech;
於諸法自在，眷屬也；
yú zhū fǎ zì zài， juàn shǔ yě；
sovereign freedom in all dharmas is its retinue;
嗟歎梵跡，則親友也；
jiē tàn fàn jī， zé qīn yǒu yě；
extolling the traces of the holy life is its friends;
得安隱眾，知識也；
dé ān yǐn zhòng， zhī shí yě；
bringing the multitude to peace is its acquaintances;
斷諸結縛開化眾人，則親暱也；
duàn zhū jié fù kāi huà zhòng rén， zé qīn nì yě；
severing bonds and converting people is its intimates;
曉了諸事，家室也；
xiǎo liǎo zhū shì， jiā shì yě；
understanding all affairs is its household;
其心清淨，母也；
qí xīn qīng jìng， mǔ yě；
purity of mind is its mother;
一切巧便無所依信，慧父也；
yī qiè qiǎo biàn wú suǒ yī xìn， huì fù yě；
every skillful means, relying on nothing, is its father of wisdom;
諸通慧心，從等也；
zhū tōng huì xīn， cóng děng yě；
the mind of penetrating wisdom is its companions;
施度無極，漿食也；
shī dù wú jí， jiāng shí yě；
the perfection of giving is its drink and food;
戒度無極，泰安也；
jiè dù wú jí， tài ān yě；
the perfection of precepts is its ease;
忍度無極，莊嚴也；
rěn dù wú jí， zhuāng yán yě；
the perfection of patience is its adornment;
精進度無極，作善剋辦也；
jīng jìn dù wú jí， zuò shàn kè bàn yě；
the perfection of vigor is its accomplishing of good;
一心度無極，飽滿也；
yī xīn dù wú jí， bǎo mǎn yě；
the perfection of single-mindedness is its satiety;
智度無極，隨時順也；
zhì dù wú jí， suí shí shùn yě；
the perfection of wisdom is its timely accord;
善權度無極，二句合義也；
shàn quán dù wú jí， èr jù hé yì yě；
the perfection of skillful means is the joining of two phrases in one meaning;
道品身支，黨也。
dào pǐn shēn zhī， dǎng yě。
the limbs of the Way as members of the body are its company.
講說至誠，未曾侵欺，
jiǎng shuō zhì chéng， wèi céng qīn qī，
Expounding with utter sincerity, never violating nor deceiving,
一切世間尊豪自由，於法自恣。」
yī qiè shì jiān zūn háo zì yóu， yú fǎ zì zì。」
honored and unhindered in all the world, he is free in the Dharma.”
佛語龍王：「是為無盡之藏總持，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「shì wéi wú jǐn zhī zàng zǒng chí，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “This is the dharani of the inexhaustible treasury,
無色像身也！
wú sè xiàng shēn yě！
a body without form or image.
其有菩薩，於是總持樂法之樂。
qí yǒu pú sà， yú shì zǒng chí lè fǎ zhī lè。
A bodhisattva herein delights in the joy of the Dharma.
譬如國王在於中宮；
pì rú guó wáng zài yú zhōng gōng；
He is like a king within his inner palace;
如天帝釋在須彌頂，威神巍巍；
rú tiān dì shì zài xū mí dǐng， wēi shén wēi wēi；
like Sakra on the summit of Sumeru, lofty in majesty;
如梵天尊豪自在；
rú fàn tiān zūn háo zì zài；
like Brahma, honored and sovereign;
如燕居阿須輪，難可制持；
rú yān jū ā xū lún， nán kě zhì chí；
like the asura Yanju, hard to restrain;
如海無邊，功德超殊；
rú hǎi wú biān， gōng dé chāo shū；
like the boundless ocean, surpassing in merit;
如寶大山，天所娛樂；
rú bǎo dà shān， tiān suǒ yú lè；
like a great mountain of jewels in which the devas take delight;
如父母獨有一子，愛重無極；
rú fù mǔ dú yǒu yī zi， ài zhòng wú jí；
like parents with an only child, boundless in love;
如月盛滿，眾星獨明，莫不稽首；
rú yuè shèng mǎn， zhòng xīng dú míng， mò bù qǐ shǒu；
like the full moon, alone bright among the stars, before which all bow;
如世尊為天、世人奮大光明；
rú shì zūn wéi tiān、 shì rén fèn dà guāng míng；
like the World-Honored One rousing great radiance for devas and men;
如日初出，光曜柔和；
rú rì chū chū， guāng yào róu hé；
like the sun newly risen, its light gentle and mild;
如孔雀在林樹間放妙音聲；
rú kǒng què zài lín shù jiān fàng miào yīn shēng；
like a peacock among the forest trees uttering its wondrous cry;
如師子在巖窟中服美飲食；
rú shī zǐ zài yán kū zhōng fú měi yǐn shí；
like a lion in its rocky cave taking fine food and drink;
如龍心意調和，以時澍雨；
rú lóng xīn yì diào hé， yǐ shí zhù yǔ；
like a dragon whose mind is harmonious, sending rain in season;
如轉輪王大法化國；
rú zhuǎn lún wáng dà fǎ huà guó；
like a wheel-turning king transforming his realm by the great Dharma;
如眾龍舞，動發雷電；
rú zhòng lóng wǔ， dòng fā léi diàn；
like a host of dragons dancing, stirring thunder and lightning;
如龍王已得自在，降大法雨；
rú lóng wáng yǐ dé zì zài， jiàng dà fǎ yǔ；
like a dragon king who has gained mastery, sending down the great rain of Dharma;
如天帝釋撫化一切諸外異道；
rú tiān dì shì fǔ huà yī qiè zhū wài yì dào；
like Sakra pacifying and converting all the heterodox paths;
如勇猛將摧伏嚴敵，除諸勞垢，降納眾魔；
rú yǒng měng jiāng cuī fú yán dí， chú zhū láo gòu， jiàng nà zhòng mó；
like a valiant general crushing a stern foe, removing toil and stain, subduing and receiving the hosts of Mara;
如水消火；如風靡草，
rú shuǐ xiāo huǒ； rú fēng mí cǎo，
like water quenching fire; like wind laying the grass low;
心計如地，開化明者，順化眾生；
xīn jì rú dì， kāi huà míng zhě， shùn huà zhòng shēng；
his mind reckoned like the earth, converting the discerning and transforming beings in accord;
如火焚草，皆忍苦樂；
rú huǒ fén cǎo， jiē rěn kǔ lè；
like fire burning grass, bearing alike both pain and pleasure;
如乳母養長者子，療治眾病，
rú rǔ mǔ yǎng zhǎng zhě zǐ， liáo zhì zhòng bìng，
like a nurse rearing an elder's son and curing every illness;
持心堅強，具眾人願；
chí xīn jiān qiáng， jù zhòng rén yuàn；
holding his mind firm and fulfilling the wishes of all;
如意珠王總持諸寶。」
rú yì zhū wáng zǒng chí zhū bǎo。」
like the wish-fulfilling jewel king that holds all treasures together.”
佛語龍王：「其有菩薩住無盡之藏總持門者，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「qí yǒu pú sà zhù wú jǐn zhī zàng zǒng chí mén zhě，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “A bodhisattva who abides in the dharani gate of the inexhaustible treasury
則可謂入佛之道場。
zé kě wèi rù fó zhī dào chǎng。
may be said to have entered the Buddha's bodhimanda (place of practice).
如大海含受眾寶、諸珍苑府，
rú dà hǎi hán shòu zhòng bǎo、 zhū zhēn yuàn fǔ，
As the great ocean holds and receives all jewels, a storehouse of every precious thing,
無盡之藏總持如是，
wú jǐn zhī zàng zǒng chí rú shì，
so is the dharani of the inexhaustible treasury:
包弘諸法道寶篋藏，
bāo hóng zhū fǎ dào bǎo qiè zàng，
it embraces and enlarges the casket-treasury of the Dharma's jewels of the Way,
如無數香篋令無量人恣意所欲。
rú wú shù xiāng qiè lìng wú liàng rén zì yì suǒ yù。
like countless caskets of incense that let immeasurable people take as they please.
菩薩已住無盡之藏總持門者，
pú sà yǐ zhù wú jǐn zhī zàng zǒng chí mén zhě，
A bodhisattva who abides in the dharani gate of the inexhaustible treasury
以真妙言開化一切，令各得所。
yǐ zhēn miào yán kāi huà yī qiè， lìng gè dé suǒ。
converts all beings with true and wondrous words, so that each obtains his own.
是總持者，入一切聲。
shì zǒng chí zhě， rù yī qiè shēng。
This dharani enters into every sound.
此土名道心者，
cǐ tǔ míng dào xīn zhě，
What in this land is called the mind of the Way
彼無盡世界佛一寶蓋如來國曰目前；
bǐ wú jǐn shì jiè fó yī bǎo gài rú lái guó yuē mù qián；
is called Before the Eyes in the land of the Tathagata One Jewel Canopy, in the world Inexhaustible;
此名諸通慧者，
cǐ míng zhū tōng huì zhě，
what here is called penetrating wisdom
彼超得度世界導龍如來佛國曰普達；
bǐ chāo dé dù shì jiè dǎo lóng rú lái fó guó yuē pǔ dá；
is called Universal Penetration in the buddha-land of the Tathagata Dragon Guide, in the world Surpassing Deliverance;
此名施度無極者，
cǐ míng shī dù wú jí zhě，
what here is called the perfection of giving
彼寂定世界吉祥如來佛國曰精氣；
bǐ jì dìng shì jiè jí xiáng rú lái fó guó yuē jīng qì；
is called Vital Essence in the buddha-land of the Tathagata Auspicious, in the world Still Concentration;
此名戒度無極者，
cǐ míng jiè dù wú jí zhě，
what here is called the perfection of precepts
彼無憂世界離憂如來佛國曰多安；
bǐ wú yōu shì jiè lí yōu rú lái fó guó yuē duō ān；
is called Much Peace in the buddha-land of the Tathagata Free of Sorrow, in the world Sorrowless;
此名忍度無極者，
cǐ míng rěn dù wú jí zhě，
what here is called the perfection of patience
彼無垢世界離垢如來佛國曰無盡句；
bǐ wú gòu shì jiè lí gòu rú lái fó guó yuē wú jǐn jù；
is called Inexhaustible Phrase in the buddha-land of the Tathagata Free of Defilement, in the world Undefiled;
此名進度無極者，
cǐ míng jìn dù wú jí zhě，
what here is called the perfection of vigor
彼普明世界無垢光如來佛國曰上度；
bǐ pǔ míng shì jiè wú gòu guāng rú lái fó guó yuē shàng dù；
is called Supreme Deliverance in the buddha-land of the Tathagata Undefiled Light, in the world Universal Brightness;
此名專度無極者，
cǐ míng zhuān dù wú jí zhě，
what here is called the perfection of single-mindedness
彼道御世界堅要如來佛國曰寂行；
bǐ dào yù shì jiè jiān yào rú lái fó guó yuē jì xíng；
is called Still Practice in the buddha-land of the Tathagata Firm Essential, in the world Guidance of the Way;
此名智度無極者，
cǐ míng zhì dù wú jí zhě，
what here is called the perfection of wisdom
彼陰雨世界雨王如來佛國曰清淨；
bǐ yīn yǔ shì jiè yǔ wáng rú lái fó guó yuē qīng jìng；
is called Purity in the buddha-land of the Tathagata Rain King, in the world Overcast Rain;
此名善權方便者，
cǐ míng shàn quán fāng biàn zhě，
what here is called skillful means
彼尊調世界離垢辟如來佛國曰隨習俗宜；
bǐ zūn diào shì jiè lí gòu pì rú lái fó guó yuē suí xí sú yí；
is called Suiting Convention in the buddha-land of the Tathagata Undefiled Opener, in the world Honored Taming;
此名慈哀喜護者，
cǐ míng cí āi xǐ hù zhě，
what here is called kindness, compassion, joy and equanimity
彼豐盛世界吉祥義如來佛國曰
bǐ fēng shèng shì jiè jí xiáng yì rú lái fó guó yuē
is called, in the buddha-land of the Tathagata Auspicious Meaning in the world Abundance,
憐傷仁攝彼我二寂；
lián shāng rén shè bǐ wǒ èr jì；
Pity and Kindness Embracing Self and Other in Twofold Stillness;
此名苦習盡道者，
cǐ míng kǔ xí jǐn dào zhě，
what here is called suffering, origin, cessation and path
彼無悅世界首寂如來佛國曰
bǐ wú yuè shì jiè shǒu jì rú lái fó guó yuē
is called, in the buddha-land of the Tathagata Foremost Stillness in the world Joyless,
本原由根根盡歸本；
běn yuán yóu gēn gēn jǐn guī běn；
The Root Returning Wholly to Its Source;
此名四意止者，他方世界曰無止；
cǐ míng sì yì zhǐ zhě， tā fāng shì jiè yuē wú zhǐ；
What here is called the four foundations of mindfulness, in another world is called Without Halting;
此名四意斷者，彼曰上勝；
cǐ míng sì yì duàn zhě， bǐ yuē shàng shèng；
what here is called the four right exertions, there is called Supreme Victory;
此名神足者，彼曰超步；
cǐ míng shén zú zhě， bǐ yuē chāo bù；
what here is called the bases of spiritual power, there is called Surpassing Stride;
此名五根者，彼曰悅原；
cǐ míng wǔ gēn zhě， bǐ yuē yuè yuán；
what here is called the five faculties, there is called Source of Gladness;
此名五力，彼曰堅強；
cǐ míng wǔ lì， bǐ yuē jiān qiáng；
what here is called the five powers, there is called Firm and Strong;
此名覺意，彼曰無冥；
cǐ míng jué yì， bǐ yuē wú míng；
what here is called the factors of awakening, there is called Without Darkness;
此名八由，彼曰所度；
cǐ míng bā yóu， bǐ yuē suǒ dù；
what here is called the eightfold path, there is called That Which Delivers;
此名分別，彼曰目見；
cǐ míng fēn bié， bǐ yuē mù jiàn；
what here is called discernment, there is called Seen by the Eye;
此名護仰，彼曰隨順；
cǐ míng hù yǎng， bǐ yuē suí shùn；
what here is called guarding and reverence, there is called Accord;
此名法施，彼曰善攝；
cǐ míng fǎ shī， bǐ yuē shàn shè；
what here is called the gift of Dharma, there is called Good Gathering;
此名寂觀，彼曰定察；
cǐ míng jì guān， bǐ yuē dìng chá；
what here is called still contemplation, there is called Settled Examination;
此名脫門，彼曰離癡；
cǐ míng tuō mén， bǐ yuē lí chī；
what here is called the gate of liberation, there is called Free of Delusion;
此名功德，彼曰嚴辦；
cǐ míng gōng dé， bǐ yuē yán bàn；
what here is called merit, there is called Adorned Accomplishment;
此名智慧，彼曰了便；
cǐ míng zhì huì， bǐ yuē le biàn；
what here is called wisdom, there is called Understanding of Means;
此名棄家，彼曰修行；
cǐ míng qì jiā， bǐ yuē xiū xíng；
what here is called leaving the household, there is called Cultivation;
此名具戒，彼曰無犯；
cǐ míng jù jiè， bǐ yuē wú fàn；
what here is called perfecting the precepts, there is called Without Transgression;
此名安隱無為，彼曰寂滅度；
cǐ míng ān yǐn wú wéi， bǐ yuē jì miè dù；
what here is called peace and the unconditioned, there is called Quiescent Deliverance;
此名曰歎佛無量，
cǐ míng yuē tàn fó wú liàng，
what here is called praising the Buddha as immeasurable,
彼曰佛曰佛眼放光。」
bǐ yuē fó yuē fó yǎn fàng guāng。」
there is called The Buddha, the Buddha's Eye Emitting Light.”
佛語龍王：「計諸佛國音聲言訓若干種教，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「jì zhū fó guó yīn shēng yán xùn ruò gān zhǒng jiào，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “Consider the sounds, words and glosses of the buddha-lands, their manifold teachings:
菩薩若逮無盡之藏，
pú sà ruò dǎi wú jǐn zhī zàng，
if a bodhisattva attains the inexhaustible treasury,
皆知一切諸佛之土所說音聲文字所誨。
jiē zhī yī qiè zhū fó zhī tǔ suǒ shuō yīn shēng wén zì suǒ huì。
he knows the sounds, letters and instruction preached in every buddha-land.
佛以一劫若過一劫，
fó yǐ yī jié ruò guò yī jié，
Were a buddha, for a kalpa or more than a kalpa,
讚歎有為言說章句之教十方所出，
zàn tàn yǒu wéi yán shuō zhāng jù zhī jiào shí fāng suǒ chū，
to extol the conditioned teaching of words, chapters and phrases issuing from the ten directions,
不能究竟諸佛國土音聲義也。」
bù néng jiū jìng zhū fó guó tǔ yīn shēng yì yě。」
he could not exhaust the meaning of the sounds of the buddha-lands.”
佛說是無盡之藏總持門時，
fó shuō shì wú jǐn zhī zàng zǒng chí mén shí，
When the Buddha preached this dharani gate of the inexhaustible treasury,
六萬菩薩皆得總持，
liù wàn pú sà jiē dé zǒng chí，
sixty thousand bodhisattvas all attained dharani,
八千菩薩得不起法忍，
bā qiān pú sà dé bù qǐ fǎ rěn，
eight thousand bodhisattvas attained the forbearance of the non-arising of dharmas,
三萬二千人皆發無上正真道意。
sān wàn èr qiān rén jiē fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì。
and thirty-two thousand people aroused the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment.
總持門品第七
zǒng chí mén pǐn dì qī
Chapter Seven: The Gate of Dharani
爾時佛告龍王：「菩薩以是離諸幽冥之路，
ěr shí fó gào lóng wáng： 「pú sà yǐ shì lí zhū yōu míng zhī lù，
At that time the Buddha told the Dragon King: “By this a bodhisattva leaves the road of darkness
趣諸通慧。
qù zhū tōng huì。
and moves toward penetrating wisdom.
往古不可計無央數劫不可思議，彼時有佛。
wǎng gǔ bù kě jì wú yāng shù jié bù kě sī yì， bǐ shí yǒu fó。
Incalculable, countless, inconceivable kalpas ago there was a buddha
號曰梵首天王如來、至真、等正覺、
hào yuē fàn shǒu tiān wáng rú lái、 zhì zhēn、 děng zhèng jué、
named the Tathagata Brahma-Crest Deva King, Truly Worthy, Perfectly Enlightened,
明行成為、善逝、世間解、
míng xíng chéng wéi、 shàn shì、 shì jiān jiě、
Perfect in Knowledge and Conduct, Well Gone, Knower of the World,
無上士、道法御、天人師，號佛、世尊，
wú shàng shì、 dào fǎ yù、 tiān rén shī， hào fó、 shì zūn，
Unsurpassed One, Tamer by the Dharma of the Way, Teacher of Devas and Men, called Buddha, World-Honored One.
世界曰集異德，劫名淨除。
shì jiè yuē jí yì dé， jié míng jìng chú。
His world was named Gathered Diverse Virtue, and the kalpa was named Pure Removal.
彼時集異德世界豐盛安隱，五穀自然；
bǐ shí jí yì dé shì jiè fēng shèng ān yǐn， wǔ gǔ zì rán；
In that time the world Gathered Diverse Virtue was abundant and at peace, the five grains growing of themselves;
快樂無極，天人繁熾。
kuài lè wú jí， tiān rén fán chì。
joy was boundless, and devas and men flourished.
如我此土百億四域合為一佛國，
rú wǒ cǐ tǔ bǎi yì sì yù hé wéi yī fó guó，
As ten billion four-continent realms of this land of mine make one buddha-land,
則為彼土一大四域，
zé wéi bǐ tǔ yī dà sì yù，
so that is but one great four-continent realm of that land,
如是之比百億須彌山，
rú shì zhī bǐ bǎi yì xū mí shān，
and by such a reckoning ten billion Mount Sumerus —
此梵首天王如來集異德世界
cǐ fàn shǒu tiān wáng rú lái jí yì dé shì jiè
the world Gathered Diverse Virtue of the Tathagata Brahma-Crest Deva King
廣大無邊，乃如茲乎！
guǎng dà wú biān， nǎi rú zī hū！
was vast and boundless — even so!
其世界如金剛光明摩尼之寶，
qí shì jiè rú jīn gāng guāng míng mó ní zhī bǎo，
That world was like a mani jewel of adamantine radiance,
自然常普大明，
zì rán cháng pǔ dà míng，
of itself always and everywhere greatly bright,
以寶交絡，周匝覆蓋懸繒、幢幡，
yǐ bǎo jiāo luò， zhōu zā fù gài xuán zēng、 chuáng fān，
netted with jewels, hung about with silks, banners and pennants,
百千伎樂於虛空中不鼓自鳴，
bǎi qiān jì yuè yú xū kōng zhōng bù gǔ zì míng，
and a hundred thousand strains of music sounded in the sky unstruck,
其伎樂音普聞佛土。
qí jì yuè yīn pǔ wén fó tǔ。
their sound heard throughout the buddha-land.
彼伎樂音，不出婬、怒、癡欲之音聲也，
bǐ jì yuè yīn， bù chū yín、 nù、 chī yù zhī yīn shēng yě，
That music gave forth no sound of lust, anger, folly or craving,
唯演寂然憺怕法樂歡喜之音。
wéi yǎn jì rán dàn pò fǎ lè huān xǐ zhī yīn。
but only the still and tranquil sound of Dharma-joy and gladness.
諸天、人民聞樂音者，則逮一心寂定，
zhū tiān、 rén mín wén lè yīn zhě， zé dǎi yī xīn jì dìng，
Devas and people who heard it attained single-minded stillness and concentration,
安隱晏然，不為塵勞之所危害也。
ān yǐn yàn rán， bù wéi chén láo zhī suǒ wēi hài yě。
peaceful and serene, unharmed by defilement and toil.
其土平等，如柔軟衣，
qí tǔ píng děng， rú róu ruǎn yī，
That land was level as a soft garment,
無有惡趣音聲誨也。
wú yǒu è qù yīn shēng huì yě。
and there was no sound or teaching of the evil destinies.
天、人清淨，皆解微妙，
tiān、 rén qīng jìng， jiē jiě wēi miào，
Devas and men were pure and all understood the subtle,
志于大乘，少求聲聞、緣覺之乘。
zhì yú dà chéng， shǎo qiú shēng wén、 yuán jué zhī chéng。
aspiring to the Great Vehicle, few seeking the vehicles of sravaka or pratyekabuddha.
心有所念，衣食、室宇所欲隨意，
xīn yǒu suǒ niàn， yī shí、 shì yǔ suǒ yù suí yì，
Whatever the mind conceived — clothing, food, dwellings, whatever was wished —
悉自然至，天、人一等，
xī zì rán zhì， tiān、 rén yī děng，
came of itself; devas and men were of one rank,
無有窮厄匱乏者也，
wú yǒu qióng è guì fá zhě yě，
and none were in want or destitution.
衣服、飲食如兜術天上，
yī fú、 yǐn shí rú dōu shù tiān shàng，
Their clothing and food were as in the Tusita heaven,
其國所有等無差特。
qí guó suǒ yǒu děng wú chā tè。
and all that land possessed was equal, without distinction.
其如來壽命滿六十七萬二千歲，
qí rú lái shòu mìng mǎn liù shí qī wàn èr qiān suì，
That Tathagata's lifespan was a full six hundred and seventy-two thousand years,
其土人民壽亦復如是，無中夭者。
qí tǔ rén mín shòu yì fù rú shì， wú zhōng yāo zhě。
and the people of that land lived as long, none dying untimely.
佛土菩薩七十二那術，聲聞甚少。
fó tǔ pú sà qī shí èr nà shù， shēng wén shèn shǎo。
In that buddha-land were seventy-two nayutas of bodhisattvas, and very few sravakas.
「爾時有轉輪聖王號無盡福，主十六四天下。
「ěr shí yǒu zhuǎn lún shèng wáng hào wú jǐn fú， zhǔ shí liù sì tiān xià。
“At that time there was a wheel-turning sage king named Inexhaustible Merit, lord of sixteen four-continent realms.
其無盡福王有八十四那術夫人，如天玉女。
qí wú jǐn fú wáng yǒu bā shí sì nà shù fū rén， rú tiān yù nǚ。
King Inexhaustible Merit had eighty-four nayutas of consorts, like celestial jade maidens.
有四太后：一名離垢，二曰無垢光，
yǒu sì tài hòu： yī míng lí gòu， èr yuē wú gòu guāng，
He had four queens: the first named Free of Defilement, the second Undefiled Light,
三曰清淨，四曰淨句。
sān yuē qīng jìng， sì yuē jìng jù。
the third Purity, the fourth Pure Phrase.
子有八萬四千，皆大猛勇身相；
zi yǒu bā wàn sì qiān， jiē dà měng yǒng shēn xiāng；
He had eighty-four thousand sons, all of great and valiant bearing;
有八端正姝好，皆志大乘。
yǒu bā duān zhèng shū hǎo， jiē zhì dà chéng。
eight of them exceedingly fair, all aspiring to the Great Vehicle.
彼時無盡福王處於大城，名曰具樂。
bǐ shí wú jǐn fú wáng chù yú dà chéng， míng yuē jù lè。
King Inexhaustible Merit dwelt in a great city named Complete Joy.
其城東西長二千四百四十里，南北亦爾。
qí chéng dōng xī zhǎng èr qiān sì bǎi sì shí lǐ， nán běi yì ěr。
The city was two thousand four hundred and forty leagues from east to west, and the same from north to south.
梵首天王如來興於彼國，
fàn shǒu tiān wáng rú lái xìng yú bǐ guó，
The Tathagata Brahma-Crest Deva King appeared in that realm,
無盡福王建立精舍，殖大林樹，
wú jǐn fú wáng jiàn lì jīng shě， zhí dà lín shù，
and King Inexhaustible Merit built a monastery and planted a great grove
名上香光園，佛所遊止。
míng shàng xiāng guāng yuán， fó suǒ yóu zhǐ。
named the Park of Supreme Fragrant Light, where the Buddha dwelt.
城之中央造王宮殿，七寶合成。
chéng zhī zhōng yāng zào wáng gōng diàn， qī bǎo hé chéng。
At the city's center stood the royal palace, made of the seven treasures.
城中有八萬四千街巷、八萬四千欄楯。
chéng zhōng yǒu bā wàn sì qiān jiē xiàng、 bā wàn sì qiān lán shǔn。
In the city were eighty-four thousand streets and eighty-four thousand railings.
一一街巷有八萬四千家居。
yī yī jiē xiàng yǒu bā wàn sì qiān jiā jū。
Each street held eighty-four thousand dwellings.
其大城壁七重，七重欄楯、
qí dà chéng bì qī chóng， qī chóng lán shǔn、
The great city wall was sevenfold, with sevenfold railings,
七重行樹、七重交路，
qī chóng xíng shù、 qī chóng jiāo lù，
sevenfold rows of trees and sevenfold crossing roads,
繞城有萬遊觀園。
rào chéng yǒu wàn yóu guān yuán。
and ten thousand pleasure gardens ringed the city.
其塹七重，滿八味水，
qí qiàn qī chóng， mǎn bā wèi shuǐ，
The moats were sevenfold, filled with water of eight flavors,
生青蓮、紅蓮、黃蓮、白蓮，皆有美香，
shēng qīng lián、 hóng lián、 huáng lián、 bái lián， jiē yǒu měi xiāng，
in which grew blue, red, yellow and white lotuses, all sweetly fragrant,
鴛鴦、鳧鴈相隨而鳴。
yuān yāng、 fú yàn xiāng suí ér míng。
and mandarin ducks, teal and geese called to one another.
其城如是，名等無量，不可思議。
qí chéng rú shì， míng děng wú liàng， bù kě sī yì。
Such was that city, its fame immeasurable and inconceivable.
「王供養佛，奉進所安，眾事具足，
「wáng gòng yǎng fó， fèng jìn suǒ ān， zhòng shì jù zú，
“The king made offerings to the Buddha, presenting whatever gave ease, complete in every service,
無所乏少，於百千歲不可稱限。
wú suǒ fá shǎo， yú bǎi qiān suì bù kě chēng xiàn。
lacking nothing, for a hundred thousand years beyond reckoning.
率其中宮、子孫、親族、友黨、眷屬、國中人民，
lǜ qí zhōng gōng、 zi sūn、 qīn zú、 yǒu dǎng、 juàn shǔ、 guó zhōng rén mín，
He led his inner palace, sons and grandsons, kin, companions, retinue and the people of the realm
往詣上香光叢林，見梵首天王如來，
wǎng yì shàng xiāng guāng cóng lín， jiàn fàn shǒu tiān wáng rú lái，
to the grove of Supreme Fragrant Light to see the Tathagata Brahma-Crest Deva King.
稽首佛足，退住一面。
qǐ shǒu fó zú， tuì zhù yí miàn。
They bowed at the Buddha's feet and withdrew to one side.
佛告王曰：『有四事為大國王、
fó gào wáng yuē： 『yǒu sì shì wéi dà guó wáng、
The Buddha told the king: ‘There are four things by which a great king,
君子、聖主猶得自在，
jūn zǐ、 shèng zhǔ yóu dé zì zài，
a noble, a sage ruler gains mastery,
與眾不同，增益善法。何等為四？
yǔ zhòng bù tóng， zēng yì shàn fǎ。 hé děng wéi sì？
differs from the multitude, and increases wholesome dharmas. Which four?
立於篤信，數詣賢聖，樂欣請益，求德慕義；
lì yú dǔ xìn， shù yì xián shèng， lè xīn qǐng yì， qiú dé mù yì；
To be established in firm faith, to visit the sages often, to delight in asking for further teaching, to seek virtue and long for what is right;
以法自娛，常觀無常、苦、空、非身之法，
yǐ fǎ zì yú， cháng guān wú cháng、 kǔ、 kōng、 fēi shēn zhī fǎ，
to take joy in the Dharma, ever contemplating impermanence, suffering, emptiness and non-self,
觀世所有有為之穢皆歸離別；
guān shì suǒ yǒu yǒu wéi zhī huì jiē guī lí bié；
and to see that all the conditioned impurity of the world ends in parting;
自攝其心，入無放逸，覺察欲樂，無所饒羨；
zì shè qí xīn， rù wú fàng yì， jué chá yù lè， wú suǒ ráo xiàn；
to restrain his own mind, entering non-laxity, examining the pleasures of desire and coveting nothing;
不以毀斷宿世福德，不廢道心，務志妙慧。
bù yǐ huǐ duàn sù shì fú dé， bù fèi dào xīn， wù zhì miào huì。
not to destroy the merit of former lives, not to abandon the mind of the Way, but to strive for subtle wisdom.
是為四事，大國聖主猶得自在，與眾不同。』
shì wéi sì shì， dà guó shèng zhǔ yóu dé zì zài， yǔ zhòng bù tóng。』
These are the four by which a sage ruler of a great realm gains mastery and differs from the multitude.’
「無盡福王白世尊曰：
「wú jǐn fú wáng bái shì zūn yuē：
“King Inexhaustible Merit said to the World-Honored One:
『菩薩有幾法而得自在？』
『pú sà yǒu jǐ fǎ ér dé zì zài？』
‘By how many dharmas does a bodhisattva gain mastery?’
佛告王曰：『菩薩有八法而得自在。何謂為八？
fó gào wáng yuē： 『pú sà yǒu bā fǎ ér dé zì zài。 hé wèi wéi bā？
The Buddha told the king: ‘A bodhisattva has eight dharmas by which he gains mastery. Which eight?
得五神通以自娛樂，未曾有退，無所罣礙；
dé wǔ shén tōng yǐ zì yú lè， wèi céng yǒu tuì， wú suǒ guà ài；
He gains the five penetrations and delights in them, never regressing, unobstructed;
并除瞋恨，而無害心，
bìng chú chēn hèn， ér wú hài xīn，
he removes hatred and holds no harmful thought,
具暢聖慧，攝于道明；
jù chàng shèng huì， shè yú dào míng；
fully expounds holy wisdom, and is gathered into the brightness of the Way;
所作已辦，現得叡達，
suǒ zuò yǐ bàn， xiàn dé ruì dá，
what he set out to do is accomplished, and he manifests keen penetration;
誠信神足，拔諸所有；
chéng xìn shén zú， bá zhū suǒ yǒu；
sincere in faith and in the bases of spiritual power, he uproots all possession;
以智慧聖捨離一切邪見塵垢，
yǐ zhì huì shèng shě lí yī qiè xié jiàn chén gòu，
by holy wisdom he casts off every wrong view and defilement,
得四解明，佛所建立，無著不住；
dé sì jiě míng， fó suǒ jiàn lì， wú zhuó bú zhù；
gains the four clarities of understanding, is established by the Buddha, unattached and abiding nowhere;
具足力處，逮于無盡福海印三昧；
jù zú lì chù， dǎi yú wú jǐn fú hǎi yìn sān mèi；
perfect in the ground of power, he attains the samadhi of the Inexhaustible Merit Ocean Seal;
能悅眾生，攝御一切諸佛之教，以成總持；
néng yuè zhòng shēng， shè yù yī qiè zhū fó zhī jiào， yǐ chéng zǒng chí；
he gladdens beings, gathers and holds the teaching of all buddhas, and so perfects dharani;
其心清淨，所聞不忘，應如所欲，而為說法；
qí xīn qīng jìng， suǒ wén bù wàng， yìng rú suǒ yù， ér wèi shuō fǎ；
his mind is pure, what he hears he does not forget, and he preaches the Dharma as each desires;
入一義味，住於本際，不計吾我，不起法忍。
rù yī yì wèi， zhù yú běn jì， bù jì wú wǒ， bù qǐ fǎ rěn。
he enters the single savor of meaning, abides at the original limit, reckons no self, and gains the forbearance of non-arising.
是為八事，菩薩而得自在。』
shì wéi bā shì， pú sà ér dé zì zài。』
These are the eight by which a bodhisattva gains mastery.’
佛告王曰：『又有總持名曰寶事，
fó gào wáng yuē： 『yòu yǒu zǒng chí míng yuē bǎo shì，
The Buddha told the king: ‘There is also a dharani named Jewel Matters;
菩薩逮得此總持者於法自在。』
pú sà dǎi dé cǐ zǒng chí zhě yú fǎ zì zài。』
a bodhisattva who attains it is free in the Dharma.’
「時佛為王說寶事總持之慧，
「shí fó wéi wáng shuō bǎo shì zǒng chí zhī huì，
“Then the Buddha preached to the king the wisdom of the Jewel Matters dharani,
滿百千歲普分別義。
mǎn bǎi qiān suì pǔ fēn bié yì。
expounding its meaning fully for a hundred thousand years.
王捨國事一切眾緣，專精一心，
wáng shě guó shì yī qiè zhòng yuán， zhuān jīng yī xīn，
The king set aside the affairs of state and all entanglements, single and intent,
及與眷屬聽受道化，
jí yǔ juàn shǔ tīng shòu dào huà，
and with his retinue received the teaching of the Way.
於百千歲未曾想欲，無瞋恨意，不含想害，
yú bǎi qiān suì wèi céng xiǎng yù， wú chēn hèn yì， bù hán xiǎng hài，
For a hundred thousand years he conceived no desire, held no hatred, harbored no thought of harm,
不顧妻子、國土、眷屬，
bù gù qī zǐ、 guó tǔ、 juàn shǔ，
gave no thought to wife and children, realm or retinue,
一切所有永不以計，唯願法樂，
yī qiè suǒ yǒu yǒng bù yǐ jì， wéi yuàn fǎ lè，
reckoned nothing he possessed, and wished only for the joy of Dharma.
立志佛道，大慈清淨，
lì zhì fó dào， dà cí qīng jìng，
He set his resolve on the buddha path, pure in great loving-kindness,
等心一切而行大哀，被大德鎧而聽受法。
děng xīn yī qiè ér xíng dà āi， bèi dà dé kǎi ér tīng shòu fǎ。
impartial toward all, practicing great compassion, clad in the armor of great virtue as he received the Dharma.
如是之比具百千歲，受佛誨已，
rú shì zhī bǐ jù bǎi qiān suì， shòu fó huì yǐ，
So it was for a full hundred thousand years; and having received the Buddha's instruction,
因此寶事總持之要，所作則辦。
yīn cǐ bǎo shì zǒng chí zhī yào， suǒ zuò zé bàn。
by the essentials of this Jewel Matters dharani what he set out to do was accomplished.
越七百萬劫終始之患，
yuè qī bǎi wàn jié zhōng shǐ zhī huàn，
He passed beyond seven million kalpas of the peril of birth and death,
積十萬劫除諸罪殃，
jī shí wàn jié chú zhū zuì yāng，
and over a hundred thousand kalpas removed all sin and calamity;
見億百千佛從受德本，
jiàn yì bǎi qiān fó cóng shòu dé běn，
he beheld a hundred thousand million buddhas and received roots of virtue from them,
於恒沙等作天帝釋。
yú héng shā děng zuò tiān dì shì。
and as often as the sands of the Ganges he became Sakra.
若為梵天、轉輪聖王，積功累德，
ruò wéi fàn tiān、 zhuǎn lún shèng wáng， jī gōng lèi dé，
Whether as Brahma or as a wheel-turning sage king, he amassed merit and virtue,
以清淨心志御諸法，用心不亂，
yǐ qīng jìng xīn zhì yù zhū fǎ， yòng xīn bù luàn，
governing all dharmas with a pure mind and resolve, his mind undisturbed;
聞百千佛，受法不忘。
wén bǎi qiān fó， shòu fǎ bù wàng。
he heard a hundred thousand buddhas and never forgot the Dharma he received.
時王諸子，皆悉逮得柔順法忍；
shí wáng zhū zi， jiē xī dǎi dé róu shùn fǎ rěn；
Then the king's sons all attained the pliant forbearance of dharmas;
中宮婇女八萬四千，普發道意，為菩薩學；
zhōng gōng cǎi nǚ bā wàn sì qiān， pǔ fā dào yì， wéi pú sà xué；
eighty-four thousand palace women all aroused the aspiration to the Way and trained as bodhisattvas;
八萬四千人逮得法忍；
bā wàn sì qiān rén dǎi dé fǎ rěn；
eighty-four thousand people attained forbearance of dharmas;
九十那術諸天人民皆發無上正真道意；
jiǔ shí nà shù zhū tiān rén mín jiē fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì；
ninety nayutas of devas and people aroused the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment;
三十六那術學聲聞乘得法眼淨；
sān shí liù nà shù xué shēng wén chéng dé fǎ yǎn jìng；
thirty-six nayutas training in the sravaka vehicle gained the pure Dharma eye;
萬六千比丘漏盡意解。
wàn liù qiān bǐ qiū lòu jǐn yì jiě。
and sixteen thousand bhiksus exhausted the outflows and their minds were released.
「無盡福王棄國捐王，
「wú jǐn fú wáng qì guó juān wáng，
“King Inexhaustible Merit abandoned his realm and cast off his kingship,
不慕天上、世間諸樂，
bù mù tiān shàng、 shì jiān zhū lè，
longing for no pleasure in heaven or in the world,
唯志無上正真之道。
wéi zhì wú shàng zhèng zhēn zhī dào。
aspiring only to unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment.
因家之信，出家為道而作沙門。
yīn jiā zhī xìn， chū jiā wéi dào ér zuò shā mén。
Out of faith he left the household for the Way and became a sramana.
諸子亦然，皆作沙門。
zhū zi yì rán， jiē zuò shā mén。
His sons did likewise and all became sramanas.
時國人見王棄國，六萬人悉為沙門；
shí guó rén jiàn wáng qì guó， liù wàn rén xī wéi shā mén；
When the people saw the king abandon his realm, sixty thousand of them became sramanas;
中宮婇女、四大夫人亦為沙門。
zhōng gōng cǎi nǚ、 sì dà fū rén yì wéi shā mén。
and the palace women and the four great queens became renunciants as well.
佛教清淨，普蒙安隱，
fó jiào qīng jìng， pǔ méng ān yǐn，
The Buddha's teaching was pure, and all received peace and ease,
殖諸德本，眾行具足。」
zhí zhū dé běn， zhòng xíng jù zú。」
planted roots of virtue, and perfected every practice.”
佛言：「龍王！爾時無盡福王轉輪聖帝非是餘人，
fó yán： 「lóng wáng！ ěr shí wú jǐn fú wáng zhuǎn lún shèng dì fēi shì yú rén，
The Buddha said: “Dragon King, that wheel-turning sage emperor Inexhaustible Merit was no other person —
則爾身是也。
zé ěr shēn shì yě。
he was you yourself.
爾時轉輪聖王諸子，
ěr shí zhuǎn lún shèng wáng zhū zi，
The sons of that wheel-turning sage king
今此會中諸菩薩大士是也。
jīn cǐ huì zhōng zhū pú sà dà shì shì yě。
are the bodhisattva great beings in this assembly now.
時彼梵首天王如來，
shí bǐ fàn shǒu tiān wáng rú lái，
And the Tathagata Brahma-Crest Deva King then,
為王無盡福所說寶事總持，
wéi wáng wú jǐn fú suǒ shuō bǎo shì zǒng chí，
in the Jewel Matters dharani he preached to King Inexhaustible Merit —
則今佛所說無盡藏總持是。」
zé jīn fó suǒ shuō wú jìn zàng zǒng chí shì。」
is the very dharani of the inexhaustible treasury the Buddha preaches now.”
佛語龍王：「今如來以無著慧
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「jīn rú lái yǐ wú zhuó huì
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “The Tathagata now, with unattached wisdom,
觀察人本而為說法，
guān chá rén běn ér wèi shuō fǎ，
examines beings' origins and preaches accordingly.
