從無央數百千億那術諸佛聞無盡藏總持，
cóng wú yāng shù bǎi qiān yì nà shù zhū fó wén wú jìn zàng zǒng chí，
From countless hundreds of thousands of millions of nayutas of buddhas he heard this dharani of the inexhaustible treasury;
以此數聞之故，今乃如斯志念強勇，
yǐ cǐ shù wén zhī gù， jīn nǎi rú sī zhì niàn qiáng yǒng，
and because he heard it so often, his resolve and recollection are now thus strong and valiant,
獨步無礙，辯才難及，志懷智慧。
dú bù wú ài， biàn cái nán jí， zhì huái zhì huì。
he walks alone unobstructed, his eloquence beyond reach, his mind holding wisdom.
若有菩薩，聞是無盡總持之名，其有說者，
ruò yǒu pú sà， wén shì wú jǐn zǒng chí zhī míng， qí yǒu shuō zhě，
If a bodhisattva hears the name of this inexhaustible dharani, or preaches it,
皆當逮得無著辯才。
jiē dāng dǎi dé wú zhuó biàn cái。
he will attain unattached eloquence.
所以者何？由是總持，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ yóu shì zǒng chí，
Why? Because through this dharani,
後當來世是離垢總持所流布處，
hòu dāng lái shì shì lí gòu zǒng chí suǒ liú bù chù，
wherever in the ages to come this undefiled dharani circulates,
皆是如來之所建立八萬四千法藏。
jiē shì rú lái zhī suǒ jiàn lì bā wàn sì qiān fǎ zàng。
there are the eighty-four thousand Dharma treasuries established by the Tathagata.
是總持門為首面也，
shì zǒng chí mén wéi shǒu miàn yě，
This gate of dharani is the head and face of them all:
八萬四千行皆來歸於總持，
bā wàn sì qiān xíng jiē lái guī yú zǒng chí，
the eighty-four thousand practices all come home to dharani,
八萬四千三昧皆從總持，
bā wàn sì qiān sān mèi jiē cóng zǒng chí，
the eighty-four thousand samadhis all proceed from dharani,
八萬四千總持無盡之藏總持為本原。」
bā wàn sì qiān zǒng chí wú jǐn zhī zàng zǒng chí wéi běn yuán。」
and of the eighty-four thousand dharanis, the dharani of the inexhaustible treasury is the source.”
佛語龍王：「假使菩薩無住無著，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「jiǎ shǐ pú sà wú zhù wú zhuó，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “If a bodhisattva abides nowhere and clings to nothing,
於四解義則降大法雨，
yú sì jiě yì zé jiàng dà fǎ yǔ，
then in the four understandings of meaning he sends down the great rain of Dharma,
皆來依猗此無盡之藏。
jiē lái yī yī cǐ wú jǐn zhī zàng。
and all come to rely upon this inexhaustible treasury.
此無盡之藏總持所入正句次第順章，
cǐ wú jǐn zhī zàng zǒng chí suǒ rù zhèng jù cì dì shùn zhāng，
The right phrases this dharani enters, in due order and concordant chapters,
諸天、龍、神、香音神、無善神、
zhū tiān、 lóng、 shén、 xiāng yīn shén、 wú shàn shén、
are guarded together by devas, dragons, spirits, gandharvas, asuras,
鳳凰神、甜柔神皆共營護：
fèng huáng shén、 tián róu shén jiē gòng yíng hù：
garudas and kinnaras:
緣應意　隨順意　欣樂跡　直意
yuán yīng yì 　 suí shùn yì 　 xīn lè jì 　 zhí yì
Mind Answering Conditions · Compliant Mind · Trace of Gladness · Upright Mind
越度　無盡句　次第　曜面
yuè dù 　 wú jǐn jù 　 cì dì 　 yào miàn
Crossing Over · Inexhaustible Phrase · In Due Order · Radiant Face
光目　光英　志造　淨意
guāng mù 　 guāng yīng 　 zhì zào 　 jìng yì
Eye of Light · Flower of Light · Aim That Makes · Pure Mind
行步入　勇力　濟冥　所持
xíng bù rù 　 yǒng lì 　 jì míng 　 suǒ chí
Entering by Stride · Valiant Strength · Rescue from Darkness · What Is Held
為上　寂門　入寂　滅塵
wéi shàng 　 jì mén 　 rù jì 　 miè chén
Being Supreme · Gate of Stillness · Entering Stillness · Extinguishing Dust
離居　居善　隨順　離次
lí jū 　 jū shàn 　 suí shùn 　 lí cì
Dwelling Apart · Dwelling in Good · Accord · Departing from Sequence
無所至　所住　無所住　至處
wú suǒ zhì 　 suǒ zhù 　 wú suǒ zhù 　 zhì chù
Arriving Nowhere · Where One Abides · Abiding Nowhere · Reaching the Place
無至處　要御　速慧　智根
wú zhì chù 　 yào yù 　 sù huì 　 zhì gēn
No Place Reached · Essential Guidance · Swift Wisdom · Root of Knowing
轉本根　月光　日轉焰
zhuǎn běn gēn 　 yuè guāng 　 rì zhuǎn yàn
Turning the Original Root · Moonlight · Sun-Turning Flame
光善離垢　無垢　淨諸垢
guāng shàn lí gòu 　 wú gòu 　 jìng zhū gòu
Light of Good Free of Defilement · Undefiled · Purifying All Defilement
覺所建立　諸天祐　護諸魅
jué suǒ jiàn lì 　 zhū tiān yòu 　 hù zhū mèi
What Awakening Establishes · Blessing of the Devas · Warding Off Sprites
告乘　梵知化　釋諮嗟　四天護
gào chéng 　 fàn zhī huà 　 shì zī jiē 　 sì tiān hù
Proclaiming the Vehicle · Brahma's Knowing Transformation · Sakra's Praise · Protection of the Four Kings
眾聖愛　仙人歸　諸姓修行
zhòng shèng ài 　 xiān rén guī 　 zhū xìng xiū xíng
Beloved of the Sages · Refuge of the Rishis · Practice of Every Clan
解牢獄縛　天人所攝　捨諸塵勞
jiě láo yù fù 　 tiān rén suǒ shè 　 shě zhū chén láo
Loosing the Bonds of Prison · Gathered by Devas and Men · Relinquishing Defilement
破壞眾魔　降伏外道　攝欲明智
pò huài zhòng mó 　 xiáng fú wài dào 　 shè yù míng zhì
Shattering the Hosts of Mara · Subduing the Heterodox · Gathering Desire into Clear Knowing
開化自大　不犯法師　不亂眾會
kāi huà zì dà 　 bù fàn fǎ shī 　 bù luàn zhòng huì
Converting the Arrogant · Not Offending the Dharma Master · Not Disturbing Assemblies
悅可樂法　護於法音　不斷三寶
yuè kě lè fǎ 　 hù yú fǎ yīn 　 bù duàn sān bǎo
Gladdening Those Who Love Dharma · Guarding the Sound of Dharma · Not Cutting Off the Three Jewels
慈愍眾生　讚慕德義(六十二事)」
cí mǐn zhòng shēng 　 zàn mù dé yì (liù shí èr shì)」
Kindness and Pity for Beings · Praising and Longing for Virtue. (Sixty-two items)”
佛告龍王：「是諸法句，為護無盡之藏總持。
fó gào lóng wáng： 「shì zhū fǎ jù， wéi hù wú jǐn zhī zàng zǒng chí。
The Buddha told the Dragon King: “These phrases of Dharma guard the dharani of the inexhaustible treasury.
其有法師受是章句六十二事，若諷誦者，
qí yǒu fǎ shī shòu shì zhāng jù liù shí èr shì， ruò fěng sòng zhě，
A Dharma master who receives these sixty-two chapter-phrases and recites them
得三十二無所畏。何謂三十二？
dé sān shí èr wú suǒ wèi。 hé wèi sān shí èr？
gains thirty-two fearlessnesses. Which thirty-two?
博聞無畏，諮嗟他人；
bó wén wú wèi， zī jiē tā rén；
Fearlessness of wide learning — he praises others;
處處無畏，言無缺短；
chù chù wú wèi， yán wú quē duǎn；
fearlessness everywhere — his words have no lack;
如應無畏，棄捐鄭重；
rú yìng wú wèi， qì juān zhèng zhòng；
fearlessness of fitness — he sets aside ponderousness;
而無所畏，隨音所入；
ér wú suǒ wèi， suí yīn suǒ rù；
fearlessness itself — he follows wherever sound enters;
辯才無畏，無所罣礙；
biàn cái wú wèi， wú suǒ guà ài；
fearlessness of eloquence — he is unobstructed;
其心無畏，奉受道心；
qí xīn wú wèi， fèng shòu dào xīn；
fearlessness of mind — he upholds the mind of the Way;
其志無畏，歡悅眾人；
qí zhì wú wèi， huān yuè zhòng rén；
fearlessness of resolve — he gladdens the multitude;
行步無畏，速決狐疑；
xíng bù wú wèi， sù jué hú yí；
fearlessness of stride — he swiftly resolves doubt;
覺意無畏，觀察眾人；
jué yì wú wèi， guān chá zhòng rén；
fearlessness of awakened mind — he examines the multitude;
無闕無畏，言行相應；
wú quē wú wèi， yán xíng xiāng yìng；
fearlessness without gap — his words and deeds accord;
無缺無畏，戒禁清淨；
wú quē wú wèi， jiè jìn qīng jìng；
fearlessness without flaw — his precepts are pure;
心面無畏，忍辱清淨；
xīn miàn wú wèi， rěn rǔ qīng jìng；
fearlessness of countenance — his patience is pure;
堅強無畏，於審諦願而不轉還；
jiān qiáng wú wèi， yú shěn dì yuàn ér bù zhuǎn hái；
fearlessness of firmness — he does not turn back from the vow of true discernment;
所處無畏，心不謬亂；
suǒ chù wú wèi， xīn bù miù luàn；
fearlessness wherever he is — his mind is not confused;
辯慧無畏，能悅眾會；
biàn huì wú wèi， néng yuè zhòng huì；
fearlessness of eloquent wisdom — he can gladden assemblies;
智慧無畏，知深妙法；
zhì huì wú wèi， zhī shēn miào fǎ；
fearlessness of wisdom — he knows the profound and subtle Dharma;
降化無畏，離於調戲；
jiàng huà wú wèi， lí yú tiáo xì；
fearlessness of subduing and converting — he is free of frivolity;
師子無畏，伏諸外道；
shī zǐ wú wèi， fú zhū wài dào；
fearlessness of the lion — he subdues the heterodox;
無受無畏，無衣食悅；
wú shòu wú wèi， wú yī shí yuè；
fearlessness of not receiving — he takes no pleasure in food or clothing;
無瑕無畏，降伏眾賊，令住正見；
wú xiá wú wèi， xiáng fú zhòng zéi， lìng zhù zhèng jiàn；
fearlessness without flaw — he subdues brigands and establishes them in right view;
無愆無畏，智者不毀；
wú qiān wú wèi， zhì zhě bù huǐ；
fearlessness without fault — the wise do not censure him;
導御無畏，不亂眾經；
dǎo yù wú wèi， bù luàn zhòng jīng；
fearlessness of guidance — he does not confuse the scriptures;
說等無畏，隨時而教；
shuō děng wú wèi， suí shí ér jiào；
fearlessness of impartial preaching — he teaches according to the time;
無諂無畏，言行相應；
wú chǎn wú wèi， yán xíng xiāng yìng；
fearlessness without flattery — his words and deeds accord;
離慢無畏，見一切人；
lí màn wú wèi， jiàn yī qiè rén；
fearlessness free of pride — he receives all people;
謙順無畏，無盡句本行修善；
qiān shùn wú wèi， wú jǐn jù běn xíng xiū shàn；
fearlessness of humility — by the inexhaustible phrases his original practice cultivates good;
發遣所問無畏，開化一切無量法教；
fā qiǎn suǒ wèn wú wèi， kāi huà yī qiè wú liàng fǎ jiào；
fearlessness in answering questions — he converts all by immeasurable Dharma teaching;
隨眾無畏，己身淨故；
suí zhòng wú wèi， jǐ shēn jìng gù；
fearlessness amid the assembly — because he himself is pure;
降魔無畏，除諸塵勞；
jiàng mó wú wèi， chú zhū chén láo；
fearlessness of subduing Mara — he removes defilement and toil;
大慈無畏，心不懷害；
dà cí wú wèi， xīn bù huái hài；
fearlessness of great loving-kindness — his mind harbors no harm;
大哀無畏，將護眾生；
dà āi wú wèi， jiāng hù zhòng shēng；
fearlessness of great compassion — he guards living beings;
智慧無畏，以法治國。」
zhì huì wú wèi， yǐ fǎ zhì guó。」
fearlessness of wisdom — he governs the realm by Dharma.”
佛語龍王：「菩薩聞是無盡之藏總持，歡喜信，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「pú sà wén shì wú jǐn zhī zàng zǒng chí， huān xǐ xìn，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “A bodhisattva who hears this dharani of the inexhaustible treasury and believes it with joy
便得三十二無畏。
biàn dé sān shí èr wú wèi。
gains these thirty-two fearlessnesses.
假使不斷是三十二無畏，
jiǎ shǐ bù duàn shì sān shí èr wú wèi，
If he does not break off these thirty-two fearlessnesses,
稍稍漸成如來四無所畏、佛之所有無所畏。
shāo shāo jiàn chéng rú lái sì wú suǒ wèi、 fó zhī suǒ yǒu wú suǒ wèi。
he gradually perfects the Tathagata's four fearlessnesses and all the fearlessness a buddha possesses.
諸天、人前為師子吼，
zhū tiān、 rén qián wéi shī zǐ hǒu，
Before devas and men he gives the lion's roar,
恣聽一切所可欲問，
zì tīng yī qiè suǒ kě yù wèn，
freely allowing every question anyone wishes to ask;
都無有人能來窮極如來之智，
dōu wú yǒu rén néng lái qióng jí rú lái zhī zhì，
and there is no one who can exhaust the Tathagata's knowledge,
亦不敢斷佛所說。
yì bù gǎn duàn fó suǒ shuō。
nor dare any cut short what the Buddha preaches.
是故菩薩欲致是無所畏者，
shì gù pú sà yù zhì shì wú suǒ wèi zhě，
Therefore a bodhisattva who wishes to attain this fearlessness
當學行無盡藏總持。
dāng xué xíng wú jìn zàng zǒng chí。
should train in the practice of the dharani of the inexhaustible treasury.
何所是總持所學行？
hé suǒ shì zǒng chí suǒ xué xíng？
What is the practice trained in for this dharani?
無眼行、無色行、無眼色識行；
wú yǎn xíng、 wú sè xíng、 wú yǎn sè shí xíng；
Practice without eye, without form, without eye-form consciousness;
無耳行、無聲行、無耳聲識行；
wú ěr xíng、 wú shēng xíng、 wú ěr shēng shí xíng；
without ear, without sound, without ear-sound consciousness;
無鼻行、無香行、無鼻香識行；
wú bí xíng、 wú xiāng xíng、 wú bí xiāng shí xíng；
without nose, without scent, without nose-scent consciousness;
無舌行、無味行、無舌味識行；
wú shé xíng、 wú wèi xíng、 wú shé wèi shí xíng；
without tongue, without taste, without tongue-taste consciousness;
無身行、無細滑行、無身細滑識行；
wú shēn xíng、 wú xì huá xíng、 wú shēn xì huá shí xíng；
without body, without touch, without body-touch consciousness;
無心行、無法行、無心法識行。
wú xīn xíng、 wú fǎ xíng、 wú xīn fǎ shí xíng。
without mind, without dharmas, without mind-dharma consciousness.
無色行、無色生行、無色滅行、無色處行；
wú sè xíng、 wú sè shēng xíng、 wú sè miè xíng、 wú sè chù xíng；
Practice without form, without the arising of form, without the ceasing of form, without the ground of form;
無痛、想、行、識行，
wú tòng、 xiǎng、 xíng、 shí xíng，
without sensation, perception, formations or consciousness;
無識生行、無識滅行、無識處行。
wú shí shēng xíng、 wú shí miè xíng、 wú shí chù xíng。
without the arising of consciousness, its ceasing, or its ground.
一切無行，是應總持行。
yī qiè wú xíng， shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
Practice in which there is nothing at all — this is the practice that answers to dharani.
「復次，龍王！其行色空，心不空色，
「fù cì， lóng wáng！ qí xíng sè kōng， xīn bù kōng sè，
“Further, Dragon King: to practice form as empty, while the mind does not empty form —
是應總持行。
shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
this is the practice that answers to dharani.
痛、想、行、識，其行識空，心不空識，
tòng、 xiǎng、 xíng、 shí， qí xíng shí kōng， xīn bù kōng shí，
So with sensation, perception, formations and consciousness: to practice consciousness as empty, while the mind does not empty consciousness —
是應總持行。
shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
this is the practice that answers to dharani.
復次，其無想色行，不念無想行，
fù cì， qí wú xiǎng sè xíng， bù niàn wú xiǎng xíng，
Further, to practice form without perception, yet not conceive of practice without perception —
是應總持行。
shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
this is the practice that answers to dharani.
痛、想、行、識亦復如是，
tòng、 xiǎng、 xíng、 shí yì fù rú shì，
So likewise with sensation, perception, formations and consciousness:
其無想識行，不念無想識行，是應總持行。
qí wú xiǎng shí xíng， bù niàn wú xiǎng shí xíng， shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
to practice consciousness without perception, yet not conceive of it — this is the practice that answers to dharani.
復次，其不斷色行，於色行無行，
fù cì， qí bù duàn sè xíng， yú sè xíng wú xíng，
Further, not to sever the practice of form, yet in the practice of form to have no practice;
不色生行，不色起行，不色寂行，
bù sè shēng xíng， bù sè qǐ xíng， bù sè jì xíng，
not to practice form as arising, as originating, or as stilled;
色如諦行，色如本淨行，
sè rú dì xíng， sè rú běn jìng xíng，
to practice form as it truly is, to practice form as originally pure,
亦不念色如諦、本淨行，是應總持行。
yì bù niàn sè rú dì、 běn jìng xíng， shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
yet not to conceive of practicing form as truly so or originally pure — this is the practice that answers to dharani.
痛、想、行、識亦復如是，
tòng、 xiǎng、 xíng、 shí yì fù rú shì，
So likewise with sensation, perception, formations and consciousness:
其不斷識行，於識行無行，
qí bù duàn shí xíng， yú shí xíng wú xíng，
not to sever the practice of consciousness, yet in it to have no practice;
不識生行，不識起行，不識寂行，
bù shí shēng xíng， bù shí qǐ xíng， bù shí jì xíng，
not to practice consciousness as arising, as originating, or as stilled;
識如諦行，識如本淨行，
shí rú dì xíng， shí rú běn jìng xíng，
to practice consciousness as it truly is and as originally pure,
亦不念識如諦、本淨行，是應總持行。
yì bù niàn shí rú dì、 běn jìng xíng， shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
yet not to conceive of it — this is the practice that answers to dharani.
復次，於種由法界行，不想法界行，
fù cì， yú zhǒng yóu fǎ jiè xíng， bù xiǎng fǎ jiè xíng，
Further, to practice from the seed by way of the dharma-realm, yet not conceive of practicing the dharma-realm;
不想法界諸入本淨空行，不想本淨空，
bù xiǎng fǎ jiè zhū rù běn jìng kōng xíng， bù xiǎng běn jìng kōng，
not to conceive of practicing the dharma-realm and sense-fields as originally pure and empty, nor conceive of original purity and emptiness —
是應總持行。
shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
this is the practice that answers to dharani.
若一切法緣起之行，不想緣起，是應總持行。
ruò yī qiè fǎ yuán qǐ zhī xíng， bù xiǎng yuán qǐ， shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
To practice all dharmas as dependently arisen, yet not conceive of dependent arising — this is the practice that answers to dharani.
不著諸法行，不猗不著行，是應總持行。
bù zhuó zhū fǎ xíng， bù yī bù zhuó xíng， shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
To practice without clinging to dharmas, and to practice neither leaning nor clinging — this is the practice that answers to dharani.
諸法如本無行，不壞諸法本無之行；
zhū fǎ rú běn wú xíng， bù huài zhū fǎ běn wú zhī xíng；
To practice dharmas as originally nonexistent, without destroying that original nonbeing;
若於諸法住本際行，
ruò yú zhū fǎ zhù běn jì xíng，
to practice abiding at the original limit of dharmas,
不念本際住諸法行，是應總持行。
bù niàn běn jì zhù zhū fǎ xíng， shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
yet not conceive of abiding there — this is the practice that answers to dharani.
復次，其知貪欲行，不於法界想念貪欲行，
fù cì， qí zhī tān yù xíng， bù yú fǎ jiè xiǎng niàn tān yù xíng，
Further, to know the practice of craving, yet not conceive of craving within the dharma-realm —
是應總持行。
shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
this is the practice that answers to dharani.
其知瞋恚行，不於法界想瞋恚行，是應總持行。
qí zhī chēn huì xíng， bù yú fǎ jiè xiǎng chēn huì xíng， shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
To know the practice of anger, yet not conceive of anger within the dharma-realm — this is the practice that answers to dharani.
其知愚癡行，不於法界想愚癡行，是應總持行。
qí zhī yú chī xíng， bù yú fǎ jiè xiǎng yú chī xíng， shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
To know the practice of folly, yet not conceive of folly within the dharma-realm — this is the practice that answers to dharani.
其等分行，不於等分行於法界有所壞行；
qí děng fēn xíng， bù yú děng fēn xíng yú fǎ jiè yǒu suǒ huài xíng；
To practice them in equal measure, without damaging the dharma-realm by that equal practice;
若於八萬四千諸所修行，
ruò yú bā wàn sì qiān zhū suǒ xiū xíng，
and among the eighty-four thousand practices
入于法界無若干行，是應總持行。
rù yú fǎ jiè wú ruò gān xíng， shì yìng zǒng chí xíng。
to enter the dharma-realm where there are no manifold practices — this is the practice that answers to dharani.
若行、若合行，於行、合行而無所行，亦無不行。
ruò xíng、 ruò hé xíng， yú xíng、 hé xíng ér wú suǒ xíng， yì wú bù xíng。
Whether practicing or practicing jointly, in practice and joint practice there is nothing practiced, and nothing not practiced.
所以者何？其行無量亦無所度，亦無所想，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ qí xíng wú liàng yì wú suǒ dù， yì wú suǒ xiǎng，
Why? That practice is immeasurable, delivers nothing, and conceives nothing;
是故彼行為平等行。
shì gù bǐ xíng wéi píng děng xíng。
therefore that practice is the practice of equality.
於平等行亦無所毀，
yú píng děng xíng yì wú suǒ huǐ，
In the practice of equality nothing is damaged;
亦不有為，亦不無為，
yì bù yǒu wéi， yì bù wú wéi，
it is neither conditioned nor unconditioned,
亦不受，亦無不受，無處無住，故曰平等行。
yì bù shòu， yì wú bù shòu， wú chù wú zhù， gù yuē píng děng xíng。
neither received nor not received, without place and without abiding — therefore it is called the practice of equality.
菩薩行如是，則得無盡藏總持之門也。」
pú sà xíng rú shì， zé dé wú jìn zàng zǒng chí zhī mén yě。」
A bodhisattva who practices thus gains the dharani gate of the inexhaustible treasury.”
於是世尊則說頌曰：
yú shì shì zūn zé shuō sòng yuē：
Thereupon the World-Honored One spoke in verse:
「其人心意則清淨，普入經卷度無極，
「qí rén xīn yì zé qīng jìng， pǔ rù jīng juǎn dù wú jí，
“Such a one's mind is pure; he enters all scriptures and the perfections without limit,
悉解眾人之音聲，得總持時乃如是。
xī jiě zhòng rén zhī yīn shēng， dé zǒng chí shí nǎi rú shì。
and understands the speech of every being — such is it when dharani is gained.
觀知群萠心所行，善惡所念及中間，
guān zhī qún méng xīn suǒ xíng， shàn è suǒ niàn jí zhōng jiān，
He sees what the multitudes' minds do, their thoughts of good and evil and between,
分別本性所造興，則為說法隨所應。
fēn bié běn xìng suǒ zào xìng， zé wèi shuō fǎ suí suǒ yìng。
discerns what their original nature has produced, and preaches as befits them.
悉了因緣之報應，令不覩常及無常，
xī le yīn yuán zhī bào yìng， lìng bù dǔ cháng jí wú cháng，
He fully knows the recompense of causes and conditions and keeps them from seeing permanence or impermanence,
皆以棄捐墮邊際，分別總持隨順化。
jiē yǐ qì juān duò biān jì， fēn bié zǒng chí suí shùn huà。
casting off all falling into extremes, discerning dharani and converting in accord.
明解文字之方便，知無央數之音響，
míng jiě wén zì zhī fāng biàn， zhī wú yāng shù zhī yīn xiǎng，
He clearly understands the skillful means of letters and knows countless sounds,
曉了義理微妙好，得總持者乃如是。
xiǎo liǎo yì lǐ wēi miào hǎo， dé zǒng chí zhě nǎi rú shì。
and comprehends the subtle beauty of meaning — such is one who gains dharani.
逮得天眼無垢污，天耳清淨亦如是，
dǎi dé tiān yǎn wú gòu wū， tiān ěr qīng jìng yì rú shì，
He attains the deva eye, unstained, and the deva ear likewise pure,
無量智慧知眾行，念億千劫去來事。
wú liàng zhì huì zhī zhòng xíng， niàn yì qiān jié qù lái shì。
immeasurable wisdom knowing all conduct, recalling the affairs of a million thousand kalpas past and future.
獲四神足亦如是，至無量國須臾頃，
huò sì shén zú yì rú shì， zhì wú liàng guó xū yú qǐng，
He gains the four bases of spiritual power, reaching immeasurable lands in a moment,
供無數億諸導師，聞所講法則總持。
gōng wú shù yì zhū dǎo shī， wén suǒ jiǎng fǎ zé zǒng chí。
makes offerings to countless millions of guiding teachers, and retains the Dharma he hears.
若干億魔至百千，不能覩知境界行，
ruò gān yì mó zhì bǎi qiān， bù néng dǔ zhī jìng jiè xíng，
Though millions upon hundreds of thousands of maras come, they cannot perceive the range of his conduct;
清淨之人無塵埃，講說經法無數千。
qīng jìng zhī rén wú chén āi， jiǎng shuō jīng fǎ wú shù qiān。
the pure one is without a speck of dust, and expounds the sutras by the thousand.
譬如蓮華無著水，不猗世法亦如是，
pì rú lián huā wú zhuó shuǐ， bù yī shì fǎ yì rú shì，
As the lotus is unstained by water, so he leans not upon worldly dharmas,
常以解脫諸有無，等心一切如虛空。
cháng yǐ jiě tuō zhū yǒu wú， děng xīn yī qiè rú xū kōng。
ever freed from being and non-being, his mind impartial toward all as empty space.
持最色相而勇猛，眾人觀仰無厭足，
chí zuì sè xiàng ér yǒng měng， zhòng rén guān yǎng wú yàn zú，
Bearing the finest form and valiant, the multitude gaze up at him and never tire,
進止安詳行無缺，愍傷群萠故遊世。
jìn zhǐ ān xiáng xíng wú quē， mǐn shāng qún méng gù yóu shì。
his going and staying serene, his conduct without flaw, and he moves in the world out of pity for the multitudes.
天帝釋梵及護世，皆以恭敬稽首禮，
tiān dì shì fàn jí hù shì， jiē yǐ gōng jìng qǐ shǒu lǐ，
Sakra, Brahma and the world-protectors all bow their heads in reverence;
其心不以憍慢說，得總持時亦如是。
qí xīn bù yǐ jiāo màn shuō， dé zǒng chí shí yì rú shì。
yet his mind speaks without arrogance — such is it when dharani is gained.
口言柔軟如梵音，為眾人說可其心，
kǒu yán róu ruǎn rú fàn yīn， wéi zhòng rén shuō kě qí xīn，
His speech is gentle as Brahma's voice, and he preaches to each in a way their minds can accept,
溫潤流利言得時，所可教化無所覆。
wēn rùn liú lì yán dé shí， suǒ kě jiào huà wú suǒ fù。
warm, flowing and timely, and what he teaches he conceals from none.
在於眾中無所畏，為師子吼妙無難，
zài yú zhòng zhōng wú suǒ wèi， wéi shī zǐ hǒu miào wú nán，
Amid the assembly he is without fear, giving the lion's roar, wondrous and unhindered,
降伏一切眾麁人，得淨總持為如是。
xiáng fú yī qiè zhòng cū rén， dé jìng zǒng chí wéi rú shì。
subduing all coarse people — such is it to gain the pure dharani.
其諛諂人難調化，興于憍傲而自大，
qí yú chǎn rén nán diào huà， xìng yú jiāo ào ér zì dà，
Flattering people are hard to tame, rising in arrogance and self-conceit;
聞彼聖明所說法，即棄貢高稽首禮。
wén bǐ shèng míng suǒ shuō fǎ， jí qì gòng gāo qǐ shǒu lǐ。
yet hearing the Dharma preached by that holy and bright one, they cast off pride and bow their heads.
入於本淨寂法界，以達義歸解諸法，
rù yú běn jìng jì fǎ jiè， yǐ dá yì guī jiě zhū fǎ，
Entering the originally pure and still dharma-realm, penetrating the resort of meaning, he understands all dharmas;
以故所說無窮已，分別文字知法律。
yǐ gù suǒ shuō wú qióng yǐ， fēn bié wén zì zhī fǎ lǜ。
therefore what he preaches has no end, and discerning letters he knows the Dharma and discipline.
人之本性法界淨，曉眾生淨亦如是，
rén zhī běn xìng fǎ jiè jìng， xiǎo zhòng shēng jìng yì rú shì，
The original nature of a person is the pure dharma-realm, and he knows beings to be pure likewise;
解知本無人本無，所說經法無罣礙。
jiě zhī běn wú rén běn wú， suǒ shuō jīng fǎ wú guà ài。
understanding original nonbeing and that the person is originally nonexistent, he preaches without obstruction.
所盡無盡不可知，無盡之事無能盡，
suǒ jǐn wú jǐn bù kě zhī， wú jǐn zhī shì wú néng jǐn，
What is exhausted and inexhaustible cannot be known; the inexhaustible none can exhaust;
覺了知是趣寂寞，則說無住億經卷。
jué liǎo zhī shì qù jì mò， zé shuō wú zhù yì jīng juǎn。
knowing this, he turns toward stillness and preaches millions of scriptures without abiding.
斯諸文字不處身，亦不在意不住心，
sī zhū wén zì bù chù shēn， yì bù zài yì bú zhù xīn，
These letters do not reside in the body, nor lie in thought, nor abide in mind;
文字本性空寂寞，譬如山中呼聲響。
wén zì běn xìng kōng jì mò， pì rú shān zhōng hū shēng xiǎng。
the original nature of letters is empty and still, like an echo called forth among the mountains.
計總持者不著字，無音無言無說聲，
jì zǒng chí zhě bù zhuó zì， wú yīn wú yán wú shuō shēng，
One reckoned to hold dharani does not cling to letters — no sound, no word, no voice of speech;
以知文字所趣然，假使所說無罣礙。
yǐ zhī wén zì suǒ qù rán， jiǎ shǐ suǒ shuō wú guà ài。
knowing whither letters tend, whatever he preaches is unobstructed.
無意無想亦無心，設有所說無所念，
wú yì wú xiǎng yì wú xīn， shè yǒu suǒ shuō wú suǒ niàn，
Without intent, without perception, without mind — and if he preaches, he conceives nothing;
又復解知去法慧，講順說經隨所應。
yòu fù jiě zhī qù fǎ huì， jiǎng shùn shuō jīng suí suǒ yìng。
understanding besides the wisdom of dharmas gone by, he expounds the sutras as befits.
以入分別四句義，曉了義理明識法，
yǐ rù fēn bié sì jù yì， xiǎo liǎo yì lǐ míng shí fǎ，
Entering the discernment of the four phrases of meaning, he comprehends the sense and clearly knows the Dharma,
究暢音聲順所聽，故講無著不可量。
jiū chàng yīn shēng shùn suǒ tīng， gù jiǎng wú zhuó bù kě liàng。
fathoms sound in accord with what is heard, and so preaches unattached and immeasurably.
翫習本原承其慧，故說深要若干法，
wán xí běn yuán chéng qí huì， gù shuō shēn yào ruò gān fǎ，
Practicing the source and receiving its wisdom, he preaches the profound essentials, dharmas manifold;
所解之慧了逆順，有趣頂法度無極。
suǒ jiě zhī huì le nì shùn， yǒu qù dǐng fǎ dù wú jí。
the wisdom he understands knows both contrary and concordant, and tends to the summit dharma and the perfections without limit.
方便所有諸怯弱，以用救攝諸卒暴，
fāng biàn suǒ yǒu zhū qiè ruò， yǐ yòng jiù shè zhū cù bào，
By skillful means he gathers all the timid and rescues all the violent,
明識所作為解說，得總持者乃如是。
míng shí suǒ zuò wéi jiě shuō， dé zǒng chí zhě nǎi rú shì。
clearly knowing what they do and explaining it — such is one who gains dharani.
其身口意皆已寂，分別諸慧不著古，
qí shēn kǒu yì jiē yǐ jì， fēn bié zhū huì bù zhuó gǔ，
His body, speech and mind are wholly stilled; discerning every wisdom, he clings not to the past;
所言無厭除瞋恨，得住總持為勇猛。
suǒ yán wú yàn chú chēn hèn， dé zhù zǒng chí wéi yǒng měng。
his words never weary, hatred is removed, and abiding in dharani he is valiant.
其所總持心執御，意之所入住法慧，
qí suǒ zǒng chí xīn zhí yù， yì zhī suǒ rù zhù fǎ huì，
What he holds by dharani his mind takes up and guides; where his thought enters, it abides in Dharma-wisdom;
其有聞者未曾忘，順如聽采等經典。
qí yǒu wén zhě wèi céng wàng， shùn rú tīng cǎi děng jīng diǎn。
whatever he hears he never forgets, gathering the scriptures alike as he listens in accord.
其總持義法不亂，計法行之無所入，
qí zǒng chí yì fǎ bù luàn， jì fǎ xíng zhī wú suǒ rù，
The meaning of his dharani is Dharma undisturbed; reckoning the practice of Dharma, he enters nowhere;
以法等故曰平等，如應平等順清淨。」
yǐ fǎ děng gù yuē píng děng， rú yìng píng děng shùn qīng jìng。」
and because Dharma is equal it is called equality — fitting, equal, and in accord with purity.”
分別名品第八
fēn bié míng pǐn dì bā
Chapter Eight: Discerning the Name
佛說此章句偈時，海龍王眷屬萬三千龍，
fó shuō cǐ zhāng jù jì shí， hǎi lóng wáng juàn shǔ wàn sān qiān lóng，
When the Buddha had spoken these chapters, phrases and verses, thirteen thousand dragons of the Sea Dragon King's retinue
皆發無上正真道意，則更啟曰：
jiē fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì， zé gèng qǐ yuē：
all aroused the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment, and further said:
「廣宣此言，唯然，世尊！
「guǎng xuān cǐ yán， wéi rán， shì zūn！
“Proclaim these words widely! So be it, World-Honored One!
我等亦當逮是無盡之藏總持也，
wǒ děng yì dāng dǎi shì wú jǐn zhī zàng zǒng chí yě，
We too shall attain this dharani of the inexhaustible treasury,
當為一切眾生之類廣說經法！」
dāng wéi yī qiè zhòng shēng zhī lèi guǎng shuō jīng fǎ！」
and shall preach the Dharma of the sutras widely for every kind of living being!”
爾時賢者舍利弗白佛言：
ěr shí xián zhě shè lì fú bái fó yán：
Then the venerable Sariputra said to the Buddha:
「至未曾有。世尊！
「zhì wèi céng yǒu。 shì zūn！
“This is unprecedented, World-Honored One!
乃令諸龍發無上正真道意，
nǎi lìng zhū lóng fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì，
That dragons should arouse the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment,
人反不能發大道也！」
rén fǎn bù néng fā dà dào yě！」
while humans, on the contrary, cannot arouse the great Way!”
佛告舍利弗：「是萬三千龍，
fó gào shè lì fú： 「shì wàn sān qiān lóng，
The Buddha told Sariputra: “These thirteen thousand dragons,
迦葉佛時皆作沙門，
jiā shè fó shí jiē zuò shā mén，
in the time of Kasyapa Buddha, were all sramanas.
從迦葉如來一反聞菩薩行，
cóng jiā shè rú lái yī fǎn wén pú sà xíng，
From the Tathagata Kasyapa they once heard of the bodhisattva's conduct
同時歡喜讚曰：
tóng shí huān xǐ zàn yuē：
and at that moment rejoiced and praised it, saying:
『善哉，善哉！說大乘事不可思議！』
『shàn zāi， shàn zāi！ shuō dà chéng shì bù kě sī yì！』
‘Excellent, excellent! What is said of the Great Vehicle is inconceivable!’
與族黨知友俱行分衛，
yǔ zú dǎng zhī yǒu jù xíng fēn wèi，
But going on the alms-round with their kin and companions,
貪利不慎，不護禁戒，
tān lì bù shèn， bù hù jìn jiè，
they were greedy for gain and careless, and did not guard the precepts;
以是之故，壽終之後墮於龍中。
yǐ shì zhī gù， shòu zhōng zhī hòu duò yú lóng zhōng。
and for this reason, when their lives ended, they fell among the dragons.
彼時從迦葉佛聞大乘教，讚迦葉佛，
bǐ shí cóng jiā shè fó wén dà chéng jiào， zàn jiā shè fó，
Yet because they had then heard the Great Vehicle teaching from Kasyapa Buddha and praised him,
因由報應德本之緣，
yīn yóu bào yìng dé běn zhī yuán，
by the condition of that recompense and root of virtue,
今聞吾說諮嗟大乘，講無盡藏總持，
jīn wén wú shuō zī jiē dà chéng， jiǎng wú jìn zàng zǒng chí，
they now hear me extol the Great Vehicle and expound the dharani of the inexhaustible treasury,
皆發無上正真道意。
jiē fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì。
and all have aroused the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment.
舍利弗！觀是至心之奇特，
shè lì fú！ guān shì zhì xīn zhī qí tè，
Sariputra, behold how extraordinary is such utmost sincerity of mind!
今吾授決，恒沙等劫供養諸佛積累道品，
jīn wú shòu jué， héng shā děng jié gòng yǎng zhū fó jī lèi dào pǐn，
I now give them the prediction: for kalpas as many as the sands of the Ganges they will make offerings to the buddhas and amass the limbs of the Way,
自致得成無上正真道，
zì zhì dé chéng wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào，
and will themselves come to accomplish unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment,
號曰慧上、智上、法上、梵上，
hào yuē huì shàng、 zhì shàng、 fǎ shàng、 fàn shàng，
named Wisdom Supreme, Knowledge Supreme, Dharma Supreme and Brahma Supreme.
得成佛時，以是四事號世界曰無垢藏，
dé chéng fó shí， yǐ shì sì shì hào shì jiè yuē wú gòu zàng，
When they become buddhas, by these four names their world will be called Undefiled Treasury
劫名大欣，
jié míng dà xīn，
and the kalpa Great Gladness.
皆同一劫得成無上正真道最正覺，
jiē tóng yī jié dé chéng wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào zuì zhèng jué，
All in that same kalpa will accomplish unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment, the supreme awakening,
猶如賢劫當興千佛。」
yóu rú xián jié dāng xìng qiān fó。」
just as in the Worthy Kalpa a thousand buddhas will arise.”
授決品第九
shòu jué pǐn dì jiǔ
Chapter Nine: Bestowing the Prediction
於是海龍王白佛言：
yú shì hǎi lóng wáng bái fó yán：
Thereupon the Sea Dragon King said to the Buddha:
「我從初劫住止大海，
「wǒ cóng chū jié zhù zhǐ dà hǎi，
“Since the first kalpa I have dwelt in the great ocean.
從拘樓秦如來興於世來，
cóng jū lóu qín rú lái xìng yú shì lái，
From the time the Tathagata Krakucchanda appeared in the world,
大海之中諸龍妻子眷屬甚少，
dà hǎi zhī zhōng zhū lóng qī zǐ juàn shǔ shèn shǎo，
the dragons in the great ocean, with their wives, children and retinues, were very few;
今海龍眾妻子、眷屬繁裔弘多，
jīn hǎi lóng zhòng qī zǐ、 juàn shǔ fán yì hóng duō，
but now the dragon host of the sea, with wives, children and retinues, has multiplied and grown vast,
設欲計挍不可窮盡。
shè yù jì jiào bù kě qióng jǐn。
so that were one to reckon them there would be no end.
唯然，世尊！如此云何有何變怪？」
wéi rán， shì zūn！ rú cǐ yún hé yǒu hé biàn guài？」
So be it, World-Honored One — how is this? What strange change has occurred?”
佛告龍王：「其於佛法出家奉律行戒，
fó gào lóng wáng： 「qí yú fó fǎ chū jiā fèng lǜ xíng jiè，
The Buddha told the Dragon King: “Those who go forth in the Buddha's Dharma, uphold the discipline and keep the precepts,
不具現戒成就，違戒犯行，
bù jù xiàn jiè chéng jiù， wéi jiè fàn xíng，
but do not fully perfect the precepts as they stand, and transgress and offend against them,
不捨直見，不墮地獄。
bù shě zhí jiàn， bù duò dì yù。
yet do not abandon right view, do not fall into hell.
如斯之類壽終已後皆生龍中。」
rú sī zhī lèi shòu zhōng yǐ hòu jiē shēng lóng zhōng。」
Such as these, when their lives end, are all born among the dragons.”
佛語龍王：「拘樓秦佛時，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「jū lóu qín fó shí，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “In the time of Krakucchanda Buddha,
九十八億居家出家，違其禁戒，皆生龍中。
jiǔ shí bā yì jū jiā chū jiā， wéi qí jìn jiè， jiē shēng lóng zhōng。
nine billion eight hundred million householders and renunciants transgressed the precepts and were all born among the dragons.
拘那含牟尼佛時，八十億居家出家毀戒恣心，
jū nà hán móu ní fó shí， bā shí yì jū jiā chū jiā huǐ jiè zì xīn，
In the time of Kanakamuni Buddha, eight billion householders and renunciants broke the precepts and followed their own whims;
壽終之後皆生龍中。
shòu zhōng zhī hòu jiē shēng lóng zhōng。
when their lives ended they were all born among the dragons.
迦葉佛時，六十四億居家出家犯戒，
jiā shè fó shí， liù shí sì yì jū jiā chū jiā fàn jiè，
In the time of Kasyapa Buddha, six billion four hundred million householders and renunciants offended against the precepts;
壽終之後皆生龍中。
shòu zhōng zhī hòu jiē shēng lóng zhōng。
when their lives ended they were all born among the dragons.
於我世中，九百九十億居家出家，
yú wǒ shì zhōng， jiǔ bǎi jiǔ shí yì jū jiā chū jiā，
In my own age, ninety-nine billion householders and renunciants,
若干鬪諍，習若干行，誹謗經戒，
ruò gān dòu zhēng， xí ruò gān xíng， fěi bàng jīng jiè，
quarrelling in many ways, cultivating many practices, slandering the sutras and precepts,
壽終之後皆生龍中，今有生者。」
shòu zhōng zhī hòu jiē shēng lóng zhōng， jīn yǒu shēng zhě。」
when their lives ended were all born among the dragons — and some are being born even now.”
佛語龍王：「以是之故，仁在大海中，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「yǐ shì zhī gù， rén zài dà hǎi zhōng，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “For this reason, worthy one, in the great ocean
諸龍妻子眷屬不可稱計。
zhū lóng qī zǐ juàn shǔ bù kě chēng jì。
the dragons with their wives, children and retinues are beyond all counting.
我般泥洹後，多有惡比丘、惡優婆塞，
wǒ bān ní huán hòu， duō yǒu è bǐ qiū、 è yōu pó sāi，
After my parinirvana there will be many evil bhiksus and evil laymen
違失禁戒，當生龍中，或墮地獄。」
wéi shī jìn jiè， dāng shēng lóng zhōng， huò duò dì yù。」
who transgress and lose the precepts; they will be born among the dragons, or fall into hell.”
海龍王白佛言：「於今棄家為道，
hǎi lóng wáng bái fó yán： 「yú jīn qì jiā wéi dào，
The Sea Dragon King said to the Buddha: “Those who now leave the household for the Way,
犯戒比丘墮龍中者，有何殊特？」
fàn jiè bǐ qiū duò lóng zhōng zhě， yǒu hé shū tè？」
bhiksus who break the precepts and fall among the dragons — what is distinctive about them?”
佛言：「棄家學行，
fó yán： 「qì jiā xué xíng，
The Buddha said: “Those who left the household to train,
於今犯戒比丘墮龍中者，
yú jīn fàn jiè bǐ qiū duò lóng zhōng zhě，
the bhiksus who now break the precepts and fall among the dragons —
行於方便，不能清淨。
xíng yú fāng biàn， bù néng qīng jìng。
they practiced by expedient means but could not be pure.
又有至心信於佛法，
yòu yǒu zhì xīn xìn yú fó fǎ，
Yet some among them believed in the Buddha's Dharma with utmost sincerity,
以至心力龍中壽終，生天上、人間，
yǐ zhì xīn lì lóng zhōng shòu zhōng， shēng tiān shàng、 rén jiān，
and by the power of that sincerity, when their lives among the dragons end, they are born among devas or men,
當見賢劫所興諸佛，皆當見之。
dāng jiàn xián jié suǒ xìng zhū fó， jiē dāng jiàn zhī。
and will behold all the buddhas who arise in the Worthy Kalpa — every one of them.
假使不以解脫者，悉於拔陀劫中般泥洹，
jiǎ shǐ bù yǐ jiě tuō zhě， xī yú bá tuó jié zhōng bān ní huán，
And those not thereby liberated will all enter parinirvana within the Bhadra Kalpa,
除志大乘者。
chú zhì dà chéng zhě。
excepting those who aspire to the Great Vehicle.
龍王！且觀佛教廣大因緣出家之奇特，
lóng wáng！ qiě guān fó jiào guǎng dà yīn yuán chū jiā zhī qí tè，
Dragon King, behold how vast are the causes and conditions of the Buddha's teaching, how extraordinary is going forth:
棄諸惡法，得超異類。」
qì zhū è fǎ， dé chāo yì lèi。」
casting off evil dharmas, one rises above one's kind.”
爾時有龍王子，號曰威首，前白佛言：
ěr shí yǒu lóng wáng zǐ， hào yuē wēi shǒu， qián bái fó yán：
At that time a dragon prince named Awesome Crest came forward and said to the Buddha:
「至未曾有。世尊！
「zhì wèi céng yǒu。 shì zūn！
“This is unprecedented, World-Honored One!
龍近如來難值難聞，
lóng jìn rú lái nán zhí nán wén，
For a dragon to draw near the Tathagata is hard to encounter and hard to hear of.
雖有所毀，作眾罪殃，
suī yǒu suǒ huǐ， zuò zhòng zuì yāng，
Though one has transgressed and committed many sins and calamities,
發一善意，心念佛法，終不失德；
fā yī shàn yì， xīn niàn fó fǎ， zhōng bù shī dé；
if he rouses a single wholesome thought and recollects the Buddha's Dharma, that virtue is never lost;
緣是之行，至得滅度。
yuán shì zhī xíng， zhì dé miè dù。
and by that conduct he comes at last to final quiescence.
今我願發無上正真道意，
jīn wǒ yuàn fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì，
Now I vow to arouse the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment,
用佛、世尊難值難聞，
yòng fó、 shì zūn nán zhí nán wén，
since the Buddha, the World-Honored One, is hard to encounter and hard to hear of.
令菩薩行無有違缺，至于道場，
lìng pú sà xíng wú yǒu wéi quē， zhì yú dào chǎng，
May my bodhisattva conduct be without transgression or lack until I reach the bodhimanda (place of practice);
莫使心中忘失德本，
mò shǐ xīn zhōng wàng shī dé běn，
may my mind never forget or lose its roots of virtue;
大慈、大哀、大喜、大護，
dà cí、 dà āi、 dà xǐ、 dà hù，
great loving-kindness, great compassion, great joy, great equanimity;
所生之處常見諸佛，得聞經法，
suǒ shēng zhī chù cháng jiàn zhū fó， dé wén jīng fǎ，
and wherever I am born may I always behold the buddhas and hear the Dharma,
供養眾僧，開化眾生。」
gòng yǎng zhòng sēng， kāi huà zhòng shēng。」
make offerings to the Sangha, and convert living beings.”
爾時世尊告威首龍王子曰：
ěr shí shì zūn gào wēi shǒu lóng wáng zǐ yuē：
Then the World-Honored One said to the dragon prince Awesome Crest:
「善哉，善哉！仁者之問，
「shàn zāi， shàn zāi！ rén zhě zhī wèn，
“Excellent, excellent! Your question, worthy one,
乃發救護一切之心。
nǎi fā jiù hù yī qiè zhī xīn。
arises from a mind that would rescue and shelter all.
今汝至心興無極哀，而起道意，
jīn rǔ zhì xīn xìng wú jí āi， ér qǐ dào yì，
Now with utmost sincerity you have roused boundless compassion and the aspiration to the Way.
緣彼德本如來嗟歎，
yuán bǐ dé běn rú lái jiē tàn，
Were the Tathagata to extol that root of virtue
七日、七月、若至一年，
qī rì、 qī yuè、 ruò zhì yī nián，
for seven days, seven months, or even a full year,
為功德福而不可盡，
wéi gōng dé fú ér bù kě jǐn，
the merit and blessing of it could not be exhausted —
所植善行乃如是也！」
suǒ zhí shàn xíng nǎi rú shì yě！」
so great is the wholesome conduct you have planted!”
佛見威首龍王子心之所念，即時欣笑。
fó jiàn wēi shǒu lóng wáng zǐ xīn zhī suǒ niàn， jí shí xīn xiào。
The Buddha, seeing what the dragon prince Awesome Crest held in mind, smiled with delight.
諸佛笑法，無央數色，色色各異，
zhū fó xiào fǎ， wú yāng shù sè， sè sè gè yì，
It is the way of buddhas when they smile that countless colors appear, each color distinct;
光從口出，照不可計諸佛世界，
guāng cóng kǒu chū， zhào bù kě jì zhū fó shì jiè，
light issued from his mouth and illumined incalculable buddha-worlds,
遶身三匝，還從頂入。
rào shēn sān zā， hái cóng dǐng rù。
circled his body three times, and returned to enter at the crown of his head.
爾時賢者阿難以偈讚佛：
ěr shí xián zhě ā nán yǐ jì zàn fó：
Then the venerable Ananda praised the Buddha in verse:
「百福功德莊嚴身，體諸相好三十二，
「bǎi fú gōng dé zhuāng yán shēn， tǐ zhū xiàng hǎo sān shí èr，
“A hundred blessings and merits adorn your body, which bears the thirty-two marks and signs,
清淨無垢如月光，今之所笑何感欣？
qīng jìng wú gòu rú yuè guāng， jīn zhī suǒ xiào hé gǎn xīn？
pure and undefiled as moonlight — what has moved you now to smile?
靜無塵埃離三垢，如百葉華行無惓，
jìng wú chén āi lí sān gòu， rú bǎi yè huā xíng wú quán，
Still, without a speck of dust, free of the three stains, like a hundred-petaled flower, tireless in conduct,
天人龍神所奉敬，安住今者何因笑？
tiān rén lóng shén suǒ fèng jìng， ān zhù jīn zhě hé yīn xiào？
revered by devas, men, dragons and spirits — Steadfast One, from what cause do you smile?
善哉平等齒普淨，十力威曜面香潔，
shàn zāi píng děng chǐ pǔ jìng， shí lì wēi yào miàn xiāng jié，
Excellent! Your teeth are even and wholly pure, your face radiant with the ten powers, fragrant and clean,
以除生死之根原，今者世尊笑何感？
yǐ chú shēng sǐ zhī gēn yuán， jīn zhě shì zūn xiào hé gǎn？
and you have removed the root of birth and death — what moves the World-Honored One to smile?
心如虛空無瑕穢，意等善友及怨家，
xīn rú xū kōng wú xiá huì， yì děng shàn yǒu jí yuàn jiā，
Your mind is like empty space, without flaw or stain; your thought is equal toward friend and foe;
持志如地無憎愛，唯願世尊笑何欣？
chí zhì rú dì wú zēng ài， wéi yuàn shì zūn xiào hé xīn？
your resolve is steady as the earth, without aversion or attachment — we pray, what gladdens you?
音聲所講喻梵天，猶雷哀鸞微妙響，
yīn shēng suǒ jiǎng yù fàn tiān， yóu léi āi luán wēi miào xiǎng，
The voice in which you preach is likened to Brahma's, like thunder, like the plaintive luan, a subtle resonance;
所說柔軟莫不歡，唯願世尊歎因緣。
suǒ shuō róu ruǎn mò bù huān， wéi yuàn shì zūn tàn yīn yuán。
your gentle words gladden all — we pray the World-Honored One declare the cause.
心於聖慧無所著，知三世人意所行，
xīn yú shèng huì wú suǒ zhuó， zhī sān shì rén yì suǒ xíng，
Your mind clings to nothing in holy wisdom; you know what beings of the three times intend,
解眾人根得喜悅，導師今笑為何感？
jiě zhòng rén gēn dé xǐ yuè， dǎo shī jīn xiào wéi hé gǎn？
and understanding their faculties you bring them joy — Guiding Teacher, what moves your smile?
以為成醫王，療治眾生病，
yǐ wéi chéng yī wáng， liáo zhì zhòng shēng bìng，
You have become the king of physicians, curing the illnesses of beings,
能施究竟安，世尊笑何緣？
néng shī jiū jìng ān， shì zūn xiào hé yuán？
able to bestow ultimate peace — World-Honored One, from what cause is your smile?
護德為我說，諸天人民聞，
hù dé wéi wǒ shuō， zhū tiān rén mín wén，
Guardian of virtue, tell it to me; when devas and people hear it
皆當懷喜踊，即志諸通慧。」
jiē dāng huái xǐ yǒng， jí zhì zhū tōng huì。」
they will all be filled with joy and set their minds at once on penetrating wisdom.”
佛告賢者阿難：「寧見威首龍王子住於佛前，
fó gào xián zhě ā nán： 「níng jiàn wēi shǒu lóng wáng zǐ zhù yú fó qián，
The Buddha told the venerable Ananda: “Do you see the dragon prince Awesome Crest standing before the Buddha,
至意發無上正真道意不？」
zhì yì fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì bù？」
arousing with utmost intent the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment?”
阿難對曰：「唯然，已見！」
ā nán duì yuē： 「wéi rán， yǐ jiàn！」
Ananda replied: “So be it, I have seen him.”
佛言：「是威首龍王子，
fó yán： 「shì wēi shǒu lóng wáng zǐ，
The Buddha said: “This dragon prince Awesome Crest,
過八百不可計會無央數劫，當得作佛，
guò bā bǎi bù kě jì huì wú yāng shù jié， dāng dé zuò fó，
after eight hundred incalculable and countless kalpas, will become a buddha
號慧見如來、至真、等正覺，
hào huì jiàn rú lái、 zhì zhēn、 děng zhèng jué，
named the Tathagata Wisdom Sight, Truly Worthy, Perfectly Enlightened;
世界名淨住，劫曰明察。
shì jiè míng jìng zhù， jié yuē míng chá。
his world will be named Pure Abiding and the kalpa Clear Discernment.
是龍王子至誠奉行菩薩之道，
shì lóng wáng zǐ zhì chéng fèng xíng pú sà zhī dào，
This dragon prince will sincerely practice the bodhisattva path,
見無央數諸如來，供養奉事，
jiàn wú yāng shù zhū rú lái， gòng yǎng fèng shì，
behold countless Tathagatas, make offerings and serve them,
常修梵行，開化度脫無量眾生，使立三乘。
cháng xiū fàn xíng， kāi huà dù tuō wú liàng zhòng shēng， shǐ lì sān chéng。
always cultivate the holy life, and convert and deliver immeasurable beings, establishing them in the three vehicles.
慧見如來淨住世界，豐熟安隱，
huì jiàn rú lái jìng zhù shì jiè， fēng shú ān yǐn，
The world Pure Abiding of the Tathagata Wisdom Sight will be fertile and at peace,
五穀平賤，快樂難量，天人充滿，
wǔ gǔ píng jiàn， kuài lè nán liàng， tiān rén chōng mǎn，
the five grains cheap and plentiful, joy beyond measure, filled with devas and men,
猶如炎天被服飲食。
yóu rú yán tiān bèi fú yǐn shí。
their clothing and food as in the Yama heaven.
其佛當壽百萬歲，
qí fó dāng shòu bǎi wàn suì，
That buddha's lifespan will be a million years;
賢聖眾僧聲聞有六十億，
xián shèng zhòng sēng shēng wén yǒu liù shí yì，
his sangha of holy sages will have six billion sravakas
菩薩百二十萬億。
pú sà bǎi èr shí wàn yì。
and a hundred and twenty million million bodhisattvas.
慧見如來其有覲者，皆得慈行三昧。
huì jiàn rú lái qí yǒu jìn zhě， jiē dé cí xíng sān mèi。
All who come to attend the Tathagata Wisdom Sight will gain the samadhi of the practice of loving-kindness.
慧見如來說經，聲聞行者，
huì jiàn rú lái shuō jīng， shēng wén xíng zhě，
When the Tathagata Wisdom Sight preaches the sutras, those practicing as sravakas:
若始見佛，則得道跡；
ruò shǐ jiàn fó， zé dé dào jī；
on first seeing the Buddha attain stream-entry;
再見，得往來；三見，得不還；
zài jiàn， dé wǎng lái； sān jiàn， dé bù huán；
on seeing him a second time, once-returning; a third time, non-returning;
四見，得無所著。
sì jiàn， dé wú suǒ zhuó。
a fourth time, the state of the unattached.
志菩薩乘者，適覲慧見如來得柔順忍；
zhì pú sà chéng zhě， shì jìn huì jiàn rú lái dé róu shùn rěn；
Those aspiring to the bodhisattva vehicle, on first attending the Tathagata Wisdom Sight, gain pliant forbearance;
再見，獲神通；
zài jiàn， huò shén tōng；
a second time, the spiritual penetrations;
三見，得總持辯才；
sān jiàn， dé zǒng chí biàn cái；
a third time, dharani and eloquence;
四見，得不起法忍。
sì jiàn， dé bù qǐ fǎ rěn。
a fourth time, the forbearance of the non-arising of dharmas.
淨住世界無毀戒者，意淨無邪，皆住正見。
jìng zhù shì jiè wú huǐ jiè zhě， yì jìng wú xié， jiē zhù zhèng jiàn。
In the world Pure Abiding none will break the precepts; their minds will be pure and free of crookedness, all abiding in right view.
壽終之後無有惡趣，悉生天上清淨佛土。」
shòu zhōng zhī hòu wú yǒu è qù， xī shēng tiān shàng qīng jìng fó tǔ。」
When their lives end there will be no evil destinies; all will be born in the heavens and in pure buddha-lands.”
時威首龍王子聞佛授決，歡喜踊躍，
shí wēi shǒu lóng wáng zǐ wén fó shòu jué， huān xǐ yǒng yuè，
Then the dragon prince Awesome Crest, hearing the Buddha's prediction, leapt with joy,
善心生焉，奉百千珠瓔用散佛上，
shàn xīn shēng yān， fèng bǎi qiān zhū yīng yòng sàn fó shàng，
a wholesome mind arose in him, and he offered a hundred thousand pearl garlands, scattering them over the Buddha.
而叉十指，以偈讚曰：
ér chā shí zhǐ， yǐ jì zàn yuē：
Then, joining his ten fingers, he praised him in verse:
「人尊無垢如月光，威神無量眾所奉，
「rén zūn wú gòu rú yuè guāng， wēi shén wú liàng zhòng suǒ fèng，
“Honored One among men, undefiled as moonlight, immeasurable in majesty, revered by all,
其力無限總持世，願稽首禮無邊慧。
qí lì wú xiàn zǒng chí shì， yuàn qǐ shǒu lǐ wú biān huì。
your power is without limit and upholds the world — we bow to your boundless wisdom.
慈哀之聖不可限，叡智無瑕不可議，
cí āi zhī shèng bù kě xiàn， ruì zhì wú xiá bù kě yì，
Sage of loving-kindness and compassion, beyond all limit; keen wisdom without flaw, beyond discussion;
禁戒廣普住正定，稽首人尊如虛空。
jìn jiè guǎng pǔ zhù zhèng dìng， qǐ shǒu rén zūn rú xū kōng。
your precepts vast and universal, abiding in right concentration — we bow to the Honored One, like empty space.
無量無限億劫數，所行究竟無不入，
wú liàng wú xiàn yì jié shù， suǒ xíng jiū jìng wú bù rù，
Through immeasurable, limitless millions of kalpas your conduct has been fathomed and enters everywhere;
以故曉知諸眾生，心性所歸諸根本。
yǐ gù xiǎo zhī zhū zhòng shēng， xīn xìng suǒ guī zhū gēn běn。
therefore you know all living beings, the resort of their mind-nature and every root.
若人覩觀尊顏容，一心察之無厭足，
ruò rén dǔ guān zūn yán róng， yī xīn chá zhī wú yàn zú，
If a person gazes on the Honored One's countenance and contemplates it single-mindedly, he never tires;
不為塵埃之所惑，愛欲之穢皆滅盡。
bù wéi chén āi zhī suǒ huò， ài yù zhī huì jiē miè jǐn。
he is not deluded by the dust of the world, and the filth of craving is wholly extinguished.
哀鸞拘夷諸鬼神，梵天之音亦如是，
āi luán jū yí zhū guǐ shén， fàn tiān zhī yīn yì rú shì，
The plaintive luan, the kuyi bird, the spirits, and the voice of Brahma likewise —
聲聞十方甚微妙，如來之音超於彼。
shēng wén shí fāng shèn wēi miào， rú lái zhī yīn chāo yú bǐ。
sounds heard through the ten directions, most subtle; yet the Tathagata's voice surpasses them all.
譬若如日墜於地，海水當竭須彌壞，
pì ruò rú rì zhuì yú dì， hǎi shuǐ dāng jié xū mí huài，
Though the sun should fall to earth, the ocean run dry and Sumeru crumble,
虛空尚裂地反覆，世尊所說終無異。
xū kōng shàng liè dì fǎn fù， shì zūn suǒ shuō zhōng wú yì。
though space should split and the earth turn over — what the World-Honored One says will never vary.
世尊至誠以諦說，授我之別大聖慧，
shì zūn zhì chéng yǐ dì shuō， shòu wǒ zhī bié dà shèng huì，
The World-Honored One, in utmost sincerity, has spoken truly and bestowed on me the prediction, great and holy wisdom;
吾無狐疑結網除，得佛自在為眾祐。
wú wú hú yí jié wǎng chú， dé fó zì zài wéi zhòng yòu。
I have no doubt, the net of fetters is removed, and I shall gain a buddha's freedom as the Blessing of the Multitude.
十方無量億萬國，滿中珍寶供導師，
shí fāng wú liàng yì wàn guó， mǎn zhōng zhēn bǎo gōng dǎo shī，
Though one filled immeasurable millions of lands in the ten directions with treasure to offer the Guiding Teacher,
假使有人發道心，前所植德不及此。
jiǎ shǐ yǒu rén fā dào xīn， qián suǒ zhí dé bù jí cǐ。
if another rouses the mind of the Way, the merit planted before does not equal this.
供養正覺德第一，若人志發尊佛道，
gòng yǎng zhèng jué dé dì yī， ruò rén zhì fā zūn fó dào，
To make offering to the Perfectly Enlightened is virtue foremost; and if one sets his resolve on the honored buddha path,
則為報恩諸十力，用不斷此導師命。」
zé wéi bào ēn zhū shí lì， yòng bù duàn cǐ dǎo shī mìng。」
he repays the kindness of the ten powers, for he does not let the Guiding Teacher's lineage be cut off.”
龍王子說此偈讚佛已，
lóng wáng zǐ shuō cǐ jì zàn fó yǐ，
When the dragon prince had praised the Buddha with this verse,
十千人皆發無上正真道意，悉說是言：
shí qiān rén jiē fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì， xī shuō shì yán：
ten thousand people aroused the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment, and all said:
「慧見如來逮得最正覺時，
「huì jiàn rú lái dǎi dé zuì zhèng jué shí，
“When the Tathagata Wisdom Sight attains supreme enlightenment,
吾等同心共生淨住世界，
wú děng tóng xīn gòng shēng jìng zhù shì jiè，
may we with one mind be born together in the world Pure Abiding,
奉彼如來正法之教，又供養之。
fèng bǐ rú lái zhèng fǎ zhī jiào， yòu gòng yǎng zhī。
uphold that Tathagata's teaching of the true Dharma, and make offerings to him.
佛滅度後，次補其處，得最正覺。」
fó miè dù hòu， cì bǔ qí chù， dé zuì zhèng jué。」
And after that buddha's final quiescence, may we succeed to his place and attain supreme enlightenment.”
佛皆授決，悉當令生淨住世界。
fó jiē shòu jué， xī dāng lìng shēng jìng zhù shì jiè。
The Buddha gave them all the prediction that they would be born in the world Pure Abiding.
佛說海龍王經卷第二
fó shuō hǎi lóng wáng jīng juǎn dì èr
Here ends Volume Two of the Sutra Spoken by the Buddha on the Sea Dragon King.
請佛品第十
qǐng fó pǐn dì shí
Chapter Ten: Inviting the Buddha
爾時海龍王白世尊曰：
ěr shí hǎi lóng wáng bái shì zūn yuē：
At that time the Sea Dragon King addressed the World-Honored One, saying:
「唯佛加哀諸天、龍、神及無量人，
「wéi fó jiā āi zhū tiān、 lóng、 shén jí wú liàng rén，
“May the Buddha extend his compassion to the devas, dragons, spirits, and immeasurable beings,
令致安隱至于大海，
lìng zhì ān yǐn zhì yú dà hǎi，
bringing them in safety and ease to the great ocean,
詣我宮中屈神小食。
yì wǒ gōng zhōng qū shén xiǎo shí。
and condescend to enter my palace to receive a modest meal.
所以者何？
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？
Why do I ask this?
大海之中有龍、鬼神、香音神，
dà hǎi zhī zhōng yǒu lóng、 guǐ shén、 xiāng yīn shén，
Within the great ocean there are dragons, ghosts and spirits, gandharvas (fragrant-sound spirits),
及餘無數眾生之類，
jí yú wú shù zhòng shēng zhī lèi，
and countless other kinds of living beings.
見如來已，皆殖德本，
jiàn rú lái yǐ， jiē zhí dé běn，
Once they have beheld the Tathagata they will all plant roots of virtue;
悉當往會，因聞法音，
xī dāng wǎng huì， yīn wén fǎ yīn，
all will come to the assembly, and through hearing the sound of the Dharma
除斷無底生死之源。
chú duàn wú dǐ shēng sǐ zhī yuán。
will cut off the bottomless source of birth and death.
吾等龍宮并蒙其恩，
wú děng lóng gōng bìng méng qí ēn，
We of the dragon palace will likewise receive that grace,
天上、世間緣得度脫，
tiān shàng、 shì jiān yuán dé dù tuō，
and by this condition the heavens and the world will gain deliverance.
如來普現佛大道心，
rú lái pǔ xiàn fó dà dào xīn，
May the Tathagata universally reveal the great Way-mind of a buddha,
令我等身近道品法。」
lìng wǒ děng shēn jìn dào pǐn fǎ。」
causing us in this very body to draw near the dharmas of the limbs of the Way.”
佛愍龍王，默受其請，
fó mǐn lóng wáng， mò shòu qí qǐng，
The Buddha, pitying the Dragon King, silently accepted his invitation,
及無量人悉當廣殖眾德之本。
jí wú liàng rén xī dāng guǎng zhí zhòng dé zhī běn。
so that immeasurable beings might broadly plant the roots of manifold virtue.
時海龍王見佛就請，歡喜踊躍，
shí hǎi lóng wáng jiàn fó jiù qǐng， huān xǐ yǒng yuè，
Then the Sea Dragon King, seeing that the Buddha had accepted, leapt up in joy;
稽首佛足，右繞三匝，
qǐ shǒu fó zú， yòu rào sān zā，
he bowed his head to the Buddha's feet and circled him three times to the right;
與眷屬俱，忽然不現。
yǔ juàn shǔ jù， hū rán bù xiàn。
then, together with his retinue, he suddenly vanished from sight.
還在大海聚會龍民，而告之曰：
hái zài dà hǎi jù huì lóng mín， ér gào zhī yuē：
Returning to the great ocean he assembled the dragon folk and announced to them:
「吾明日請佛，佛垂矜許，
「wú míng rì qǐng fó， fó chuí jīn xǔ，
“Tomorrow I have invited the Buddha, and the Buddha has graciously consented.
汝等同心當俱供養。」
rǔ děng tóng xīn dāng jù gòng yǎng。」
You must all, of one mind, make offerings together.”
海龍王又告燕居無善神、
hǎi lóng wáng yòu gào yān jū wú shàn shén、
The Sea Dragon King further addressed the asura Yanju,
誑惑縛、補離垢錦等曰：
kuáng huò fù、 bǔ lí gòu jǐn děng yuē：
Kuanghuo-fu, Buli-goujin and the others, saying:
「諸仁當知！如來降神，
「zhū rén dāng zhī！ rú lái jiàng shén，
“Worthy ones, you should know! The Tathagata is descending in spirit
當詣此海乎！宜用身故，
dāng yì cǐ hǎi hū！ yí yòng shēn gù，
and will come to this very ocean! Therefore, in person,
率諸眷屬來集吾宮，獻饌世尊。」
lǜ zhū juàn shǔ lái jí wú gōng， xiàn zhuàn shì zūn。」
lead your retinues to gather at my palace and present a feast to the World-Honored One.”
又勅龍王，名曰主度王、
yòu chì lóng wáng， míng yuē zhǔ dù wáng、
He further commanded the dragon kings named King Lord of Deliverance,
歎無量王、離垢王、焰光王、
tàn wú liàng wáng、 lí gòu wáng、 yàn guāng wáng、
King Immeasurable Praise, King Free of Defilement, King Flaming Light,
戲樂王、清淨王、
xì lè wáng、 qīng jìng wáng、
King Joyful Play, King Purity,
妙曜意王、現諸難王，
miào yào yì wáng、 xiàn zhū nàn wáng，
King of Wondrously Radiant Mind, and King Who Manifests All Hardships,
及餘龍王百千之眾，
jí yú lóng wáng bǎi qiān zhī zhòng，
together with a hundred thousand other dragon kings and their hosts,
悉當來會，至吾宮裏，奉覲如來。
xī dāng lái huì， zhì wú gōng lǐ， fèng jìn rú lái。
that all should come to the assembly, enter my palace, and reverently attend upon the Tathagata.
又勅龍子威首曰：「仁者！
yòu chì lóng zi wēi shǒu yuē： 「rén zhě！
He also commanded the dragon prince Awesome Crest: “Worthy one!
致敬宣吾命於無焚龍王，
zhì jìng xuān wú mìng yú wú fén lóng wáng，
Convey my respects and my message to the Dragon King Anavatapta (Free of Burning),
命諸海宮供養如來、至真、等正覺。」
mìng zhū hǎi gōng gòng yǎng rú lái、 zhì zhēn、 děng zhèng jué。」
bidding all the ocean palaces make offerings to the Tathagata, the Truly Worthy, the Perfectly Enlightened One.”
即時受教。
jí shí shòu jiào。
He at once received the charge.
又勅龍王子強威，詣安明山頂，
yòu chì lóng wáng zǐ qiáng wēi， yì ān míng shān dǐng，
He further commanded the dragon prince Mighty Majesty to go to the summit of Mount Sumeru
請歡喜龍王、迦歡喜龍王，及天帝釋，
qǐng huān xǐ lóng wáng、 jiā huān xǐ lóng wáng， jí tiān dì shì，
and invite the Dragon King Nanda (Joy), the Dragon King Upananda, and Sakra, Lord of the Devas,
使諸仁者令詣大海，集吾宮內，供養如來。
shǐ zhū rén zhě lìng yì dà hǎi， jí wú gōng nèi， gòng yǎng rú lái。
asking those worthy ones to come to the great ocean, gather within my palace, and make offerings to the Tathagata.
時強威即時受教，宣令如是。
shí qiáng wēi jí shí shòu jiào， xuān lìng rú shì。
Mighty Majesty at once received the charge and proclaimed the summons accordingly.
時海龍王化作大殿，
shí hǎi lóng wáng huà zuò dà diàn，
Then the Sea Dragon King conjured forth a great hall,
以紺琉璃、紫磨黃金而雜挍成，
yǐ gàn liú lí、 zǐ mó huáng jīn ér zá jiào chéng，
wrought of deep-blue lapis lazuli and refined purple gold intermingled;
則建幢幡，造金交露，
zé jiàn chuáng fān， zào jīn jiāo lù，
he raised banners and pennants and fashioned golden latticed canopies,
寶珠、瓔珞、七寶為欄楯，而極廣大，
bǎo zhū、 yīng luò、 qī bǎo wéi lán shǔn， ér jí guǎng dà，
with precious pearls, jeweled garlands, and railings of the seven treasures, exceedingly vast and wide.
若干種香而以熏之，
ruò gān zhǒng xiāng ér yǐ xūn zhī，
With manifold kinds of incense he perfumed it,
散眾色華，紛紛如雪。
sàn zhòng sè huā， fēn fēn rú xuě。
and scattered flowers of every color, falling thick as snow.
於大殿上化立師子之座，
yú dà diàn shàng huà lì shī zǐ zhī zuò，
Within the great hall he conjured up a lion throne,
高四百八十里，皆以眾寶而合成，
gāo sì bǎi bā shí lǐ， jiē yǐ zhòng bǎo ér hé chéng，
four hundred and eighty leagues high, composed entirely of the manifold treasures,
敷無數百千天繒，以為綩綖。
fū wú shù bǎi qiān tiān zēng， yǐ wéi wǎn yán。
spread with countless hundreds of thousands of celestial silks for cushions.
諸菩薩及比丘眾所坐師子座，
zhū pú sà jí bǐ qiū zhòng suǒ zuò shī zǐ zuò，
The lion seats prepared for the bodhisattvas and the assembly of bhiksus
各各嚴麗，階級殊別。
gè gè yán lì， jiē jí shū bié。
were each majestic and beautiful, distinguished by rank and order.
饌具兼重，若干種味，
zhuàn jù jiān chóng， ruò gān zhǒng wèi，
The dishes set out were doubled and manifold, of many kinds of flavor,
寂然飲食，供設以具。
jì rán yǐn shí， gōng shè yǐ jù。
food to be taken in stillness, the offerings fully arrayed.
爾時龍王明旦修敬，
ěr shí lóng wáng míng dàn xiū jìng，
At dawn the next day the Dragon King performed his reverences,
住安明山十二之坎，
zhù ān míng shān shí èr zhī kǎn，
standing upon the twelfth terrace of Mount Sumeru,
與眷屬俱遙請世尊，以偈頌曰：
yǔ juàn shǔ jù yáo qǐng shì zūn， yǐ jì sòng yuē：
and together with his retinue invited the World-Honored One from afar, speaking in verse:
「殊特慧無量，於法得自在，
「shū tè huì wú liàng， yú fǎ dé zì zài，
“Peerless is your wisdom, immeasurable; in the Dharma you have gained mastery.
明知成眾事，如空聖無限，
míng zhī chéng zhòng shì， rú kōng shèng wú xiàn，
Clear knowing accomplishes all things; like space, the Sage is without limit.
離垢眼清淨，於世為最上，
lí gòu yǎn qīng jìng， yú shì wéi zuì shàng，
Free of defilement, your eye is pure; in this world you are supreme.
日時今已到，唯加哀自屈。
rì shí jīn yǐ dào， wéi jiā āi zì qū。
The very hour has now arrived; we pray you in compassion condescend to come.
清淨音如梵，柔軟聲和仁，
qīng jìng yīn rú fàn， róu ruǎn shēng hé rén，
Your pure voice is as Brahma's; your gentle tones are kind and harmonious.
響雷如哀鸞，為眾現甘露，
xiǎng léi rú āi luán， wèi zhòng xiàn gān lù，
Resounding like thunder, plaintive as the luan bird, you reveal sweet dew to the multitude.
除若干塵冥，為眾最上醫，
chú ruò gān chén míng， wéi zhòng zuì shàng yī，
Dispelling manifold dust and darkness, you are the supreme physician of all.
人中寶願來，今正是其時。
rén zhōng bǎo yuàn lái， jīn zhèng shì qí shí。
Jewel among men, we pray you come; now is truly the time.
心調柔寂寞，志軟常安和，
xīn diào róu jì mò， zhì ruǎn cháng ān hé，
Your mind is tamed, gentle and still; your resolve is supple, ever calm and concordant.
自度濟眾生，願救諸人民，
zì dù jì zhòng shēng， yuàn jiù zhū rén mín，
Having crossed over yourself, you deliver beings; we pray you rescue all people.
開化眾黎庶，使越彼四瀆，
kāi huà zhòng lí shù， shǐ yuè bǐ sì dú，
Transforming the common multitudes, you make them cross beyond the four great floods.
造安度彼岸，惟屈今是時。
zào ān dù bǐ àn， wéi qū jīn shì shí。
Establishing peace, you ferry them to the other shore; condescend — now is the time.
調仁樂布施，學道戒清淨，
diào rén lè bù shī， xué dào jiè qīng jìng，
Tamed and kind, delighting in giving; studying the Way with precepts pure;
忍辱力最上，已獲大精進，
rěn rǔ lì zuì shàng， yǐ huò dà jīng jìn，
supreme in the power of patience, you have gained great vigor;
滅除禪脫門，智慧普無量，
miè chú chán tuō mén， zhì huì pǔ wú liàng，
extinguishing all through the gates of dhyana and liberation, your wisdom is universal and measureless;
言誨如月明，住聖時已到。
yán huì rú yuè míng， zhù shèng shí yǐ dào。
your instructing words are bright as the moon — abiding Sage, the hour has come.
智跡方別路，邪徑永已斷，
zhì jī fāng bié lù， xié jìng yǒng yǐ duàn，
The traces of wisdom mark out the road; deviant byways are forever cut off.
七覺意根力，化現以四諦，
qī jué yì gēn lì， huà xiàn yǐ sì dì，
By the seven factors of awakening, the faculties and the powers, you manifest the Four Truths;
平等四意止，四神足意定，
píng děng sì yì zhǐ， sì shén zú yì dìng，
the four foundations of mindfulness in equality, the four bases of spiritual power and concentration —
總得普通達，時到宜屈神。
zǒng dé pǔ tōng dá， shí dào yí qū shén。
all of these you have wholly penetrated; the hour has come, condescend in spirit.
三十二相明，英妙百功德，
sān shí èr xiàng míng， yīng miào bǎi gōng dé，
The thirty-two marks shine forth, splendid and wondrous with a hundred merits;
為存德義者，示現大福田，
wèi cún dé yì zhě， shì xiàn dà fú tián，
for those who cherish virtue and right you reveal the great field of merit.
尊稱為眾祐，如春萌滋茂，
zūn chēng wéi zhòng yòu， rú chūn méng zī mào，
Honored, you are called the Blessing of the Multitude, like spring shoots growing lush.
唯愍傷加慈，大哀自屈神。
wéi mǐn shāng jiā cí， dà āi zì qū shén。
We pray you take pity and add your kindness; in great compassion condescend in spirit.
志如須彌山，心等譬如地，
zhì rú xū mí shān， xīn děng pì rú dì，
Your resolve is like Mount Sumeru; your mind is even, likened to the earth.
除愛及瞋冥，所說如演空，
chú ài jí chēn míng， suǒ shuō rú yǎn kōng，
Craving and the gloom of anger removed, what you preach expounds emptiness.
人尊不自卑，未曾有貢高，
rén zūn bù zì bēi， wèi céng yǒu gòng gāo，
Honored among men, you neither demean yourself nor were ever haughty;
歸於空脫門，屈神今正時。
guī yú kōng tuō mén， qū shén jīn zhèng shí。
you return to the gate of emptiness and liberation — condescend, now is the time.
知義尋分別，曉了隨順要，
zhī yì xún fēn bié， xiǎo liǎo suí shùn yào，
Knowing the meaning, you discern it fully; you understand the essentials of accord;
究暢解經法，心行常如斯，
jiū chàng jiě jīng fǎ， xīn xíng cháng rú sī，
you fathom and expound the Dharma of the sutras; your mind's conduct is ever thus.
顯進人本性，觀察諸慧義，
xiǎn jìn rén běn xìng， guān chá zhū huì yì，
You reveal and advance the fundamental nature of beings, contemplating every meaning of wisdom.
稽首最勝足，時到屈神臨。」
qǐ shǒu zuì shèng zú， shí dào qū shén lín。」
We bow our heads at the feet of the Most Victorious; the hour has come — condescend to draw near.”
爾時世尊遙聞龍王啟白，時到，
ěr shí shì zūn yáo wén lóng wáng qǐ bái， shí dào，
Then the World-Honored One heard from afar the Dragon King's entreaty, and the hour having come,
告諸比丘：「著衣持鉢，
gào zhū bǐ qiū： 「zhuó yī chí bō，
he told the bhiksus: “Put on your robes and take up your bowls;
當詣大海開化眾生就龍宮食。」
dāng yì dà hǎi kāi huà zhòng shēng jiù lóng gōng shí。」
we shall go to the great ocean, transform living beings, and take the meal in the dragon palace.”
比丘應曰：「唯然！」
bǐ qiū yìng yuē： 「wéi rán！」
The bhiksus answered: “So be it!”
於時世尊與諸菩薩、比丘眾俱眷屬圍繞，
yú shí shì zūn yǔ zhū pú sà、 bǐ qiū zhòng jù juàn shǔ wéi rào，
Thereupon the World-Honored One, surrounded by the bodhisattvas, the assembly of bhiksus and their retinues,
踊在虛空，身放大光明而雨天華，
yǒng zài xū kōng， shēn fàng dà guāng míng ér yǔ tiān huā，
rose up into empty space, his body emitting great light while celestial flowers rained down,
百千伎樂相和而鳴，
bǎi qiān jì yuè xiāng hé ér míng，
and a hundred thousand strains of music sounded in harmony.
集于海邊至欣樂園。
jí yú hǎi biān zhì xīn lè yuán。
They gathered at the ocean's shore and came to the Garden of Delight.
有思夷華名曰意樂，佛住止彼。
yǒu sī yí huā míng yuē yì lè， fó zhù zhǐ bǐ。
There was a siyi flowering tree named Mind's Delight, and the Buddha halted beneath it.
時海龍王往詣佛所，
shí hǎi lóng wáng wǎng yì fó suǒ，
Then the Sea Dragon King went to where the Buddha was,
稽首佛足陳敬已畢，却住一面。
qǐ shǒu fó zú chén jìng yǐ bì， què zhù yí miàn。
bowed his head to the Buddha's feet, completed his reverences, and withdrew to one side.
龍王自念：「吾欲化作寶階，
lóng wáng zì niàn： 「wú yù huà zuò bǎo jiē，
The Dragon King thought to himself: “I wish to conjure a jeweled stairway
從海邊至海底，
cóng hǎi biān zhì hǎi dǐ，
reaching from the ocean's shore down to the ocean floor,
令佛及比丘眾及諸菩薩，
lìng fó jí bǐ qiū zhòng jí zhū pú sà，
so that the Buddha, the assembly of bhiksus, and the bodhisattvas
由是下海至我宮中，
yóu shì xià hǎi zhì wǒ gōng zhōng，
may thereby descend into the sea and come to my palace,
如昔世尊化作寶階，
rú xī shì zūn huà zuò bǎo jiē，
just as the World-Honored One once conjured a jeweled stairway
從忉利天至閻浮利。」
cóng dāo lì tiān zhì yán fú lì。」
from the Trayastrimsa Heaven down to Jambudvipa.”
適設此念，便從海邊化作三寶階金銀、琉璃，
shì shè cǐ niàn， biàn cóng hǎi biān huà zuò sān bǎo jiē jīn yín、 liú lí，
No sooner had he formed this thought than from the shore he conjured a threefold jeweled stairway of gold, silver, and lapis lazuli,
下至其宮甚微妙好。
xià zhì qí gōng shèn wēi miào hǎo。
descending to his palace, exceedingly subtle and fair.
於是世尊以威神力，
yú shì shì zūn yǐ wēi shén lì，
Then the World-Honored One, by his majestic spiritual power,
化大海水令不復現，
huà dà hǎi shuǐ lìng bù fù xiàn，
transformed the waters of the great ocean so that they no longer appeared,
使海生類不以為患。
shǐ hǎi shēng lèi bù yǐ wéi huàn。
yet the creatures living in the sea suffered no distress by it.
佛身放光，照于大海，
fó shēn fàng guāng， zhào yú dà hǎi，
The Buddha's body emitted light that illumined the great ocean
普至三千大千世界。
pǔ zhì sān qiān dà qiān shì jiè。
and reached everywhere throughout the three-thousandfold great-thousand world.
其海居類身蒙此光，
qí hǎi jū lèi shēn méng cǐ guāng，
The bodies of the sea-dwelling creatures were bathed in this light,
皆懷慈愍柔仁之心，不相嬈害，
jiē huái cí mǐn róu rén zhī xīn， bù xiāng ráo hài，
and all conceived hearts of kindness, pity, and gentleness, no longer troubling or harming one another,
相視如父、如母、如兄、如弟、如子無異。
xiāng shì rú fù、 rú mǔ、 rú xiōng、 rú dì、 rú zi wú yì。
regarding each other as father, as mother, as elder brother, as younger brother, as child, with no difference.
於時欲行天人、色行天人侍從世尊，
yú shí yù xíng tiān rén、 sè xíng tiān rén shì cóng shì zūn，
At that time the devas of the desire realm and the devas of the form realm attended upon the World-Honored One,
欲聽道化，猶欲觀龍王莊嚴宮殿。
yù tīng dào huà， yóu yù guān lóng wáng zhuāng yán gōng diàn。
wishing to hear the teaching of the Way and also to behold the Dragon King's adorned palace.
時佛與諸菩薩及大聲聞、
shí fó yǔ zhū pú sà jí dà shēng wén、
Then the Buddha, with the bodhisattvas and the great sravakas,
諸天、龍、神、香音神、無善神、
zhū tiān、 lóng、 shén、 xiāng yīn shén、 wú shàn shén、
the devas, dragons, spirits, gandharvas, asuras,
鳳凰神、山神、甜柔神、
fèng huáng shén、 shān shén、 tián róu shén、
garudas, mountain spirits, and kinnaras,
釋、梵、四天王，
shì、 fàn、 sì tiān wáng，
together with Sakra, Brahma, and the Four Heavenly Kings,
從欣樂園思夷華樹欲詣龍宮。
cóng xīn lè yuán sī yí huā shù yù yì lóng gōng。
set out from the siyi flower-tree in the Garden of Delight toward the dragon palace.
佛昇寶階，涉於中階。
fó shēng bǎo jiē， shè yú zhōng jiē。
The Buddha ascended the jeweled stairway, treading upon the middle flight.
諸菩薩眾住于右階，
zhū pú sà zhòng zhù yú yòu jiē，
The host of bodhisattvas kept to the right flight,
諸大聲聞住在左階。
zhū dà shēng wén zhù zài zuǒ jiē。
and the great sravakas kept to the left.
時六十億釋而在前導；
shí liù shí yì shì ér zài qián dǎo；
Then six billion Sakras led the way in front;
六十億梵天皆在虛空，各執寶蓋；
liù shí yì fàn tiān jiē zài xū kōng， gè zhí bǎo gài；
six billion Brahma gods stood in empty space, each holding a jeweled canopy;
六十億天皆在佛後而雨天華；
liù shí yì tiān jiē zài fó hòu ér yǔ tiān huā；
six billion devas followed behind the Buddha, raining down celestial flowers;
六十億諸欲天人作諸伎樂而供養佛；
liù shí yì zhū yù tiān rén zuò zhū jì yuè ér gòng yǎng fó；
six billion devas of the desire realm made music as an offering to the Buddha;
六十億魔眾皆於佛前香汁灑地；
liù shí yì mó zhòng jiē yú fó qián xiāng zhī sǎ dì；
six billion of Mara's host sprinkled fragrant liquid on the ground before the Buddha;
六十億龍后在虛空中，
liù shí yì lóng hòu zài xū kōng zhōng，
six billion dragon queens were in the open sky,
各現半身手執珠瓔垂散佛上；
gè xiàn bàn shēn shǒu zhí zhū yīng chuí sàn fó shàng；
each revealing half her body, holding pearl garlands and letting them fall upon the Buddha;
六十億山神皆鼓伎樂，歌佛功德；
liù shí yì shān shén jiē gǔ jì yuè， gē fó gōng dé；
six billion mountain spirits struck up music and sang the Buddha's merits;
六十億香音神手執華蓋，以用奉佛；
liù shí yì xiāng yīn shén shǒu zhí huā gài， yǐ yòng fèng fó；
six billion gandharvas held flower canopies and offered them to the Buddha;
六十億無善神各持若干百千種衣，
liù shí yì wú shàn shén gè chí ruò gān bǎi qiān zhǒng yī，
six billion asuras each held many hundreds of thousands of kinds of garments
以覆佛上；
yǐ fù fó shàng；
to spread above the Buddha;
無焚龍王與億百千眷屬在於虛空，
wú fén lóng wáng yǔ yì bǎi qiān juàn shǔ zài yú xū kōng，
and the Dragon King Anavatapta with a hundred thousand million of his retinue stood in space,
皆以華香、雜香、擣香作眾伎樂，
jiē yǐ huā xiāng、 zá xiāng、 dǎo xiāng zuò zhòng jì yuè，
all with flower incense, blended incense, and powdered incense, making manifold music,
莊嚴諸龍，及諸天華以供養佛。
zhuāng yán zhū lóng， jí zhū tiān huā yǐ gòng yǎng fó。
adorning the dragons and offering celestial flowers to the Buddha.
如是比類，六萬龍王皆供養佛，
rú shì bǐ lèi， liù wàn lóng wáng jiē gòng yǎng fó，
In like manner sixty thousand dragon kings all made offerings to the Buddha,
欲見世尊，勸海龍王。
yù jiàn shì zūn， quàn hǎi lóng wáng。
desiring to behold the World-Honored One, and they encouraged the Sea Dragon King.
安樂世界無量壽如來佛土菩薩，
ān lè shì jiè wú liàng shòu rú lái fó tǔ pú sà，
From the buddha-land of the Tathagata Amitayus in the world Sukhavati came the bodhisattvas
號光世音、大勢至大士，
hào guāng shì yīn、 dà shì zhì dà shì，
named the great beings Avalokitesvara and Mahasthamaprapta,
與無央數億諸菩薩俱，
yǔ wú yāng shù yì zhū pú sà jù，
together with incalculable millions of bodhisattvas,
為佛、世尊示現莊嚴諸所有供養，
wèi fó、 shì zūn shì xiàn zhuāng yán zhū suǒ yǒu gòng yǎng，
who displayed for the Buddha, the World-Honored One, every kind of adorned offering,
皆令前所嚴供隱蔽不現，無能知者。
jiē lìng qián suǒ yán gōng yǐn bì bù xiàn， wú néng zhī zhě。
causing all the splendid offerings made before to be eclipsed and unseen, beyond anyone's reckoning.
焰氣世界難逮如來佛土菩薩，
yàn qì shì jiè nán dài rú lái fó tǔ pú sà，
From the buddha-land of the Tathagata Hard-to-Attain in the world Blazing Vapor came the bodhisattvas
號法英、法道大士；
hào fǎ yīng、 fǎ dào dà shì；
named the great beings Dharma Flower and Dharma Path;
妙樂世界無怒如來佛土菩薩，
miào lè shì jiè wú nù rú lái fó tǔ pú sà，
from the buddha-land of the Tathagata Without Anger in the world Wondrous Joy,
號香首、眾香首大士；
hào xiāng shǒu、 zhòng xiāng shǒu dà shì；
the great beings Fragrance Crest and Manifold Fragrance Crest;
照明世界月辯如來佛土菩薩，
zhào míng shì jiè yuè biàn rú lái fó tǔ pú sà，
from the buddha-land of the Tathagata Moon Eloquence in the world Illumination,
號師子、師子音大士；
hào shī zǐ、 shī zǐ yīn dà shì；
the great beings Lion and Lion's Voice;
不眴世界善目如來佛土菩薩，
bù shùn shì jiè shàn mù rú lái fó tǔ pú sà，
from the buddha-land of the Tathagata Good Eyes in the world Unblinking,
號導御、諸法自在大士；
hào dǎo yù、 zhū fǎ zì zài dà shì；
the great beings Guiding Charioteer and Master of All Dharmas;
光曜世界普世如來佛土菩薩，
guāng yào shì jiè pǔ shì rú lái fó tǔ pú sà，
from the buddha-land of the Tathagata Universal World in the world Radiance,
號寶場、寶焰大士；
hào bǎo chǎng、 bǎo yàn dà shì；
the great beings Jewel Field and Jewel Flame;
樂御世界寶首如來佛土菩薩，
lè yù shì jiè bǎo shǒu rú lái fó tǔ pú sà，
from the buddha-land of the Tathagata Jewel Crest in the world Joyful Guidance,
號慧步、慧見大士；
hào huì bù、 huì jiàn dà shì；
the great beings Wisdom Step and Wisdom Sight;
光察世界普觀如來佛土菩薩，
guāng chá shì jiè pǔ guān rú lái fó tǔ pú sà，
from the buddha-land of the Tathagata Universal Contemplation in the world Light of Discernment,
號雨王、法王大士；
hào yǔ wáng、 fǎ wáng dà shì；
the great beings Rain King and Dharma King;
愛見世界尊自在王如來佛土菩薩，
ài jiàn shì jiè zūn zì zài wáng rú lái fó tǔ pú sà，
from the buddha-land of the Tathagata Honored Sovereign King in the world Beloved Sight,
號退魔、后魔王大士。
hào tuì mó、 hòu mó wáng dà shì。
the great beings Repeller of Mara and Sovereign over Mara.
取要言之，如是十方各各無央數億諸菩薩，
qǔ yào yán zhī， rú shì shí fāng gè gè wú yāng shù yì zhū pú sà，
In sum, from each of the ten directions came incalculable millions of bodhisattvas,
皆來勸樂海中龍王欲見如來供養奉事。
jiē lái quàn lè hǎi zhōng lóng wáng yù jiàn rú lái gòng yǎng fèng shì。
all coming to gladden and encourage the dragon kings of the sea, who longed to behold the Tathagata and serve him with offerings.
於是世尊以大道力諸佛感動，
yú shì shì zūn yǐ dà dào lì zhū fó gǎn dòng，
Then the World-Honored One, by the power of the great Way, moved all the buddhas to respond;
威德所監，以佛弘威勸化無戲，供養諸佛，
wēi dé suǒ jiàn， yǐ fó hóng wēi quàn huà wú xì， gòng yǎng zhū fó，
overseen by his majestic virtue, by the Buddha's vast power he exhorted without frivolity and made offerings to all buddhas.
放大光明，徹照十方無量世界，
fàng dà guāng míng， chè zhào shí fāng wú liàng shì jiè，
He emitted a great light that shone through the immeasurable worlds of the ten directions,
以佛洪音大師子吼，而講言化諸天百千，
yǐ fó hóng yīn dà shī zǐ hǒu， ér jiǎng yán huà zhū tiān bǎi qiān，
and with the Buddha's mighty voice, the great lion's roar, he preached and transformed hundreds of thousands of devas,
皆作音樂而雨天華，滅諸惡趣，
jiē zuò yīn yuè ér yǔ tiān huā， miè zhū è qù，
who all made music and rained down celestial flowers, so that the evil destinies were extinguished
施於一切安隱之具。
shī yú yī qiè ān yǐn zhī jù。
and the means of peace and ease were bestowed upon all.
有三昧名曰立於大哀歡悅群萌，
yǒu sān mèi míng yuē lì yú dà āi huān yuè qún méng，
There is a samadhi called Establishing Great Compassion and Gladdening the Multitudes;
以佛三昧正受已，
yǐ fó sān mèi zhèng shòu yǐ，
and when the Buddha had entered rightly into that samadhi,
所作莊嚴光飾大海不可思議。
suǒ zuò zhuāng yán guāng shì dà hǎi bù kě sī yì。
the adornments he wrought, illumining the great ocean, were beyond conception.
佛從寶階降神海宮，
fó cóng bǎo jiē jiàng shén hǎi gōng，
The Buddha descended in spirit by the jeweled stairway to the ocean palace,
自然音樂普聞十方無量世界。
zì rán yīn yuè pǔ wén shí fāng wú liàng shì jiè。
and spontaneous music was heard throughout the immeasurable worlds of the ten directions.
佛之威神如來所感，
fó zhī wēi shén rú lái suǒ gǎn，
By the Buddha's majestic power and the Tathagata's responsive influence,
皆見能仁如來下于大海。
jiē jiàn néng rén rú lái xià yú dà hǎi。
all beheld the Tathagata Sakyamuni descending into the great ocean.
彼時億百千玉女、魔妻、
bǐ shí yì bǎi qiān yù nǚ、 mó qī、
At that time hundreds of thousands of millions of jade maidens, wives of Mara,
無善神、鳳凰神、山神、甜柔神、
wú shàn shén、 fèng huáng shén、 shān shén、 tián róu shén、
asura women, garuda women, mountain-spirit women, kinnara women,
群神婦女，皆以伎樂而行迎佛，
qún shén fù nǚ， jiē yǐ jì yuè ér xíng yíng fó，
and the womenfolk of the assembled spirits all went forth with music to welcome the Buddha,
調諸音樂而歌頌佛德：
diào zhū yīn yuè ér gē sòng fó dé：
tuning their instruments and singing in praise of the Buddha's virtue:
「施上戒清淨，忍力慈心尊，
「shī shàng jiè qīng jìng， rěn lì cí xīn zūn，
“Supreme in giving, pure in precepts; honored for the strength of patience and a heart of kindness;
精進勤御義，禮樂禪脫門，
jīng jìn qín yù yì， lǐ lè chán tuō mén，
vigorous and diligent in mastering the meaning, revering and delighting in the gates of dhyana and release;
心淨光慧智，嚴明奮威神，
xīn jìng guāng huì zhì， yán míng fèn wēi shén，
mind pure and radiant with wisdom and knowing, august and bright, rousing majestic power;
現在示解脫，故來除垢塵。
xiàn zài shì jiě tuō， gù lái chú gòu chén。
now revealing liberation, you have come to sweep away defilement and dust.
施以甘露安，道御罄眾穢，
shī yǐ gān lù ān， dào yù qìng zhòng huì，
You bestow the peace of sweet dew; guiding by the Way, you exhaust every impurity.
無盡德如空，慧海願降海，
wú jǐn dé rú kōng， huì hǎi yuàn jiàng hǎi，
Your inexhaustible virtue is like space; Ocean of Wisdom, we pray you descend into the ocean.
所說具足要，講歎度無極，
suǒ shuō jù zú yào， jiǎng tàn dù wú jí，
What you preach is complete in essentials; you expound and extol the perfections without limit;
施眼明清淨，一切人中上。
shī yǎn míng qīng jìng， yī qiè rén zhōng shàng。
you bestow the eye, bright and pure — supreme among all people.
歎頌深義句，愍人光無倫，
tàn sòng shēn yì jù， mǐn rén guāng wú lún，
We praise in song the phrases of profound meaning; your pity for beings shines beyond compare.
等祠所宣普，降伏諸異道，
děng cí suǒ xuān pǔ， xiáng fú zhū yì dào，
Impartial in offering, your proclamation is universal; you subdue every heterodox path.
施以法無慳，講經淨恣塵，
shī yǐ fǎ wú qiān， jiǎng jīng jìng zì chén，
You give the Dharma without stinting; expounding the sutras, you purify the dust of indulgence.
讚歎實慧光，道財敷演珍。
zàn tàn shí huì guāng， dào cái fū yǎn zhēn。
We extol the light of true wisdom; the wealth of the Way you set forth as a treasure.
見諦莫不受，正觀斷結著，
jiàn dì mò bù shòu， zhèng guān duàn jié zhuó，
None who see the truth fail to receive it; right contemplation severs fetters and attachments.
不動如山根，願稽首導師。
bù dòng rú shān gēn， yuàn qǐ shǒu dǎo shī。
Unmoving as a mountain's root — we bow our heads to the Guiding Teacher.
諸天金翅鳥，須倫真陀羅，
zhū tiān jīn chì niǎo， xū lún zhēn tuó luó，
Devas and garudas, asuras and kinnaras,
迦留鳩垣師，願稽首足下。
jiā liú jiū yuán shī， yuàn qǐ shǒu zú xià。
the hosts of Jialiu and Jiuyuan — we bow our heads at your feet.
尊相三十二，無比妙善現，
zūn xiàng sān shí èr， wú bǐ miào shàn xiàn，
The Honored One's thirty-two marks appear, incomparably wondrous and fair;
體柔紫金色，爪足下安平，
tǐ róu zǐ jīn sè， zhǎo zú xià ān píng，
his body supple, of purple-gold hue, his nails and soles level and even;
妙響如哀鸞，其聲踰梵天，
miào xiǎng rú āi luán， qí shēng yú fàn tiān，
his wondrous resonance like the plaintive luan, his voice surpassing the Brahma heavens,
大音超三千，稽首柔軟音。
dà yīn chāo sān qiān， qǐ shǒu róu ruǎn yīn。
his great sound transcending the three-thousandfold world — we bow to that gentle voice.
根調心寂寞，猶如月電光，
gēn diào xīn jì mò， yóu rú yuè diàn guāng，
His faculties tamed, his mind still, like the moon and like the lightning's flash;
言誠常平等，願稽首樂法。
yán chéng cháng píng děng， yuàn qǐ shǒu lè fǎ。
his words sincere and ever impartial — we bow to him who delights in Dharma.
已度老病苦，救一切令脫，
yǐ dù lǎo bìng kǔ， jiù yī qiè lìng tuō，
He has crossed beyond the pain of age and sickness and rescues all to freedom;
得勝伏眾魔，滅除生現盡。
dé shèng fú zhòng mó， miè chú shēng xiàn jǐn。
victorious, he subdues the host of maras; birth is extinguished and utterly ended.
無著蠲塵勞，為諸天所敬，
wú zhuó juān chén láo， wéi zhū tiān suǒ jìng，
Unattached, he cleanses away defilement and toil, and is revered by all the devas.
歸尊普救護，導師開化眾。」
guī zūn pǔ jiù hù， dǎo shī kāi huà zhòng。」
We take refuge in the Honored One, universal savior and protector; the Guiding Teacher transforms the multitude.”
十德六度品第十一
shí dé liù dù pǐn dì shí yī
Chapter Eleven: The Ten Virtues and the Six Perfections
於是王女及諸龍后、
yú shì wáng nǚ jí zhū lóng hòu、
Thereupon the dragon king's daughter and the dragon queens,
無善神、鳳凰神、山神、甜柔神后共讚佛已，
wú shàn shén、 fèng huáng shén、 shān shén、 tián róu shén hòu gòng zàn fó yǐ，
with the asura, garuda, mountain-spirit and kinnara queens, having together praised the Buddha,
一切同等皆發無上正真道意，
yī qiè tóng děng jiē fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì，
all alike aroused the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment;
脫身瓔珞，用散佛上。
tuō shēn yīng luò， yòng sàn fó shàng。
they loosed the jeweled garlands from their bodies and scattered them over the Buddha.
佛與眾俱降于大海，到其海城，
fó yǔ zhòng jù jiàng yú dà hǎi， dào qí hǎi chéng，
The Buddha and the assembly together descended into the great ocean and reached the sea city;
詣海龍王莊嚴大殿，坐師子座。
yì hǎi lóng wáng zhuāng yán dà diàn， zuò shī zǐ zuò。
they came to the Sea Dragon King's adorned great hall, and he took the lion throne.
時諸菩薩及比丘眾，各各次坐其座。
shí zhū pú sà jí bǐ qiū zhòng， gè gè cì zuò qí zuò。
Then the bodhisattvas and the assembly of bhiksus each sat in order upon their seats.
於時海龍王與中宮眷屬俱見佛坐已，
yú shí hǎi lóng wáng yǔ zhōng gōng juàn shǔ jù jiàn fó zuò yǐ，
When the Sea Dragon King and the retinue of his inner palace saw the Buddha seated,
手自斟酌，寂然飲食，
shǒu zì zhēn zhuó， jì rán yǐn shí，
he served with his own hands, food to be taken in stillness,
無央數味供養佛及比丘僧。
wú yāng shù wèi gòng yǎng fó jí bǐ qiū sēng。
offering countless flavors to the Buddha and the sangha of bhiksus.
飲食畢訖，行澡水竟，
yǐn shí bì qì， xíng zǎo shuǐ jìng，
When the meal was ended and the washing water had been passed round,
坐佛前聽經，
zuò fó qián tīng jīng，
he sat before the Buddha to hear the sutra,
及諸天、龍、神、香音神、無善神、
jí zhū tiān、 lóng、 shén、 xiāng yīn shén、 wú shàn shén、
together with the devas, dragons, spirits, gandharvas, asuras,
鳳凰神、山神、甜柔神、釋、梵、四天王
fèng huáng shén、 shān shén、 tián róu shén、 shì、 fàn、 sì tiān wáng
garudas, mountain spirits, kinnaras, Sakra, Brahma, the Four Heavenly Kings,
及十方諸來會菩薩。
jí shí fāng zhū lái huì pú sà。
and the bodhisattvas who had come to the gathering from the ten directions.
於是佛見眾會坐定，從身放光，
yú shì fó jiàn zhòng huì zuò dìng， cóng shēn fàng guāng，
Then the Buddha, seeing the assembly settled, sent forth light from his body,
光名善度脫法柔和，悉照大海。
guāng míng shàn dù tuō fǎ róu hé， xī zhào dà hǎi。
a light named Gentle Dharma of Good Deliverance, which illumined the whole ocean.
諸居之類上、中、下品，普自見佛，
zhū jū zhī lèi shàng、 zhōng、 xià pǐn， pǔ zì jiàn fó，
The creatures dwelling there, of high, middle and low grade, all beheld the Buddha for themselves,
歡喜踊躍，願樂聞法，
huān xǐ yǒng yuè， yuàn lè wén fǎ，
leapt with joy and longed to hear the Dharma,
各以恭敬遙稽首佛。
gè yǐ gōng jìng yáo qǐ shǒu fó。
each bowing his head reverently to the Buddha from afar.
爾時世尊告海龍王：
ěr shí shì zūn gào hǎi lóng wáng：
At that time the World-Honored One said to the Sea Dragon King:
「猗世間者，作若干緣，心行不同，
「yī shì jiān zhě， zuò ruò gān yuán， xīn xíng bù tóng，
“Those who lean upon the world make manifold conditions; their minds and conduct differ,
罪福各異，以是之故所生殊別。
zuì fú gè yì， yǐ shì zhī gù suǒ shēng shū bié。
their sin and merit differ, and for this reason their births are distinct.
龍王！且觀眾會及大海若干種類形貌不同，
lóng wáng！ qiě guān zhòng huì jí dà hǎi ruò gān zhǒng lèi xíng mào bù tóng，
Dragon King, observe this assembly and the great ocean: their many kinds differ in shape and appearance,
是諸形貌皆心所畫。
shì zhū xíng mào jiē xīn suǒ huà。
and all these shapes are painted by the mind.
又心無色而不可見，
yòu xīn wú sè ér bù kě jiàn，
