Yet the mind has no form and cannot be seen;
一切諸法誑詐如是，
yī qiè zhū fǎ kuáng zhà rú shì，
all dharmas are deceptive in just this way.
因或興相都無有主，
yīn huò xīng xiàng dōu wú yǒu zhǔ，
Arising from delusion, their marks have no owner at all;
隨其所作各各自受。
suí qí suǒ zuò gè gè zì shòu。
each receives according to what he himself has done.
譬如畫師本無造像，
pì rú huà shī běn wú zào xiàng，
Just as a painter has no image within him to begin with,
諸法如是而不可議，
zhū fǎ rú shì ér bù kě yì，
so are all dharmas, beyond discussion,
自然如幻化相，皆心所作。
zì rán rú huàn huà xiāng， jiē xīn suǒ zuò。
naturally like conjured forms, all made by the mind.
明者，見諸法因或興相，則當奉行諸善；
míng zhě， jiàn zhū fǎ yīn huò xīng xiàng， zé dāng fèng xíng zhū shàn；
The clear-sighted, seeing that dharmas take on marks through delusion, should practice every good;
德者，其解或相興成諸法、蔭種諸入，
dé zhě， qí jiě huò xiāng xìng chéng zhū fǎ、 yìn zhǒng zhū rù，
the virtuous, understanding how deluded marks form the dharmas and sow the aggregates and sense-fields,
當歡喜悅，得好端正。
dāng huān xǐ yuè， dé hǎo duān zhèng。
should rejoice, and will obtain fair and upright form.
龍王！具觀如來之身，
lóng wáng！ jù guān rú lái zhī shēn，
Dragon King, look fully upon the body of the Tathagata:
以百千福而得合成，
yǐ bǎi qiān fú ér dé hé chéng，
it is composed of a hundred thousand merits,
超於眾會普現巍巍，
chāo yú zhòng huì pǔ xiàn wēi wēi，
surpassing the assembly, manifesting everywhere in majesty.
其百千德由得自在，
qí bǎi qiān dé yóu de zì zài，
By its hundred thousand virtues it attains sovereign freedom,
而使梵、釋覆蔽不現。
ér shǐ fàn、 shì fù bì bù xiàn。
so that Brahma and Sakra are eclipsed and no longer seen.
觀如來身，目不敢視，
guān rú lái shēn， mù bù gǎn shì，
Gazing upon the Tathagata's body, the eye dares not look;
當其威光察諸大士，
dāng qí wēi guāng chá zhū dà shì，
and under that majestic radiance behold the great beings:
色身相好，莊嚴具足，
sè shēn xiàng hǎo， zhuāng yán jù zú，
their form-bodies with the marks and signs, fully adorned,
皆以善德挍飾其體。」
jiē yǐ shàn dé jiào shì qí tǐ。」
their very bodies wrought and ornamented by good virtue.”
佛語龍王：「仁所嚴淨皆因福成。
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「rén suǒ yán jìng jiē yīn fú chéng。
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “Worthy one, all your splendor and purity is accomplished through merit.
諸釋、梵天、龍、鬼神、香音神、無善神、
zhū shì、 fàn tiān、 lóng、 guǐ shén、 xiāng yīn shén、 wú shàn shén、
Whatever adornment belongs to Sakra, the Brahma gods, dragons, ghosts and spirits, gandharvas, asuras,
鳳凰神、山神、甜柔神，所有莊嚴皆因福生。
fèng huáng shén、 shān shén、 tián róu shén， suǒ yǒu zhuāng yán jiē yīn fú shēng。
garudas, mountain spirits and kinnaras — all of it is born of merit.
今此大海若干種身，
jīn cǐ dà hǎi ruò gān zhǒng shēn，
In this great ocean now there are bodies of many kinds:
善惡大小、廣狹好醜、強羸細微，
shàn è dà xiǎo、 guǎng xiá hǎo chǒu、 qiáng léi xì wēi，
good and evil, great and small, broad and narrow, fair and ugly, strong and frail, coarse and minute,
皆自從心而已獲之，為若干貌，
jiē zì cóng xīn ér yǐ huò zhī， wéi ruò gān mào，
all obtained from the mind itself, taking on their manifold appearances,
悉身、口、意之所作為。
xī shēn、 kǒu、 yì zhī suǒ zuò wéi。
all of them the work of body, speech and mind.
是故，龍王！自護身行，救濟罪福，當作是學。
shì gù， lóng wáng！ zì hù shēn xíng， jiù jì zuì fú， dāng zuò shì xué。
Therefore, Dragon King, guard your own conduct and attend to sin and merit; train yourself thus.
汝等以護身行救濟罪福，奉行諸善，得成佛道，
rǔ děng yǐ hù shēn xíng jiù jì zuì fú， fèng xíng zhū shàn， dé chéng fó dào，
By guarding your conduct, attending to sin and merit, and practicing every good, you will accomplish the buddha path,
滅棄邪見，不住有常、無常之見，
miè qì xié jiàn， bú zhù yǒu cháng、 wú cháng zhī jiàn，
cast away wrong views, and abide in neither eternalism nor annihilationism.
當求眾祐，已殖供養。
dāng qiú zhòng yòu， yǐ zhí gòng yǎng。
Seek the Blessing of the Multitude, and plant your offerings.
因供養故，當為諸天、世人所敬！」
yīn gòng yǎng gù， dāng wéi zhū tiān、 shì rén suǒ jìng！」
Because of those offerings you will be revered by devas and by men.”
佛語龍王：「菩薩有一法，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「pú sà yǒu yī fǎ，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “A bodhisattva has one dharma
皆斷一切惡趣眾難。何等為一？
jiē duàn yī qiè è qù zhòng nàn。 hé děng wéi yī？
that cuts off every evil destiny and all hardship. What is that one?
專察妙法，云何正諦？
zhuān chá miào fǎ， yún hé zhèng dì？
To examine the wondrous Dharma single-mindedly, asking: what is the right truth?
入於法樂，多觀善法，
rù yú fǎ lè， duō guān shàn fǎ，
To enter the joy of Dharma, to contemplate wholesome dharmas often,
不聽諸惡眾邪之想。
bù tīng zhū è zhòng xié zhī xiǎng。
and not to give ear to evil and deviant thoughts.
已斷惡法，奉行眾善。
yǐ duàn è fǎ， fèng xíng zhòng shàn。
Having severed evil dharmas, one practices every good,
在在所生與佛、菩薩賢善性俱。」
zài zài suǒ shēng yǔ fó、 pú sà xián shàn xìng jù。」
and wherever one is born one shares the worthy and good nature of buddhas and bodhisattvas.”
佛言：「何等善事，
fó yán： 「hé děng shàn shì，
The Buddha said: “What good deed is it that
已立德根天、人之安，
yǐ lì dé gēn tiān、 rén zhī ān，
establishes the root of virtue and the peace of devas and men,
不為聲聞、緣覺之本？
bù wéi shēng wén、 yuán jué zhī běn？
yet is not the basis of the sravaka or the pratyekabuddha?
立道本者，志無上正真道。
lì dào běn zhě， zhì wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào。
To establish the root of the Way is to aspire to unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment.
何謂立本？謂行十事。何謂十？
hé wèi lì běn？ wèi xíng shí shì。 hé wèi shí？
What is establishing that root? It is the practice of ten things. Which ten?
身不殺、盜、婬，
shēn bù shā、 dào、 yín，
With the body, not to kill, not to steal, not to commit sexual misconduct;
口不妄言、兩舌、惡口、綺語，
kǒu bù wàng yán、 liǎng shé、 è kǒu、 qǐ yǔ，
with the mouth, not to lie, not to speak divisively, not to speak harshly, not to speak frivolously;
意不嫉、恚、癡，是謂立本。」
yì bù jí、 huì、 chī， shì wèi lì běn。」
with the mind, not to envy, not to hate, not to be deluded. This is called establishing the root.”
佛語龍王：「人不殺生得十善寂法。何謂十？
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「rén bù shā shēng dé shí shàn jì fǎ。 hé wèi shí？
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “One who does not kill gains ten good and peaceful dharmas. Which ten?
常施安隱於一切人，
cháng shī ān yǐn yú yī qiè rén，
He always bestows peace and ease upon all people;
常樂慈心，斷瞋恨心，
cháng lè cí xīn， duàn chēn hèn xīn，
he always delights in a heart of kindness and severs hatred;
所生之處常無疾病，
suǒ shēng zhī chù cháng wú jí bìng，
wherever he is born he is free of sickness;
常種長命，為非人所護，
cháng zhòng cháng mìng， wéi fēi rén suǒ hù，
he always plants long life and is guarded by non-human beings;
臥安寤歡，未曾惡夢，
wò ān wù huān， wèi céng è mèng，
he sleeps at ease and wakes glad, never troubled by evil dreams;
不懷怨結，不畏惡趣，
bù huái yuàn jié， bù wèi è qù，
he harbors no bonds of enmity and does not fear the evil destinies;
壽終之後得生安處。
shòu zhōng zhī hòu dé shēng ān chù。
and when his life ends he is born in a place of peace.
人不殺生得斯寂法，
rén bù shā shēng dé sī jì fǎ，
One who does not kill gains these peaceful dharmas;
以不殺生善本之德，願志無上正真之道。
yǐ bù shā shēng shàn běn zhī dé， yuàn zhì wú shàng zhèng zhēn zhī dào。
and by the virtue of this good root of not killing he aspires to unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment,
若成佛時而得自在於壽命也。」
ruò chéng fó shí ér dé zì zài yú shòu mìng yě。」
so that when he becomes a buddha he attains sovereign mastery over lifespan.”
佛告龍王：「人不盜竊得五信法。何等五？
fó gào lóng wáng： 「rén bù dào qiè dé wǔ xìn fǎ。 hé děng wǔ？
The Buddha told the Dragon King: “One who does not steal gains five dharmas of trust. Which five?
得大富，
dé dà fù，
He obtains great wealth;
無有縣官、水火、盜賊、怨家、惡子能竊取者，
wú yǒu xiàn guān、 shuǐ huǒ、 dào zéi、 yuàn jiā、 è zi néng qiè qǔ zhě，
no magistrate, flood or fire, thief, enemy, or wayward son can carry it off;
眾所愛敬，到處寂然，
zhòng suǒ ài jìng， dào chù jì rán，
he is loved and honored by all, and wherever he goes there is calm;
所至無難，患畏永除。
suǒ zhì wú nán， huàn wèi yǒng chú。
wherever he arrives there is no hardship, and dread is forever removed.
以不取之福、志存慧施，殖眾德本，
yǐ bù qǔ zhī fú、 zhì cún huì shī， zhí zhòng dé běn，
By the merit of not taking, holding wise generosity in mind, and planting manifold roots of virtue,
志願無上正真之道，以依如來無見之慧，
zhì yuàn wú shàng zhèng zhēn zhī dào， yǐ yī rú lái wú jiàn zhī huì，
he aspires to unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment, relying on the Tathagata's wisdom that has no seeing,
成最正覺使立神通。」
chéng zuì zhèng jué shǐ lì shén tōng。」
attains supreme enlightenment, and establishes the spiritual penetrations.”
佛語龍王：「人不犯邪婬，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「rén bù fàn xié yín，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “One who does not commit sexual misconduct
得四明智所歎之德。何等四？
dé sì míng zhì suǒ tàn zhī dé。 hé děng sì？
gains four virtues extolled by the wise. Which four?
攝護諸根，離諸調戲，
shè hù zhū gēn， lí zhū tiáo xì，
He guards his faculties and is free of dalliance and flirtation;
一切世間悉共稱歎。
yī qiè shì jiān xī gòng chēng tàn。
and the whole world joins in praising him.
已離邪婬，無敢輕眄其妻室者。
yǐ lí xié yín， wú gǎn qīng miǎn qí qī shì zhě。
Having left sexual misconduct behind, none dare cast a wanton glance upon his wife and household.
以是德本，志願無上正真之道，
yǐ shì dé běn， zhì yuàn wú shàng zhèng zhēn zhī dào，
By this root of virtue he aspires to unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment,
得大人相馬陰之藏。」
dé dà rén xiāng mǎ yīn zhī zàng。」
and gains the mark of a great man: the sheathed organ, like a horse's.”
佛語龍王：「人不妄語，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「rén bù wàng yǔ，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “One who does not lie
諸天、世人以八法歎。何謂八？
zhū tiān、 shì rén yǐ bā fǎ tàn。 hé wèi bā？
is praised by devas and men in eight ways. Which eight?
得面清淨；
dé miàn qīng jìng；
His countenance becomes pure;
語言中當一切世人所見任信；
yǔ yán zhòng dāng yī qiè shì rén suǒ jiàn rèn xìn；
his words strike the mark and are trusted by all the world;
自成其證，天人所敬；
zì chéng qí zhèng， tiān rén suǒ jìng；
he establishes his own testimony and is revered by devas and men;
心懷至誠而無邪想；
xīn huái zhì chéng ér wú xié xiǎng；
his heart holds utter sincerity without deviant thought;
心意清淨而無諛諂；
xīn yì qīng jìng ér wú yú chǎn；
his mind is pure and free of flattery;
多所歡悅，無患厭者；
duō suǒ huān yuè， wú huàn yàn zhě；
he gives much gladness, and none grow weary of him;
能受禁誨，無有麁言；
néng shòu jìn huì， wú yǒu cū yán；
he can accept restraint and instruction, and has no coarse speech;
生天上、人間，獨見任信，無有疑者。
shēng tiān shàng、 rén jiān， dú jiàn rèn xìn， wú yǒu yí zhě。
and born among devas or men, he alone is trusted, with none who doubt him.
以至誠言善德之本，志願無上正真之道，
yǐ zhì chéng yán shàn dé zhī běn， zhì yuàn wú shàng zhèng zhēn zhī dào，
By this good root of truthful speech he aspires to unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment,
因此所行常得至誠。」
yīn cǐ suǒ xíng cháng dé zhì chéng。」
and through this practice he ever attains utter sincerity.”
佛語龍王：「人不兩舌，得五不別離。何等五？
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「rén bù liǎng shé， dé wǔ bù bié lí。 hé děng wǔ？
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “One who does not speak divisively gains five kinds of non-separation. Which five?
身不別離，無散亂者；
shēn bù bié lí， wú sàn luàn zhě；
His person is not divided, and none can scatter him;
眷屬不散，不傲他人；
juàn shǔ bù sàn， bù ào tā rén；
his retinue does not disperse, and he does not slight others;
得信無壞，見於緣報；
dé xìn wú huài， jiàn yú yuán bào；
he gains an unbreakable trust and perceives the recompense of conditions;
他無壞法，以行為要；
tā wú huài fǎ， yǐ xíng wéi yào；
others cannot break his Dharma, for practice is his essential;
得親友和，用無欺故。
dé qīn yǒu hé， yòng wú qī gù。
and he gains concord with kin and friends, because he does not deceive.
以是德本求最正覺，
yǐ shì dé běn qiú zuì zhèng jué，
By this root of virtue he seeks supreme enlightenment,
得成如來眷屬無亂，
dé chéng rú lái juàn shǔ wú luàn，
and becomes a Tathagata whose retinue is without disorder,
一切眾魔及與怨敵終不能壞如來眷屬。」
yī qiè zhòng mó jí yǔ yuàn dí zhōng bù néng huài rú lái juàn shǔ。」
so that no host of maras nor any foe can ever break the Tathagata's retinue.”
佛語龍王：「人不惡口，得八清淨言辭之報，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「rén bù è kǒu， dé bā qīng jìng yán cí zhī bào，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “One who does not speak harshly gains the reward of eight kinds of pure speech,
壽終之後得生天上。何等八？
shòu zhōng zhī hòu dé shēng tiān shàng。 hé děng bā？
and when his life ends he is born in the heavens. Which eight?
所說如諦，所言柔軟，
suǒ shuō rú dì， suǒ yán róu ruǎn，
What he says accords with truth; his words are gentle;
所言如應，所言和順，
suǒ yán rú yìng， suǒ yán hé shùn，
his words are apt; his words are harmonious and agreeable;
所言能受，所言光曜，
suǒ yán néng shòu， suǒ yán guāng yào，
his words can be received; his words are radiant;
所言眾人莫不承樂，
suǒ yán zhòng rén mò bù chéng lè，
his words are gladly accepted by everyone;
所言眾所不譏。
suǒ yán zhòng suǒ bù jī。
and his words draw reproach from none.
因是德本，志願無上正真之道，
yīn shì dé běn， zhì yuàn wú shàng zhèng zhēn zhī dào，
By this root of virtue he aspires to unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment,
得成如來音聲超梵。」
dé chéng rú lái yīn shēng chāo fàn。」
and becomes a Tathagata whose voice surpasses that of Brahma.”
佛語龍王：「人不綺語，得三正行。何等為三？
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「rén bù qǐ yǔ， dé sān zhèng xíng。 hé děng wéi sān？
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “One who does not speak frivolously gains three right practices. Which three?
常為眾明諸等敬愛；
cháng wéi zhòng míng zhū děng jìng ài；
He is always a light to the multitude, revered and loved by his fellows;
心常專一，入于至誠；
xīn cháng zhuān yī， rù yú zhì chéng；
his mind is ever single and enters utter sincerity;
不以多言，於天上、人間常得大尊，不為雜碎。
bù yǐ duō yán， yú tiān shàng、 rén jiān cháng dé dà zūn， bù wéi zá suì。
he is not given to much talk, and among devas and men he is always greatly honored, never trifling.
以是德本，志願無上正真之道，為佛所授決，
yǐ shì dé běn， zhì yuàn wú shàng zhèng zhēn zhī dào， wèi fó suǒ shòu jué，
By this root of virtue he aspires to unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment, receives the Buddha's prediction,
得成如來所言無異。」
dé chéng rú lái suǒ yán wú yì。」
and becomes a Tathagata whose words never vary.”
佛語龍王：「人不嫉妬，得五威神。何等五？
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「rén bù jí dù， dé wǔ wēi shén。 hé děng wǔ？
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “One who is not envious gains five majestic powers. Which five?
身、口、意明，諸根具足；
shēn、 kǒu、 yì míng， zhū gēn jù zú；
His body, speech and mind are clear, and his faculties are complete;
得極財富而以自恣；
dé jí cái fù ér yǐ zì zì；
he gains utmost wealth and disposes of it as he pleases;
降伏諸冤，樂於飲食、美味生活之業；
xiáng fú zhū yuān， lè yú yǐn shí、 měi wèi shēng huó zhī yè；
he subdues all enmity and delights in food, fine flavors, and the affairs of livelihood;
福德巍巍，為諸國王所見恭敬，而蒙覆蓋；
fú dé wēi wēi， wéi zhū guó wáng suǒ jiàn gōng jìng， ér méng fù gài；
his merit is lofty; kings revere him and extend their protection over him;
如己所有微妙之寶，致差特家功德宿本，
rú jǐ suǒ yǒu wēi miào zhī bǎo， zhì chā tè jiā gōng dé sù běn，
the subtle treasures he possesses come from the merit of former lives in an eminent house,
不嫉他財。
bù jí tā cái。
for he did not envy the wealth of others.
因是德本，志願無上正真之道，
yīn shì dé běn， zhì yuàn wú shàng zhèng zhēn zhī dào，
By this root of virtue he aspires to unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment,
成等世尊，三界所奉。」
chéng děng shì zūn， sān jiè suǒ fèng。」
becomes an equal World-Honored One, honored throughout the three realms.”
佛語龍王：「人不瞋恚，得八心歡喜法。何等為八？
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「rén bù chēn huì， dé bā xīn huān xǐ fǎ。 hé děng wéi bā？
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “One free of anger gains eight dharmas of a joyful mind. Which eight?
無害樂諦，滅除瞋恚；
wú hài lè dì， miè chú chēn huì；
He is harmless and delights in truth, extinguishing anger;
樂於誠實，心不樂諍；
lè yú chéng shí， xīn bù lè zhèng；
he delights in honesty and takes no pleasure in strife;
心樂質直，安祥而和；
xīn lè zhì zhí， ān xiáng ér hé；
his mind delights in uprightness, tranquil and harmonious;
等於聖賢，常懷慈心；
děng yú shèng xián， cháng huái cí xīn；
he is the equal of the holy sages and ever holds a heart of kindness;
愍傷具足見人安悅，
mǐn shāng jù zú jiàn rén ān yuè，
his compassion is complete, and those who see him are at ease and glad;
端正殊好眾人所敬，
duān zhèng shū hǎo zhòng rén suǒ jìng，
he is upright and exceedingly fair, revered by all;
生於梵天不以為難，
shēng yú fàn tiān bù yǐ wéi nán，
birth in the Brahma heaven is no difficulty for him;
心以方便哀和之故，是為八。
xīn yǐ fāng biàn āi hé zhī gù， shì wéi bā。
because his mind is skilled in compassion and concord. These are the eight.
因是八德本，志願無上正真道意，
yīn shì bā dé běn， zhì yuàn wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì，
By these eight roots of virtue he aspires to the mind of unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment,
得為如來至真等正覺觀無厭者。」
dé wéi rú lái zhì zhēn děng zhèng jué guān wú yàn zhě。」
and becomes a Tathagata, Truly Worthy and Perfectly Enlightened, upon whom none tire of gazing.”
佛語龍王：「人不邪見，得十法德。何等十？
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「rén bù xié jiàn， dé shí fǎ dé。 hé děng shí？
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “One free of wrong views gains ten virtuous dharmas. Which ten?
志性真實，得人善友；
zhì xìng zhēn shí， dé rén shàn yǒu；
His nature and resolve are true, and he gains good friends;
信善惡之報；
xìn shàn è zhī bào；
he believes in the recompense of good and evil;
若已沒命，不傷犯人；
ruò yǐ mò mìng， bù shāng fàn rén；
even at the cost of his life he will not injure another;
念行佛道，心無有異；
niàn xíng fó dào， xīn wú yǒu yì；
he is mindful of practicing the buddha path with an undivided mind;
不事天神，志懷質朴；
bù shì tiān shén， zhì huái zhì pǔ；
he does not serve gods and spirits, and his resolve is plain and honest;
捨於諛諂神呪之術；
shě yú yú chǎn shén zhòu zhī shù；
he abandons flattery and the arts of spells;
與諸天人以為朋友，
yǔ zhū tiān rén yǐ wéi péng yǒu，
he takes devas and men as his friends,
不與地獄、餓鬼、畜生而作伴侶；
bù yǔ dì yù、 è guǐ、 chù sheng ér zuò bàn lǚ；
and is no companion to hell-beings, hungry ghosts, or animals;
與眾特異功德巍巍聖道為上；
yǔ zhòng tè yì gōng dé wēi wēi shèng dào wéi shàng；
he stands apart from the crowd, lofty in merit, holding the sage's path supreme;
離於邪見，離於貪身，離於惡見；
lí yú xié jiàn， lí yú tān shēn， lí yú è jiàn；
he departs from wrong views, from craving for the body, from evil views;
都無罣礙，於聖平等，
dōu wú guà ài， yú shèng píng děng，
he is wholly without obstruction and equal to the sages;
須臾之間生天上、人間。
xū yú zhī jiān shēng tiān shàng、 rén jiān。
and in a moment he is born among devas or men.
是為十德法，已離邪見得本，
shì wéi shí dé fǎ， yǐ lí xié jiàn dé běn，
These are the ten virtuous dharmas. Having left wrong views and gained the root,
志願無上正真之道，近得諸佛道法，
zhì yuàn wú shàng zhèng zhēn zhī dào， jìn dé zhū fó dào fǎ，
he aspires to unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment, draws near the dharmas of all buddhas,
速逮神通，成為如來。」
sù dǎi shén tōng， chéng wéi rú lái。」
swiftly attains the spiritual penetrations, and becomes a Tathagata.”
佛語龍王：「菩薩離於殺生而行布施，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「pú sà lí yú shā shēng ér xíng bù shī，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “A bodhisattva who gives while free of killing
常得大富、長壽、無極行、菩薩道，
cháng dé dà fù、 cháng shòu、 wú jí xíng、 pú sà dào，
always gains great wealth, long life, boundless practice and the bodhisattva path,
一切外怨莫能當者。
yī qiè wài yuàn mò néng dāng zhě。
and no outer foe can withstand him.
已離盜竊而布施者，既饒財寶，人不敢取，
yǐ lí dào qiè ér bù shī zhě， jì ráo cái bǎo， rén bù gǎn qǔ，
One who gives while free of stealing abounds in treasure that none dare take;
行菩薩道無能妨廢，合聚一切功德之法。
xíng pú sà dào wú néng fáng fèi， hé jù yī qiè gōng dé zhī fǎ。
none can obstruct his bodhisattva practice, and he gathers together all dharmas of merit.
離於邪婬而布施者，後常大富，
lí yú xié yín ér bù shī zhě， hòu cháng dà fù，
One who gives while free of sexual misconduct will ever after be greatly wealthy;
妻無逸態，在於人間無敢犯者，
qī wú yì tài， zài yú rén jiān wú gǎn fàn zhě，
his wife is without wantonness, and among men none dare violate her;
其家女人而不色視。
qí jiā nǚ rén ér bù sè shì。
the women of his household are not looked upon with lust.
離於妄語而布施者，常大富有，不被誹謗，
lí yú wàng yǔ ér bù shī zhě， cháng dà fù yǒu， bù bèi fěi bàng，
One who gives while free of lying is always greatly wealthy and suffers no slander;
以下劣人皆蒙擁護，
yǐ xià liè rén jiē méng yōng hù，
even the lowly are sheltered by him;
行菩薩道言行相應，所願堅強。
xíng pú sà dào yán xíng xiāng yìng， suǒ yuàn jiān qiáng。
in bodhisattva practice his words and deeds accord, and his vows are firm.
離於兩舌而布施者，常大富有，眷屬不別，
lí yú liǎng shé ér bù shī zhě， cháng dà fù yǒu， juàn shǔ bù bié，
One who gives while free of divisive speech is always greatly wealthy and his retinue is not divided;
行菩薩道則得菩薩一切眷屬質直等性。
xíng pú sà dào zé dé pú sà yī qiè juàn shǔ zhì zhí děng xìng。
in bodhisattva practice he gains a retinue of bodhisattvas, upright and of one nature.
已離惡口而布施者，常大富有，所言人受，
yǐ lí è kǒu ér bù shī zhě， cháng dà fù yǒu， suǒ yán rén shòu，
One who gives while free of harsh speech is always greatly wealthy and his words are accepted;
行菩薩道入於眾會莫不欣樂。
xíng pú sà dào rù yú zhòng huì mò bù xīn lè。
entering any assembly in his bodhisattva practice, all are delighted.
離於綺語而布施者，常大富有，所言輒行，
lí yú qǐ yǔ ér bù shī zhě， cháng dà fù yǒu， suǒ yán zhé xíng，
One who gives while free of frivolous speech is always greatly wealthy and his words are at once carried out;
行菩薩道斷一切疑。
xíng pú sà dào duàn yī qiè yí。
in bodhisattva practice he severs every doubt.
離於嫉妬而布施者，常大富有，
lí yú jí dù ér bù shī zhě， cháng dà fù yǒu，
One who gives while free of envy is always greatly wealthy,
喜好、衣食、床臥具足，
xǐ hǎo、 yī shí、 chuáng wò jù zú，
complete in comforts, clothing, food and bedding;
行菩薩道已所喜者，而以加施得大尊豪。
xíng pú sà dào yǐ suǒ xǐ zhě， ér yǐ jiā shī dé dà zūn háo。
in bodhisattva practice he gives away even what he cherishes, and gains great eminence.
離瞋恚心而布施者，常大富有，威耀端正，
lí chēn huì xīn ér bù shī zhě， cháng dà fù yǒu， wēi yào duān zhèng，
One who gives with a mind free of anger is always greatly wealthy, majestic and comely;
所言說者眾人愛樂，
suǒ yán shuō zhě zhòng rén ài lè，
whatever he says is loved by all;
行菩薩道心無加害，諸根具足。
xíng pú sà dào xīn wú jiā hài， zhū gēn jù zú。
in bodhisattva practice his mind does no harm and his faculties are complete.
離於邪見而布施者，常大富有，立於正見，
lí yú xié jiàn ér bù shī zhě， cháng dà fù yǒu， lì yú zhèng jiàn，
One who gives while free of wrong views is always greatly wealthy and established in right view;
生於族姓，值佛、世尊，
shēng yú zú xìng， zhí fó、 shì zūn，
he is born into a noble clan and meets the Buddha, the World-Honored One;
行菩薩道不離諸佛，常得聞法，發菩薩心。」
xíng pú sà dào bù lí zhū fó， cháng dé wén fǎ， fā pú sà xīn。」
in bodhisattva practice he is never apart from the buddhas, ever hears the Dharma, and arouses the bodhisattva mind.”
佛語龍王：「是謂十善布施，莊嚴廣大乃爾！
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「shì wèi shí shàn bù shī， zhuāng yán guǎng dà nǎi ěr！
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “Such is giving joined to the ten wholesome deeds — its adornment is vast indeed!
此十善行，以戒莊嚴，以自具願，得諸佛法；
cǐ shí shàn xíng， yǐ jiè zhuāng yán， yǐ zì jù yuàn， dé zhū fó fǎ；
These ten good practices, adorned with precepts, fulfill one's own vows and gain the dharmas of the buddhas;
以忍莊嚴，諸相種好，成佛音聲；
yǐ rěn zhuāng yán， zhū xiàng zhǒng hǎo， chéng fó yīn shēng；
adorned with patience, they yield the marks and signs and the voice of a buddha;
以精進莊嚴，降伏魔怨，以佛道法有所超度；
yǐ jīng jìn zhuāng yán， xiáng fú mó yuàn， yǐ fó dào fǎ yǒu suǒ chāo dù；
adorned with vigor, they subdue Mara and his foes and carry one beyond by the buddha path;
以禪莊嚴，心意所趣而以清淨；
yǐ chán zhuāng yán， xīn yì suǒ qù ér yǐ qīng jìng；
adorned with dhyana, wherever the mind inclines is pure;
以智慧莊嚴，除諸枉見；
yǐ zhì huì zhuāng yán， chú zhū wǎng jiàn；
adorned with wisdom, they remove all crooked views;
行慈莊嚴，當以仁和，不害眾生；
xíng cí zhuāng yán， dāng yǐ rén hé， bù hài zhòng shēng；
adorned with the practice of loving-kindness, one is gentle and harms no living being;
行哀莊嚴，不捨黎庶；
xíng āi zhuāng yán， bù shě lí shù；
adorned with the practice of compassion, one does not forsake the common folk;
行喜莊嚴，無懈厭心；
xíng xǐ zhuāng yán， wú xiè yàn xīn；
adorned with the practice of sympathetic joy, one is never slack or weary;
行護莊嚴，得無所著，斷諸疑結；
xíng hù zhuāng yán， dé wú suǒ zhuó， duàn zhū yí jié；
adorned with the practice of equanimity, one becomes unattached and severs the knots of doubt;
行恩莊嚴，勸化群萌；
xíng ēn zhuāng yán， quàn huà qún méng；
adorned with the practice of kindness bestowed, one exhorts and transforms the multitudes;
行意止莊嚴，止身諸痛痒，心法具足；
xíng yì zhǐ zhuāng yán， zhǐ shēn zhū tòng yǎng， xīn fǎ jù zú；
adorned with the foundations of mindfulness, one stills the body's aches and completes the dharmas of mind;
行意斷莊嚴，斷諸惡法，具足善德；
xíng yì duàn zhuāng yán， duàn zhū è fǎ， jù zú shàn dé；
adorned with the right exertions, one severs evil dharmas and completes good virtue;
神足莊嚴，神足輕舉；
shén zú zhuāng yán， shén zú qīng jǔ；
adorned with the bases of spiritual power, one rises lightly by those powers;
五根莊嚴，堅固其行，
wǔ gēn zhuāng yán， jiān gù qí xíng，
adorned with the five faculties, one's practice is firm,
以上精進而無放逸，以心修治除諸塵勞；
yǐ shàng jīng jìn ér wú fàng yì， yǐ xīn xiū zhì chú zhū chén láo；
with supreme vigor and no laxity, cultivating the mind to remove defilement and toil;
五力莊嚴，以質直心降化眾怨；
wǔ lì zhuāng yán， yǐ zhì zhí xīn jiàng huà zhòng yuàn；
adorned with the five powers, with an upright mind one subdues and converts all foes;
覺意莊嚴，曉了諸法，如本所由；
jué yì zhuāng yán， xiǎo liǎo zhū fǎ， rú běn suǒ yóu；
adorned with the factors of awakening, one understands all dharmas as they originally are;
八路莊嚴，懷來正慧；
bā lù zhuāng yán， huái lái zhèng huì；
adorned with the eightfold path, one embraces right wisdom;
寂然莊嚴，滅除一切諸垢塵勞；
jì rán zhuāng yán， miè chú yī qiè zhū gòu chén láo；
adorned with stillness, one extinguishes every defilement, dust and toil;
以觀莊嚴，觀諸法本，審諦悉無；
yǐ guān zhuāng yán， guān zhū fǎ běn， shěn dì xī wú；
adorned with contemplation, one contemplates the root of dharmas and discerns that all is nothing;
善權莊嚴，有數無數、有為無為，具足安隱。」
shàn quán zhuāng yán， yǒu shù wú shù、 yǒu wéi wú wéi， jù zú ān yǐn。」
adorned with skillful means, whether countable or countless, conditioned or unconditioned, all is complete in peace and ease.”
佛語龍王：「取要言之，十善之德，
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「qǔ yào yán zhī， shí shàn zhī dé，
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “In sum, the virtue of the ten wholesome deeds
具足十力、四無所畏，成諸佛法。
jù zú shí lì、 sì wú suǒ wèi， chéng zhū fó fǎ。
completes the ten powers and the four fearlessnesses and accomplishes the dharmas of the buddhas.
以具足之故，於是十善之德，
yǐ jù zú zhī gù， yú shì shí shàn zhī dé，
Because of this completeness, the virtue of the ten wholesome deeds
廣普莊嚴，常當精進。
guǎng pǔ zhuāng yán， cháng dāng jīng jìn。
should be adorned far and wide, with unceasing diligence.
譬如郡國、縣邑、村落、丘聚、
pì rú jùn guó、 xiàn yì、 cūn luò、 qiū jù、
Just as commanderies and states, districts and towns, villages and hamlets,
百穀、藥草、樹木、華果種殖刈穫，皆因地立；
bǎi gǔ、 yào cǎo、 shù mù、 huā guǒ zhòng zhí yì huò， jiē yīn dì lì；
the hundred grains, medicinal herbs, trees, flowers and fruits, their sowing and their reaping, all rest upon the earth,
十善之德，天上、人間皆依因之。
shí shàn zhī dé， tiān shàng、 rén jiān jiē yī yīn zhī。
so devas and men alike depend upon the virtue of the ten wholesome deeds.
若學、不學及得果證、住緣覺道、
ruò xué、 bù xué jí dé guǒ zhèng、 zhù yuán jué dào、
Whether learner or adept, whether one attains the fruits, abides on the pratyekabuddha path,
菩薩道行、諸佛道法，皆由從之。」
pú sà dào xíng、 zhū fó dào fǎ， jiē yóu cóng zhī。」
practices the bodhisattva path, or realizes the dharmas of the buddhas — all proceed from it.”
於是海龍王白世尊曰：
yú shì hǎi lóng wáng bái shì zūn yuē：
Thereupon the Sea Dragon King addressed the World-Honored One:
「何謂入法門菩薩所行？
「hé wèi rù fǎ mén pú sà suǒ xíng？
“What is the gate of entry into the Dharma that bodhisattvas practice?
入法門者，除於宿世陰蓋之罪。
rù fǎ mén zhě， chú yú sù shì yīn gài zhī zuì。
One who enters that gate removes the sins of the aggregate-hindrances of former lives.
已除陰蓋，得至超異。」
yǐ chú yīn gài， dé zhì chāo yì。」
Having removed those hindrances, he arrives at surpassing distinction.”
佛語龍王：「菩薩有一法除諸罪蓋。何等一？
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「pú sà yǒu yī fǎ chú zhū zuì gài。 hé děng yī？
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “A bodhisattva has one dharma that removes the hindrances of sin. Which one?
立於擁護，不捨所說，悔過首罪。
lì yú yōng hù， bù shě suǒ shuō， huǐ guò shǒu zuì。
To stand in protection, not to forsake what has been taught, and to repent and confess his offenses.
復有二法除諸罪蓋。何等二？
fù yǒu èr fǎ chú zhū zuì gài。 hé děng èr？
There are two more dharmas that remove the hindrances of sin. Which two?
常觀淨法，不造現在。
cháng guān jìng fǎ， bù zào xiàn zài。
Always to contemplate pure dharmas, and not to fabricate the present.
復有三法除諸罪蓋。何等三？
fù yǒu sān fǎ chú zhū zuì gài。 hé děng sān？
There are three more. Which three?
入因緣慧，具足悅心，依本淨法了知本無。
rù yīn yuán huì， jù zú yuè xīn， yī běn jìng fǎ liǎo zhī běn wú。
To enter the wisdom of causes and conditions, to perfect a joyful mind, and, relying on the originally pure Dharma, to know original nonbeing.
復有四法除諸罪蓋。何等四？
fù yǒu sì fǎ chú zhū zuì gài。 hé děng sì？
There are four more. Which four?
曉了於空，不住無想，趣於無願，慧無所造。
xiǎo liǎo yú kōng， bú zhù wú xiǎng， qù yú wú yuàn， huì wú suǒ zào。
To understand emptiness, not to abide in signlessness, to incline toward wishlessness, and to have a wisdom that fabricates nothing.
復有五法除諸罪蓋。何等五？
fù yǒu wǔ fǎ chú zhū zuì gài。 hé děng wǔ？
There are five more. Which five?
無我、無人、無壽、無命、無識。
wú wǒ、 wú rén、 wú shòu、 wú mìng、 wú shí。
No self, no person, no lifespan, no living being, no consciousness.
復有六法除諸罪蓋。何等六？
fù yǒu liù fǎ chú zhū zuì gài。 hé děng liù？
There are six more. Which six?
歡喜篤信，而無狐疑，往返進止，
huān xǐ dǔ xìn， ér wú hú yí， wǎng fǎn jìn zhǐ，
Joyful and firm in faith, free of doubt, whether going or returning, advancing or halting,
觀察審諦，所作至誠，不失正信；
guān chá shěn dì， suǒ zuò zhì chéng， bù shī zhèng xìn；
observing with careful discernment, acting with utter sincerity, never losing right faith.
是為六法除諸罪蓋。」
shì wéi liù fǎ chú zhū zuì gài。」
These are the six dharmas that remove the hindrances of sin.”
龍王白佛：「何謂菩薩得至超異？」
lóng wáng bái fó： 「hé wèi pú sà dé zhì chāo yì？」
The Dragon King said to the Buddha: “What is it for a bodhisattva to arrive at surpassing distinction?”
世尊曰：「菩薩有十事得至超異。何等十？
shì zūn yuē： 「pú sà yǒu shí shì dé zhì chāo yì。 hé děng shí？
The World-Honored One said: “A bodhisattva has ten things by which he arrives at surpassing distinction. Which ten?
常念歡悅；心性清淨；
cháng niàn huān yuè； xīn xìng qīng jìng；
Constant mindfulness and gladness; purity of mind-nature;
善權方便；堅強精進；
shàn quán fāng biàn； jiān qiáng jīng jìn；
skillful means; firm and strong vigor;
觀察人物，行無極哀；
guān chá rén wù， xíng wú jí āi；
observing beings and practicing boundless compassion;
修德無厭，博聞不惓；
xiū dé wú yàn， bó wén bù quán；
cultivating virtue without weariness and hearing widely without fatigue;
奉無放逸；念於道場；
fèng wú fàng yì； niàn yú dào chǎng；
practicing without laxity; mindfulness of the bodhimanda (place of practice);
令得佛慧；不捨道心。
lìng dé fó huì； bù shě dào xīn。
causing others to gain the Buddha's wisdom; never abandoning the mind of the Way.
是為十事，菩薩所行得至超異。」
shì wéi shí shì， pú sà suǒ xíng dé zhì chāo yì。」
These are the ten things by which the bodhisattva's practice arrives at surpassing distinction.”
燕居阿須倫受決品第十二
yān jū ā xū lún shòu jué pǐn dì shí èr
Chapter Twelve: The Prediction for the Asura Yanju
於是燕居無善神白世尊曰：
yú shì yān jū wú shàn shén bái shì zūn yuē：
Thereupon the asura Yanju said to the World-Honored One:
「何謂菩薩超諸德上？」
「hé wèi pú sà chāo zhū dé shàng？」
“How does a bodhisattva rise above all virtues?”
佛告無善神：「菩薩有八法超諸德上。何等為八？
fó gào wú shàn shén： 「pú sà yǒu bā fǎ chāo zhū dé shàng。 hé děng wéi bā？
The Buddha told the asura: “A bodhisattva has eight dharmas by which he rises above all virtues. Which eight?
菩薩於是離於貢高為一切人下屈謙敬；
pú sà yú shì lí yú gòng gāo wèi yī qiè rén xià qū qiān jìng；
Herein a bodhisattva departs from pride and bows humbly and respectfully before all;
受教恭順言行相副謙順尊長；
shòu jiào gōng shùn yán xíng xiāng fù qiān shùn zūn zhǎng；
he receives instruction reverently, his words and deeds matching, humble and deferential to his elders;
一切德行諸法為本，所行堅強超諸善德；
yī qiè dé xíng zhū fǎ wéi běn， suǒ xíng jiān qiáng chāo zhū shàn dé；
taking all virtuous conduct and dharmas as his root, his practice is firm and surpasses every good virtue;
樂於微妙若干種施；
lè yú wēi miào ruò gān zhǒng shī；
he delights in the subtle and in giving of many kinds;
寧失身命不求人便；
níng shī shēn mìng bù qiú rén biàn；
he would sooner lose his life than seek advantage over another;
見有危懼施以無畏；
jiàn yǒu wēi jù shī yǐ wú wèi；
seeing those in peril and dread, he bestows fearlessness;
來歸命者不以捨棄；
lái guī mìng zhě bù yǐ shě qì；
those who come to him for refuge he does not cast aside;
求於一切福慧之業不以厭足；是為八法。」
qiú yú yī qiè fú huì zhī yè bù yǐ yàn zú； shì wéi bā fǎ。」
and he seeks every work of merit and wisdom without ever having enough. These are the eight.”
無善神又問佛言：「菩薩有幾法行，
wú shàn shén yòu wèn fó yán： 「pú sà yǒu jǐ fǎ xíng，
The asura further asked the Buddha: “By how many dharmas and practices does a bodhisattva
得身長大，面部弘滿，
dé shēn zhǎng dà， miàn bù hóng mǎn，
gain a body tall and great, a face broad and full,
眷屬繁熾，意廣無極？」
juàn shǔ fán chì， yì guǎng wú jí？」
a retinue flourishing, and a mind vast and boundless?”
佛告燕居無善神：
fó gào yān jū wú shàn shén：
The Buddha told the asura Yanju:
「菩薩有四事得身長大。何等為四？
「pú sà yǒu sì shì dé shēn zhǎng dà。 hé děng wéi sì？
“A bodhisattva has four things by which he gains a tall and great body. Which four?
不說他人所作貪嫉；
bù shuō tā rén suǒ zuò tān jí；
He does not speak of the greed and envy that others do;
作佛形像，根相具足；
zuò fó xíng xiàng， gēn xiāng jù zú；
he has images of the Buddha made, complete in faculties and marks;
和合離別，勸令志於無上正真之道；
hé hé lí bié， quàn lìng zhì yú wú shàng zhèng zhēn zhī dào；
he reconciles those who are estranged and urges them to aspire to unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment;
向於眾生無傷害貌。
xiàng yú zhòng shēng wú shāng hài mào。
and toward living beings he wears no look of harm.
是為四事得身長大。
shì wéi sì shì dé shēn zhǎng dà。
These are the four by which he gains a tall and great body.
菩薩有四事面部弘滿。何等四？
pú sà yǒu sì shì miàn bù hóng mǎn。 hé děng sì？
A bodhisattva has four things by which his face becomes broad and full. Which four?
若干瓔珞而用布施，
ruò gān yīng luò ér yòng bù shī，
He gives away jeweled garlands of many kinds;
一切所愛施而不惜，
yī qiè suǒ ài shī ér bù xī，
he gives all he loves without begrudging it;
常以慈眼觀於如來，
cháng yǐ cí yǎn guān yú rú lái，
he always looks upon the Tathagata with kindly eyes;
見人端正不生嫉妬；
jiàn rén duān zhèng bù shēng jí dù；
and seeing others fair of form he conceives no envy.
是為四事得面色弘滿。
shì wéi sì shì dé miàn sè hóng mǎn。
These are the four by which his countenance becomes broad and full.
菩薩有四事得眷屬繁熾。何等四？
pú sà yǒu sì shì dé juàn shǔ fán chì。 hé děng sì？
A bodhisattva has four things by which his retinue flourishes. Which four?
離於兩舌，未曾破壞他人眷屬；
lí yú liǎng shé， wèi céng pò huài tā rén juàn shǔ；
He departs from divisive speech and never breaks up another's retinue;
見他友黨代其歡喜；
jiàn tā yǒu dǎng dài qí huān xǐ；
seeing others' companions he rejoices on their behalf;
不捨道心；
bù shě dào xīn；
he does not abandon the mind of the Way;
并化他人，令發道意；是為四。
bìng huà tā rén， lìng fā dào yì； shì wéi sì。
and he converts others so that they too arouse the aspiration to the Way. These are the four.
菩薩有四事意廣無極。何等為四？
pú sà yǒu sì shì yì guǎng wú jí。 hé děng wéi sì？
A bodhisattva has four things by which his mind becomes vast and boundless. Which four?
其心清淨而無諛諂，除重愛欲；
qí xīn qīng jìng ér wú yú chǎn， chú zhòng ài yù；
His mind is pure and without flattery, and he removes heavy craving and desire;
所在中間而無厭疲；
suǒ zài zhōng jiān ér wú yàn pí；
wherever he is he is never weary;
志於微妙，深入要法；
zhì yú wēi miào， shēn rù yào fǎ；
he aspires to the subtle and enters deeply into the essential Dharma;
一切諸法本末皆空。
yī qiè zhū fǎ běn mò jiē kōng。
and all dharmas, from beginning to end, are empty.
是為四，菩薩意廣無極。」
shì wéi sì， pú sà yì guǎng wú jí。」
These are the four by which a bodhisattva's mind becomes vast and boundless.”
於是燕居無善神與三萬二千眷屬，
yú shì yān jū wú shàn shén yǔ sān wàn èr qiān juàn shǔ，
Thereupon the asura Yanju, with thirty-two thousand of his retinue,
各各以若干種八千天華散世尊上，以偈讚曰：
gè gè yǐ ruò gān zhǒng bā qiān tiān huā sàn shì zūn shàng， yǐ jì zàn yuē：
each scattered eight thousand celestial flowers of many kinds over the World-Honored One, and praised him in verse:
「其有於十方，人種不可量，
「qí yǒu yú shí fāng， rén zhǒng bù kě liàng，
“Were all the kinds of beings in the ten directions, beyond measuring,
須臾一時頃，則供養世尊。
xū yú yī shí qǐng， zé gòng yǎng shì zūn。
in a single instant to make offering to the World-Honored One;
假使十方人，一切供養德，
jiǎ shǐ shí fāng rén， yī qiè gòng yǎng dé，
and were the people of the ten directions, with all the merit of their offerings,
在於百千劫，等心給足人，
zài yú bǎi qiān jié， děng xīn gěi zú rén，
for a hundred thousand kalpas to supply all beings with impartial mind —
不能及慈心，愍傷之福行，
bù néng jí cí xīn， mǐn shāng zhī fú xíng，
it could not match the merit of a mind of loving-kindness and pity.
寂然極如應，供養安能報。
jì rán jí rú yìng， gòng yǎng ān néng bào。
Still and utterly fitting: how could offerings ever repay it?
是為諸十方，供養之奉事，
shì wéi zhū shí fāng， gòng yǎng zhī fèng shì，
This is the offering and service of all the ten directions;
發心無與等，正諦住如應。
fā xīn wú yǔ děng， zhèng dì zhù rú yìng。
the arousing of the mind has no equal, abiding truly and fittingly.
於是吾自由，立志無諛諂，
yú shì wú zì yóu， lì zhì wú yú chǎn，
Herein I am free, my resolve set without flattery;
已覺了本無，自致得佛道。
yǐ jué le běn wú， zì zhì dé fó dào。
having awakened to original nonbeing, I shall myself attain the buddha path.
人中尊見愍，三界無諛諂，
rén zhōng zūn jiàn mǐn， sān jiè wú yú chǎn，
Honored One among men, look on me with pity — in the three realms there is no flattery;
十力證明我，解我心性行。
shí lì zhèng míng wǒ， jiě wǒ xīn xìng xíng。
Ten Powers, bear witness for me and understand the conduct of my mind and nature.
柔軟無怯弱，以離諸恐懼，
róu ruǎn wú qiè ruò， yǐ lí zhū kǒng jù，
Gentle and without timidity, having left all fear behind,
布施調禁勝，持戒及忍辱，
bù shī diào jìn shèng， chí jiè jí rěn rǔ，
excelling in giving, taming and restraint, in precepts and in patience,
精進于定意，為應住智慧，
jīng jìn yú dìng yì， wéi yīng zhù zhì huì，
in vigor and in concentration, fittingly abiding in wisdom,
順從慈愍哀，常奉至誠行。
shùn cóng cí mǐn āi， cháng fèng zhì chéng xíng。
following loving-kindness, pity and compassion, ever upholding sincere conduct.
加哀不為我，勝不受善決，
jiā āi bù wéi wǒ， shèng bù shòu shàn jué，
If compassion be not shown me, and I receive no good prediction,
人尊我便當，自別受佛決，
rén zūn wǒ biàn dāng， zì bié shòu fó jué，
Honored One among men, then I shall myself receive the Buddha's prediction apart;
吾悉不狐疑，謂不成自由，
wú xī bù hú yí， wèi bù chéng zì yóu，
I have no doubt at all that I shall not fail to become free;
吾了了究竟，志淨在于道。」
wú liǎo liǎo jiū jìng， zhì jìng zài yú dào。」
I understand it wholly and to the end, my resolve pure and set upon the Way.”
人中尊時笑，月英尋問曰：
rén zhōng zūn shí xiào， yuè yīng xún wèn yuē：
Then the Honored One among men smiled, and Moon Blossom at once asked:
「今何故欣笑？唯聖解說之。」
「jīn hé gù xīn xiào？ wéi shèng jiě shuō zhī。」
“Why do you smile with delight? We pray the Sage explain it.”
化度時告曰：「月英具聽之，
huà dù shí gào yuē： 「yuè yīng jù tīng zhī，
The Converter and Deliverer said: “Moon Blossom, listen fully:
吾所以示現，奮出大光明。
wú suǒ yǐ shì xiàn， fèn chū dà guāng míng。
the reason I have manifested and sent forth this great radiance.
時燕居廣普，志願尊大道，
shí yān jū guǎng pǔ， zhì yuàn zūn dà dào，
Yanju, vast and universal, aspires to the honored great Way;
三萬二千人，諸所從眷屬，
sān wàn èr qiān rén， zhū suǒ cóng juàn shǔ，
and thirty-two thousand people, all his attendant retinue,
於百千劫中，常行菩薩道，
yú bǎi qiān jié zhōng， cháng xíng pú sà dào，
through a hundred thousand kalpas will constantly practice the bodhisattva path,
一切志同等，當得聖佛道。
yī qiè zhì tóng děng， dāng dé shèng fó dào。
all of one aspiration, and will attain the holy buddha path.
當供養諸佛，等如恒邊沙，
dāng gòng yǎng zhū fó， děng rú héng biān shā，
They will make offerings to buddhas as many as the sands of the Ganges,
所開化人民，其數復過此。
suǒ kāi huà rén mín， qí shù fù guò cǐ。
and the people they convert will exceed even that number.
其劫曰歡喜，又號淨復淨，
qí jié yuē huān xǐ， yòu hào jìng fù jìng，
That kalpa will be called Joy, and also Pure and Pure Again;
得佛離寂塵，覺了至無憂。
dé fó lí jì chén， jué le zhì wú yōu。
attaining buddhahood, free of dust in stillness, awakened and come to sorrowlessness.
此燕居廣普，當逮為法王，
cǐ yān jū guǎng pǔ， dāng dǎi wéi fǎ wáng，
This Yanju, vast and universal, will become a King of Dharma,
超出精進力，號曰帝幡幢。
chāo chū jīng jìn lì， hào yuē dì fān chuáng。
surpassing in the power of vigor, named Sovereign Banner and Pennant.
其世界名曰，欣樂仁莊嚴，
qí shì jiè míng yuē， xīn lè rén zhuāng yán，
His world will be named Gladness, Kindness and Adornment,
衣食豐平賤，譬如兜術天。
yī shí fēng píng jiàn， pì rú dōu shù tiān。
with clothing and food abundant and cheap, like the Tusita heaven.
其十力壽命，七十億萬歲，
qí shí lì shòu mìng， qī shí yì wàn suì，
That One of Ten Powers will live seven hundred million million years,
滿六十那術，比丘比丘尼，
mǎn liù shí nà shù， bǐ qiū bǐ qiū ní，
with a full sixty nayutas of bhiksus and bhiksunis,
諸菩薩之眾，有八百那術，
zhū pú sà zhī zhòng， yǒu bā bǎi nà shù，
and a host of bodhisattvas numbering eight hundred nayutas,
堅住於總持，所聞悉解了。
jiān zhù yú zǒng chí， suǒ wén xī jiě le。
firmly abiding in dharani and understanding all they hear.
彼於歡喜劫，又號淨復淨，
bǐ yú huān xǐ jié， yòu hào jìng fù jìng，
In that kalpa of Joy, also named Pure and Pure Again,
皆以如是比，當成尊佛道。
jiē yǐ rú shì bǐ， dāng chéng zūn fó dào。
all of them likewise will accomplish the honored buddha path.
開化百千人，悉使住佛道，
kāi huà bǎi qiān rén， xī shǐ zhù fó dào，
Converting a hundred thousand people, they will establish them all in the buddha path.
具觀是德勝，菩薩之道心。」
jù guān shì dé shèng， pú sà zhī dào xīn。」
Behold in full this surpassing virtue: the bodhisattva's mind of the Way.”
燕居無善神，彼聞佛授決，
yān jū wú shàn shén， bǐ wén fó shòu jué，
When the asura Yanju heard the Buddha's prediction,
四千萬大眾，悉發菩薩心。
sì qiān wàn dà zhòng， xī fā pú sà xīn。
forty million of the great assembly all aroused the bodhisattva mind.
三千界震動，則時雨天華，
sān qiān jiè zhèn dòng， zé shí yǔ tiān huā，
The three-thousandfold world quaked, and at once celestial flowers rained down:
善哉總要德，心意之所持。
shàn zāi zǒng yào dé， xīn yì zhī suǒ chí。
excellent, the virtue that gathers all essentials, held fast in mind and thought.
無焚龍王受決品第十三
wú fén lóng wáng shòu jué pǐn dì shí sān
Chapter Thirteen: The Prediction for the Dragon King Anavatapta
於是無焚龍王白佛言：
yú shì wú fén lóng wáng bái fó yán：
Thereupon the Dragon King Anavatapta said to the Buddha:
「一切諸法皆無所住，亦無有人。
「yī qiè zhū fǎ jiē wú suǒ zhù， yì wú yǒu rén。
“All dharmas abide nowhere, and there is no person.
何有受決者？
hé yǒu shòu jué zhě？
Who then is there to receive a prediction?
誰當成至無上正真道，為最正覺乎？」
shuí dāng chéng zhì wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào， wéi zuì zhèng jué hū？」
Who is it that shall come to unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment and be perfectly awakened?”
佛言：「如是！如仁所言：
fó yán： 「rú shì！ rú rén suǒ yán：
The Buddha said: “Just so! As you have said:
『一切諸法皆無所住，亦無有人。』
『yī qiè zhū fǎ jiē wú suǒ zhù， yì wú yǒu rén。』
‘All dharmas abide nowhere, and there is no person.’
一切諸法亦復如是。
yī qiè zhū fǎ yì fù rú shì。
So it is with all dharmas.
凡夫愚人處於顛倒，住吾我人，
fán fū yú rén chù yú diān dào， zhù wú wǒ rén，
Ordinary foolish people dwell in inversion, abiding in self and person;
無人起人想。
wú rén qǐ rén xiǎng。
where there is no person they conceive the notion of a person.
菩薩發大哀為除顛倒，去吾我人，
pú sà fā dà āi wéi chú diān dào， qù wú wǒ rén，
The bodhisattva rouses great compassion to remove that inversion, doing away with self and person,
被覺德鎧。
bèi jué dé kǎi。
and puts on the armor of awakened virtue.
此正士等，曉了諸法無吾我人，
cǐ zhèng shì děng， xiǎo liǎo zhū fǎ wú wú wǒ rén，
Such right-minded ones understand that dharmas have no self and no person,
開化誘立吾我人命。
kāi huà yòu lì wú wǒ rén mìng。
and convert and draw in those who cling to self, person and living being.
屬仁所云：『誰受決者？』
shǔ rén suǒ yún： 『shuí shòu jué zhě？』
As to what you asked — ‘Who receives the prediction?’ —
諸仁等解人空無我，則為受決；
zhū rén děng jiě rén kōng wú wǒ， zé wéi shòu jué；
worthy ones who understand that the person is empty and selfless: that is receiving the prediction;
一切法等觀諸法寂則為受決；
yī qiè fǎ děng guān zhū fǎ jì zé wéi shòu jué；
to contemplate all dharmas as equal and still: that is receiving the prediction;
諸佛國等而無所取，心淨無垢，則為受決；
zhū fó guó děng ér wú suǒ qǔ， xīn jìng wú gòu， zé wéi shòu jué；
to hold the buddha-lands equal and grasp nothing, the mind pure and undefiled: that is receiving the prediction;
慧觀諸佛等諸佛道不壞法界，則為受決；
huì guān zhū fó děng zhū fó dào bù huài fǎ jiè， zé wéi shòu jué；
to contemplate with wisdom that the buddhas are equal and the buddha path does not destroy the dharma-realm: that is receiving the prediction;
於諸魔眾等一切魔，
yú zhū mó zhòng děng yī qiè mó，
to regard all the hosts of Mara as equal,
於塵無塵了心本已，則為受決；
yú chén wú chén le xīn běn yǐ， zé wéi shòu jué；
and in defilement to find no defilement, understanding the mind's origin: that is receiving the prediction;
無名無相，無應不應，
wú míng wú xiàng， wú yìng bù yìng，
without name and without mark, neither responding nor not responding,
無念不念，不受不捨，則為受決。」
wú niàn bù niàn， bù shòu bù shě， zé wéi shòu jué。」
neither conceiving nor not conceiving, neither receiving nor relinquishing: that is receiving the prediction.”
佛語龍王：「其心意識無所住立，則為受決。
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「qí xīn yì shí wú suǒ zhù lì， zé wéi shòu jué。
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “When mind, thought and consciousness abide and stand nowhere, that is receiving the prediction.
諸法如是，以無因緣諸法本諦，
zhū fǎ rú shì， yǐ wú yīn yuán zhū fǎ běn dì，
Such are dharmas: without causes and conditions, the original truth of dharmas;
覺了諸法平等無異，
jué le zhū fǎ píng děng wú yì，
and to awaken to dharmas as equal and undifferentiated
則成無上正真之道。
zé chéng wú shàng zhèng zhēn zhī dào。
is to accomplish unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment.
究竟求本，無有受決及成佛道，
jiū jìng qiú běn， wú yǒu shòu jué jí chéng fó dào，
Seek the root to the end: there is no receiving of a prediction and no accomplishing of the buddha path,
若受決者，若受決已。
ruò shòu jué zhě， ruò shòu jué yǐ。
neither one who receives it nor one who has received it.
所以者何？諸法無形，本末悉斷，皆無有主。
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ zhū fǎ wú xíng， běn mò xī duàn， jiē wú yǒu zhǔ。
Why? Dharmas have no form; from beginning to end they are cut off; none has an owner.
一切諸法從因緣轉，
yī qiè zhū fǎ cóng yīn yuán zhuǎn，
All dharmas turn from causes and conditions.
諸法如空，無從生相故；
zhū fǎ rú kōng， wú cóng shēng xiāng gù；
Dharmas are like space, for they have no mark of arising;
諸法無從生相，無所至相故；
zhū fǎ wú cóng shēng xiāng， wú suǒ zhì xiāng gù；
dharmas have no mark of arising, for they have no mark of arrival;
諸法無所從來，計本空故；
zhū fǎ wú suǒ cóng lái， jì běn kōng gù；
dharmas come from nowhere, for reckoned at root they are empty;
諸法無所至，未發起故；
zhū fǎ wú suǒ zhì， wèi fā qǐ gù；
dharmas arrive nowhere, for they have not been set in motion;
諸法無所住，不可得處故；
zhū fǎ wú suǒ zhù， bù kě dé chù gù；
dharmas abide nowhere, for no place can be found for them;
諸法悉空，用無身故；
zhū fǎ xī kōng， yòng wú shēn gù；
dharmas are wholly empty, for they have no body;
諸法無著，用無猗故；
zhū fǎ wú zhuó， yòng wú yī gù；
dharmas are unattached, for they lean on nothing;
諸法無所猗，不可動故；
zhū fǎ wú suǒ yī， bù kě dòng gù；
dharmas lean on nothing, for they cannot be moved;
諸法不可動，無處所故；
zhū fǎ bù kě dòng， wú chù suǒ gù；
dharmas cannot be moved, for they have no place;
諸法皆愚，用自然故；
zhū fǎ jiē yú， yòng zì rán gù；
dharmas are all dumb, for they are so of themselves;
諸法自然，無言教故；
zhū fǎ zì rán， wú yán jiào gù；
dharmas are so of themselves, for they have no spoken teaching;
諸法無言教，無色像故；
zhū fǎ wú yán jiào， wú sè xiàng gù；
dharmas have no spoken teaching, for they have no form or image;
諸法無色像，用無念故；
zhū fǎ wú sè xiàng， yòng wú niàn gù；
dharmas have no form or image, for they have no conceiving;
諸法無念，無因緣故；
zhū fǎ wú niàn， wú yīn yuán gù；
dharmas have no conceiving, for they have no causes and conditions;
諸法無因緣，無所行故；
zhū fǎ wú yīn yuán， wú suǒ xíng gù；
dharmas have no causes and conditions, for they have no practice;
諸法無所行，用寂然故；
zhū fǎ wú suǒ xíng， yòng jì rán gù；
dharmas have no practice, for they are still;
諸法寂然，無受陰故；
zhū fǎ jì rán， wú shòu yīn gù；
dharmas are still, for they take on no aggregates;
諸法無受陰，本淨空故；
zhū fǎ wú shòu yīn， běn jìng kōng gù；
dharmas take on no aggregates, for they are originally pure and empty;
諸法脫相，用無二故；
zhū fǎ tuō xiàng， yòng wú èr gù；
dharmas have the mark of release, for there is no duality;
諸法無二，用本一故；
zhū fǎ wú èr， yòng běn yī gù；
dharmas are not two, for they are originally one;
諸法本一，離若干故；
zhū fǎ běn yī， lí ruò gān gù；
dharmas are originally one, for they are apart from multiplicity;
諸法等無差特，用等覺故。」
zhū fǎ děng wú chā tè， yòng děng jué gù。」
dharmas are equal and without distinction, for awakening is equal.”
佛語龍王：「解諸法等，無受決者、不成等覺。
fó yǔ lóng wáng： 「jiě zhū fǎ děng， wú shòu jué zhě、 bù chéng děng jué。
The Buddha said to the Dragon King: “Understanding dharmas as equal, there is none who receives a prediction and none who accomplishes equal awakening.
且觀于是，如來堅固，興無極哀，
qiě guān yú shì， rú lái jiān gù， xìng wú jí āi，
Yet behold: the Tathagata is firm, rousing boundless compassion,
及諸菩薩勸樂之力，
jí zhū pú sà quàn lè zhī lì，
and the bodhisattvas have the power to exhort and gladden.
諸法如是，以無央數為人解說，合會有數。
zhū fǎ rú shì， yǐ wú yāng shù wéi rén jiě shuō， hé huì yǒu shù。
Such are dharmas: by the countless he explains them to people, and gathers them into number.
於諸法觀無解脫人、法亦不度。
yú zhū fǎ guān wú jiě tuō rén、 fǎ yì bù dù。
Contemplating dharmas, no person is liberated and no dharma is ferried across.
人如法者，人亦如也，道亦如也，
rén rú fǎ zhě， rén yì rú yě， dào yì rú yě，
If the person is as the Dharma is, then the person is thus, and the Way is thus,
佛亦如也，決亦如也，諸法亦如也，故曰如來。
fó yì rú yě， jué yì rú yě， zhū fǎ yì rú yě， gù yuē rú lái。
the Buddha is thus, the prediction is thus, and all dharmas are thus — therefore he is called the Thus-Come.
了於本無，住本無故，而不可動，故曰本無。
le yú běn wú， zhù běn wú gù， ér bù kě dòng， gù yuē běn wú。
Understanding original nonbeing and abiding in it, he cannot be moved — therefore it is called original nonbeing.
本無像、本無壞，解了本無，故曰如來。
běn wú xiàng、 běn wú huài， jiě le běn wú， gù yuē rú lái。
Original nonbeing has no image and no destruction; understanding it, he is therefore called the Thus-Come.
以如來故，等住本無，
yǐ rú lái gù， děng zhù běn wú，
Because he is the Thus-Come, he abides equally in original nonbeing;
諸法如是等如本無住，
zhū fǎ rú shì děng rú běn wú zhù，
and dharmas likewise abide equally as original nonbeing.
是為如來等住之地。
shì wéi rú lái děng zhù zhī dì。
This is the ground of the Tathagata's equal abiding.
其有菩薩聞是說者，不恐不怖，不畏不難，
qí yǒu pú sà wén shì shuō zhě， bù kǒng bù bù， bù wèi bù nán，
A bodhisattva who hears this preached is not alarmed, not frightened, not afraid, not daunted,
以是如來等住之地，為人解說。」
yǐ shì rú lái děng zhù zhī dì， wéi rén jiě shuō。」
and explains to others this ground of the Tathagata's equal abiding.”
佛說是時，三千菩薩皆得法忍，
fó shuō shì shí， sān qiān pú sà jiē dé fǎ rěn，
When the Buddha preached this, three thousand bodhisattvas attained forbearance of dharmas,
阿耨達龍王歡喜踊躍，
ā nòu dá lóng wáng huān xǐ yǒng yuè，
and the Dragon King Anavatapta leapt with joy;
以白珠瓔珞價當是世，而覆佛上。
yǐ bái zhū yīng luò jià dāng shì shì， ér fù fó shàng。
he took a garland of white pearls worth all the world and spread it over the Buddha.
女寶錦受決品第十四
nǚ bǎo jǐn shòu jué pǐn dì shí sì
Chapter Fourteen: The Prediction for the Girl Jewel Brocade
爾時海龍王有女，號名寶錦離垢錦，
ěr shí hǎi lóng wáng yǒu nǚ， hào míng bǎo jǐn lí gòu jǐn，
At that time the Sea Dragon King had a daughter named Jewel Brocade, Undefiled Brocade,
端正姝好，容顏英艶，
duān zhèng shū hǎo， róng yán yīng yàn，
upright and fair, her countenance splendid and lovely.
與萬龍夫人俱，各以右手執珠瓔珞，
yǔ wàn lóng fū rén jù， gè yǐ yòu shǒu zhí zhū yīng luò，
Together with ten thousand dragon women, each holding a pearl garland in her right hand,
一心視佛，目未曾眴，禮佛而立。
yī xīn shì fó， mù wèi céng shùn， lǐ fó ér lì。
she gazed at the Buddha single-mindedly, her eyes never blinking, and stood after bowing to him.
時寶錦女及萬夫人以珠瓔珞奉上世尊，
shí bǎo jǐn nǚ jí wàn fū rén yǐ zhū yīng luò fèng shàng shì zūn，
Then the girl Jewel Brocade and the ten thousand women offered their pearl garlands to the World-Honored One
同音歎曰：
tóng yīn tàn yuē：
and exclaimed with one voice:
「今日吾等一類平心皆發無上正真道意！
「jīn rì wú děng yī lèi píng xīn jiē fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì！
“Today we of one kind, with even minds, all arouse the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment!
吾等來世得為如來、至真、等正覺，
wú děng lái shì dé wéi rú lái、 zhì zhēn、 děng zhèng jué，
In an age to come may we become Tathagatas, Truly Worthy, Perfectly Enlightened,
當說經法，將護眾僧，如今如來！」
dāng shuō jīng fǎ， jiāng hù zhòng sēng， rú jīn rú lái！」
preaching the Dharma and guarding the Sangha, as the Tathagata does now!”
於時賢者大迦葉謂女及諸夫人：
yú shí xián zhě dà jiā shè wèi nǚ jí zhū fū rén：
Then the venerable Mahakasyapa said to the girl and the women:
「無上正覺甚難可獲，
「wú shàng zhèng jué shèn nán kě huò，
“Unsurpassed right awakening is very hard to obtain,
不可以女身得成佛道。」
bù kě yǐ nǚ shēn dé chéng fó dào。」
and one cannot accomplish the buddha path in a woman's body.”
寶錦女謂大迦葉：「心志本淨，
bǎo jǐn nǚ wèi dà jiā shè： 「xīn zhì běn jìng，
The girl Jewel Brocade said to Mahakasyapa: “Mind and resolve are originally pure;
行菩薩者得佛不難！
xíng pú sà zhě dé fó bù nán！
for one who practices as a bodhisattva, becoming a buddha is not hard.
彼發道心，成佛如觀手掌。
bǐ fā dào xīn， chéng fó rú guān shǒu zhǎng。
Once he rouses the mind of the Way, becoming a buddha is like looking at his own palm.
適以能發諸通慧心，則便攝取一切佛法。」
shì yǐ néng fā zhū tōng huì xīn， zé biàn shè qǔ yī qiè fó fǎ。」
As soon as he can rouse the mind of penetrating wisdom, he gathers in the whole Dharma of the buddhas.”
女謂迦葉：「又如所云：
nǚ wèi jiā shè： 「yòu rú suǒ yún：
The girl said to Kasyapa: “As to what you said —
『不可以女身得成佛道。』
『bù kě yǐ nǚ shēn dé chéng fó dào。』
‘one cannot accomplish the buddha path in a woman's body’ —
男子之身亦不可得！
nán zi zhī shēn yì bù kě dé！
a man's body cannot be obtained either!
所以者何？其道心者，無男無女。
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ qí dào xīn zhě， wú nán wú nǚ。
Why? In the mind of the Way there is neither male nor female.
如佛所言：『計於目者，無男無女。』
rú fó suǒ yán： 『jì yú mù zhě， wú nán wú nǚ。』
As the Buddha has said: ‘Reckoned in the eye there is neither male nor female.’
耳鼻口身心亦復如是，無男無女。
ěr bí kǒu shēn xīn yì fù rú shì， wú nán wú nǚ。
So too with ear, nose, mouth, body and mind: neither male nor female.
所以者何？唯仁者眼空故。
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ wéi rén zhě yǎn kōng gù。
Why? Because, worthy one, the eye is empty.
計於空者，無男無女，
jì yú kōng zhě， wú nán wú nǚ，
Reckoned in emptiness there is neither male nor female;
耳、鼻、口、身、心俱空，
ěr、 bí、 kǒu、 shēn、 xīn jù kōng，
ear, nose, mouth, body and mind are all empty,
如是虛空及寂，無男無女。
rú shì xū kōng jí jì， wú nán wú nǚ。
and thus in space and stillness there is neither male nor female.
若能解了分別眼本，則名曰道。
ruò néng jiě le fēn bié yǎn běn， zé míng yuē dào。
If one can understand and discern the origin of the eye, that is called the Way.
耳、鼻、口、身、心亦復如是，
ěr、 bí、 kǒu、 shēn、 xīn yì fù rú shì，
So too with ear, nose, mouth, body and mind.
計於道者，無男無女法。
jì yú dào zhě， wú nán wú nǚ fǎ。
Reckoned in the Way, there is no dharma of male or female.
是故，迦葉！又如諸法皆在自然，
shì gù， jiā shè！ yòu rú zhū fǎ jiē zài zì rán，
Therefore, Kasyapa, as all dharmas are so of themselves,
道亦自然，吾亦自然。」
dào yì zì rán， wú yì zì rán。」
the Way is so of itself, and I too am so of myself.”
迦葉問女：「汝是道耶？」
jiā shè wèn nǚ： 「rǔ shì dào yé？」
Kasyapa asked the girl: “Are you the Way?”
其女答曰：「尊者耆年，謂我是非道乎？」
qí nǚ dá yuē： 「zūn zhě qí nián， wèi wǒ shì fēi dào hū？」
She answered: “Venerable elder, do you say that I am not the Way?”
迦葉答曰：「吾非佛道，是聲聞耳！」
jiā shè dá yuē： 「wú fēi fó dào， shì shēng wén ěr！」
Kasyapa answered: “I am not the buddha path; I am only a sravaka.”
女又問曰：「誰開化仁？」
nǚ yòu wèn yuē： 「shuí kāi huà rén？」
She asked again: “Who converted you, worthy one?”
答曰：「如來。」
dá yuē： 「rú lái。」
He answered: “The Tathagata.”
女曰：「假令如來不成正覺，
nǚ yuē： 「jiǎ lìng rú lái bù chéng zhèng jué，
She said: “If the Tathagata had not accomplished right awakening,
寧能開化於耆年不？」
níng néng kāi huà yú qí nián bù？」
could he have converted you, venerable elder?”
答曰：「不也。」
dá yuē： 「bù yě。」
He answered: “He could not.”
「是故仁者當知，在彼則為以道，無不覺道。」
「shì gù rén zhě dāng zhī， zài bǐ zé wéi yǐ dào， wú bù jué dào。」
“Therefore, worthy one, know this: in him it is by the Way, and there is nothing that does not awaken to the Way.”
迦葉問女：「逆為道乎？」
jiā shè wèn nǚ： 「nì wéi dào hū？」
Kasyapa asked the girl: “Is the contrary the Way?”
答曰：「唯然，迦葉！逆則道也。
dá yuē： 「wéi rán， jiā shè！ nì zé dào yě。
She answered: “So it is, Kasyapa — the contrary is indeed the Way.
所以者何？以別本淨可覺了，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ yǐ bié běn jìng kě jué le，
Why? Because by discerning original purity it can be understood,
道者則無有逆解，逆本淨則名曰道。
dào zhě zé wú yǒu nì jiě， nì běn jìng zé míng yuē dào。
and in the Way there is no contrary understanding; the contrary, being originally pure, is called the Way.
空者本無分別，諸逆則名曰道。
kōng zhě běn wú fēn bié， zhū nì zé míng yuē dào。
In emptiness there is originally no discrimination, so all that is contrary is called the Way.
假使諸法有合有散，則非道也。
jiǎ shǐ zhū fǎ yǒu hé yǒu sàn， zé fēi dào yě。
If dharmas had joining and scattering, that would not be the Way.
等一切法、順如應者，乃為道耳。」
děng yī qiè fǎ、 shùn rú yìng zhě， nǎi wéi dào ěr。」
To hold all dharmas equal and to accord as is fitting — that alone is the Way.”
迦葉問女：「誰以如此辯才相施？」
jiā shè wèn nǚ： 「shuí yǐ rú cǐ biàn cái xiāng shī？」
Kasyapa asked: “Who bestowed such eloquence upon you?”
女答曰：「尊者迦葉施我辯才。
nǚ dá yuē： 「zūn zhě jiā shè shī wǒ biàn cái。
She answered: “Venerable Kasyapa, you bestowed my eloquence.
設使仁者不問於我，何因發辯？
shè shǐ rén zhě bù wèn yú wǒ， hé yīn fā biàn？
Had you not questioned me, what would have called it forth?
譬如，迦葉！無有呼者，何緣響應？
pì rú， jiā shè！ wú yǒu hū zhě， hé yuán xiǎng yìng？
It is as if, Kasyapa, there were no one calling — what then would the echo answer?
假使無問菩薩義者，無因發辯。」
jiǎ shǐ wú wèn pú sà yì zhě， wú yīn fā biàn。」
If none asked about the bodhisattva's meaning, there would be no cause for eloquence.”
迦葉問女：「汝為供養幾何佛乎？」
jiā shè wèn nǚ： 「rǔ wéi gòng yǎng jǐ hé fó hū？」
Kasyapa asked: “How many buddhas have you made offerings to?”
女答曰：「如仁者所斷塵勞。」
nǚ dá yuē： 「rú rén zhě suǒ duàn chén láo。」
She answered: “As many as the defilements you have severed.”
迦葉答女曰：「吾不斷塵勞。」
jiā shè dá nǚ yuē： 「wú bù duàn chén láo。」
Kasyapa said: “I have not severed defilements.”
女又問：「仁者！俗有塵勞穢耶？」
nǚ yòu wèn： 「rén zhě！ sú yǒu chén láo huì yé？」
She asked: “Worthy one, do you then have the filth of worldly defilement?”
迦葉答曰：「吾無塵勞，亦不斷矣。」
jiā shè dá yuē： 「wú wú chén láo， yì bù duàn yǐ。」
Kasyapa answered: “I have no defilement, and I have not severed any.”
女又問曰：「安置諸塵勞？」
nǚ yòu wèn yuē： 「ān zhì zhū chén láo？」
She asked: “Then where have the defilements been put?”
答曰：「不起不滅，亦無所置。
dá yuē： 「bù qǐ bù miè， yì wú suǒ zhì。
He answered: “They neither arise nor cease, and are put nowhere.
所解如此，如本無也。」
suǒ jiě rú cǐ， rú běn wú yě。」
So I understand it — as original nonbeing.”
又問：「本無寧可知耶？」
yòu wèn： 「běn wú níng kě zhī yé？」
She asked: “Can original nonbeing be known?”
答曰：「不也！」
dá yuē： 「bù yě！」
He answered: “It cannot.”
又問：「何故仁言：
yòu wèn： 「hé gù rén yán：
She asked: “Then why did you say,
『其慧如道，如所知了。』？」
『qí huì rú dào， rú suǒ zhī le。』？」
‘That wisdom is like the Way, understood as it is known’?”
「明無為，知如此，如解本無，
「míng wú wéi， zhī rú cǐ， rú jiě běn wú，
“It is clear in the unconditioned; knowing it thus, understanding it as original nonbeing —
是故名曰慧與凡夫等。」
shì gù míng yuē huì yǔ fán fū děng。」
therefore it is said that wisdom is equal to the ordinary person.”
又問女：「汝所辯者斷一切言？」
yòu wèn nǚ： 「rǔ suǒ biàn zhě duàn yī qiè yán？」
He asked the girl: “Does your eloquence cut off all speech?”
答曰：「吾無所斷，亦無有言。
dá yuē： 「wú wú suǒ duàn， yì wú yǒu yán。
She answered: “I cut off nothing, and there is no speech.
所以者何？法界無所斷，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ fǎ jiè wú suǒ duàn，
Why? In the dharma-realm nothing is cut off,
一切所說皆應法界。」
yī qiè suǒ shuō jiē yīng fǎ jiè。」
and all that is said answers to the dharma-realm.”
迦葉又問女：「汝等我於凡夫法，寧不有疑乎？」
jiā shè yòu wèn nǚ： 「rǔ děng wǒ yú fán fū fǎ， níng bù yǒu yí hū？」
Kasyapa asked again: “Do you not doubt when you hold me equal to the dharma of an ordinary person?”
女答曰：「假使立仁凡夫慧法，而各異者，
nǚ dá yuē： 「jiǎ shǐ lì rén fán fū huì fǎ， ér gè yì zhě，
She answered: “If your wisdom-dharma and the ordinary person's were set apart as different,
吾當有疑。
wú dāng yǒu yí。
then I should doubt.
吾謂仁者凡夫無異，以故無疑。
wú wèi rén zhě fán fū wú yì， yǐ gù wú yí。
I hold that you and the ordinary person are not different, and therefore I do not doubt.
諸法皆等，無若干故，是謂平等，
zhū fǎ jiē děng， wú ruò gān gù， shì wèi píng děng，
All dharmas are equal, without multiplicity — this is called equality,
如虛空是謂平等。」
rú xū kōng shì wèi píng děng。」
and like empty space it is called equality.”
又問女：「汝於凡夫等聖賢耶？」
yòu wèn nǚ： 「rǔ yú fán fū děng shèng xián yé？」
He asked: “Do you hold ordinary people equal to the holy sages?”
答曰：「吾不凡夫，亦非聖賢。
dá yuē： 「wú bù fán fū， yì fēi shèng xián。
She answered: “I am not an ordinary person, nor am I a holy sage.
所以者何？假使吾等身與凡夫等，不行菩薩；
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ jiǎ shǐ wú děng shēn yǔ fán fū děng， bù xíng pú sà；
Why? If I held my own body equal to the ordinary person's, I would not practice as a bodhisattva;
設使賢聖等，則斷佛法。」
shè shǐ xián shèng děng， zé duàn fó fǎ。」
and if I held it equal to the sages', I would cut off the Buddha's Dharma.”
又問女：「設汝不與凡夫等，
yòu wèn nǚ： 「shè rǔ bù yǔ fán fū děng，
He asked: “If you are equal neither to the ordinary person
亦不與聖賢等，寧與佛等乎？」
yì bù yǔ shèng xián děng， níng yǔ fó děng hū？」
nor to the holy sages, are you then equal to the Buddha?”
女答曰：「不也！所以者何？吾身本於佛法等。」
nǚ dá yuē： 「bù yě！ suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ wú shēn běn yú fó fǎ děng。」
She answered: “No. Why? Because my body is originally equal to the Buddha's Dharma.”
又問女曰：「假使汝於佛法等者，寧逮佛法乎？」
yòu wèn nǚ yuē： 「jiǎ shǐ rǔ yú fó fǎ děng zhě， níng dǎi fó fǎ hū？」
He asked: “If you are equal to the Buddha's Dharma, have you then attained it?”
女答曰：「仁者耆年，
nǚ dá yuē： 「rén zhě qí nián，
She answered: “Venerable elder,
寧信佛法有去來、今現在緣耶？
níng xìn fó fǎ yǒu qù lái、 jīn xiàn zài yuán yé？
do you believe that the Buddha's Dharma has conditions past, future or present?
有方面乎？有所處青、黃、赤、白、黑不？」
yǒu fāng miàn hū？ yǒu suǒ chù qīng、 huáng、 chì、 bái、 hēi bù？」
Does it have a direction? Is it anywhere blue, yellow, red, white or black?”
答女曰：「諸佛之法無有形貌。」
dá nǚ yuē： 「zhū fó zhī fǎ wú yǒu xíng mào。」
He answered: “The Dharma of the buddhas has no shape or appearance.”
女答曰：「假令諸佛法無有形貌，
nǚ dá yuē： 「jiǎ lìng zhū fó fǎ wú yǒu xíng mào，
She answered: “If the Dharma of the buddhas has no shape or appearance,
云何從我求乎？」
yún hé cóng wǒ qiú hū？」
how can you seek it from me?”
迦葉問女：「佛法當於何求？」
jiā shè wèn nǚ： 「fó fǎ dāng yú hé qiú？」
Kasyapa asked: “Where then should the Buddha's Dharma be sought?”
答曰：「當於六十二見中求。」
dá yuē： 「dāng yú liù shí èr jiàn zhōng qiú。」
She answered: “It should be sought among the sixty-two views.”
又問：「六十二見當於何求？」
yòu wèn： 「liù shí èr jiàn dāng yú hé qiú？」
He asked: “And where should the sixty-two views be sought?”
答曰：「當於如來解脫中求。」
dá yuē： 「dāng yú rú lái jiě tuō zhōng qiú。」
She answered: “They should be sought in the Tathagata's liberation.”
又問：「如來解脫者當於何求？」
yòu wèn： 「rú lái jiě tuō zhě dāng yú hé qiú？」
He asked: “And where should the Tathagata's liberation be sought?”
答曰：「當於五逆中求。」
dá yuē： 「dāng yú wǔ nì zhōng qiú。」
She answered: “It should be sought among the five heinous acts.”
又問：「五逆當於何求？」
yòu wèn： 「wǔ nì dāng yú hé qiú？」
He asked: “And where should the five heinous acts be sought?”
答曰：「當於度知見求。」
dá yuē： 「dāng yú dù zhī jiàn qiú。」
She answered: “They should be sought in the knowledge and vision of deliverance.”
又問：「此言何謂？」
yòu wèn： 「cǐ yán hé wèi？」
He asked: “What do these words mean?”
女答曰：「無縛無脫、無取無捨，此為本淨，
nǚ dá yuē： 「wú fù wú tuō、 wú qǔ wú shě， cǐ wéi běn jìng，
She answered: “No bondage and no release, no grasping and no relinquishing — this is original purity;
是為諸法之深教言，非若干言。」
shì wéi zhū fǎ zhī shēn jiào yán， fēi ruò gān yán。」
this is the profound teaching-word of all dharmas, and not a manifold word.”
又問女：「是之言教，不違如來言乎？」
yòu wèn nǚ： 「shì zhī yán jiào， bù wéi rú lái yán hū？」
He asked: “Does this teaching not contradict the Tathagata's word?”
女答曰：「是真諦言，不為違失如來之教。
nǚ dá yuē： 「shì zhēn dì yán， bù wéi wéi shī rú lái zhī jiào。
She answered: “This is a word of true truth and does not transgress or lose the Tathagata's teaching.
所以者何？如如來之道而無所得，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ rú rú lái zhī dào ér wú suǒ de，
Why? The Tathagata's Way attains nothing,
亦不可持，亦無言說。
yì bù kě chí， yì wú yán shuō。
cannot be held, and has no speech.
一切所言皆音聲耳，
yī qiè suǒ yán jiē yīn shēng ěr，
All that is spoken is mere sound;
曉了道本亦無音聲。
xiǎo liǎo dào běn yì wú yīn shēng。
and understanding the root of the Way, there is no sound at all.
唯仁者！解道寂然無跡，
wéi rén zhě！ jiě dào jì rán wú jī，
Worthy one, understand that the Way is still and traceless,
以名跡自愛跡。」
yǐ míng jī zì ài jī。」
and that by naming a trace one comes to cherish a trace.”
迦葉又問：「假使道無跡，如是比相，
jiā shè yòu wèn： 「jiǎ shǐ dào wú jī， rú shì bǐ xiāng，
Kasyapa asked again: “If the Way is traceless, and of such a character,
云何成最正覺？」
yún hé chéng zuì zhèng jué？」
how is supreme awakening accomplished?”
答曰：「亦不從身、亦不從意得最正覺。
dá yuē： 「yì bù cóng shēn、 yì bù cóng yì dé zuì zhèng jué。
She answered: “Supreme awakening is gained neither from the body nor from the mind.
所以者何？身心自然乃成道耳！
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ shēn xīn zì rán nǎi chéng dào ěr！
Why? Body and mind being so of themselves, the Way is accomplished.
其自然者都無所覺，
qí zì rán zhě dōu wú suǒ jué，
That which is so of itself awakens to nothing at all;
吾則是道，不以為道成最正覺。」
wú zé shì dào， bù yǐ wéi dào chéng zuì zhèng jué。」
I am the Way, and not by taking it as the Way is supreme awakening accomplished.”
迦葉問女：「汝設是道，何不轉法輪？」
jiā shè wèn nǚ： 「rǔ shè shì dào， hé bù zhuǎn fǎ lún？」
Kasyapa asked: “If you are the Way, why do you not turn the wheel of Dharma?”
女答曰：「我轉法輪耳！」
nǚ dá yuē： 「wǒ zhuǎn fǎ lún ěr！」
She answered: “I do turn the wheel of Dharma.”
迦葉問曰：「所轉法輪為何等類？」
jiā shè wèn yuē： 「suǒ zhuǎn fǎ lún wéi hé děng lèi？」
Kasyapa asked: “And of what kind is the wheel you turn?”
女答曰：「無動之輪，遠離一切諸所猗住。
nǚ dá yuē： 「wú dòng zhī lún， yuǎn lí yī qiè zhū suǒ yī zhù。
She answered: “An unmoving wheel, far from every leaning and abiding.
其法輪者，謂法界住故。
qí fǎ lún zhě， wèi fǎ jiè zhù gù。
It is a Dharma wheel, because it abides in the dharma-realm.
本無輪者，順本無故。
běn wú lún zhě， shùn běn wú gù。
It is a wheel of original nonbeing, because it accords with original nonbeing.
無斷輪者，如本淨住故。
wú duàn lún zhě， rú běn jìng zhù gù。
It is an unbroken wheel, because it abides as originally pure.
無著輪者，覺了一切諸法，無所著故。
wú zhuó lún zhě， jué le yī qiè zhū fǎ， wú suǒ zhuó gù。
It is an unattached wheel, because awakening to all dharmas it clings to nothing.
無二輪者，等於一切法故。
wú èr lún zhě， děng yú yī qiè fǎ gù。
It is a non-dual wheel, because it is equal in all dharmas.
無若干輪，忍一行故。
wú ruò gān lún， rěn yī xíng gù。
It is a wheel without multiplicity, because it bears a single practice.
無言法輪者，化諸音聲，皆無所想，入一味故。
wú yán fǎ lún zhě， huà zhū yīn shēng， jiē wú suǒ xiǎng， rù yī wèi gù。
It is a wordless Dharma wheel, because it transforms all sounds, conceives nothing, and enters a single savor.
清淨輪者，一切無塵故。
qīng jìng lún zhě， yī qiè wú chén gù。
It is a pure wheel, because in all there is no dust.
斷諸不調輪者，不得有常、無常故。
duàn zhū bù tiáo lún zhě， bù dé yǒu cháng、 wú cháng gù。
It is a wheel severing all that is untamed, because it apprehends neither permanence nor impermanence.
無亂輪者，善觀報應故。
wú luàn lún zhě， shàn guān bào yìng gù。
It is an undisturbed wheel, because it contemplates recompense well.
至誠輪者，無起無滅故。
zhì chéng lún zhě， wú qǐ wú miè gù。
It is a wheel of utter sincerity, because there is no arising and no ceasing.
空無輪者，無相、無願故。
kōng wú lún zhě， wú xiàng、 wú yuàn gù。
It is a wheel of emptiness, because it is signless and wishless.
唯迦葉！輪已如是，何所轉者？」
wéi jiā shè！ lún yǐ rú shì， hé suǒ zhuǎn zhě？」
Kasyapa, the wheel being thus, what is there to turn?”
大迦葉曰：「如女之辯才，
dà jiā shè yuē： 「rú nǚ zhī biàn cái，
Mahakasyapa said: “With eloquence such as yours,
不久當成無上正真道最正覺！」
bù jiǔ dāng chéng wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào zuì zhèng jué！」
before long you will accomplish unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment, the supreme awakening!”
女答曰：「假令迦葉成最正覺時，
nǚ dá yuē： 「jiǎ lìng jiā shè chéng zuì zhèng jué shí，
She answered: “When Kasyapa accomplishes supreme awakening,
吾亦當成最正覺！」
wú yì dāng chéng zuì zhèng jué！」
then I too shall accomplish it.”
迦葉答曰：「吾終不得成最正覺！」
jiā shè dá yuē： 「wú zhōng bù dé chéng zuì zhèng jué！」
Kasyapa answered: “I shall never accomplish supreme awakening.”
女答曰：「如是了法身者，道住無所住，
nǚ dá yuē： 「rú shì le fǎ shēn zhě， dào zhù wú suǒ zhù，
She answered: “Just so — for one who understands the Dharma body, the Way abides in no abiding,
無能得致，成最正覺。」
wú néng dé zhì， chéng zuì zhèng jué。」
and none can reach it, and so supreme awakening is accomplished.”
女說是語時，五百菩薩逮得法忍。
nǚ shuō shì yǔ shí， wǔ bǎi pú sà dǎi dé fǎ rěn。
When she said this, five hundred bodhisattvas attained forbearance of dharmas.
佛時讚曰：「善哉，善哉！快說此法！」
fó shí zàn yuē： 「shàn zāi， shàn zāi！ kuài shuō cǐ fǎ！」
The Buddha then praised her: “Excellent, excellent! Well have you spoken this Dharma!”
爾時眾會中天、龍、鬼神、
ěr shí zhòng huì zhōng tiān、 lóng、 guǐ shén、
Then in the assembly the devas, dragons, ghosts and spirits,
無善神、香音神心自念言：
wú shàn shén、 xiāng yīn shén xīn zì niàn yán：
asuras and gandharvas thought to themselves:
「是寶錦女，何時當成無上正真道最正覺？」
「shì bǎo jǐn nǚ， hé shí dāng chéng wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào zuì zhèng jué？」
“When will this girl Jewel Brocade accomplish unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment?”
佛知諸天、龍、神、香音神心之所念，告諸比丘：
fó zhī zhū tiān、 lóng、 shén、 xiāng yīn shén xīn zhī suǒ niàn， gào zhū bǐ qiū：
The Buddha, knowing what the devas, dragons, spirits and gandharvas held in mind, told the bhiksus:
「此寶錦女，三百不可計劫後，當得作佛，
「cǐ bǎo jǐn nǚ， sān bǎi bù kě jì jié hòu， dāng dé zuò fó，
“This girl Jewel Brocade, after three hundred incalculable kalpas, will become a buddha
號曰普世如來、至真、等正覺，
hào yuē pǔ shì rú lái、 zhì zhēn、 děng zhèng jué，
named the Tathagata Universal World, Truly Worthy, Perfectly Enlightened;
世界曰光明，劫曰清淨。
shì jiè yuē guāng míng， jié yuē qīng jìng。
her world will be named Radiance and the kalpa Purity.
其光明世界，如來光常當大明。
qí guāng míng shì jiè， rú lái guāng cháng dāng dà míng。
In that world Radiance the Tathagata's light will always shine greatly.
菩薩九十二億，佛壽十小劫。」
pú sà jiǔ shí èr yì， fó shòu shí xiǎo jié。」
There will be nine billion two hundred million bodhisattvas, and that buddha's lifespan will be ten small kalpas.”
於是萬龍后白佛言：
yú shì wàn lóng hòu bái fó yán：
Thereupon the ten thousand dragon women said to the Buddha:
「普世如來得為佛時，吾等願生彼國！」
「pǔ shì rú lái dé wèi fó shí， wú děng yuàn shēng bǐ guó！」
“When the Tathagata Universal World becomes a buddha, may we be born in that land!”
佛即記之當生其國。
fó jí jì zhī dāng shēng qí guó。
And the Buddha at once foretold that they would be born there.
天帝釋品第十五
tiān dì shì pǐn dì shí wǔ
Chapter Fifteen: Sakra, Lord of the Devas
於是天帝釋，白世尊曰：
yú shì tiān dì shì， bái shì zūn yuē：
Thereupon Sakra, Lord of the Devas, said to the World-Honored One:
「此忉利天常懷恐懼，難無善神。
「cǐ dāo lì tiān cháng huái kǒng jù， nán wú shàn shén。
“These devas of Trayastrimsa live in constant dread, harassed by the asuras.
天與無善神共戰鬪時，展轉共諍，
tiān yǔ wú shàn shén gòng zhàn dòu shí， zhǎn zhuǎn gòng zhèng，
When devas and asuras do battle they quarrel back and forth,
懷其怨結，各有瞋毒。
huái qí yuàn jié， gè yǒu chēn dú。
harboring bonds of enmity, each with the poison of anger.
唯願世尊慈愍眾生，
wéi yuàn shì zūn cí mǐn zhòng shēng，
We pray the World-Honored One, in kindness and pity for beings,
諸無善神皆悉來會，此諸忉利天悉令共和！」
zhū wú shàn shén jiē xī lái huì， cǐ zhū dāo lì tiān xī lìng gòng hé！」
that since all the asuras have come to this assembly, the devas of Trayastrimsa may be reconciled with them!”
佛告燕居無善神、誑詐超度離垢錦無善神言：
fó gào yān jū wú shàn shén、 kuáng zhà chāo dù lí gòu jǐn wú shàn shén yán：
The Buddha said to the asura Yanju and to the asuras Deceit, Surpassing Deliverance and Undefiled Brocade:
「諸仁者！其仁慈心，諸佛所歎。
「zhū rén zhě！ qí rén cí xīn， zhū fó suǒ tàn。
“Worthy ones! A kindly mind is praised by all the buddhas.
人能行慈，現世、後世具足利義。
rén néng xíng cí， xiàn shì、 hòu shì jù zú lì yì。
One who can practice loving-kindness is fully benefited in this world and the next.
其命甚短，當就後世。
qí mìng shèn duǎn， dāng jiù hòu shì。
Life is very short, and one must go on to the next world.
合會有離，國土、豪貴皆歸無常。
hé huì yǒu lí， guó tǔ、 háo guì jiē guī wú cháng。
What is joined must part; realms and eminence all come to impermanence.
汝等之身，不免此患，
rǔ děng zhī shēn， bù miǎn cǐ huàn，
Your own bodies do not escape this affliction.
當觀後世，和順同心，無得懷瞋，
dāng guān hòu shì， hé shùn tóng xīn， wú dé huái chēn，
Consider the world to come: be harmonious and of one mind, and harbor no anger,
將護罪福因緣之對。」
jiāng hù zuì fú yīn yuán zhī duì。」
guarding well the requital that answers to the causes of sin and merit.”
於是世尊為說辛酸悲哀之言，
yú shì shì zūn wèi shuō xīn suān bēi āi zhī yán，
Then the World-Honored One spoke words bitter and sorrowful to them,
使無善神及忉利天悉共和解。
shǐ wú shàn shén jí dāo lì tiān xī gòng hé jiě。
and the asuras and the devas of Trayastrimsa were all reconciled.
各自說言：「吾從今始當為親友順于等行，
gè zì shuō yán： 「wú cóng jīn shǐ dāng wéi qīn yǒu shùn yú děng xíng，
Each of them said: “From this day on we shall be friends and follow a conduct of equality,
各各當懷悲心愍哀，無瞋恚意。」
gè gè dāng huái bēi xīn mǐn āi， wú chēn huì yì。」
each holding a mind of compassion and pity, with no thought of anger.”
佛言：「善哉，善哉！諸仁者！
fó yán： 「shàn zāi， shàn zāi！ zhū rén zhě！
The Buddha said: “Excellent, excellent, worthy ones!
是則第一供養如來，用行慈故！」
shì zé dì yī gòng yǎng rú lái， yòng xíng cí gù！」
This is the foremost offering to the Tathagata, because it is the practice of loving-kindness!”
說是語時，所教造福共和不諍，謂慈心三昧。
shuō shì yǔ shí， suǒ jiào zào fú gòng hé bù zhēng， wèi cí xīn sān mèi。
When he said this, the merit made by that teaching, reconciled and without dispute, was called the samadhi of the kindly mind.
「有四事尊敬如來。何等四？
「yǒu sì shì zūn jìng rú lái。 hé děng sì？
“There are four things by which one honors the Tathagata. Which four?
不違犯戒；身、口、意慈；
bù wéi fàn jiè； shēn、 kǒu、 yì cí；
Not transgressing or breaking the precepts; kindness of body, speech and mind;
不斷三寶，志於佛道；
bù duàn sān bǎo， zhì yú fó dào；
not cutting off the Three Jewels, but aspiring to the buddha path;
如所聞法為人講說。
rú suǒ wén fǎ wéi rén jiǎng shuō。
and expounding to others the Dharma as one has heard it.
是為四事尊敬如來，為供養也。」
shì wéi sì shì zūn jìng rú lái， wéi gòng yǎng yě。」
These are the four by which one honors the Tathagata, and this is making offerings.”
於是會中二萬三千天無善神，
yú shì huì zhōng èr wàn sān qiān tiān wú shàn shén，
Thereupon twenty-three thousand devas and asuras in the assembly,
聞說此言皆發無上正真道意。
wén shuō cǐ yán jiē fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì。
hearing these words, all aroused the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment.
佛說海龍王經卷第三
fó shuō hǎi lóng wáng jīng juǎn dì sān
Here ends Volume Three of the Sutra Spoken by the Buddha on the Sea Dragon King.
金翅鳥品第十六
jīn chì niǎo pǐn dì shí liù
Chapter Sixteen: The Garudas
爾時有龍王，一名噏氣，二名大噏氣，
ěr shí yǒu lóng wáng， yī míng xī qì， èr míng dà xī qì，
At that time there were dragon kings — the first named Indrawn Breath, the second Great Indrawn Breath,
三名熊羆，四名無量色，而白世尊曰：
sān míng xióng pí， sì míng wú liàng sè， ér bái shì zūn yuē：
the third Bear, the fourth Immeasurable Color — who said to the World-Honored One:
「於此海中無數種龍，
「yú cǐ hǎi zhōng wú shù zhǒng lóng，
“In this ocean there are countless kinds of dragons,
若干種行、因緣之報來生於是，
ruò gān zhǒng xíng、 yīn yuán zhī bào lái shēng yú shì，
born here by the recompense of many kinds of conduct and conditions;
或有大種，或有小種，
huò yǒu dà zhǒng， huò yǒu xiǎo zhǒng，
some of great kind, some of small,
或有羸劣，獨見輕侮。
huò yǒu léi liè， dú jiàn qīng wǔ。
and some frail and weak, alone made light of and abused.
有四種金翅鳥，常食斯龍及龍妻子，
yǒu sì zhǒng jīn chì niǎo， cháng shí sī lóng jí lóng qī zǐ，
There are four kinds of garuda that constantly devour these dragons and their wives and children,
恐怖海中諸龍種類。
kǒng bù hǎi zhōng zhū lóng zhǒng lèi。
terrifying every kind of dragon in the sea.
願佛擁護，令海諸龍常得安隱，不懷恐怖。」
yuàn fó yōng hù， lìng hǎi zhū lóng cháng dé ān yǐn， bù huái kǒng bù。」
We pray the Buddha shelter us, so that the dragons of the sea may always be at peace and harbor no fear.”
於是世尊脫身皂衣，告海龍王：
yú shì shì zūn tuō shēn zào yī， gào hǎi lóng wáng：
Thereupon the World-Honored One took off the black robe from his body and told the Sea Dragon King:
「汝當取是如來皂衣，
「rǔ dāng qǔ shì rú lái zào yī，
“Take this black robe of the Tathagata
分與諸龍王皆令周遍。
fēn yǔ zhū lóng wáng jiē lìng zhōu biàn。
and divide it among the dragon kings so that all are covered.
所以者何？其在大海中有值一縷者，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ qí zài dà hǎi zhōng yǒu zhí yī lǚ zhě，
Why? Whoever in the great ocean obtains even a single thread of it,
金翅鳥王不能犯觸。
jīn chì niǎo wáng bù néng fàn chù。
the garuda kings cannot assail or touch.
所以者何？持禁戒者所願必得。」
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ chí jìn jiè zhě suǒ yuàn bì děi。」
Why? Because one who upholds the precepts surely obtains what he vows.”
爾時諸大龍王皆懷驚懅，各心念言：
ěr shí zhū dà lóng wáng jiē huái jīng jù， gè xīn niàn yán：
Then the great dragon kings were all alarmed, and each thought:
「是佛皂衣甚為少小，安得周遍大海諸龍？」
「shì fó zào yī shèn wéi shǎo xiǎo， ān dé zhōu biàn dà hǎi zhū lóng？」
“This black robe of the Buddha's is very small — how can it cover all the dragons of the great ocean?”
時佛即知諸龍王心所懷疑恐，告海龍王：
shí fó jí zhī zhū lóng wáng xīn suǒ huái yí kǒng， gào hǎi lóng wáng：
The Buddha, knowing at once the doubt and dread in the dragon kings' minds, told the Sea Dragon King:
「假使三千大千世界所有人民，
「jiǎ shǐ sān qiān dà qiān shì jiè suǒ yǒu rén mín，
“Even if all the people of the three-thousandfold great-thousand world
各各共分如來皂衣，終不減盡。
gè gè gòng fēn rú lái zào yī， zhōng bù jiǎn jǐn。
were each to share out the Tathagata's black robe, it would never be lessened or used up.
其欲取衣，譬如虛空，隨其所欲則自然生。
qí yù qǔ yī， pì rú xū kōng， suí qí suǒ yù zé zì rán shēng。
For those who wish to take the robe it is like empty space: as each desires, so it appears of itself.
佛所建立不可思議巍巍之德，其如斯矣！」
fó suǒ jiàn lì bù kě sī yì wēi wēi zhī dé， qí rú sī yǐ！」
Such is the inconceivable and lofty virtue the Buddha has established!”
時海龍王即取佛衣，
shí hǎi lóng wáng jí qǔ fó yī，
Then the Sea Dragon King took the Buddha's robe
而自分作無央數百千叚，
ér zì fēn zuò wú yāng shù bǎi qiān jiǎ，
and divided it into countless hundreds of thousands of pieces,
各各部分分與龍王，
gè gè bù fèn fēn yǔ lóng wáng，
distributing a portion to each dragon king;
龍王之宮隨其所之廣狹大小自然給與，
lóng wáng zhī gōng suí qí suǒ zhī guǎng xiá dà xiǎo zì rán gěi yǔ，
and to each dragon king's palace, according to its breadth or narrowness, its size, it was supplied of itself,
其衣如故，終不知盡。
qí yī rú gù， zhōng bù zhī jǐn。
while the robe remained as before and was never known to be exhausted.
於時海龍王告諸龍王：
yú shí hǎi lóng wáng gào zhū lóng wáng：
Then the Sea Dragon King told the dragon kings:
「當敬此衣如敬世尊、如敬塔寺。
「dāng jìng cǐ yī rú jìng shì zūn、 rú jìng tǎ sì。
“Revere this robe as you revere the World-Honored One, as you revere a stupa.
所以者何？今此衣者如來所服，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ jīn cǐ yī zhě rú lái suǒ fú，
Why? Because this robe is what the Tathagata wore;
以是之故如敬塔寺也。
yǐ shì zhī gù rú jìng tǎ sì yě。
and for this reason revere it as a stupa.
假使一切供養如來，有奉此衣等無差特。」
jiǎ shǐ yī qiè gòng yǎng rú lái， yǒu fèng cǐ yī děng wú chā tè。」
To make every offering to the Tathagata and to honor this robe are equal, without distinction.”
於是世尊告海龍王：「如是，如是！如仁所言！
yú shì shì zūn gào hǎi lóng wáng： 「rú shì， rú shì！ rú rén suǒ yán！
Then the World-Honored One told the Sea Dragon King: “Just so, just so, as you have said!
其奉此衣則供養如來。
qí fèng cǐ yī zé gòng yǎng rú lái。
To honor this robe is to make offering to the Tathagata.
且觀諸龍及龍妻息各各所分如來衣不？」
qiě guān zhū lóng jí lóng qī xī gè gè suǒ fēn rú lái yī bù？」
Now, do you see the dragons and their wives and offspring, each with a share of the Tathagata's robe?”
對曰：「唯然，已見！」
duì yuē： 「wéi rán， yǐ jiàn！」
He replied: “So be it, I have seen them.”
佛言：「我皆授決即脫龍身，
fó yán： 「wǒ jiē shòu jué jí tuō lóng shēn，
The Buddha said: “I give them all the prediction that they will be released from dragon bodies;
於是賢劫除志大乘，
yú shì xián jié chú zhì dà chéng，
and in this Worthy Kalpa, excepting those who aspire to the Great Vehicle,
其餘諸龍皆得無著、當般泥洹。
qí yú zhū lóng jiē dé wú zhuó、 dāng bān ní huán。
the rest of the dragons will all attain the state of the unattached and enter parinirvana.
如是，龍王！如來在世，
rú shì， lóng wáng！ rú lái zài shì，
Thus it is, Dragon King: while a Tathagata is in the world,
一切眾祐發一善心，緣當致佛，未曾有失。」
yī qiè zhòng yòu fā yī shàn xīn， yuán dāng zhì fó， wèi céng yǒu shī。」
if any among the blessed multitude rouses a single wholesome thought, by that condition he will reach buddhahood, and it is never lost.”
爾時海中諸龍及龍妻息欣然大悅，
ěr shí hǎi zhōng zhū lóng jí lóng qī xī xīn rán dà yuè，
Then the dragons of the sea with their wives and offspring were filled with delight,
自投佛前同音說言：
zì tóu fó qián tóng yīn shuō yán：
cast themselves down before the Buddha and said with one voice:
「如來所語終無有二，至誠不虛，
「rú lái suǒ yǔ zhōng wú yǒu èr， zhì chéng bù xū，
“What the Tathagata says is never twofold, utterly sincere and never false.
授我等決，至無為度。
shòu wǒ děng jué， zhì wú wéi dù。
He has given us the prediction, on to deliverance in the unconditioned.
吾等今日住於大海，
wú děng jīn rì zhù yú dà hǎi，
From this day, dwelling in the great ocean,
歸命佛法及諸聖眾，奉受禁戒，
guī mìng fó fǎ jí zhū shèng zhòng， fèng shòu jìn jiè，
we take refuge in the Buddha, the Dharma and the holy assembly, and receive the precepts,
恭順如來反復之義。
gōng shùn rú lái fǎn fù zhī yì。
reverently repaying the kindness we owe the Tathagata.
如來現在，數數往造，見佛稽首，聽采法義；
rú lái xiàn zài， shù shù wǎng zào， jiàn fó qǐ shǒu， tīng cǎi fǎ yì；
