While the Tathagata is present we shall come again and again, bow at his feet, and gather the meaning of the Dharma;
般泥洹後，供養舍利，
bān ní huán hòu， gòng yǎng shě lì，
and after his parinirvana we shall make offerings to the relics,
一切眾具而以奉事世尊舍利。」
yī qiè zhòng jù ér yǐ fèng shì shì zūn shě lì。」
serving the World-Honored One's relics with every kind of provision.”
於是四金翅鳥王聞佛所建立，
yú shì sì jīn chì niǎo wáng wén fó suǒ jiàn lì，
Then the four garuda kings, hearing what the Buddha had established,
惶懅速疾往詣佛所前稽首足：
huáng jù sù jí wǎng yì fó suǒ qián qǐ shǒu zú：
hurried in alarm to where the Buddha was, bowed at his feet and said:
「何故世尊奪吾等食？」
「hé gù shì zūn duó wú děng shí？」
“Why does the World-Honored One take away our food?”
佛言：「都有四食坐趣三處。何等四？
fó yán： 「dōu yǒu sì shí zuò qù sān chù。 hé děng sì？
The Buddha said: “There are four kinds of food that lead to the three destinies. Which four?
一曰網獵禽獸、殘害群畜、
yī yuē wǎng liè qín shòu、 cán hài qún chù、
First, to net and hunt birds and beasts, to injure the herds,
殺生抂命以為飲食，是趣惡處。
shā shēng kuáng mìng yǐ wéi yǐn shí， shì qù è chù。
to kill and wrongfully take life for food — this leads to an evil place.
二曰執帶兵杖、刀矛斫刺、
èr yuē zhí dài bīng zhàng、 dāo máo zhuó cì、
Second, to bear arms and staves, to hack and stab with blade and spear,
逼迫格射、劫奪他財以用飲食，是趣惡處。
bī pò gé shè、 jié duó tā cái yǐ yòng yǐn shí， shì qù è chù。
to press and shoot down, to rob others of their goods for food — this leads to an evil place.
三曰慳貪諛諂、憒亂犯禁、
sān yuē qiān tān yú chǎn、 kuì luàn fàn jìn、
Third, by stinginess, greed and flattery, by confusion and breaking restraints,
邪見巧欺而以得食，是趣惡處。
xié jiàn qiǎo qī ér yǐ dé shí， shì qù è chù。
by wrong views and clever deceit to obtain food — this leads to an evil place.
四曰非師稱師、非世尊稱世尊、
sì yuē fēi shī chēng shī、 fēi shì zūn chēng shì zūn、
Fourth, being no teacher to call oneself a teacher, being no World-Honored One to claim that title,
墮邪稱正、非寂志稱寂志、
duò xié chēng zhèng、 fēi jì zhì chēng jì zhì、
having fallen into error to call oneself right, being no sramana to claim to be one,
非清淨稱清淨、非梵行自稱梵行、
fēi qīng jìng chēng qīng jìng、 fēi fàn xíng zì chēng fàn xíng、
being impure to claim purity, not living the holy life to claim it,
自稱詐求而以得食。
zì chēng zhà qiú ér yǐ dé shí。
and by such false claims and deceitful seeking to obtain food.
是為四食，坐趣地獄、餓鬼、畜生三惡之處。
shì wéi sì shí， zuò qù dì yù、 è guǐ、 chù sheng sān è zhī chù。
These are the four foods, which lead to the three evil destinies of hell, hungry ghosts and animals.
吾所說法除此四食，
wú suǒ shuō fǎ chú cǐ sì shí，
The Dharma I preach removes these four foods,
不當以此養身、害眾生命。
bù dāng yǐ cǐ yǎng shēn、 hài zhòng shēng mìng。
and one should not nourish the body by them or harm the lives of beings.
所以者何？一切眾生各自愛命，無自憎者，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ yī qiè zhòng shēng gè zì ài mìng， wú zì zēng zhě，
Why? Every living being loves its own life, and none hates itself;
以是之故，欲自護身當護他人，安隱眾生。
yǐ shì zhī gù， yù zì hù shēn dāng hù tā rén， ān yǐn zhòng shēng。
therefore one who would guard himself should guard others and bring beings to peace.
明者如是，不以危逼人，
míng zhě rú shì， bù yǐ wēi bī rén，
The discerning are like this: they do not imperil or oppress others.
所不當作慎勿為也！」
suǒ bù dāng zuò shèn wù wéi yě！」
What ought not to be done, take care not to do!”
爾時四金翅鳥王，各與千眷屬俱而白佛言：
ěr shí sì jīn chì niǎo wáng， gè yǔ qiān juàn shǔ jù ér bái fó yán：
Then the four garuda kings, each with a thousand of his retinue, said to the Buddha:
「今日吾等自歸命佛及法眾僧，
「jīn rì wú děng zì guī mìng fó jí fǎ zhòng sēng，
“Today we take refuge in the Buddha, the Dharma and the Sangha,
自首悔過前所犯殃，奉持禁戒，
zì shǒu huǐ guò qián suǒ fàn yāng， fèng chí jìn jiè，
confess and repent the offenses we have committed, and uphold the precepts.
從今日始常以無畏施一切龍，
cóng jīn rì shǐ cháng yǐ wú wèi shī yī qiè lóng，
From this day we shall always bestow fearlessness on all dragons,
擁護正法，至佛法住，
yōng hù zhèng fǎ， zhì fó fǎ zhù，
shelter the true Dharma for as long as the Buddha's Dharma abides,
將順道法到于滅盡，不違佛教。」
jiāng shùn dào fǎ dào yú miè jǐn， bù wéi fó jiào。」
follow the dharma of the Way to its very end, and never transgress the Buddha's teaching.”
佛告四金翅鳥王：「汝等之身，
fó gào sì jīn chì niǎo wáng： 「rǔ děng zhī shēn，
The Buddha told the four garuda kings: “In your own persons,
金仁佛時為四比丘，
jīn rén fó shí wéi sì bǐ qiū，
in the time of the Buddha Kanakamuni, you were four bhiksus
名曰欣樂、大欣樂、上勝、上友。
míng yuē xīn lè、 dà xīn lè、 shàng shèng、 shàng yǒu。
named Gladness, Great Gladness, Supreme Victory and Supreme Friend.
是四比丘，違犯戒法，貪於供養，
shì sì bǐ qiū， wéi fàn jiè fǎ， tān yú gòng yǎng，
These four bhiksus transgressed the precepts, were greedy for offerings,
志迷醉惑，隨親友種逐於豪貴，
zhì mí zuì huò， suí qīn yǒu zhǒng zhú yú háo guì，
their resolve deluded and intoxicated; they followed their kin and chased after the eminent,
意亂吾我，墮於邪見，
yì luàn wú wǒ， duò yú xié jiàn，
their minds disordered by self, and fell into wrong views;
輕諸比丘逼迫惱之，
qīng zhū bǐ qiū bī pò nǎo zhī，
they slighted the other bhiksus and oppressed and troubled them,
不護身、口、意，作惡眾多，
bù hù shēn、 kǒu、 yì， zuò è zhòng duō，
did not guard body, speech and mind, and did much evil.
供養金仁佛亦不可計，
gòng yǎng jīn rén fó yì bù kě jì，
Yet their offerings to the Buddha Kanakamuni were beyond counting,
以是之故不墮地獄，墮此禽獸。
yǐ shì zhī gù bù duò dì yù， duò cǐ qín shòu。
and for this reason they did not fall into hell, but fell among these birds and beasts.
前後殺生不可稱計，
qián hòu shā shēng bù kě chēng jì，
The lives they have taken, before and since, are beyond all reckoning,
多所恐怖用不自護。」
duō suǒ kǒng bù yòng bù zì hù。」
and they have terrified many, because they did not guard themselves.”
世尊應時即如其像現其神足，
shì zūn yìng shí jí rú qí xiàng xiàn qí shén zú，
The World-Honored One at once, in that very form, manifested his spiritual power,
令四金翅鳥識念宿命，
lìng sì jīn chì niǎo shí niàn sù mìng，
causing the four garudas to recall their former lives
所可供養金仁佛及諸弟子。
suǒ kě gòng yǎng jīn rén fó jí zhū dì zǐ。
and the offerings they had made to the Buddha Kanakamuni and his disciples.
彼時所作罪福普悉念之，
bǐ shí suǒ zuò zuì fú pǔ xī niàn zhī，
All the sin and merit they had made at that time they recalled entirely,
目覩悉見前世所作，白世尊曰：
mù dǔ xī jiàn qián shì suǒ zuò， bái shì zūn yuē：
seeing with their own eyes all they had done in former lives, and they said to the World-Honored One:
「其心剛䩕，難可調伏，
「qí xīn gāng áng， nán kě diào fú，
“Our minds were hard and stubborn, difficult to tame;
坐心貪嫉，多所危害，違金仁尊教。
zuò xīn tān jí， duō suǒ wēi hài， wéi jīn rén zūn jiào。
because of greed and envy we did much harm and transgressed the honored Kanakamuni's teaching.
我等今始寧沒身命不敢犯惡！」
wǒ děng jīn shǐ níng mò shēn mìng bù gǎn fàn è！」
From this day we would sooner lose our lives than dare commit evil!”
佛為說經，授其決言：
fó wèi shuō jīng， shòu qí jué yán：
The Buddha preached the sutra for them and gave them the prediction:
「彌勒佛時，在第一會皆當得度！」
「mí lè fó shí， zài dì yī huì jiē dāng dé dù！」
“In the time of Maitreya Buddha, at his first assembly, you will all be delivered!”
舍利品第十七
shě lì pǐn dì shí qī
Chapter Seventeen: The Relics
爾時海龍王子及一切龍白佛言：
ěr shí hǎi lóng wáng zǐ jí yī qiè lóng bái fó yán：
At that time the sons of the Sea Dragon King and all the dragons said to the Buddha:
「未曾有。世尊！
「wèi céng yǒu。 shì zūn！
“This is unprecedented, World-Honored One!
如來所說普安一切，
rú lái suǒ shuō pǔ ān yī qiè，
What the Tathagata preaches brings peace to all;
授諸龍決開化眷屬皆發道意。
shòu zhū lóng jué kāi huà juàn shǔ jiē fā dào yì。
he has given the dragons the prediction and converted our retinues, so that all arouse the aspiration to the Way.
又以加恩乞施皂衣，使諸龍分。
yòu yǐ jiā ēn qǐ shī zào yī， shǐ zhū lóng fēn。
Moreover in kindness he granted the black robe for the dragons to share,
各以供養稽首奉事。
gè yǐ gòng yǎng qǐ shǒu fèng shì。
and each makes offering, bows the head and serves it.
緣是得護因發道意，
yuán shì dé hù yīn fā dào yì，
By this condition we gain protection and rouse the aspiration to the Way,
慈愍眾生遵四等心慈悲喜護，
cí mǐn zhòng shēng zūn sì děng xīn cí bēi xǐ hù，
pity living beings and follow the four impartial minds — kindness, compassion, joy and equanimity —
興隆四恩惠施仁愛，
xìng lóng sì ēn huì shī rén ài，
raise up the four kindnesses, bestow gifts and love,
利人等利一切救濟合聚。
lì rén děng lì yī qiè jiù jì hé jù。
benefit others equally and gather to rescue all.
由此行德沙不雨身離於眾患，
yóu cǐ xíng dé shā bù yǔ shēn lí yú zhòng huàn，
By this conduct of virtue, sand does not rain upon our bodies and we are free of many afflictions;
又寂意時不失天身，
yòu jì yì shí bù shī tiān shēn，
and when the mind is stilled we do not lose the celestial body,
變為蛇虺臨食意後不遭蝦蟇，
biàn wéi shé huī lín shí yì hòu bù zāo xiā má，
nor turn into snakes and vipers, nor meet with toads at the moment of feeding,
金翅鳥王不取食之。
jīn chì niǎo wáng bù qǔ shí zhī。
and the garuda kings do not seize and devour us.
佛化四鳥皆識宿命，
fó huà sì niǎo jiē shí sù mìng，
The Buddha has converted the four garudas so that all recall their former lives:
金仁佛時為四比丘，
jīn rén fó shí wéi sì bǐ qiū，
in the time of the Buddha Kanakamuni they were four bhiksus
坐行兇暴不順正法逼迫同學故，
zuò xíng xiōng bào bù shùn zhèng fǎ bī pò tóng xué gù，
who, because of violent conduct, disregard of the true Dharma and oppression of their fellow students,
墮金翅鳥自首悔過，
duò jīn chì niǎo zì shǒu huǐ guò，
fell among the garudas; and they have confessed and repented,
改心易行發大道意，
gǎi xīn yì xíng fā dà dào yì，
changed their minds and their conduct, and roused the aspiration to the great Way,
行四等心不害群黎，
xíng sì děng xīn bù hài qún lí，
practicing the four impartial minds and harming no living thing.
以得善護吾等永安不復見食，
yǐ dé shàn hù wú děng yǒng ān bù fù jiàn shí，
Thus well protected, we are forever at peace and are no longer devoured;
志不懷懼，長夜無難，皆蒙佛恩。
zhì bù huái jù， zhǎng yè wú nán， jiē méng fó ēn。
our minds hold no dread and the long night is free of hardship — all by the Buddha's grace.
今如來受龍王請，所演廣覆，
jīn rú lái shòu lóng wáng qǐng， suǒ yǎn guǎng fù，
Now the Tathagata has accepted the Dragon King's invitation, and what he expounds covers all,
譬如虛空無所不蓋。
pì rú xū kōng wú suǒ bù gài。
like empty space, which shelters everything.
于今世尊還閻浮利，海中諸龍無所依仰，
yú jīn shì zūn hái yán fú lì， hǎi zhōng zhū lóng wú suǒ yī yǎng，
But when the World-Honored One returns to Jambudvipa, the dragons of the sea will have nothing to look to.
唯加大哀，佛滅度時在此大海留全舍利，
wéi jiā dà āi， fó miè dù shí zài cǐ dà hǎi liú quán shě lì，
We pray for your great compassion: when the Buddha enters final quiescence, leave a whole relic here in this great ocean,
一切眾類皆得供養華香、伎樂、被服、幢幡，
yī qiè zhòng lèi jiē dé gòng yǎng huā xiāng、 jì yuè、 bèi fú、 chuáng fān，
so that all kinds of beings may make offerings of flowers and incense, music, robes, banners and pennants,
轉加功德，速脫龍身，
zhuǎn jiā gōng dé， sù tuō lóng shēn，
increasing their merit, swiftly freed from dragon bodies,
疾得無上正真之道。
jí dé wú shàng zhèng zhēn zhī dào。
and quickly attaining unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment.
續見救濟，惟佛垂恩，
xù jiàn jiù jì， wéi fó chuí ēn，
May we continue to be rescued; we pray the Buddha bestow his grace,
威德兼加，所願得果。」
wēi dé jiān jiā， suǒ yuàn dé guǒ。」
add his majestic virtue, and let our wish bear fruit.”
佛言：「善哉，從爾所志！」
fó yán： 「shàn zāi， cóng ěr suǒ zhì！」
The Buddha said: “Excellent! It shall be as you wish.”
時須菩提謂諸龍子：
shí xū pú tí wèi zhū lóng zi：
Then Subhuti said to the dragon princes:
「諸仁者等勿建此心，則為妨廢一切之德。
「zhū rén zhě děng wù jiàn cǐ xīn， zé wéi fáng fèi yī qiè zhī dé。
“Worthy ones, do not set your minds on this, for it would obstruct and waste the virtue of all.
所以者何？佛泥洹後舍利分布八方，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ fó ní huán hòu shě lì fēn bù bā fāng，
Why? After the Buddha's nirvana the relics are distributed to the eight quarters,
上下天、龍、鬼神、一切人民、
shàng xià tiān、 lóng、 guǐ shén、 yī qiè rén mín、
and above and below, devas, dragons, ghosts and spirits, all people,
蚑行喘息、人物之類，
qí xíng chuǎn xī、 rén wù zhī lèi，
creeping things and breathing things, every kind of being,
皆當供養華香伎樂稽首自歸，
jiē dāng gòng yǎng huā xiāng jì yuè qǐ shǒu zì guī，
make offerings of flowers, incense and music, bow their heads and take refuge.
變化現光見者歡喜，
biàn huà xiàn guāng jiàn zhě huān xǐ，
The relics transform and give forth light, and those who see it rejoice,
知佛威神巍巍無極。
zhī fó wēi shén wēi wēi wú jí。
knowing the Buddha's majestic power to be lofty and without limit.
緣是信之，皆發無上正真道意；
yuán shì xìn zhī， jiē fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì；
Believing because of this, they all arouse the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment;
或成緣覺，或得聲聞，
huò chéng yuán jué， huò dé shēng wén，
some become pratyekabuddhas, some sravakas,
或生天上，還得人身，
huò shēng tiān shàng， hái dé rén shēn，
some are born in the heavens and return to human birth,
與法相值，世世得度；
yǔ fǎ xiàng zhí， shì shì dé dù；
meet with the Dharma, and life after life are delivered.
如是之計普蒙獲濟。
rú shì zhī jì pǔ méng huò jì。
By such a reckoning all obtain rescue.
今者卿等各自求願，
jīn zhě qīng děng gè zì qiú yuàn，
Now you each seek your own wish:
使佛、世尊在於大海而取滅度，
shǐ fó、 shì zūn zài yú dà hǎi ér qǔ miè dù，
that the Buddha, the World-Honored One, should enter final quiescence in the great ocean,
供全舍利，獨欲奉侍。
gōng quán shě lì， dú yù fèng shì。
leaving a whole relic, which you alone wish to serve.
一切眾生何緣得度？
yī qiè zhòng shēng hé yuán dé dù？
By what condition then would all other beings be delivered?
永為窮厄無一救護！
yǒng wéi qióng è wú yī jiù hù！
They would be in distress forever with none to rescue them.
以故吾言勿發此心，
yǐ gù wú yán wù fā cǐ xīn，
Therefore I say: do not rouse this thought,
令佛世尊海中滅度，獨奉全舍利而供養乎！」
lìng fó shì zūn hǎi zhōng miè dù， dú fèng quán shě lì ér gòng yǎng hū！」
that the World-Honored One should pass into quiescence in the sea and you alone honor a whole relic with offerings!”
諸龍答曰：「唯須菩提勿宣斯言！
zhū lóng dá yuē： 「wéi xū pú tí wù xuān sī yán！
The dragons answered: “Subhuti, do not utter such words!
無以己身限礙之智，
wú yǐ jǐ shēn xiàn ài zhī zhì，
Do not take the limited and obstructed knowledge of your own person
以限如來無極之慧。
yǐ xiàn rú lái wú jí zhī huì。
and use it to limit the Tathagata's boundless wisdom.
如來功德聖道自在，無不變現，
rú lái gōng dé shèng dào zì zài， wú bù biàn xiàn，
The Tathagata's merit and holy Way are sovereign, and there is nothing he does not manifest;
無遠無近，無彼無此，
wú yuǎn wú jìn， wú bǐ wú cǐ，
for him there is no far and no near, no there and no here;
普遊十方其若虛空。
pǔ yóu shí fāng qí ruò xū kōng。
he roams the ten directions like empty space.
發意之頃，能令海中諸龍神宮，
fā yì zhī qǐng， néng lìng hǎi zhōng zhū lóng shén gōng，
In the instant of a thought he can cause the dragon and spirit palaces of the sea,
三千大千世界州城、郡國、縣邑、丘聚，
sān qiān dà qiān shì jiè zhōu chéng、 jùn guó、 xiàn yì、 qiū jù，
and the provinces, cities, commanderies, districts and hamlets of the three-thousandfold great-thousand world,
人中曠野天上世間，
rén zhōng kuàng yě tiān shàng shì jiān，
the wilds among men, the heavens and the world,
各各化現佛全舍利一切供養，
gè gè huà xiàn fó quán shě lì yī qiè gòng yǎng，
each to manifest a whole relic of the Buddha to receive every offering,
於佛身體不增不減；
yú fó shēn tǐ bù zēng bù jiǎn；
while the Buddha's body is neither increased nor diminished.
分身十方無數佛土，亦無所分，
fēn shēn shí fāng wú shù fó tǔ， yì wú suǒ fēn，
He divides his body into countless buddha-lands in the ten directions, yet nothing is divided;
普現一切，不去不來。
pǔ xiàn yī qiè， bù qù bù lái。
he appears everywhere, yet neither goes nor comes.
譬如日影現於水中，
pì rú rì yǐng xiàn yú shuǐ zhōng，
It is like the sun's reflection appearing in water:
佛亦不生亦不滅度，
fó yì bù shēng yì bù miè dù，
the Buddha neither arises nor passes into quiescence.
云何欲限如來慧乎？
yún hé yù xiàn rú lái huì hū？
How then would you limit the Tathagata's wisdom?
欲限如來為限虛空。」
yù xiàn rú lái wéi xiàn xū kōng。」
To limit the Tathagata would be to limit empty space.”
時須菩提聞諸龍子歎如來德，
shí xū pú tí wén zhū lóng zi tàn rú lái dé，
Then Subhuti, hearing the dragon princes extol the Tathagata's virtue
無窮無極，不可譬喻，默而無言。
wú qióng wú jí， bù kě pì yù， mò ér wú yán。
as endless, limitless and beyond comparison, fell silent and said nothing.
海中諸龍、虛空諸天及諸鬼、神踊躍歡喜，
hǎi zhōng zhū lóng、 xū kōng zhū tiān jí zhū guǐ、 shén yǒng yuè huān xǐ，
The dragons of the sea, the devas in the sky, and the ghosts and spirits leapt with joy,
皆發無上正真道意。
jiē fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì。
and all aroused the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment.
時佛歎嗟諸龍子曰：「善哉，善哉！仁等鑒明！
shí fó tàn jiē zhū lóng zi yuē： 「shàn zāi， shàn zāi！ rén děng jiàn míng！
Then the Buddha praised the dragon princes: “Excellent, excellent! Your discernment is clear.
誠如所云，無有異也。
chéng rú suǒ yún， wú yǒu yì yě。
It is truly as you have said, without difference.
佛道高妙，無邊無際，
fó dào gāo miào， wú biān wú jì，
The buddha path is lofty and subtle, boundless and without limit,
無方無圓，無廣無狹，無遠無近，
wú fāng wú yuán， wú guǎng wú xiá， wú yuǎn wú jìn，
neither square nor round, neither broad nor narrow, neither far nor near,
譬如虛空，不可為喻。」
pì rú xū kōng， bù kě wéi yù。」
like empty space, beyond all comparison.”
法供養品第十八
fǎ gòng yǎng pǐn dì shí bā
Chapter Eighteen: The Offering of Dharma
於是世尊告海龍王：
yú shì shì zūn gào hǎi lóng wáng：
Thereupon the World-Honored One told the Sea Dragon King:
「吾於大海所當教化皆以周畢，欲還精舍！」
「wú yú dà hǎi suǒ dāng jiào huà jiē yǐ zhōu bì， yù hái jīng shě！」
“All I was to teach and transform in the great ocean is now complete; I shall return to the monastery.”
即從坐起，與大眾俱，
jí cóng zuò qǐ， yǔ dà zhòng jù，
He rose at once from his seat and, with the great assembly,
尋從寶階出于大海，
xún cóng bǎo jiē chū yú dà hǎi，
went up by the jeweled stairway out of the great ocean,
以無極莊嚴廣普威神住於海邊。
yǐ wú jí zhuāng yán guǎng pǔ wēi shén zhù yú hǎi biān。
and with boundless adornment and vast majestic power stood upon the shore.
爾時大海神名曰光耀，則以此偈，而讚歎曰：
ěr shí dà hǎi shén míng yuē guāng yào， zé yǐ cǐ jì， ér zàn tàn yuē：
Then the god of the great ocean, named Radiance, praised him in verse:
「身相三十二，天人所恭敬，
「shēn xiāng sān shí èr， tiān rén suǒ gōng jìng，
“Your body bears the thirty-two marks, revered by devas and men;
無善神奉宗，稽首人中上。
wú shàn shén fèng zōng， qǐ shǒu rén zhōng shàng。
the asuras honor you as their ancestor — we bow to the Supreme among men.
光如百葉華，猶若月盛明，
guāng rú bǎi yè huā， yóu ruò yuè shèng míng，
Your radiance is like a hundred-petaled flower, like the moon at its fullest;
清淨德超異，稽首施安隱。
qīng jìng dé chāo yì， qǐ shǒu shī ān yǐn。
your pure virtue is surpassing — we bow to the giver of peace.
顏容殊妙好，百福功德相，
yán róng shū miào hǎo， bǎi fú gōng dé xiāng，
Your countenance is exceedingly wondrous and fair, marked with a hundred blessings and merits;
德慧度無極，稽首於導師。
dé huì dù wú jí， qǐ shǒu yú dǎo shī。
your virtue and wisdom are perfections without limit — we bow to the Guiding Teacher.
施與調順上，積於清淨戒，
shī yǔ diào shùn shàng， jī yú qīng jìng jiè，
Supreme in giving, taming and accord, amassing pure precepts,
忍辱力最勝，稽首世之上。
rěn rǔ lì zuì shèng， qǐ shǒu shì zhī shàng。
most victorious in the strength of patience — we bow to the Supreme of the world.
過於精進力，禪定思清淨，
guò yú jīng jìn lì， chán dìng sī qīng jìng，
Surpassing in the power of vigor, pure in dhyana and contemplation,
智慧如虛空，稽首以自歸。
zhì huì rú xū kōng， qǐ shǒu yǐ zì guī。
your wisdom like empty space — we bow and take refuge.
行慈以等心，修哀攝眾生，
xíng cí yǐ děng xīn， xiū āi shè zhòng shēng，
You practice loving-kindness with impartial mind, cultivate compassion and gather beings in,
喜心導御眾，常護度彼岸，
xǐ xīn dǎo yù zhòng， cháng hù dù bǐ àn，
guide the multitude with a joyful mind, and with constant equanimity ferry them to the far shore.
妙音如哀鸞，所說喻梵聲，
miào yīn rú āi luán， suǒ shuō yù fàn shēng，
Your wondrous voice is like the plaintive luan, your speech likened to Brahma's,
其響甚柔軟，願以稽首禮。
qí xiǎng shèn róu ruǎn， yuàn yǐ qǐ shǒu lǐ。
its resonance most gentle — we bow in reverence.
降伏於魔怨，其力無等倫，
xiáng fú yú mó yuàn， qí lì wú děng lún，
You subdue Mara and his foes, your strength beyond compare;
遵修願道法，三處所奉持，
zūn xiū yuàn dào fǎ， sān chù suǒ fèng chí，
you cultivate the vowed dharma of the Way, upheld in the three abodes;
淨除於三垢，講說三脫門，
jìng chú yú sān gòu， jiǎng shuō sān tuō mén，
you purge the three stains and expound the three gates of liberation;
其名聞三千，是故稽首禮。
qí míng wén sān qiān， shì gù qǐ shǒu lǐ。
your name is heard through the three-thousandfold world — therefore we bow.
善願常至誠，超度諸法上，
shàn yuàn cháng zhì chéng， chāo dù zhū fǎ shàng，
Your good vows are ever sincere, carrying you above all dharmas;
尊勝諸國王，常施惠大財，
zūn shèng zhū guó wáng， cháng shī huì dà cái，
honored above all kings, you always bestow great wealth;
以離諛諂塵，威神甚巍巍，
yǐ lí yú chǎn chén， wēi shén shèn wēi wēi，
free of the dust of flattery, your majesty is most lofty,
光顏大殊妙，是以稽首禮。
guāng yán dà shū miào， shì yǐ qǐ shǒu lǐ。
your radiant countenance exceedingly wondrous — therefore we bow.
心堅如金剛，譬如須彌山，
xīn jiān rú jīn gāng， pì rú xū mí shān，
Your mind is firm as adamant, like Mount Sumeru;
意至猶若地，故稽首德海。
yì zhì yóu ruò dì， gù qǐ shǒu dé hǎi。
your thought reaches like the earth — so we bow to the ocean of virtue.
為人說空義，常寂度無極，
wèi rén shuō kōng yì， cháng jì dù wú jí，
For others you preach the meaning of emptiness, ever still, the perfections without limit;
湛然心平等，願以自歸命。
zhàn rán xīn píng děng， yuàn yǐ zì guī mìng。
serene, your mind impartial — we take refuge in you.
開演如甘露，無趣斷所趣，
kāi yǎn rú gān lù， wú qù duàn suǒ qù，
You expound as with sweet dew, without destination, severing all destinations;
天人人所敬，願稽首最勝。
tiān rén rén suǒ jìng， yuàn qǐ shǒu zuì shèng。
revered by devas and men — we bow to the Most Victorious.
聞天上人間，名稱無有比，
wén tiān shàng rén jiān， míng chēng wú yǒu bǐ，
Heard in the heavens and among men, your renown is beyond compare,
德普不可量，稽首于德海。
dé pǔ bù kě liàng， qǐ shǒu yú dé hǎi。
your virtue universal and immeasurable — we bow to the ocean of virtue.
所作如所言，為人說本行，
suǒ zuò rú suǒ yán， wèi rén shuō běn xíng，
You do as you say and preach to others the original practice;
所興為人講，稽首善調御。
suǒ xìng wéi rén jiǎng， qǐ shǒu shàn diào yù。
what you undertake you expound for them — we bow to the good Tamer.
度於老病死，為賢眾所奉，
dù yú lǎo bìng sǐ， wéi xián zhòng suǒ fèng，
You have crossed beyond age, sickness and death, honored by the worthy assembly,
演說解脫句，稽首歸命佛。
yǎn shuō jiě tuō jù， qǐ shǒu guī mìng fó。
and set forth the phrases of liberation — we bow and take refuge in the Buddha.
分別罪福應，淨除邪見冥，
fēn bié zuì fú yìng， jìng chú xié jiàn míng，
You discern the recompense of sin and merit and purge the darkness of wrong views,
為現正道行，歸命於最勝。
wèi xiàn zhèng dào xíng， guī mìng yú zuì shèng。
revealing the practice of the right Way — we take refuge in the Most Victorious.
以經寶布施，究暢于法樂，
yǐ jīng bǎo bù shī， jiū chàng yú fǎ lè，
You give the treasure of the sutras and fathom the joy of Dharma,
等心於怨友，以歸命世尊。
děng xīn yú yuàn yǒu， yǐ guī mìng shì zūn。
impartial toward foe and friend — we take refuge in the World-Honored One.
我嗟歎導師，於德度無極，
wǒ jiē tàn dǎo shī， yú dé dù wú jí，
I extol the Guiding Teacher, boundless in the perfection of virtue;
所以諮嗟福，願後如世尊。」
suǒ yǐ zī jiē fú， yuàn hòu rú shì zūn。」
and by the merit of this praise, may I hereafter be as the World-Honored One.”
於是海神光耀，說是偈讚佛已，顯揚大海：
yú shì hǎi shén guāng yào， shuō shì jì zàn fó yǐ， xiǎn yáng dà hǎi：
Then the sea god Radiance, having praised the Buddha in verse, extolled the great ocean:
「如來出海，海無威神，
「rú lái chū hǎi， hǎi wú wēi shén，
“Now that the Tathagata has left the sea, the sea has no majesty.
願佛垂恩遺以法教，
yuàn fó chuí ēn yí yǐ fǎ jiào，
We pray the Buddha bestow his grace and leave behind a teaching of Dharma,
使此海中蒙其莊挍而獲度脫。」
shǐ cǐ hǎi zhōng méng qí zhuāng jiào ér huò dù tuō。」
so that this ocean may receive its adornment and gain deliverance.”
佛告海神：「有十法行得至莊挍。何等為十？
fó gào hǎi shén： 「yǒu shí fǎ xíng dé zhì zhuāng jiào。 hé děng wéi shí？
The Buddha told the sea god: “There are ten dharma-practices that bring one to adornment. Which ten?
護於諸根，十善清淨；
hù yú zhū gēn， shí shàn qīng jìng；
Guarding the faculties, with the ten wholesome deeds pure;
志于慈心，不害眾生；
zhì yú cí xīn， bù hài zhòng shēng；
setting the mind on loving-kindness and harming no being;
意立大哀，發無上正真道；
yì lì dà āi， fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào；
establishing great compassion in thought and arousing unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment;
一切布施以若干行莊嚴其願；
yī qiè bù shī yǐ ruò gān xíng zhuāng yán qí yuàn；
giving all things and adorning one's vow with practices of many kinds;
以大精進具足善法，心常寂然；
yǐ dà jīng jìn jù zú shàn fǎ， xīn cháng jì rán；
with great vigor perfecting wholesome dharmas, the mind ever still;
不違本德，好樂經法；
bù wéi běn dé， hǎo lè jīng fǎ；
not transgressing original virtue and delighting in the Dharma of the sutras;
智慧清淨，以愍哀行；
zhì huì qīng jìng， yǐ mǐn āi xíng；
wisdom pure, acting out of pity and compassion;
開化眾生，立於正德；
kāi huà zhòng shēng， lì yú zhèng dé；
converting beings and establishing them in right virtue;
入乎殊異，以致欣悅；
rù hū shū yì， yǐ zhì xīn yuè；
entering what is extraordinary and attaining gladness;
逮得佛意，導以佛教。
dǎi dé fó yì， dǎo yǐ fó jiào。
and attaining the Buddha's intent, guiding others by the Buddha's teaching.
是為十法致乎莊嚴。」
shì wéi shí fǎ zhì hū zhuāng yán。」
These are the ten dharmas that bring one to adornment.”
時大海神光耀與二萬天神，發無上正真道意，
shí dà hǎi shén guāng yào yǔ èr wàn tiān shén， fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì，
Then the sea god Radiance with twenty thousand gods aroused the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment,
俱共歎曰：「今以莊嚴於是大海。
jù gòng tàn yuē： 「jīn yǐ zhuāng yán yú shì dà hǎi。
and together exclaimed: “Now this great ocean is adorned.
所以者何？若發道意則為莊嚴三千世界，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ ruò fā dào yì zé wéi zhuāng yán sān qiān shì jiè，
Why? To arouse the aspiration to the Way adorns the three-thousandfold world —
何況海乎！
hé kuàng hǎi hū！
how much more this ocean!
我等以發諸通慧心，一切莊嚴之功德也。
wǒ děng yǐ fā zhū tōng huì xīn， yī qiè zhuāng yán zhī gōng dé yě。
We have aroused the mind of penetrating wisdom, which is the merit of every adornment.
我在於海，如來現在、若滅度後，
wǒ zài yú hǎi， rú lái xiàn zài、 ruò miè dù hòu，
Dwelling in the sea, whether the Tathagata is present or after his final quiescence,
擁護佛法，令其宣布。
yōng hù fó fǎ， lìng qí xuān bù。
we shall shelter the Buddha's Dharma and cause it to be proclaimed.
如來入城，現眾莊嚴，蒙以法恩化作講堂。」
rú lái rù chéng， xiàn zhòng zhuāng yán， méng yǐ fǎ ēn huà zuò jiǎng táng。」
When the Tathagata enters the city and displays every adornment, by the grace of the Dharma may a preaching hall be conjured forth.”
佛告光燿海神：「汝前後供養萬佛，普立大殿，
fó gào guāng yào hǎi shén： 「rǔ qián hòu gòng yǎng wàn fó， pǔ lì dà diàn，
The Buddha told the sea god Radiance: “You have made offerings to ten thousand buddhas and everywhere raised great halls,
又護正法，
yòu hù zhèng fǎ，
and have guarded the true Dharma.
次當供養賢劫興佛，將導正法。
cì dāng gòng yǎng xián jié xìng fó， jiāng dǎo zhèng fǎ。
Next you shall make offerings to the buddhas arising in the Worthy Kalpa and lead the true Dharma.
竟賢劫已，當生無怒佛國妙樂世界，
jìng xián jié yǐ， dāng shēng wú nù fó guó miào lè shì jiè，
When the Worthy Kalpa is ended you will be born in the buddha-land of the Tathagata Without Anger, in the world Wondrous Joy,
轉女人身，得為男子。
zhuǎn nǚ rén shēn， dé wéi nán zi。
and turning from a woman's body will become a man.
無怒如來當授汝決，為無上正真道。」
wú nù rú lái dāng shòu rǔ jué， wéi wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào。」
The Tathagata Without Anger will give you the prediction of unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment.”
時大海神光燿，聞佛授決，歡喜踊躍，
shí dà hǎi shén guāng yào， wén fó shòu jué， huān xǐ yǒng yuè，
Then the sea god Radiance, hearing the Buddha's prediction, leapt with joy,
則取珠瓔，價踰海寶，用散佛上，即而頌曰：
zé qǔ zhū yīng， jià yú hǎi bǎo， yòng sàn fó shàng， jí ér sòng yuē：
took a pearl garland worth more than the sea's treasure, scattered it over the Buddha, and spoke in verse:
「佛以聖諦音，能仁授吾決，
「fó yǐ shèng dì yīn， néng rén shòu wú jué，
“With the voice of holy truth Sakyamuni has given me the prediction;
我不懷猶豫，後當成佛不？
wǒ bù huái yóu yù， hòu dāng chéng fó bù？
shall I harbor any doubt that I will hereafter become a buddha?
可令三千界，地所有劈裂，
kě lìng sān qiān jiè， dì suǒ yǒu pī liè，
Though the three-thousandfold world and all its earth should split asunder,
亦令月墮地，佛所說不妄。
yì lìng yuè duò dì， fó suǒ shuō bù wàng。
though the moon should fall to earth — what the Buddha says is never false.
觀慈志境界，心淨修精進，
guān cí zhì jìng jiè， xīn jìng xiū jīng jìn，
Contemplating the realm of kindly resolve, with pure mind cultivating vigor,
今察我莊嚴，恭敬慧法義，
jīn chá wǒ zhuāng yán， gōng jìng huì fǎ yì，
now examining my adornment, revering the meaning of wisdom and Dharma,
覩安住所行，以慧決狐疑，
dǔ ān zhù suǒ xíng， yǐ huì jué hú yí，
beholding the Steadfast One's conduct, resolving doubt by wisdom,
了心如野馬，所說審至誠。
le xīn rú yě mǎ， suǒ shuō shěn zhì chéng。
understanding the mind to be like a shimmering mirage — what he says is truly sincere.
施安除眾苦，歿命救恐畏，
shī ān chú zhòng kǔ， mò mìng jiù kǒng wèi，
You bestow peace and remove all suffering, and at the cost of life rescue the fearful;
所療益無難，稽首最正覺。
suǒ liáo yì wú nán， qǐ shǒu zuì zhèng jué。
your healing benefits without hardship — we bow to the Perfectly Enlightened.
度脫諸惡趣，歸命光照世，
dù tuō zhū è qù， guī mìng guāng zhào shì，
Delivering from the evil destinies — we take refuge in the Light that illumines the world;
導師明無上，稽首佛說道。
dǎo shī míng wú shàng， qǐ shǒu fó shuō dào。
Guiding Teacher, unsurpassed in brightness — we bow to the Way the Buddha preaches.
其有聞佛聲，諸天人快利，
qí yǒu wén fó shēng， zhū tiān rén kuài lì，
Whoever hears the Buddha's voice, devas and men are gladdened and profited,
佛法清淨行，志在于佛道。
fó fǎ qīng jìng xíng， zhì zài yú fó dào。
the Buddha's Dharma a pure practice, their resolve set on the buddha path.
不得歸惡趣，便棄于八難，
bù dé guī è qù， biàn qì yú bā nàn，
They cannot fall to the evil destinies but cast off the eight difficulties,
生天在人間，後得寂然跡。」
shēng tiān zài rén jiān， hòu dé jì rán jī。」
are born among devas and men, and afterward attain the trace of stillness.”
於是海龍王子，名曰受現，白佛言：
yú shì hǎi lóng wáng zǐ， míng yuē shòu xiàn， bái fó yán：
Thereupon a son of the Sea Dragon King, named Receiving Manifestation, said to the Buddha:
「無央數天、龍、神、香音神、無善神、
「wú yāng shù tiān、 lóng、 shén、 xiāng yīn shén、 wú shàn shén、
“Countless devas, dragons, spirits, gandharvas, asuras,
鳳凰神、山神、恬柔神供養佛，
fèng huáng shén、 shān shén、 tián róu shén gòng yǎng fó，
garudas, mountain spirits and kinnaras have made offerings to the Buddha;
我身亦當少少供養如來、正覺。
wǒ shēn yì dāng shǎo shǎo gòng yǎng rú lái、 zhèng jué。
I too would make some small offering to the Tathagata, the Perfectly Enlightened.
假使世尊愍傷聽者，
jiǎ shǐ shì zūn mǐn shāng tīng zhě，
If the World-Honored One in pity will permit it,
化作大殿，譬如忉利最上選宮，
huà zuò dà diàn， pì rú dāo lì zuì shàng xuǎn gōng，
let a great hall be conjured, like the finest chosen palace of Trayastrimsa,
佛及弟子悉處其上，送至靈鷲山。」
fó jí dì zǐ xī chù qí shàng， sòng zhì líng jiù shān。」
with the Buddha and his disciples upon it, to convey them to Vulture Peak.”
世尊曰：「如仁者願。宜知是時！」
shì zūn yuē： 「rú rén zhě yuàn。 yí zhī shì shí！」
The World-Honored One said: “As you wish, worthy one. Know that the time is right.”
受現龍子自以神力承佛聖旨，
shòu xiàn lóng zi zì yǐ shén lì chéng fó shèng zhǐ，
The dragon prince Receiving Manifestation, by his own spiritual power and receiving the Buddha's holy intent,
化大嚴殿如忉利宮，佛及眾會皆處其上，
huà dà yán diàn rú dāo lì gōng， fó jí zhòng huì jiē chù qí shàng，
conjured a great adorned hall like the Trayastrimsa palace, with the Buddha and the whole assembly upon it,
在虛空中與八萬四千龍王諸后，
zài xū kōng zhōng yǔ bā wàn sì qiān lóng wáng zhū hòu，
in the open sky; and with eighty-four thousand dragon kings and their queens,
鼓諸琴瑟而雨眾華及一切香，
gǔ zhū qín sè ér yǔ zhòng huā jí yī qiè xiāng，
striking lutes and zithers and raining down flowers and every fragrance,
送佛、世尊詣靈鷲山。
sòng fó、 shì zūn yì líng jiù shān。
they conveyed the Buddha, the World-Honored One, to Vulture Peak.
海龍王子與中宮俱，在世尊前自投歸命：
hǎi lóng wáng zǐ yǔ zhōng gōng jù， zài shì zūn qián zì tóu guī mìng：
The dragon prince with the inner palace cast himself down before the World-Honored One and took refuge:
「願周不及。所以者何？所興供養當如其寶。
「yuàn zhōu bù jí。 suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ suǒ xìng gòng yǎng dāng rú qí bǎo。
“Our wish falls short of its aim. Why? Offerings should match their treasure.
佛為一切無上福田，世尊大寶，
fó wéi yī qiè wú shàng fú tián， shì zūn dà bǎo，
The Buddha is the unsurpassed field of merit for all, the World-Honored One a great treasure.
正使三千大千世界滿中羅漢，
zhèng shǐ sān qiān dà qiān shì jiè mǎn zhōng luó hàn，
Even if arhats filled the three-thousandfold great-thousand world
皆共供養於百千劫，
jiē gòng gòng yǎng yú bǎi qiān jié，
and made offerings together for a hundred thousand kalpas,
不如奉侍如來、世尊，如是乃應供養。
bù rú fèng shì rú lái、 shì zūn， rú shì nǎi yīng gòng yǎng。
it would not equal serving the Tathagata, the World-Honored One — such is a fitting offering.
又世尊！何謂菩薩供養如來？」
yòu shì zūn！ hé wèi pú sà gòng yǎng rú lái？」
And, World-Honored One, what is a bodhisattva's offering to the Tathagata?”
佛言：「且聽菩薩所行供養如來：
fó yán： 「qiě tīng pú sà suǒ xíng gòng yǎng rú lái：
The Buddha said: “Listen, then, to how a bodhisattva makes offering to the Tathagata.
其心清淨，除於瑕穢；
qí xīn qīng jìng， chú yú xiá huì；
His mind is pure, free of flaw and stain;
而無諛諂，本性自然；
ér wú yú chǎn， běn xìng zì rán；
without flattery, his original nature so of itself;
不著一切諸善之本；
bù zhuó yī qiè zhū shàn zhī běn；
he clings to no root of good whatsoever;
無加害心，等光眾生；
wú jiā hài xīn， děng guāng zhòng shēng；
he has no thought of harm and shines equally on all beings;
除妖邪心，所行鮮潔；
chú yāo xié xīn， suǒ xíng xiān jié；
he removes crooked and perverse thoughts and his conduct is clean;
言行相應，不侵欺世；
yán xíng xiāng yìng， bù qīn qī shì；
his words and deeds accord, and he does not violate or deceive the world;
賢聖智足，威儀至德；
xián shèng zhì zú， wēi yí zhì dé；
sagely in knowledge and content, his bearing of utmost virtue;
一切平平，而不違捨。
yī qiè píng píng， ér bù wéi shě。
level in all things, forsaking nothing.
賢聖大願；樂於空閑，捨眾憒閙；
xián shèng dà yuàn； lè yú kōng xián， shě zhòng kuì nào；
The great vow of the sages: delighting in solitude and leaving crowds and clamor;
自調其心，聞法靜思；
zì diào qí xīn， wén fǎ jìng sī；
taming his own mind, hearing the Dharma and pondering in stillness;
解知本諦，無我、無人、無壽、無命；
jiě zhī běn dì， wú wǒ、 wú rén、 wú shòu、 wú mìng；
understanding the original truth — no self, no person, no lifespan, no living being;
入於空無，入普達寂；
rù yú kōng wú， rù pǔ dá jì；
entering emptiness and nothingness, entering universal penetrating stillness;
觀空、無想、無願，至三脫法；
guān kōng、 wú xiǎng、 wú yuàn， zhì sān tuō fǎ；
contemplating emptiness, signlessness and wishlessness, reaching the three dharmas of liberation;
如是像法，調諸邪見；
rú shì xiàng fǎ， diào zhū xié jiàn；
and by such a Dharma taming all wrong views;
棄有常無常，不起不滅；
qì yǒu cháng wú cháng， bù qǐ bù miè；
casting off both permanence and impermanence, neither arising nor ceasing;
逮得法忍，本淨無人；
dǎi dé fǎ rěn， běn jìng wú rén；
attaining forbearance of dharmas, originally pure and without person;
無身、口、心志行因緣。
wú shēn、 kǒu、 xīn zhì xíng yīn yuán。
without the causes and conditions of the resolve and conduct of body, speech and mind.
是則應法供養如來。
shì zé yīng fǎ gòng yǎng rú lái。
This is the offering of Dharma answering to the Tathagata.
身、口、心行則不供養；
shēn、 kǒu、 xīn xíng zé bù gòng yǎng；
Conduct of body, speech and mind alone is not an offering;
無所造行，不進不退；
wú suǒ zào xíng， bù jìn bù tuì；
he fabricates no practice, neither advancing nor retreating;
淨三道場，等於三世；
jìng sān dào chǎng， děng yú sān shì；
he purifies the three bodhimandas and is equal in the three times;
除去三垢，不著三界；
chú qù sān gòu， bù zhuó sān jiè；
removes the three stains and clings not to the three realms;
入三脫門，得三達智，
rù sān tuō mén， dé sān dá zhì，
enters the three gates of liberation and gains the three insights —
是則名曰供養如來。」
shì zé míng yuē gòng yǎng rú lái。」
this is what is called making offering to the Tathagata.”
時海龍王問世尊曰：
shí hǎi lóng wáng wèn shì zūn yuē：
Then the Sea Dragon King asked the World-Honored One:
「其有人以華香、雜香、擣香、繒蓋、幢幡、
「qí yǒu rén yǐ huā xiāng、 zá xiāng、 dǎo xiāng、 zēng gài、 chuáng fān、
“If a person makes offerings to the Tathagata with flowers and incense, blended incense, powdered incense, silk canopies, banners and pennants,
伎樂、衣被、飲食、床臥、病瘦、醫藥供養如來，
jì yuè、 yī bèi、 yǐn shí、 chuáng wò、 bìng shòu、 yī yào gòng yǎng rú lái，
music, robes and bedding, food and drink, and medicines for the sick,
寧應供養不？」
níng yīng gòng yǎng bù？」
is that a fitting offering?”
佛言：「龍王！隨其所種各得其類，
fó yán： 「lóng wáng！ suí qí suǒ zhǒng gè dé qí lèi，
The Buddha said: “Dragon King, each reaps according to what he plants;
此之供養不為究竟，
cǐ zhī gòng yǎng bù wéi jiū jìng，
but such offerings are not ultimate.
離於垢塵，殖于德本，
lí yú gòu chén， zhí yú dé běn，
They part one from defilement and plant roots of virtue,
逮至賢聖心之解脫，
dǎi zhì xián shèng xīn zhī jiě tuō，
and bring one to the mind-liberation of the sages;
不為無德、不至無上。
bù wéi wú dé、 bù zhì wú shàng。
they are not without virtue, yet they do not reach the unsurpassed.
菩薩有四事應供養如來。何等四？
pú sà yǒu sì shì yīng gòng yǎng rú lái。 hé děng sì？
A bodhisattva has four things that are a fitting offering to the Tathagata. Which four?
不捨道心，殖諸德本；
bù shě dào xīn， zhí zhū dé běn；
Not abandoning the mind of the Way and planting roots of virtue;
心立大哀，合集慧品；
xīn lì dà āi， hé jí huì pǐn；
establishing great compassion in the mind and gathering the limbs of wisdom;
建大精進，嚴淨佛土；
jiàn dà jīng jìn， yán jìng fó tǔ；
raising great vigor and adorning and purifying a buddha-land;
入深妙法，心得法忍。
rù shēn miào fǎ， xīn dé fǎ rěn。
entering the profound and subtle Dharma and gaining forbearance of dharmas in the mind.
是為四尊敬如來，為供養也。」
shì wéi sì zūn jìng rú lái， wéi gòng yǎng yě。」
These are the four by which one honors the Tathagata, and this is making offerings.”
爾時王舍城中梵志長者及無央數人民、
ěr shí wáng shě chéng zhōng fàn zhì zhǎng zhě jí wú yāng shù rén mín、
At that time in Rajagrha the brahmins and elders, with countless people,
尊者、大臣，上至摩竭國王阿闍世，
zūn zhě、 dà chén， shàng zhì mó jié guó wáng ā dū shì，
notables and great ministers, up to Ajatasatru king of Magadha,
聞佛在大海龍王宮，就請來還靈鷲山。
wén fó zài dà hǎi lóng wáng gōng， jiù qǐng lái hái líng jiù shān。
heard that the Buddha had been in the dragon king's palace in the great ocean at his invitation and had returned to Vulture Peak.
時七萬二千人皆詣佛所，
shí qī wàn èr qiān rén jiē yì fó suǒ，
Then seventy-two thousand people went to where the Buddha was;
王阿闍世與官屬三萬二千，
wáng ā dū shì yǔ guān shǔ sān wàn èr qiān，
and King Ajatasatru with thirty-two thousand officials
出王舍城詣靈鷲山，稽首佛足，
chū wáng shě chéng yì líng jiù shān， qǐ shǒu fó zú，
left Rajagrha for Vulture Peak, bowed at the Buddha's feet,
遶佛三匝，却住一面，前白佛言：
rào fó sān zā， què zhù yí miàn， qián bái fó yán：
circled him three times, withdrew to one side, and said:
「佛入大海，水何所至？」
「fó rù dà hǎi， shuǐ hé suǒ zhì？」
“When the Buddha entered the great ocean, where did the water go?”
佛言：「大王！其有比丘威光定意心正受者，
fó yán： 「dà wáng！ qí yǒu bǐ qiū wēi guāng dìng yì xīn zhèng shòu zhě，
The Buddha said: “Great king, when a bhiksu of majestic radiance enters concentration and right absorption
普見滿火，其水安在？」
pǔ jiàn mǎn huǒ， qí shuǐ ān zài？」
he sees fire filling everything — where then is the water?”
王對曰：「三昧自在之所致也，天中天！
wáng duì yuē： 「sān mèi zì zài zhī suǒ zhì yě， tiān zhōng tiān！
The king replied: “It is brought about by mastery of samadhi, Deva among devas —
志心所為也。」
zhì xīn suǒ wéi yě。」
it is the doing of the resolved mind.”
佛言：「王寧得聲聞所興三昧自在已耶？
fó yán： 「wáng níng dé shēng wén suǒ xìng sān mèi zì zài yǐ yé？
The Buddha said: “King, do you grasp the mastery of samadhi that sravakas rouse?
心之所為乎！
xīn zhī suǒ wéi hū！
It is the doing of the mind.
如來常定等一切法，
rú lái cháng dìng děng yī qiè fǎ，
The Tathagata is always in concentration, equal in all dharmas,
曉了坐起而得自在，
xiǎo liǎo zuò qǐ ér dé zì zài，
understanding sitting and rising and gaining mastery,
於法為尊心無所礙。
yú fǎ wéi zūn xīn wú suǒ ài。
honored in the Dharma, his mind unobstructed.
佛入大海，其處水類續見如故，
fó rù dà hǎi， qí chù shuǐ lèi xù jiàn rú gù，
When the Buddha entered the great ocean, the water-dwellers there still saw it as before,
其陸地人覩乎大海其水枯涸，
qí lù dì rén dǔ hū dà hǎi qí shuǐ kū hé，
while people on land saw the great ocean with its waters dried up,
但見眾寶而莊嚴之，
dàn jiàn zhòng bǎo ér zhuāng yán zhī，
beholding only its many jewels adorning it,
譬如第六他化自在諸天宮殿所莊嚴也。
pì rú dì liù tā huà zì zài zhū tiān gōng diàn suǒ zhuāng yán yě。
like the adorned palaces of the sixth heaven, the Paranirmitavasavartin devas.
佛光普照諸龍宮殿、香音神宮、無善神宮，
fó guāng pǔ zhào zhū lóng gōng diàn、 xiāng yīn shén gōng、 wú shàn shén gōng，
The Buddha's light shone throughout the palaces of the dragons, the gandharvas and the asuras,
其大海中含血之類，皆行慈心，
qí dà hǎi zhōng hán xuè zhī lèi， jiē xíng cí xīn，
and every creature of flesh and blood in the great ocean practiced loving-kindness,
仁意相向，無懷害者。」
rén yì xiāng xiàng， wú huái hài zhě。」
turning kindly toward one another, none harboring harm.”
阿闍世王白佛：
ā dū shì wáng bái fó：
King Ajatasatru said to the Buddha:
「其海龍王久如發無上正真道意？
「qí hǎi lóng wáng jiǔ rú fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì？
“How long ago did the Sea Dragon King arouse the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment?
奉事幾佛何時當逮最正覺？所號為何？」
fèng shì jǐ fó hé shí dāng dǎi zuì zhèng jué？ suǒ hào wéi hé？」
How many buddhas has he served, when will he attain supreme awakening, and by what name?”
佛告王曰：「乃往久遠無央數劫不可思議，
fó gào wáng yuē： 「nǎi wǎng jiǔ yuǎn wú yāng shù jié bù kě sī yì，
The Buddha told the king: “Long ago, countless inconceivable kalpas past,
彼時有佛，號光淨照耀如來、至真、等正覺、
bǐ shí yǒu fó， hào guāng jìng zhào yào rú lái、 zhì zhēn、 děng zhèng jué、
there was a buddha named the Tathagata Pure Light Illumining, Truly Worthy, Perfectly Enlightened,
明行成為、善逝、世間解、無上士、
míng xíng chéng wéi、 shàn shì、 shì jiān jiě、 wú shàng shì、
Perfect in Knowledge and Conduct, Well Gone, Knower of the World, Unsurpassed One,
道法御、天人師，號佛、世尊，
dào fǎ yù、 tiān rén shī， hào fó、 shì zūn，
Tamer by the Dharma of the Way, Teacher of Devas and Men, called Buddha, World-Honored One.
乃在東方，世界曰善淨現，劫名可意寂。
nǎi zài dōng fāng， shì jiè yuē shàn jìng xiàn， jié míng kě yì jì。
He was in the east; his world was named Good Pure Manifestation and the kalpa Pleasing Stillness.
其善淨現世界，平正而悉莊嚴，
qí shàn jìng xiàn shì jiè， píng zhèng ér xī zhuāng yán，
That world Good Pure Manifestation was level and wholly adorned;
假使一切嗟歎其德，不能究竟。
jiǎ shǐ yī qiè jiē tàn qí dé， bù néng jiū jìng。
were all beings to extol its virtues they could not exhaust them.
時有轉輪聖王，號曰護天，
shí yǒu zhuǎn lún shèng wáng， hào yuē hù tiān，
At that time there was a wheel-turning sage king named Guardian of Devas,
常供養光淨照耀如來，
cháng gòng yǎng guāng jìng zhào yào rú lái，
who constantly made offerings to the Tathagata Pure Light Illumining
四百二十萬歲一切施安，
sì bǎi èr shí wàn suì yī qiè shī ān，
and for four million two hundred thousand years bestowed peace upon all.
竟此之數寐於夢中，自然瑞應聞此偈曰：
jìng cǐ zhī shù mèi yú mèng zhōng， zì rán ruì yīng wén cǐ jì yuē：
At the end of that span, while asleep, an auspicious sign arose of itself and he heard this verse:
「『王以供養大聖人，甚多無量難思議，
「『wáng yǐ gòng yǎng dà shèng rén， shèn duō wú liàng nán sī yì，
“‘King, your offerings to the great Sage are many, immeasurable, inconceivable;
常興慈哀於眾生，當發最上菩薩心。
cháng xìng cí āi yú zhòng shēng， dāng fā zuì shàng pú sà xīn。
you constantly raise kindness and compassion for beings — now rouse the supreme bodhisattva mind.
是供養尊妙第一，此則奉敬諸如來，
shì gòng yǎng zūn miào dì yī， cǐ zé fèng jìng zhū rú lái，
That offering is the most honored and wondrous, the true reverencing of all Tathagatas;
其有能發菩薩志，則為度世威神德。』」
qí yǒu néng fā pú sà zhì， zé wéi dù shì wēi shén dé。』」
and whoever can rouse the bodhisattva resolve has the majestic virtue that delivers the world.’”
佛告阿闍世王：「其護天轉輪聖王，
fó gào ā dū shì wáng： 「qí hù tiān zhuǎn lún shèng wáng，
The Buddha told King Ajatasatru: “The wheel-turning sage king Guardian of Devas,
夢聞此已，寤自驚怪：
mèng wén cǐ yǐ， wù zì jīng guài：
having heard this in his dream, awoke startled and wondering:
『吾供養佛四百二十萬歲，
『wú gòng yǎng fó sì bǎi èr shí wàn suì，
‘I have made offerings to the Buddha for four million two hundred thousand years,
佛說經法章句各異，
fó shuō jīng fǎ zhāng jù gè yì，
and the chapters and phrases of the Dharma he preached were all various;
初未曾聞如此偈經，是佛說邪？魔所云乎？』
chū wèi céng wén rú cǐ jì jīng， shì fó shuō xié？ mó suǒ yún hū？』
but never have I heard a verse-scripture like this. Is it the Buddha's word, or Mara's?’
即䟽偈文而諷誦之。
jí 䟽 jì wén ér fěng sòng zhī。
He wrote out the verse and recited it.
時光淨照耀如來行遊諸國，
shí guāng jìng zhào yào rú lái xíng yóu zhū guó，
At that time the Tathagata Pure Light Illumining was travelling through the realms,
聖王即與八萬四千王及八萬四千后、
shèng wáng jí yǔ bā wàn sì qiān wáng jí bā wàn sì qiān hòu、
and the sage king, with eighty-four thousand kings and eighty-four thousand queens,
國中臣民，
guó zhōng chén mín，
and the ministers and people of his realm,
其諸往者各八萬四千，俱往追佛，欲決斯疑。
qí zhū wǎng zhě gè bā wàn sì qiān， jù wǎng zhuī fó， yù jué sī yí。
eighty-four thousand of each going, went together after the Buddha to resolve this doubt.
即逮見佛，稽首足下，敬問無量，即白佛言：
jí dǎi jiàn fó， qǐ shǒu zú xià， jìng wèn wú liàng， jí bái fó yán：
On reaching him he bowed at his feet, paid boundless respects, and said:
『吾供養佛四百二十萬歲，佛說經法若干種義。
『wú gòng yǎng fó sì bǎi èr shí wàn suì， fó shuō jīng fǎ ruò gān zhǒng yì。
‘I have made offerings to the Buddha for four million two hundred thousand years, and he has preached the Dharma in many kinds of meaning.
我昨夜夢，夢中見佛說此二偈，
wǒ zuó yè mèng， mèng zhōng jiàn fó shuō cǐ èr jì，
Last night I dreamed, and in the dream saw the Buddha speak these two verses;
寤甚驚怪，未從如來聞此偈教，
wù shèn jīng guài， wèi cóng rú lái wén cǐ jì jiào，
I awoke greatly startled, for I have never heard this verse-teaching from the Tathagata.
不審佛所歎乎？魔所說耶？
bù shěn fó suǒ tàn hū？ mó suǒ shuō yé？
Is it what the Buddha extols, or what Mara says?
今故遠來欲決此疑！唯願世尊分別說之！』
jīn gù yuǎn lái yù jué cǐ yí！ wéi yuàn shì zūn fēn bié shuō zhī！』
I have come far to resolve this doubt — we pray the World-Honored One explain it!’
佛告護天：『是吾所讚，非魔之所云。』
fó gào hù tiān： 『shì wú suǒ zàn， fēi mó zhī suǒ yún。』
The Buddha told Guardian of Devas: ‘It is what I praise, not what Mara says.’
王又白佛言：『我奉事世尊若干億歲，
wáng yòu bái fó yán： 『wǒ fèng shì shì zūn ruò gān yì suì，
The king said again: ‘I have served the World-Honored One for many millions of years,
供養衣食無所乏少，
gòng yǎng yī shí wú suǒ fá shǎo，
offering robes and food with nothing lacking,
為我說經章句各異，
wéi wǒ shuō jīng zhāng jù gè yì，
and he has preached the sutras to me in chapters and phrases of every kind.
爾時何故不歎此義？』
ěr shí hé gù bù tàn cǐ yì？』
Why then did he not extol this meaning?’
佛即以偈答王曰：
fó jí yǐ jì dá wáng yuē：
The Buddha answered the king in verse:
「『人心羸劣未有識，初習福事未見深，
「『rén xīn léi liè wèi yǒu shí， chū xí fú shì wèi jiàn shēn，
“‘People's minds are frail and without discernment; new to works of merit, they have not seen the depths.
不可為說微妙法，心中驚疑或作却。
bù kě wèi shuō wēi miào fǎ， xīn zhōng jīng yí huò zuò què。
One cannot preach the subtle Dharma to them; startled and doubting, they may turn back.
以解罪福信佛法，心堅意固不迴動，
yǐ jiě zuì fú xìn fó fǎ， xīn jiān yì gù bù huí dòng，
But once they understand sin and merit and trust the Buddha's Dharma, their minds firm and unshaken,
乃可為說菩薩事，爾乃解至無極慧。』
nǎi kě wèi shuō pú sà shì， ěr nǎi jiě zhì wú jí huì。』
then one may preach to them the bodhisattva's matter, and they will understand and reach boundless wisdom.’”
「時王及群臣后民心中大悅，
「shí wáng jí qún chén hòu mín xīn zhōng dà yuè，
“Then the king, his ministers, queens and people rejoiced greatly,
皆發無上正真道意，尋立不退轉地，
jiē fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì， xún lì bù tuì zhuǎn dì，
all aroused the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment, and at once stood on the ground of non-regression,
則以此偈而讚世尊曰：
zé yǐ cǐ jì ér zàn shì zūn yuē：
and praised the World-Honored One in verse:
「『不以貪諸色，亦不猗音聲，
「『bù yǐ tān zhū sè， yì bù yī yīn shēng，
“‘Not by craving forms, nor by leaning on sounds,
香薰眾細滑，不以此得佛。
xiāng xūn zhòng xì huá， bù yǐ cǐ dé fó。
on fragrance or on the touch of things — not by these does one attain buddhahood.
離懈怠怯弱，避貪嫉犯法，
lí xiè dài qiè ruò， bì tān jí fàn fǎ，
Leaving indolence and timidity, avoiding greed, envy and transgression,
除瞋恚憒亂，乃得成正覺。
chú chēn huì kuì luàn， nǎi dé chéng zhèng jué。
removing anger and confusion — thus is right awakening accomplished.
捨身諸所安，代眾生受惱，
shě shēn zhū suǒ ān， dài zhòng shēng shòu nǎo，
Giving up the body's every comfort, bearing affliction in place of beings,
精進常樂法，如是乃得佛。
jīng jìn cháng lè fǎ， rú shì nǎi dé fó。
vigorous and ever delighting in Dharma — thus does one become a buddha.
我今志大道，佛與天證我，
wǒ jīn zhì dà dào， fó yǔ tiān zhèng wǒ，
Now I set my resolve on the great Way; let the Buddha and the devas bear witness,
唯導師造行，令所言無異。
wéi dǎo shī zào xíng， lìng suǒ yán wú yì。
and by the Guiding Teacher's practice let my words prove true.
夢中聞道心，適聞志大乘，
mèng zhōng wén dào xīn， shì wén zhì dà chéng，
In a dream I heard the mind of the Way; hearing it, I aspired to the Great Vehicle;
所作慧無疑，得至佛法王。』」
suǒ zuò huì wú yí， dé zhì fó fǎ wáng。』」
what I do is wisdom without doubt, and I shall reach the King of the Buddha's Dharma.’”
佛告阿闍世王：「欲知爾時轉輪聖王護天者不？
fó gào ā dū shì wáng： 「yù zhī ěr shí zhuǎn lún shèng wáng hù tiān zhě bù？
The Buddha told King Ajatasatru: “Would you know who that wheel-turning sage king Guardian of Devas was?
今海龍王是！彼時初發無上正真道意。
jīn hǎi lóng wáng shì！ bǐ shí chū fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì。
He is the Sea Dragon King now! At that time he first aroused the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment.
又王所問何時成佛？王具當聽：
yòu wáng suǒ wèn hé shí chéng fó？ wáng jù dāng tīng：
And as to when he will become a buddha — king, listen fully:
過二百無央數劫當成為佛，
guò èr bǎi wú yāng shù jié dāng chéng wèi fó，
after two hundred countless kalpas he will become a buddha
號無垢淨無量德超所有王如來、至真、等正覺，
hào wú gòu jìng wú liàng dé chāo suǒ yǒu wáng rú lái、 zhì zhēn、 děng zhèng jué，
named the Tathagata Undefiled Purity, King of Immeasurable Virtue Surpassing All, Truly Worthy, Perfectly Enlightened;
世界曰法音聲，劫曰首華。
shì jiè yuē fǎ yīn shēng， jié yuē shǒu huā。
his world will be named Sound of Dharma and the kalpa Foremost Flower.
其法音聲世界，眾寶合成若干種色，
qí fǎ yīn shēng shì jiè， zhòng bǎo hé chéng ruò gān zhǒng sè，
That world Sound of Dharma will be made of many jewels of every color,
地平如掌，其地柔軟，如天綩綖，
dì píng rú zhǎng， qí dì róu ruǎn， rú tiān wǎn yán，
its ground level as a palm and soft as celestial cushions;
有萬億安明山廣普難量，
yǒu wàn yì ān míng shān guǎng pǔ nán liàng，
it will have ten billion Mount Sumerus, vast beyond measuring,
安隱豐熟，五穀平賤，天人充滿，
ān yǐn fēng shú， wǔ gǔ píng jiàn， tiān rén chōng mǎn，
peaceful and fruitful, the five grains cheap, filled with devas and men,
衣服飲食如第六天人所居。
yī fú yǐn shí rú dì liù tiān rén suǒ jū。
their clothing and food as in the dwelling of the devas of the sixth heaven.
法音聲世界諸樹，
fǎ yīn shēng shì jiè zhū shù，
In the world Sound of Dharma the trees —
根、葉、莖、節、華、實皆以七寶，
gēn、 yè、 jīng、 jié、 huā、 shí jiē yǐ qī bǎo，
root, leaf, stem, joint, flower and fruit — will all be of the seven treasures,
悉出無數若干百千道法音聲。
xī chū wú shù ruò gān bǎi qiān dào fǎ yīn shēng。
and all give forth countless hundreds of thousands of sounds of the Dharma of the Way.
其界人民猶若諸天，
qí jiè rén mín yóu ruò zhū tiān，
The people of that realm will be like devas,
所語變化柔軟之聲，皆承法音，
suǒ yǔ biàn huà róu ruǎn zhī shēng， jiē chéng fǎ yīn，
and the gentle transforming sounds they speak will all carry the sound of Dharma,
寂然澹泊，及佛法眾智度無極四恩之教，
jì rán dàn pō， jí fó fǎ zhòng zhì dù wú jí sì ēn zhī jiào，
still and tranquil — the teaching of the Buddha, Dharma and Sangha, the perfection of wisdom and the four kindnesses,
善權方便，滅定離欲，
shàn quán fāng biàn， miè dìng lí yù，
skillful means, the absorption of cessation and freedom from desire,
空、無想、不願無為無數，
kōng、 wú xiǎng、 bù yuàn wú wéi wú shù，
emptiness, signlessness, wishlessness, the unconditioned and the numberless.
以故其界名法音聲。
yǐ gù qí jiè míng fǎ yīn shēng。
For this reason that realm is named Sound of Dharma.
其有天人在彼土者，樂法歡喜，
qí yǒu tiān rén zài bǐ tǔ zhě， lè fǎ huān xǐ，
The devas and men in that land will delight in the Dharma and rejoice,
皆當入大聖，分別諸慧，
jiē dāng rù dà shèng， fēn bié zhū huì，
all entering into great sagehood, discerning every wisdom,
審諦究竟，發無上正真道意。
shěn dì jiū jìng， fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì。
fathoming the truth, and arousing the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment.
其佛欲說經時，身放大光普照佛界，
qí fó yù shuō jīng shí， shēn fàng dà guāng pǔ zhào fó jiè，
When that buddha is about to preach, his body will emit a great light illumining the whole buddha-realm,
其光明中則出億佛說講法聲。
qí guāng míng zhōng zé chū yì fó shuō jiǎng fǎ shēng。
and from that light will issue the sound of a hundred million buddhas expounding the Dharma.
時諸天人見光聞法欣然大悅，
shí zhū tiān rén jiàn guāng wén fǎ xīn rán dà yuè，
Then devas and men, seeing the light and hearing the Dharma, will rejoice greatly,
往詣如來，自歸供養，
wǎng yì rú lái， zì guī gòng yǎng，
go to the Tathagata, take refuge and make offerings;
則無央數以神足力飛行虛空，
zé wú yāng shù yǐ shén zú lì fēi xíng xū kōng，
and countless of them, by the power of spiritual flight, will move through the sky,
化寶蓮華而坐其上。
huà bǎo lián huā ér zuò qí shàng。
conjure jeweled lotuses and sit upon them.
如來應時亦在虛空坐師子座，
rú lái yìng shí yì zài xū kōng zuò shī zǐ zuò，
The Tathagata will at that moment also sit on a lion throne in the sky,
為諸菩薩講說經道。
wéi zhū pú sà jiǎng shuō jīng dào。
expounding the Way of the sutras for the bodhisattvas.
時諸十方無央數百千諸菩薩者，
shí zhū shí fāng wú yāng shù bǎi qiān zhū pú sà zhě，
Then countless hundreds of thousands of bodhisattvas from the ten directions
皆當來會聽受經法，
jiē dāng lái huì tīng shòu jīng fǎ，
will come to the assembly to hear and receive the Dharma.
其國人民皆樂經法，
qí guó rén mín jiē lè jīng fǎ，
The people of that land will all delight in the Dharma;
亦無諸魔有所嬈害，
yì wú zhū mó yǒu suǒ ráo hài，
no host of Mara will trouble or harm them,
亦無眾邪異道，亦無橫死。
yì wú zhòng xié yì dào， yì wú héng sǐ。
there will be no crooked or heterodox paths, and no untimely death.
其佛壽十二劫，諸菩薩行超過於空。
qí fó shòu shí èr jié， zhū pú sà xíng chāo guò yú kōng。
That buddha's lifespan will be twelve kalpas, and the bodhisattvas' conduct will surpass even space.
其國如是莊嚴無量，說法無限，菩薩無數。」
qí guó rú shì zhuāng yán wú liàng， shuō fǎ wú xiàn， pú sà wú shù。」
Such is that land: immeasurable in adornment, limitless in preaching, countless in bodhisattvas.”
空淨品第十九
kōng jìng pǐn dì shí jiǔ
Chapter Nineteen: Emptiness and Purity
王阿闍世前白佛言：「唯然，世尊！
wáng ā dū shì qián bái fó yán： 「wéi rán， shì zūn！
King Ajatasatru came forward and said: “So be it, World-Honored One!
常於諸法有大愍哀，
cháng yú zhū fǎ yǒu dà mǐn āi，
You always hold great pity and compassion toward all dharmas.
諸法誑詐，因惑起想，
zhū fǎ kuáng zhà， yīn huò qǐ xiǎng，
Dharmas are deceptive; from delusion notions arise,
隨其所欲展轉相惑。
suí qí suǒ yù zhǎn zhuǎn xiāng huò。
and according to what each desires they delude one another in turn.
菩薩之行不可計量，
pú sà zhī xíng bù kě jì liàng，
The bodhisattva's conduct is beyond measuring.
彼為菩薩當修道行，
bǐ wéi pú sà dāng xiū dào xíng，
One who would be a bodhisattva should cultivate the practice of the Way
至彼佛土具足嚴淨，
zhì bǐ fó tǔ jù zú yán jìng，
until that buddha-land is fully adorned and pure.
諸菩薩行皆當追學攝取佛土，
zhū pú sà xíng jiē dāng zhuī xué shè qǔ fó tǔ，
All bodhisattva practices should be pursued and studied so as to take up a buddha-land,
如海龍王國土嚴淨。」
rú hǎi lóng wáng guó tǔ yán jìng。」
as the Sea Dragon King's land will be adorned and pure.”
佛言：「如是，如是！大王！
fó yán： 「rú shì， rú shì！ dà wáng！
The Buddha said: “Just so, just so, great king!
一切諸法皆從念興，
yī qiè zhū fǎ jiē cóng niàn xìng，
All dharmas arise from thought;
隨其所作各各悉成，
suí qí suǒ zuò gè gè xī chéng，
according to what each does, so each is accomplished;
諸法無住亦無有處。」
zhū fǎ wú zhù yì wú yǒu chù。」
yet dharmas do not abide and have no place.”
爾時阿闍世王謂海龍王：
ěr shí ā dū shì wáng wèi hǎi lóng wáng：
Then King Ajatasatru said to the Sea Dragon King:
「快哉，龍王！為得善利，
「kuài zāi， lóng wáng！ wéi dé shàn lì，
“How glad a thing, Dragon King! You have gained a fine benefit,
乃令如來授仁之決，
nǎi lìng rú lái shòu rén zhī jué，
that the Tathagata has given you the prediction;
當成佛時，國土清嚴不可思議。」
dāng chéng fó shí， guó tǔ qīng yán bù kě sī yì。」
and when you become a buddha your land will be pure and adorned beyond conceiving.”
龍王答曰：「法無有決。所以者何？
lóng wáng dá yuē： 「fǎ wú yǒu jué。 suǒ yǐ zhě hé？
The Dragon King answered: “In the Dharma there is no prediction. Why?
諸法皆靜，因種、陰、入，假名曰人。
zhū fǎ jiē jìng， yīn zhǒng、 yīn、 rù， jiǎ míng yuē rén。
All dharmas are still; from the seeds, aggregates and sense-fields a person is provisionally named.
其受決者，無陰、種、入，
qí shòu jué zhě， wú yīn、 zhǒng、 rù，
For one who receives a prediction there are no aggregates, seeds or sense-fields;
以有名色，假名為人。
yǐ yǒu míng sè， jiǎ míng wéi rén。
it is by having name-and-form that a person is provisionally named.
其受決者，無名無色，
qí shòu jué zhě， wú míng wú sè，
For one who receives a prediction there is no name and no form;
因緣報應，見思想念，假名曰人。
yīn yuán bào yìng， jiàn sī xiǎng niàn， jiǎ míng yuē rén。
it is by the recompense of causes and conditions, by seeing, thinking and conceiving, that a person is provisionally named.
其受決者，無有報應，無見無思，無想無念；
qí shòu jué zhě， wú yǒu bào yìng， wú jiàn wú sī， wú xiǎng wú niàn；
For one who receives a prediction there is no recompense, no seeing, no thinking, no conceiving, no thought.
假使菩薩等行德本，彼之德本亦無決矣。
jiǎ shǐ pú sà děng xíng dé běn， bǐ zhī dé běn yì wú jué yǐ。
Even if a bodhisattva practices roots of virtue impartially, those roots too receive no prediction.
諸法相空，虛空無決，
zhū fǎ xiàng kōng， xū kōng wú jué，
The marks of dharmas are empty, and empty space receives no prediction.
一切諸法無想無願、無為無數，
yī qiè zhū fǎ wú xiǎng wú yuàn、 wú wéi wú shù，
All dharmas are signless, wishless, unconditioned and beyond number,
不授無想無願、無為無數之決也。」
bù shòu wú xiǎng wú yuàn、 wú wéi wú shù zhī jué yě。」
and no prediction is bestowed upon the signless, the wishless, the unconditioned and the numberless.”
龍王復謂王阿闍世：
lóng wáng fù wèi wáng ā dū shì：
The Dragon King said further to King Ajatasatru:
「諸佛大哀不可思議！
「zhū fó dà āi bù kě sī yì！
“The great compassion of the buddhas is inconceivable!
諸經無名，無有思想，而說名想。
zhū jīng wú míng， wú yǒu sī xiǎng， ér shuō míng xiǎng。
The sutras have no name and no conceiving, yet name and notion are spoken.
諸佛、世尊無名相識，
zhū fó、 shì zūn wú míng xiāng shí，
The buddhas, the World-Honored Ones, have no consciousness of name and mark,
而隨習俗因而示現有授決也！
ér suí xí sú yīn ér shì xiàn yǒu shòu jué yě！
yet in accord with convention they display the bestowing of predictions.
彼亦無法受決，
bǐ yì wú fǎ shòu jué，
There is no dharma there that receives a prediction,
亦無內外當受決者。」
yì wú nèi wài dāng shòu jué zhě。」
and neither within nor without is there one who should receive it.”
阿闍世又問龍王言：
ā dū shì yòu wèn lóng wáng yán：
Ajatasatru asked the Dragon King again:
「以得法忍逮平等行，菩薩如是乃得決乎？」
「yǐ dé fǎ rěn dǎi píng děng xíng， pú sà rú shì nǎi dé jué hū？」
“When he has gained forbearance of dharmas and attained the practice of equality, does a bodhisattva then receive the prediction?”
海龍王曰：「其忍悉空，想不可盡；
hǎi lóng wáng yuē： 「qí rěn xī kōng， xiǎng bù kě jǐn；
The Sea Dragon King said: “That forbearance is wholly empty, and notions cannot be exhausted.
究竟曉了至于本際，無盡之際；
jiū jìng xiǎo liǎo zhì yú běn jì， wú jǐn zhī jì；
Fathoming it fully he reaches the original limit, the limit of the inexhaustible;
平等之際，無我之際；
píng děng zhī jì， wú wǒ zhī jì；
the limit of equality, the limit of no-self;
吾我之際，審諦之際；
wú wǒ zhī jì， shěn dì zhī jì；
the limit of self, the limit of true discernment;
至于究竟，無成就際。
zhì yú jiū jìng， wú chéng jiù jì。
and reaching the ultimate, the limit where nothing is accomplished.
其際以空至于脫際，
qí jì yǐ kōng zhì yú tuō jì，
That limit, being empty, reaches the limit of liberation;
婬怒癡際有了是際，則無所猗。
yín nù chī jì yǒu le shì jì， zé wú suǒ yī。
and understanding that the limits of lust, anger and folly are this same limit, he leans on nothing.
無所猗者，設於音聲無合會；
wú suǒ yī zhě， shè yú yīn shēng wú hé huì；
Leaning on nothing, he does not join with sound;
無合會者，不著無脫。
wú hé huì zhě， bù zhuó wú tuō。
not joining, he neither clings nor is released.
無脫無行，行無所行，
wú tuō wú xíng， xíng wú suǒ xíng，
Without release there is no practice; practicing, he practices nothing,
亦無不行，亦無所憂。
yì wú bù xíng， yì wú suǒ yōu。
yet does not fail to practice, and has nothing to grieve over.
已無所憂，於斯菩薩則都一切諸所作行，
yǐ wú suǒ yōu， yú sī pú sà zé dōu yī qiè zhū suǒ zuò xíng，
Having nothing to grieve over, in all his practices
所見無見。
suǒ jiàn wú jiàn。
such a bodhisattva sees nothing.
假使無所見，不作審諦，而已平等。
jiǎ shǐ wú suǒ jiàn， bù zuò shěn dì， ér yǐ píng děng。
Seeing nothing, he does not fabricate true discernment, and is thereby equal.
入已、所住已平等度，
rù yǐ、 suǒ zhù yǐ píng děng dù，
Having entered and abided, he crosses over in equality,
不殘不禮，不卒不暴，
bù cán bù lǐ， bù zú bù bào，
neither cruel nor ceremonious, neither hasty nor violent,
以等諸法然後得忍。
yǐ děng zhū fǎ rán hòu dé rěn。
and holding dharmas equal he then gains forbearance.
彼所謂忍及所授決，
bǐ suǒ wèi rěn jí suǒ shòu jué，
That which is called forbearance, and the prediction bestowed,
若受決已及受決者，
ruò shòu jué yǐ jí shòu jué zhě，
and the one who has received it and the one who receives it —
是一切法皆平等法乎。
shì yī qiè fǎ jiē píng děng fǎ hū。
are not all these dharmas dharmas of equality?
諸法界究竟無法界，
zhū fǎ jiè jiū jìng wú fǎ jiè，
Fathomed to the end, the dharma-realm of dharmas is no dharma-realm;
無不以決了亦無所成，
wú bù yǐ jué le yì wú suǒ chéng，
there is nothing not resolved by it, and nothing accomplished.
觀此法已，察其義理，
guān cǐ fǎ yǐ， chá qí yì lǐ，
Having contemplated this Dharma and examined its meaning,
諸法無能計者，
zhū fǎ wú néng jì zhě，
no one can reckon dharmas;
譬如虛空不可計數以度諸數，
pì rú xū kōng bù kě jì shù yǐ dù zhū shù，
like empty space, they cannot be counted, and pass beyond all number.
諸法如是。」
zhū fǎ rú shì。」
Such are dharmas.”
海龍王說是語時，二萬菩薩得不起法忍，
hǎi lóng wáng shuō shì yǔ shí， èr wàn pú sà dé bù qǐ fǎ rěn，
When the Sea Dragon King said this, twenty thousand bodhisattvas attained the forbearance of the non-arising of dharmas,
百比丘漏盡意解。
bǎi bǐ qiū lòu jǐn yì jiě。
and a hundred bhiksus exhausted the outflows and their minds were released.
爾時王阿闍世白佛：「至未曾有。世尊！龍王之辯。」
ěr shí wáng ā dū shì bái fó： 「zhì wèi céng yǒu。 shì zūn！ lóng wáng zhī biàn。」
Then King Ajatasatru said to the Buddha: “Unprecedented, World-Honored One, is the Dragon King's eloquence!”
佛言：「未足為怪，不以為難！
fó yán： 「wèi zú wéi guài， bù yǐ wéi nán！
The Buddha said: “That is no great wonder, and no hard thing.
新發意者聞是不恐、不難、不畏，是乃為難。
xīn fā yì zhě wén shì bù kǒng、 bù nán、 bù wèi， shì nǎi wéi nán。
That one newly aroused should hear this without alarm, without dismay, without fear — that is the hard thing.
所以者何？諸佛、世尊本之道法，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ zhū fó、 shì zūn běn zhī dào fǎ，
Why? The original dharma of the Way of the buddhas, the World-Honored Ones,
如是難及，少有信者。
rú shì nán jí， shǎo yǒu xìn zhě。
is thus hard to reach, and few have faith in it.
天上、世間人不能受，不入不信而不喜樂。
tiān shàng、 shì jiān rén bù néng shòu， bù rù bù xìn ér bù xǐ lè。
Devas and people of the world cannot receive it; they do not enter it, do not believe, and take no joy in it.
以故當知其有聞是深經法者，
yǐ gù dāng zhī qí yǒu wén shì shēn jīng fǎ zhě，
Therefore know that one who hears this profound Dharma
不以恐畏，此乃為難。
bù yǐ kǒng wèi， cǐ nǎi wéi nán。
and is not afraid — this is the hard thing.
前世供養無央數佛，安以為難？
qián shì gòng yǎng wú yāng shù fó， ān yǐ wéi nán？
Having made offerings to countless buddhas in former lives, how should it be hard for him?
譬如有人，虛空無形而現形像，是為難不？」
pì rú yǒu rén， xū kōng wú xíng ér xiàn xíng xiàng， shì wéi nán bù？」
Suppose a man made forms appear in formless space — would that not be hard?”
對曰：「甚難！天中天！」
duì yuē： 「shèn nán！ tiān zhōng tiān！」
He replied: “Very hard, Deva among devas!”
佛言：「其聞此經深奧之義，一切信樂，
fó yán： 「qí wén cǐ jīng shēn ào zhī yì， yī qiè xìn lè，
The Buddha said: “Those who hear the profound meaning of this sutra and believe and delight in it entirely,
皆知無我、無人、無壽、無命。
jiē zhī wú wǒ、 wú rén、 wú shòu、 wú mìng。
knowing that there is no self, no person, no lifespan, no living being —
其有信者、甫當信者，
qí yǒu xìn zhě、 fǔ dāng xìn zhě，
those who believe and those about to believe —
此等則是如來順明平夷之類親友善師也。
cǐ děng zé shì rú lái shùn míng píng yí zhī lèi qīn yǒu shàn shī yě。
such are the Tathagata's own kin in clear accord and level plainness, his friends and good teachers.
則能堪任信除垢塵，
zé néng kān rèn xìn chú gòu chén，
They are able to bear it, and by faith remove defilement,
為一切人講說經法，
wèi yī qiè rén jiǎng shuō jīng fǎ，
expound the Dharma of the sutras for all,
能詣道場以慈降伏百千億魔及餘官屬，
néng yì dào chǎng yǐ cí xiáng fú bǎi qiān yì mó jí yú guān shǔ，
reach the bodhimanda (place of practice) and by loving-kindness subdue a hundred thousand million maras and their retinues,
為於諸法而得自在。
wéi yú zhū fǎ ér dé zì zài。
and gain mastery in all dharmas.
其心清淨，諸通之慧近在目前；
qí xīn qīng jìng， zhū tōng zhī huì jìn zài mù qián；
Their minds pure, the wisdom of the penetrations stands near before them;
發意之頃成智慧明，逮最正覺；
fā yì zhī qǐng chéng zhì huì míng， dǎi zuì zhèng jué；
in the instant of a thought they perfect the brightness of wisdom and attain supreme awakening;
勸訹眾生知一切心群萌諸根，
quàn xù zhòng shēng zhī yī qiè xīn qún méng zhū gēn，
they exhort beings, knowing every mind and all the faculties of the multitudes,
而轉無上大法輪也；
ér zhuǎn wú shàng dà fǎ lún yě；
and turn the unsurpassed great wheel of Dharma;
療一切病，開化異道，
liáo yī qiè bìng， kāi huà yì dào，
they heal every illness, convert the heterodox,
破壞怨敵，吹大法䗍。」
pò huài yuàn dí， chuī dà fǎ lǐ。」
shatter their adversaries, and blow the great conch of Dharma.”
於是海龍王所願皆獲，不失本志，
yú shì hǎi lóng wáng suǒ yuàn jiē huò， bù shī běn zhì，
Thereupon the Sea Dragon King, having obtained all he wished and not lost his original resolve,
聞所受決，欣然大悅，善心生焉，
wén suǒ shòu jué， xīn rán dà yuè， shàn xīn shēng yān，
hearing the prediction he had received, rejoiced greatly and a wholesome mind arose in him;
踊在虛空以偈讚佛：
yǒng zài xū kōng yǐ jì zàn fó：
he rose into the sky and praised the Buddha in verse:
「如虛空本清淨，無色無受無數，
「rú xū kōng běn qīng jìng， wú sè wú shòu wú shù，
“As space is originally pure, formless, without sensation, beyond number,
安住說法如是，若虛空普自然，
ān zhù shuō fǎ rú shì， ruò xū kōng pǔ zì rán，
so the Steadfast One preaches the Dharma, universal and self-so as space.
名不有而不無，造因緣及報應，
míng bù yǒu ér bù wú， zào yīn yuán jí bào yìng，
Name is neither existent nor non-existent, making causes, conditions and recompense;
安住講調不諍，無人命無壽識。
ān zhù jiǎng diào bù zhēng， wú rén mìng wú shòu shí。
the Steadfast One expounds harmony without dispute — no person, no living being, no lifespan, no consciousness.
一切法甚清淨，吾我清悉平等，
yī qiè fǎ shèn qīng jìng， wú wǒ qīng xī píng děng，
All dharmas are utterly pure; self is pure and wholly equal;
吾我靜法等靜，解是者則受決。
wú wǒ jìng fǎ děng jìng， jiě shì zhě zé shòu jué。
self is still and dharmas equally still — one who understands this receives the prediction.
察法界本清淨，人界靜亦如是，
chá fǎ jiè běn qīng jìng， rén jiè jìng yì rú shì，
Examine the dharma-realm, originally pure; the realm of persons is still likewise;
如眾生界靜者，佛法靜亦復然。
rú zhòng shēng jiè jìng zhě， fó fǎ jìng yì fù rán。
as the realm of beings is still, so too is the Buddha's Dharma.
若佛法清淨者，諸佛土靜亦等，
ruò fó fǎ qīng jìng zhě， zhū fó tǔ jìng yì děng，
If the Buddha's Dharma is pure, the buddha-lands are equally still;
設佛土清淨者，諸慧靜無差特。
shè fó tǔ qīng jìng zhě， zhū huì jìng wú chā tè。
if the buddha-lands are pure, all wisdom is still without distinction.
諸法淨因數號，以計數不得名，
zhū fǎ jìng yīn shù hào， yǐ jì shù bù dé míng，
Dharmas are pure, their designations from number; reckoned by number, no name is found;
由想數名本空，其諸名數無礙。
yóu xiǎng shù míng běn kōng， qí zhū míng shù wú ài。
since notion, number and name are originally empty, all names and numbers are unobstructed.
群萌類志所念，無見無色無成，
qún méng lèi zhì suǒ niàn， wú jiàn wú sè wú chéng，
What the multitudes intend and conceive has no seeing, no form, no accomplishing;
如無成心意識，諸法爾空無心。
rú wú chéng xīn yì shí， zhū fǎ ěr kōng wú xīn。
as mind, thought and consciousness accomplish nothing, so dharmas are empty and mindless.
或有作則無作，有緣罪無受者，
huò yǒu zuò zé wú zuò， yǒu yuán zuì wú shòu zhě，
Where there is doing there is no doer; where conditions bring sin there is none who receives it;
終始瑕無行者，名泥洹無寂然。
zhōng shǐ xiá wú xíng zhě， míng ní huán wú jì rán。
in the flaw of birth and death there is no one who practices; nirvana is named, yet there is no stillness.
入本際無礙原，虛空本無我際，
rù běn jì wú ài yuán， xū kōng běn wú wǒ jì，
Entering the original limit, the unobstructed source; space is originally the limit of no-self;
選諸際得等原，知眾生本審門。
xuǎn zhū jì dé děng yuán， zhī zhòng shēng běn shěn mén。
choosing among the limits he finds the equal source, and knows the true gate of beings' origin.
去來今現在際，順明智此諸際，
qù lái jīn xiàn zài jì， shùn míng zhì cǐ zhū jì，
The limits of past, future and present — clear wisdom accords with all these limits;
於慧等無際門，究法界佛種性。
yú huì děng wú jì mén， jiū fǎ jiè fó zhǒng xìng。
in wisdom, equal and without a gate of limits, he fathoms the dharma-realm, the buddha-nature.
不起滅最勝幢，空無想願本靜，
bù qǐ miè zuì shèng chuáng， kōng wú xiǎng yuàn běn jìng，
Neither arising nor ceasing, the most victorious banner; emptiness, signlessness and wishlessness originally still;
無言聲真諦法，是諸聖寂寞地。
wú yán shēng zhēn dì fǎ， shì zhū shèng jì mò dì。
the true dharma without word or sound — this is the quiescent ground of the sages.
若解諸法澹泊，自然悅如捉空，
ruò jiě zhū fǎ dàn pō， zì rán yuè rú zhuō kōng，
If one understands dharmas as tranquil, self-so and glad as grasping space,
彼無吾我身寂，假如此樂聖法。
bǐ wú wú wǒ shēn jì， jiǎ rú cǐ lè shèng fǎ。
therein without self the body is stilled — such is the joy of the holy Dharma.
十方佛所說法，當來佛所宣揚，
shí fāng fó suǒ shuō fǎ， dāng lái fó suǒ xuān yáng，
The Dharma preached by the buddhas of the ten directions, proclaimed by the buddhas to come,
一切等未來聲，因音現入無聲。
yī qiè děng wèi lái shēng， yīn yīn xiàn rù wú shēng。
all equal to the sound of the future — through sound it appears and enters soundlessness.
聲空自然如響，諸法空猶虛念，
shēng kōng zì rán rú xiǎng， zhū fǎ kōng yóu xū niàn，
Sound is empty and self-so as an echo; dharmas are empty as an idle thought;
無法非法教化，誠本無可不得。
wú fǎ fēi fǎ jiào huà， chéng běn wú kě bù dé。
neither dharma nor non-dharma teaches and transforms — truly, original nonbeing cannot be got.
一切法無主名，若干想念非明，
yī qiè fǎ wú zhǔ míng， ruò gān xiǎng niàn fēi míng，
No dharma has an owner or a name; manifold notions are not clarity;
人名靜不可得，諸法本靜如是。」
rén míng jìng bù kě dé， zhū fǎ běn jìng rú shì。」
the name of a person is still and cannot be found — such is the original stillness of dharmas.”
囑累受持品第二十
zhǔ lèi shòu chí pǐn dì èr shí
Chapter Twenty: Entrusting and Upholding
於是世尊告諸大士言：「諸正士！
yú shì shì zūn gào zhū dà shì yán： 「zhū zhèng shì！
Thereupon the World-Honored One said to the great beings: “Right-minded ones!
汝等當持此如來說無上正真道，使得久存。
rǔ děng dāng chí cǐ rú lái shuō wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào， shǐ de jiǔ cún。
You should uphold this unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment which the Tathagata has preached, and make it long endure.
誰能堪任受持誦說如是像比？」
shuí néng kān rèn shòu chí sòng shuō rú shì xiàng bǐ？」
Who is able to receive, uphold, recite and preach a text such as this?”
即時二萬菩薩、萬天子起住佛前，
jí shí èr wàn pú sà、 wàn tiān zǐ qǐ zhù fó qián，
At once twenty thousand bodhisattvas and ten thousand devaputras rose and stood before the Buddha,
同音白世尊曰：
tóng yīn bái shì zūn yuē：
saying with one voice:
「吾等當受如是像經，當令流布，普周遠近。」
「wú děng dāng shòu rú shì xiàng jīng， dāng lìng liú bù， pǔ zhōu yuǎn jìn。」
“We shall receive a sutra such as this and cause it to circulate everywhere, far and near.”
佛又問言：「汝等云何而將御法，
fó yòu wèn yán： 「rǔ děng yún hé ér jiāng yù fǎ，
The Buddha asked again: “And how will you guide and guard the Dharma,
持斯如來無上正真道乎？」
chí sī rú lái wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào hū？」
upholding this unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment of the Tathagata?”
彼有菩薩名曰慧英幢，前白佛言：
bǐ yǒu pú sà míng yuē huì yīng chuáng， qián bái fó yán：
There was a bodhisattva named Wisdom Blossom Banner, who said:
「唯然，世尊！隱省諸法都無所持，
「wéi rán， shì zūn！ yǐn shěng zhū fǎ dōu wú suǒ chí，
“So be it, World-Honored One. Examining dharmas closely, there is nothing at all to uphold;
寧能髣髴持佛道乎？」
níng néng fǎng fú chí fó dào hū？」
how then could one even seem to uphold the buddha path?”
世尊答曰：「族姓子！如斯則應持佛道。」
shì zūn dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ rú sī zé yīng chí fó dào。」
The World-Honored One answered: “Son of good family, just so does one rightly uphold the buddha path.”
等見菩薩白佛言：
děng jiàn pú sà bái fó yán：
The bodhisattva Equal Sight said:
「揆察佛道等乎五逆，寧少髣髴持佛道乎？」
「kuí chá fó dào děng hū wǔ nì， níng shǎo fǎng fú chí fó dào hū？」
“Weighing the buddha path as equal to the five heinous acts — could one thus even faintly seem to uphold it?”
世尊曰：「族姓子！
shì zūn yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！
The World-Honored One said: “Son of good family,
是故仁者則應持如來佛大道也。」
shì gù rén zhě zé yīng chí rú lái fó dà dào yě。」
therefore you rightly uphold the Tathagata's great buddha path.”
無見菩薩曰：「唯然，世尊！我不見凡夫法，
wú jiàn pú sà yuē： 「wéi rán， shì zūn！ wǒ bù jiàn fán fū fǎ，
The bodhisattva No Seeing said: “So be it, World-Honored One. I do not see the dharma of ordinary beings,
亦不見學、不學法，
yì bù jiàn xué、 bù xué fǎ，
nor the dharma of the learner or of one beyond learning,
不見緣覺及菩薩法，亦不見佛法，
bù jiàn yuán jué jí pú sà fǎ， yì bù jiàn fó fǎ，
nor the dharma of pratyekabuddhas or bodhisattvas, nor the Buddha's Dharma.
我有持如來法乎？」
wǒ yǒu chí rú lái fǎ hū？」
Do I then uphold the Tathagata's Dharma?”
世尊曰：「族姓子！以是寂故，
shì zūn yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ yǐ shì jì gù，
The World-Honored One said: “Son of good family, because of that stillness,
應持如來佛大道也。」
yīng chí rú lái fó dà dào yě。」
you rightly uphold the Tathagata's great buddha path.”
諸法無所願菩薩曰：「唯，世尊！
zhū fǎ wú suǒ yuàn pú sà yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The bodhisattva Wishlessness in All Dharmas said: “World-Honored One,
我永不知一切諸法當所持者，
wǒ yǒng bù zhī yī qiè zhū fǎ dāng suǒ chí zhě，
I never know of any dharma at all that should be upheld;
寧能為持如來法乎？」
níng néng wéi chí rú lái fǎ hū？」
how then could I uphold the Tathagata's Dharma?”
世尊曰：「族姓子！以是寂故，
shì zūn yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ yǐ shì jì gù，
The World-Honored One said: “Son of good family, because of that stillness,
應持如來佛大道也。」
yīng chí rú lái fó dà dào yě。」
you rightly uphold the Tathagata's great buddha path.”
不眴菩薩曰：「唯，世尊！我自不行，
bù shùn pú sà yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！ wǒ zì bù xíng，
The bodhisattva Unblinking said: “World-Honored One, I myself do not practice,
亦不遣心，亦不發意，
yì bù qiǎn xīn， yì bù fā yì，
nor send forth the mind, nor rouse a thought.
我寧為持如來法乎？」
wǒ níng wéi chí rú lái fǎ hū？」
Do I then uphold the Tathagata's Dharma?”
世尊曰：「族姓子！以是寂故，
shì zūn yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ yǐ shì jì gù，
The World-Honored One said: “Son of good family, because of that stillness,
應持如來佛大道也。」
yīng chí rú lái fó dà dào yě。」
you rightly uphold the Tathagata's great buddha path.”
無得菩薩曰：「唯，世尊！
wú dé pú sà yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The bodhisattva No Attainment said: “World-Honored One,
其不說法及非法，不演法聲，除諸法想，
qí bù shuō fǎ jí fēi fǎ， bù yǎn fǎ shēng， chú zhū fǎ xiǎng，
one who preaches neither dharma nor non-dharma, sets forth no sound of Dharma, and removes all notions of dharmas —
如是行者為護一切法乎？」
rú shì xíng zhě wéi hù yī qiè fǎ hū？」
does such a practitioner guard all dharmas?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to stillness.”
無在菩薩曰：「唯，世尊！
wú zài pú sà yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The bodhisattva Nowhere Present said: “World-Honored One,
其不聽受法應不應，為護正法乎？」
qí bù tīng shòu fǎ yīng bù yīng， wéi hù zhèng fǎ hū？」
one who does not accept whether a dharma answers or does not answer — does he guard the true Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to stillness.”
虛空藏菩薩曰：「唯，世尊！
xū kōng zàng pú sà yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The bodhisattva Space Treasury said: “World-Honored One,
其觀諸法等如虛空，不見彼法，有所執持，
qí guān zhū fǎ děng rú xū kōng， bù jiàn bǐ fǎ， yǒu suǒ zhí chí，
one who contemplates dharmas as equal to space, sees no such dharma, and yet grasps and holds —
為護一切法乎？」
wéi hù yī qiè fǎ hū？」
does he guard all dharmas?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to stillness.”
度金剛作菩薩曰：「唯，世尊！
dù jīn gāng zuò pú sà yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The bodhisattva Adamantine Deliverance said: “World-Honored One,
不壞法界，入於人界及以法界，
bù huài fǎ jiè， rù yú rén jiè jí yǐ fǎ jiè，
one who does not destroy the dharma-realm but enters the realm of persons and the dharma-realm —
為持正法乎？」
wéi chí zhèng fǎ hū？」
does he uphold the true Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to stillness.”
度不動跡菩薩曰：「唯，世尊！
dù bù dòng jī pú sà yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The bodhisattva Unmoving Trace said: “World-Honored One,
其於諸法無所動轉，不猗法及非法，
qí yú zhū fǎ wú suǒ dòng zhuǎn， bù yī fǎ jí fēi fǎ，
one who in dharmas neither moves nor turns, and leans on neither dharma nor non-dharma —
為持正法乎？」
wéi chí zhèng fǎ hū？」
does he uphold the true Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to stillness.”
嘲魔菩薩曰：「唯，世尊！
cháo mó pú sà yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The bodhisattva Mocking Mara said: “World-Honored One,
其至魔界、佛界，則於佛界及與魔界，
qí zhì mó jiè、 fó jiè， zé yú fó jiè jí yǔ mó jiè，
one who goes to Mara's realm and the Buddha's realm, and in both realms alike
悉以為入諸法境界，為護正法乎？」
xī yǐ wéi rù zhū fǎ jìng jiè， wéi hù zhèng fǎ hū？」
takes them as entering the domain of dharmas — does he guard the true Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to stillness.”
無著菩薩曰：「唯然，世尊！
wú zhuó pú sà yuē： 「wéi rán， shì zūn！
The bodhisattva Unattached said: “So be it, World-Honored One.
其於諸法都無所得，一切毛孔皆出法聲，
qí yú zhū fǎ dōu wú suǒ de， yī qiè máo kǒng jiē chū fǎ shēng，
One who attains nothing at all in dharmas, and from whose every pore issues the sound of Dharma —
為護正法乎？」
wéi hù zhèng fǎ hū？」
does he guard the true Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to stillness.”
普寂菩薩曰：「唯世尊！
pǔ jì pú sà yuē： 「wéi shì zūn！
The bodhisattva Universal Stillness said: “World-Honored One,
不護諸魔，行菩薩道，為持正法乎？」
bù hù zhū mó， xíng pú sà dào， wéi chí zhèng fǎ hū？」
one who does not guard against the maras but practices the bodhisattva path — does he uphold the true Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to stillness.”
海意菩薩曰：「唯，世尊！
hǎi yì pú sà yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The bodhisattva Ocean Mind said: “World-Honored One,
其以海印等一切法，
qí yǐ hǎi yìn děng yī qiè fǎ，
one who by the ocean seal holds all dharmas equal,
修一切解味而知自然，為護法乎？」
xiū yī qiè jiě wèi ér zhī zì rán， wéi hù fǎ hū？」
cultivates the savor of every understanding and knows what is so of itself — does he guard the Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應自然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng zì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to what is so of itself.”
須深天子曰：「唯，世尊！
xū shēn tiān zǐ yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The devaputra Susima said: “World-Honored One,
其有所生不起不生，
qí yǒu suǒ shēng bù qǐ bù shēng，
one for whom whatever is born neither arises nor is born,
諸陰、種、入無心意識，為護正法乎？」
zhū yīn、 zhǒng、 rù wú xīn yì shí， wéi hù zhèng fǎ hū？」
and in whose aggregates, seeds and sense-fields there is no mind, thought or consciousness — does he guard the true Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to stillness.”
無垢光天子曰：「唯，世尊！
wú gòu guāng tiān zǐ yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The devaputra Undefiled Light said: “World-Honored One,
其見諸法無垢塵者，無眾瑕穢，解脫諸受，
qí jiàn zhū fǎ wú gòu chén zhě， wú zhòng xiá huì， jiě tuō zhū shòu，
one who sees dharmas as without defilement, free of every flaw, released from all sensation —
為護法乎？」
wéi hù fǎ hū？」
does he guard the Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to stillness.”
度人天子曰：「唯，世尊！
dù rén tiān zǐ yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The devaputra Deliverer of Men said: “World-Honored One,
其度眾生不知萌類度無所度，
qí dù zhòng shēng bù zhī méng lèi dù wú suǒ dù，
one who delivers beings without knowing the multitudes, delivering yet delivering nothing,
既有所度現周旋還不住彼此，為受法乎？」
jì yǒu suǒ dù xiàn zhōu xuán hái bú zhù bǐ cǐ， wéi shòu fǎ hū？」
and having delivered them turns about and abides in neither there nor here — does he receive the Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to stillness.”
賢王天子曰：「唯然，世尊！
xián wáng tiān zǐ yuē： 「wéi rán， shì zūn！
The devaputra Worthy King said: “So be it, World-Honored One.
其於眾生而等一切，諸法已等，
qí yú zhòng shēng ér děng yī qiè， zhū fǎ yǐ děng，
One who holds all beings equal, and dharmas already equal,
等諸國土，等諸佛道，為受正法乎？」
děng zhū guó tǔ， děng zhū fó dào， wéi shòu zhèng fǎ hū？」
the lands equal and the buddha paths equal — does he receive the true Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to stillness.”
自在天子曰：「唯世尊！
zì zài tiān zǐ yuē： 「wéi shì zūn！
The devaputra Sovereign said: “World-Honored One,
其於諸法而得自在，普於諸法不起不滅，
qí yú zhū fǎ ér dé zì zài， pǔ yú zhū fǎ bù qǐ bù miè，
one who gains mastery in dharmas, and in all dharmas neither arises nor ceases —
為受正法乎？」
wéi shòu zhèng fǎ hū？」
does he receive the true Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to stillness.”
