善念天子曰：「唯，世尊！
shàn niàn tiān zǐ yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The devaputra Good Mindfulness said: “World-Honored One,
我不念法亦無所得，亦不有想，為受正法乎？」
wǒ bù niàn fǎ yì wú suǒ de， yì bù yǒu xiǎng， wéi shòu zhèng fǎ hū？」
I do not conceive of dharmas, attain nothing, and hold no notion — do I receive the true Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓子！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng zǐ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Son of good family, that answers to stillness.”
蓮華天女曰：「唯，世尊！
lián huā tiān nǚ yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The goddess Lotus said: “World-Honored One,
知一切法皆為佛法，不成正覺，無所不覺，
zhī yī qiè fǎ jiē wèi fó fǎ， bù chéng zhèng jué， wú suǒ bù jué，
to know that all dharmas are the Buddha's Dharma, accomplishing no right awakening yet awakening to everything —
為受正法乎？」
wéi shòu zhèng fǎ hū？」
is that receiving the true Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓女！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng nǚ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Daughter of good family, that answers to stillness.”
麻油上天女曰：「唯，世尊！
má yóu shàng tiān nǚ yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The goddess Supreme Sesame Oil said: “World-Honored One,
我不得女亦不得男，
wǒ bù dé nǚ yì bù dé nán，
I apprehend neither woman nor man;
如佛法想及男女法想，亦復同等，
rú fó fǎ xiǎng jí nán nǚ fǎ xiǎng， yì fù tóng děng，
the notion of the Buddha's Dharma and the notion of male and female dharmas are alike equal;
此諸法想則為非法，亦非無法，
cǐ zhū fǎ xiǎng zé wéi fēi fǎ， yì fēi wú fǎ，
these notions of dharmas are not dharma, yet not non-dharma,
無二無一亦無所至，我為受法乎？」
wú èr wú yī yì wú suǒ zhì， wǒ wéi shòu fǎ hū？」
neither two nor one and going nowhere — do I receive the Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓女！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng nǚ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Daughter of good family, that answers to stillness.”
寶女曰：「唯，世尊！我不見佛、佛道，
bǎo nǚ yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！ wǒ bù jiàn fó、 fó dào，
The girl Jewel said: “World-Honored One, I see neither Buddha nor buddha path,
覩菩薩行，為被一切志德之鎧，
dǔ pú sà xíng， wéi bèi yī qiè zhì dé zhī kǎi，
yet beholding the bodhisattva's conduct I put on the armor of every resolve and virtue,
不察於本末，為受法乎？」
bù chá yú běn mò， wéi shòu fǎ hū？」
without examining beginning or end — do I receive the Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓女！是應寂然。」
dá yuē： 「zú xìng nǚ！ shì yìng jì rán。」
He answered: “Daughter of good family, that answers to stillness.”
無垢光女曰：「唯，世尊！
wú gòu guāng nǚ yuē： 「wéi， shì zūn！
The girl Undefiled Light said: “World-Honored One,
於一切法不起法想，於一切人不起人想，
yú yī qiè fǎ bù qǐ fǎ xiǎng， yú yī qiè rén bù qǐ rén xiǎng，
in all dharmas I raise no notion of dharma, and in all persons no notion of person;
亦不想念人法佛法，
yì bù xiǎng niàn rén fǎ fó fǎ，
I do not conceive of the dharma of persons or the Dharma of buddhas;
覩諸佛法入一切法，不見本末，
dǔ zhū fó fǎ rù yī qiè fǎ， bù jiàn běn mò，
beholding the buddhas' Dharma I enter all dharmas, seeing neither beginning nor end —
我為受佛正法乎？」
wǒ wéi shòu fó zhèng fǎ hū？」
do I receive the Buddha's true Dharma?”
答曰：「族姓女！是應寂然。
dá yuē： 「zú xìng nǚ！ shì yìng jì rán。
He answered: “Daughter of good family, that answers to stillness.
建行如是，為受佛法。」
jiàn xíng rú shì， wéi shòu fó fǎ。」
To establish such a practice is to receive the Buddha's Dharma.”
於是天帝釋白佛言：「未曾有也。世尊！
yú shì tiān dì shì bái fó yán： 「wèi céng yǒu yě。 shì zūn！
Thereupon Sakra said to the Buddha: “Unprecedented, World-Honored One!
此女人等所說辯才不可思議，分別方便；
cǐ nǚ rén děng suǒ shuō biàn cái bù kě sī yì， fēn bié fāng biàn；
The eloquence these women have spoken is inconceivable, discerning in skillful means;
合若干音、文字之說，講于法界；
hé ruò gān yīn、 wén zì zhī shuō， jiǎng yú fǎ jiè；
joining sounds and letters of many kinds, they expound the dharma-realm;
不亂諸法，諸法平等；
bù luàn zhū fǎ， zhū fǎ píng děng；
they do not confuse dharmas, and dharmas are equal;
如演說道，等不差特。」
rú yǎn shuō dào， děng bù chà tè。」
as they set forth the Way, it is equal and without distinction.”
佛言：「如是，拘翼！
fó yán： 「rú shì， jū yì！
The Buddha said: “Just so, Kausika!
是諸女等分別無量不可思議法，
shì zhū nǚ děng fēn bié wú liàng bù kě sī yì fǎ，
These women discern immeasurable and inconceivable dharmas;
供養奉事不可計佛，以得法忍。
gòng yǎng fèng shì bù kě jì fó， yǐ dé fǎ rěn。
they have made offerings and served incalculable buddhas, and so gained forbearance of dharmas.
又，拘翼！是經卷者，
yòu， jū yì！ shì jīng juǎn zhě，
Moreover, Kausika, this scripture
號『不起忍持無所御』，
hào 『bù qǐ rěn chí wú suǒ yù』，
is named ‘The Forbearance of Non-Arising, Upheld and Unguided’;
當為眾會廣說其義。
dāng wéi zhòng huì guǎng shuō qí yì。
you should expound its meaning widely for the assembly.
如來滅度後受是法者，護持法城，
rú lái miè dù hòu shòu shì fǎ zhě， hù chí fǎ chéng，
Whoever receives this Dharma after the Tathagata's final quiescence guards the citadel of Dharma,
則為供養佛、世尊已。」
zé wéi gòng yǎng fó、 shì zūn yǐ。」
and has thereby made offering to the Buddha, the World-Honored One.”
天帝釋白佛：「我已奉受此經本已，
tiān dì shì bái fó： 「wǒ yǐ fèng shòu cǐ jīng běn yǐ，
Sakra said to the Buddha: “I have already received this scripture,
佛所建立當令廣普，
fó suǒ jiàn lì dāng lìng guǎng pǔ，
and what the Buddha has established I shall cause to spread everywhere;
當為將來諸大士故，分別說之，
dāng wéi jiāng lái zhū dà shì gù， fēn bié shuō zhī，
for the sake of the great beings to come I shall expound and discern it,
終不迷謬違佛之教。
zhōng bù mí miù wéi fó zhī jiào。
never straying or transgressing the Buddha's teaching.
所以者何？如來護我授以法忍，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ rú lái hù wǒ shòu yǐ fǎ rěn，
Why? The Tathagata has guarded me and bestowed forbearance of dharmas;
佛當建立此之經典，令降眾魔，志行於斯。」
fó dāng jiàn lì cǐ zhī jīng diǎn， lìng jiàng zhòng mó， zhì xíng yú sī。」
and the Buddha will establish this scripture, subduing the hosts of Mara — herein is my resolve and practice.”
佛言拘翼：「有神呪，名曰遮諸妨礙。
fó yán jū yì： 「yǒu shén zhòu， míng yuē zhē zhū fáng ài。
The Buddha said: “Kausika, there is a spell named Barring All Hindrance.
具聽！今為汝說神呪要言，
jù tīng！ jīn wéi rǔ shuō shén zhòu yào yán，
Listen fully: I shall now speak for you the essential words of the spell,
使一切魔及諸外徑令諸官屬，自然降伏，
shǐ yī qiè mó jí zhū wài jìng lìng zhū guān shǔ， zì rán xiáng fú，
so that all maras, the heterodox paths and their retinues may be subdued of themselves,
使如來法光明久立。」
shǐ rú lái fǎ guāng míng jiǔ lì。」
and the light of the Tathagata's Dharma long endure.”
爾時世尊說神呪曰：
ěr shí shì zūn shuō shén zhòu yuē：
Then the World-Honored One spoke the spell:
「無畏　離畏　淨諸恐懼　施無畏
「wú wèi 　 lí wèi 　 jìng zhū kǒng jù 　 shī wú wèi
“Fearless · Free of Fear · Purifying All Dread · Bestowing Fearlessness
度於滅度無所亂　淨所亂　無所諍
dù yú miè dù wú suǒ luàn 　 jìng suǒ luàn 　 wú suǒ zhèng
Crossing to Quiescence Undisturbed · Purifying Disturbance · Without Dispute
不鬪訟　無懷瞋　無以歿　淨威神
bù dòu sòng 　 wú huái chēn 　 wú yǐ mò 　 jìng wēi shén
Without Quarrel · Harboring No Anger · Not Perishing · Pure Majesty
威神跡　大威神寂滅　趣慈心
wēi shén jī 　 dà wēi shén jì miè 　 qù cí xīn
Trace of Majesty · Great Majesty Quiescent · Tending to Loving-Kindness
除於瑕　示現諦　無蹉踖　其同義
chú yú xiá 　 shì xiàn dì 　 wú cuō jí 　 qí tóng yì
Removing Flaw · Revealing Truth · Without Stumbling · Of One Meaning
吉祥義　甘露句　見於要　以導御
jí xiáng yì 　 gān lù jù 　 jiàn yú yào 　 yǐ dǎo yù
Auspicious Meaning · Phrase of Sweet Dew · Seeing the Essential · Guiding
無所懷　行次第　無所盡　光無生
wú suǒ huái 　 xíng cì dì 　 wú suǒ jǐn 　 guāng wú shēng
Harboring Nothing · Practicing in Order · Never Exhausted · Light Unborn
清淨生　鮮潔光照句　等順於等心
qīng jìng shēng 　 xiān jié guāng zhào jù 　 děng shùn yú děng xīn
Purely Born · Bright Clean Illumining Phrase · Equal and Accordant in Equal Mind
至無上　佛所建立戒清淨　無所犯
zhì wú shàng 　 fó suǒ jiàn lì jiè qīng jìng 　 wú suǒ fàn
Reaching the Unsurpassed · Precepts Pure as the Buddha Established · Without Transgression
無所負　制魔場　降外徑
wú suǒ fù 　 zhì mó chǎng 　 jiàng wài jìng
Owing Nothing · Mastering Mara's Field · Subduing the Heterodox
光耀法明　攝以法施　開法藏
guāng yào fǎ míng 　 shè yǐ fǎ shī 　 kāi fǎ zàng
Radiant Dharma Brightness · Gathering by the Gift of Dharma · Opening the Dharma Treasury
「今是神呪，過去、當來、今現在佛所說，
「jīn shì shén zhòu， guò qù、 dāng lái、 jīn xiàn zài fó suǒ shuō，
“This spell is spoken by the buddhas of past, future and present,
以擁護法而順句義，
yǐ yōng hù fǎ ér shùn jù yì，
to shelter the Dharma and accord with the meaning of its phrases;
以此章句總執降伏一切諸魔塵勞跡也！」
yǐ cǐ zhāng jù zǒng zhí xiáng fú yī qiè zhū mó chén láo jī yě！」
by these chapters and phrases one wholly grasps and subdues every trace of Mara and defilement.”
於是普首天，白佛言：
yú shì pǔ shǒu tiān， bái fó yán：
Thereupon the deva Universal Crest said to the Buddha:
「我當受此經典之要，精進諷誦，當令廣普！
「wǒ dāng shòu cǐ jīng diǎn zhī yào， jīng jìn fěng sòng， dāng lìng guǎng pǔ！
“I shall receive the essentials of this scripture, recite it with vigor, and cause it to spread everywhere!
所以者何？於如來法則有反復，
suǒ yǐ zhě hé？ yú rú lái fǎ zé yǒu fǎn fù，
Why? Toward the Tathagata's Dharma there is a debt to be repaid;
長益法律，布清白典。
cháng yì fǎ lǜ， bù qīng bái diǎn。
I shall increase the Dharma and discipline and spread the pure white canon.
我修反覆，擁護如來所齎法目。」
wǒ xiū fǎn fù， yōng hù rú lái suǒ jī fǎ mù。」
Cultivating that requital, I shall shelter the eye of Dharma the Tathagata has brought.”
時有天子，名曰德超，白佛言：
shí yǒu tiān zǐ， míng yuē dé chāo， bái fó yán：
Then a devaputra named Surpassing Virtue said to the Buddha:
「若有受此如來之法，其福云何？」
「ruò yǒu shòu cǐ rú lái zhī fǎ， qí fú yún hé？」
“If one receives this Dharma of the Tathagata, what is the merit?”
於是世尊即說頌曰：
yú shì shì zūn jí shuō sòng yuē：
Thereupon the World-Honored One spoke in verse:
「今吾所見國，佛眼覩十方，
「jīn wú suǒ jiàn guó， fó yǎn dǔ shí fāng，
“The lands I now behold, seen with a buddha's eye through the ten directions,
皆滿中珍寶，則以持布施，
jiē mǎn zhōng zhēn bǎo， zé yǐ chí bù shī，
were they all filled with treasure and given away in charity,
其人所獲福，計當過於彼。
qí rén suǒ huò fú， jì dāng guò yú bǐ。
the merit gained by that person would still be surpassed by this.
至心受世吼，所說之經法，
zhì xīn shòu shì hǒu， suǒ shuō zhī jīng fǎ，
To receive with utmost sincerity the Dharma preached by the Roar of the World —
合集諸譬喻，一切歎廣說，
hé jí zhū pì yù， yī qiè tàn guǎng shuō，
though one gathered every parable and extolled it at length,
終不能究竟，總持正法德。」
zhōng bù néng jiū jìng， zǒng chí zhèng fǎ dé。」
one could never exhaust the virtue of upholding the true Dharma.”
佛說是經時，七十六那術人
fó shuō shì jīng shí， qī shí liù nà shù rén
When the Buddha preached this sutra, seventy-six nayutas of people
皆發無上正真道意，
jiē fā wú shàng zhèng zhēn dào yì，
aroused the aspiration for unsurpassed, right and true enlightenment,
六萬菩薩得不起法忍，
liù wàn pú sà dé bù qǐ fǎ rěn，
sixty thousand bodhisattvas attained the forbearance of the non-arising of dharmas,
三千大千世界六返震動，
sān qiān dà qiān shì jiè liù fǎn zhèn dòng，
the three-thousandfold great-thousand world quaked in six ways,
其大光明普照世間，而雨天華，
qí dà guāng míng pǔ zhào shì jiān， ér yǔ tiān huā，
a great radiance illumined the world, celestial flowers rained down,
百千伎樂不鼓自鳴，
bǎi qiān jì yuè bù gǔ zì míng，
and a hundred thousand strains of music sounded unstruck,
諸伎樂音出是輩聲：
zhū jì yuè yīn chū shì bèi shēng：
the music giving forth these words:
「如來之尊建立是經，降伏眾魔，化諸外徑。
「rú lái zhī zūn jiàn lì shì jīng， xiáng fú zhòng mó， huà zhū wài jìng。
“The Honored Tathagata has established this sutra, subduing the hosts of Mara and converting the heterodox.
如來以印印此經已，順而不荒！」
rú lái yǐ yìn yìn cǐ jīng yǐ， shùn ér bù huāng！」
The Tathagata has sealed this sutra with his seal — accord with it and do not let it lie waste!”
爾時海龍王雨大珠瓔供養此經，
ěr shí hǎi lóng wáng yǔ dà zhū yīng gòng yǎng cǐ jīng，
Then the Sea Dragon King rained down great pearl garlands in offering to this sutra,
周遍三千大千世界。
zhōu biàn sān qiān dà qiān shì jiè。
filling the whole three-thousandfold great-thousand world.
佛告賢者阿難：「以是經卷用為囑累，
fó gào xián zhě ā nán： 「yǐ shì jīng juǎn yòng wéi zhǔ lèi，
The Buddha told the venerable Ananda: “This scripture I entrust to you;
受持諷誦，普令流布，為他人說。」
shòu chí fěng sòng， pǔ lìng liú bù， wèi tā rén shuō。」
receive it, uphold it, recite it, cause it to circulate everywhere, and preach it to others.”
阿難對曰：「唯然，世尊！已受斯經。
ā nán duì yuē： 「wéi rán， shì zūn！ yǐ shòu sī jīng。
Ananda replied: “So be it, World-Honored One. I have received this sutra.
經名何等？云何奉持？」
jīng míng hé děng？ yún hé fèng chí？」
By what name is it known, and how should it be upheld?”
佛言：「是經名曰『海龍王問龍總持品』，
fó yán： 「shì jīng míng yuē 『hǎi lóng wáng wèn lóng zǒng chí pǐn』，
The Buddha said: “This sutra is named ‘The Sea Dragon King's Questions: the Chapter of the Dragon's Dharani’,
又名『集諸法寶淨法門品』，當善奉持！」
yòu míng 『jí zhū fǎ bǎo jìng fǎ mén pǐn』， dāng shàn fèng chí！」
and also ‘The Chapter of the Pure Dharma Gate Gathering the Jewels of All Dharmas’. Uphold it well!”
佛說如是，海龍王及龍王子、諸天、人民、
fó shuō rú shì， hǎi lóng wáng jí lóng wáng zǐ、 zhū tiān、 rén mín、
Thus the Buddha spoke; and the Sea Dragon King and the dragon princes, the devas and people,
十方諸來大會菩薩、諸大聲聞、
shí fāng zhū lái dà huì pú sà、 zhū dà shēng wén、
the bodhisattvas come from the ten directions to the great assembly, the great sravakas,
釋、梵、四天王、賢者阿難、
shì、 fàn、 sì tiān wáng、 xián zhě ā nán、
Sakra, Brahma, the Four Heavenly Kings, the venerable Ananda,
一切魔眾、諸天、龍、神、香音神、
yī qiè mó zhòng、 zhū tiān、 lóng、 shén、 xiāng yīn shén、
all the hosts of Mara, the devas, dragons, spirits, gandharvas,
無善神、鳳凰神、山神、甜柔神、及世間人，
wú shàn shén、 fèng huáng shén、 shān shén、 tián róu shén、 jí shì jiān rén，
asuras, garudas, mountain spirits, kinnaras, and the people of the world,
莫不歡喜，作禮奉行。
mò bù huān xǐ， zuò lǐ fèng xíng。
all rejoiced, made obeisance, and undertook to practice it.
佛說海龍王經卷第四
fó shuō hǎi lóng wáng jīng juǎn dì sì
Here ends Volume Four of the Sutra Spoken by the Buddha on the Sea Dragon King.
